

################################################################################
# FICHIER: App.tsx
################################################################################

import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Toaster } from 'react-hot-toast';

// Auth
import { AuthProvider } from './lib/auth';
import { AbonnementProvider } from './lib/AbonnementContext';

// Abonnement
import { AbonnementGuard } from './abonnement/src/components/AbonnementGuard';
import { TrialBanner } from './abonnement/src/components/TrialBanner';
import AbonnementPage from './abonnement/src/pages/AbonnementPage';
import AbonnementUpgradePage from './abonnement/src/pages/AbonnementUpgradePage';
import AbonnementVirementPage from './abonnement/src/pages/VirementPage';
import PaypalSuccessPage from './abonnement/src/pages/PaypalSuccessPage';
import PaypalCancelPage from './abonnement/src/pages/PaypalCancelPage';
import AbonnementExpirePage from './abonnement/src/pages/AbonnementExpirePage';
import AbonnementDashboardPage from './abonnement/src/pages/DashboardPage';

// Auth pages
import LoginPage from './pages/LoginPage';
import RegisterPage from './pages/RegisterPage';

// École
import { EcoleLayout } from './components/layout/EcoleLayout';

import { Dashboard as EcoleDashboard } from './pages/Dashboard';
import { Parametres } from './pages/Parametres';
import { Registres } from './pages/Registres';
import { NotFound } from './pages/NotFound';

import { Analyses } from './pages/pedagogie/Analyses';
import { Bulletins } from './pages/pedagogie/Bulletins';
import { Classes } from './pages/pedagogie/Classes';
import { Devoirs } from './pages/pedagogie/Devoirs';
import { EmploiDuTemps } from './pages/pedagogie/EmploiDuTemps';
import { Notes } from './pages/pedagogie/Notes';
import { Presences } from './pages/pedagogie/Presences';

import { Admissions } from './pages/personnes/Admissions';
import { Eleves } from './pages/personnes/Eleves';
import { Enseignants } from './pages/personnes/Enseignants';
import { Parents } from './pages/personnes/Parents';

import { Bibliotheque } from './pages/services/Bibliotheque';
import { Cartes } from './pages/services/Cartes';
import { Discipline } from './pages/services/Discipline';
import { Finance } from './pages/services/Finance';
import { Infirmerie } from './pages/services/Infirmerie';
import { Patrimoine } from './pages/services/Patrimoine';
import { Transport } from './pages/services/Transport';
import { VieScolaire } from './pages/services/VieScolaire';

export default function App() {
  return (
    <BrowserRouter basename="/ecole">
      <AuthProvider>
        <AbonnementProvider>
          <Toaster position="top-right" />
          <TrialBanner />

          <Routes>
            {/* ── Routes publiques ───────────────────────────── */}
            <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
            <Route path="/signup" element={<RegisterPage />} />

            {/* ── Zone protégée (AbonnementGuard → EcoleLayout) ── */}
            <Route path="/" element={<AbonnementGuard />}>
              <Route element={<EcoleLayout />}>

                {/* Dashboard école */}
                <Route index element={<EcoleDashboard />} />

                {/* Dashboard abonnement */}
                <Route path="dashboard" element={<AbonnementDashboardPage />} />

                {/* Abonnement — accessible même si bloqué */}
                <Route path="abonnement">
                  <Route index element={<AbonnementPage />} />
                  <Route path="upgrade" element={<AbonnementUpgradePage />} />
                  <Route path="virement" element={<AbonnementVirementPage />} />
                  <Route path="paypal/succes" element={<PaypalSuccessPage />} />
                  <Route path="paypal/annule" element={<PaypalCancelPage />} />
                  <Route path="expire" element={<AbonnementExpirePage />} />
                </Route>

                {/* ── Module école ─────────────────────────── */}
                <Route path="eleves" element={<Eleves />} />
                <Route path="classes" element={<Classes />} />
                <Route path="enseignants" element={<Enseignants />} />
                <Route path="parents" element={<Parents />} />
                <Route path="emploi-du-temps" element={<EmploiDuTemps />} />
                <Route path="notes" element={<Notes />} />
                <Route path="bulletins" element={<Bulletins />} />
                <Route path="presences" element={<Presences />} />
                <Route path="devoirs" element={<Devoirs />} />
                <Route path="finance" element={<Finance />} />
                <Route path="admissions" element={<Admissions />} />
                <Route path="vie-scolaire" element={<VieScolaire />} />
                <Route path="infirmerie" element={<Infirmerie />} />
                <Route path="discipline" element={<Discipline />} />
                <Route path="bibliotheque" element={<Bibliotheque />} />
                <Route path="transport" element={<Transport />} />
                <Route path="patrimoine" element={<Patrimoine />} />
                <Route path="cartes" element={<Cartes />} />
                <Route path="analyses" element={<Analyses />} />
                <Route path="parametres" element={<Parametres />} />
                <Route path="registres" element={<Registres />} />

              </Route>
            </Route>

            {/* ── Fallback ───────────────────────────────────── */}
            <Route path="*" element={<NotFound />} />
          </Routes>
        </AbonnementProvider>
      </AuthProvider>
    </BrowserRouter>
  );
}

################################################################################
# FICHIER: abonnement/src/components/AbonnementGuard.tsx
################################################################################

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useLocation, useNavigate, Outlet } from 'react-router-dom';
import { LoaderCircleIcon, AlertCircleIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAbonnement } from '@/lib/AbonnementContext';

// Routes accessibles même si l'abonnement est bloqué (expiré / suspendu / introuvable)
const ALLOWED_WHEN_BLOCKED = [
  '/abonnement',
  '/abonnement/upgrade',
  '/abonnement/virement',
  '/abonnement/paypal/succes',
  '/abonnement/paypal/annule',
  '/abonnement/expire',
  '/parametres',
];

export function AbonnementGuard() {
  const { loading, status, isBloque, statutLive, error } = useAbonnement();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();

  const isOnAllowed = ALLOWED_WHEN_BLOCKED.some((p) =>
    location.pathname.startsWith(p)
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (loading && !status) return;
    if (isBloque && !isOnAllowed) {
      navigate('/abonnement/expire', {
        replace: true,
        state: {
          from: location.pathname,
          statut: statutLive,
        },
      });
    }
  }, [loading, status, isBloque, statutLive, isOnAllowed, location.pathname, navigate]);

  // ── Premier chargement — pas encore de statut connu ──────────────
  if (loading && !status) {
    return (
      <div className="ecole_m ecole_m-desktop flex min-h-[60vh] items-center justify-center">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 text-[var(--em-text-2)]">
          <LoaderCircleIcon className="h-7 w-7 animate-spin text-[var(--em-accent)]" />
          <p className="text-[13.5px] font-semibold">
            Vérification de votre abonnement…
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // ── Erreur réseau au tout premier chargement (pas de status) ─────
  if (error && !status) {
    return (
      <div className="ecole_m ecole_m-desktop flex min-h-[60vh] items-center justify-center p-6">
        <div className="ecole_m-card max-w-md p-6 text-center">
          <span className="mb-3 inline-grid h-12 w-12 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-[var(--em-danger-soft)] text-[var(--em-danger)]">
            <AlertCircleIcon className="h-6 w-6" />
          </span>
          <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-danger)]">
            Impossible de vérifier votre abonnement
          </p>
          <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-1 text-[12.5px]">{error}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // ── Abonnement bloqué : on affiche un loader le temps de la redirection
  if (!loading && status && isBloque && !isOnAllowed) {
    return (
      <div className="ecole_m ecole_m-desktop flex min-h-[60vh] items-center justify-center">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 text-[var(--em-text-2)]">
          <LoaderCircleIcon className="h-7 w-7 animate-spin text-[var(--em-accent)]" />
          <p className="text-[13.5px] font-semibold">Redirection…</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // ── Accès autorisé ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  return <Outlet />;
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: abonnement/src/components/PaiementsHistorique.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  CheckCircle2, Clock, XCircle, Receipt, Wallet, CreditCard,
  ChevronDown, FileText, ShieldCheck, ExternalLink,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { API_BASE } from '@/lib/api';
import { PaiementAbo } from '@/lib/api_routes/abonnement_api';
import { formatDateFR, formatDateTimeFR, formatFCFA } from '@/lib/utils';
import { Panel, EmptyState } from './ui/Panel';
import { Badge } from './ui/Badge';

const STATUT_BADGE: Record<PaiementAbo['statut'], { label: string; tone: 'success' | 'warning' | 'danger'; Icon: any }> = {
  confirme:   { label: 'Confirmé',   tone: 'success', Icon: CheckCircle2 },
  en_attente: { label: 'En attente', tone: 'warning', Icon: Clock },
  rejete:     { label: 'Rejeté',     tone: 'danger',  Icon: XCircle },
};

function preuveFilename(p: PaiementAbo): string | null {
  if (!p.preuve_url) return null;
  const segs = p.preuve_url.split('/');
  return segs[segs.length - 1] || null;
}
function preuveFullUrl(p: PaiementAbo): string | null {
  if (!p.preuve_url) return null;
  return p.preuve_url.startsWith('http') ? p.preuve_url : `${API_BASE}${p.preuve_url}`;
}

export function PaiementsHistorique({ paiements }: { paiements: PaiementAbo[] }) {
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<string | null>(null);

  return (
    <Panel
      title="Historique des paiements"
      subtitle={`${paiements.length} transaction${paiements.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}`}
      icon={<Receipt className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />}
    >
      {paiements.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="Aucun paiement enregistré"
          description="Vos paiements et reçus apparaîtront ici après le premier règlement."
          icon={<Wallet className="h-6 w-6" strokeWidth={1.8} />}
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {paiements.map((p, i) => {
            const sb = STATUT_BADGE[p.statut];
            const isOpen = expanded === p.id;
            const isPaypal = p.reference_virement?.startsWith('PAYPAL-') ?? false;
            const filename = preuveFilename(p);
            const fullUrl = preuveFullUrl(p);
            const isParEmploye = p.formule === 'par_employe';

            return (
              <div
                key={p.id}
                className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white"
                style={{ animationDelay: `${i * 40}ms`, animationFillMode: 'both' }}
              >
                <button
                  onClick={() => setExpanded(isOpen ? null : p.id)}
                  className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 p-3.5 text-left transition-colors hover:bg-[#F9F9F9]"
                >
                  <div
                    className="grid h-10 w-10 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg text-white"
                    style={{ background: isPaypal ? 'var(--em-info)' : 'var(--em-success)' }}
                  >
                    {isPaypal ? <CreditCard className="h-5 w-5" /> : <Wallet className="h-5 w-5" />}
                  </div>
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <div className="mb-0.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      <p className="text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">
                        {formatFCFA(p.montant_fcfa)}
                        <span className="ml-1.5 font-medium text-[var(--em-text-2)]">· {p.mois_couverts} mois</span>
                      </p>
                      <Badge tone={sb.tone}>{sb.label}</Badge>
                      <Badge tone="neutral">
                        {isParEmploye ? `Par employé${p.nb_employes ? ` (${p.nb_employes})` : ''}` : 'Forfait'}
                      </Badge>
                    </div>
                    <p className="truncate text-[11.5px] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">
                      {isPaypal ? 'Carte bancaire (PayPal)' : 'Virement bancaire'} · <span className="font-mono">{p.reference_virement || '—'}</span>
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="hidden shrink-0 text-right sm:block">
                    <p className="text-[11.5px] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">
                      {formatDateFR(p.created_at, { day: '2-digit', month: 'short' })}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <ChevronDown className={`h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-[var(--em-text-2)] transition-transform ${isOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`} />
                </button>

                {isOpen && (
                  <div className="space-y-3 border-t border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[#F9F9F9]/60 px-4 pb-4 pt-3">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-[12px]">
                      <Detail label="Montant" value={formatFCFA(p.montant_fcfa)} />
                      <Detail label="Mois couverts" value={`${p.mois_couverts}`} />
                      <Detail label="Formule" value={isParEmploye ? 'Par employé' : 'Forfait'} />
                      <Detail label="Nb employés" value={p.nb_employes !== null ? String(p.nb_employes) : '—'} />
                      <Detail label="Soumis le" value={formatDateTimeFR(p.created_at)} />
                      <Detail label="Confirmé le" value={p.confirme_le ? formatDateTimeFR(p.confirme_le) : '—'} />
                      <Detail label="Début période" value={formatDateFR(p.periode_debut)} />
                      <Detail label="Fin période" value={formatDateFR(p.periode_fin)} />
                    </div>

                    {p.note_entreprise && (
                      <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white p-3">
                        <p className="mb-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[.12em] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">Votre note</p>
                        <p className="text-[12px] text-[var(--em-text)]">{p.note_entreprise}</p>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {p.note_admin && (
                      <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-info)]/30 bg-[var(--em-info-soft)] p-3">
                        <p className="mb-1 inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[.12em] text-[var(--em-info)]">
                          <ShieldCheck className="h-3 w-3" /> Réponse administrateur
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-[12px] text-[var(--em-text)]">{p.note_admin}</p>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {filename && (
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white p-3">
                        <FileText className="h-4 w-4 text-[var(--em-text-2)]" />
                        <span className="flex-1 truncate font-mono text-[12px] text-[var(--em-text)]">{filename}</span>
                        {fullUrl && (
                          <a
                            href={fullUrl}
                            target="_blank"
                            rel="noopener noreferrer"
                            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-[var(--em-accent)] hover:underline"
                          >
                            Voir <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3" />
                          </a>
                        )}
                        <Badge tone="info">Preuve jointe</Badge>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </Panel>
  );
}

function Detail({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <p className="mb-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[.12em] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">{label}</p>
      <p className="text-[12px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">{value}</p>
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: abonnement/src/components/StatutAbonnementCard.tsx
################################################################################

import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  Sparkles, Clock, CheckCircle2, AlertOctagon, ArrowRight,
  CalendarDays, Wallet, Repeat, Users,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useAbonnement } from '@/lib/AbonnementContext';
import { formatDateFR, formatFCFA } from '@/lib/utils';
import { Button } from './ui/Button';
import { Badge, StatusDot } from './ui/Badge';

const STATUT_META: Record<string, {
  label: string; solid: string; bg: string; text: string; Icon: any;
}> = {
  trial:       { label: 'Essai gratuit', Icon: Sparkles,      solid: '#c19c00', bg: '#fff4ce', text: '#8a5300' },
  actif:       { label: 'Actif',         Icon: CheckCircle2,  solid: '#0e7a0d', bg: '#dff6dd', text: '#0e7a0d' },
  expire:      { label: 'Expiré',        Icon: AlertOctagon,  solid: '#c42b1c', bg: '#fde7e9', text: '#c42b1c' },
  suspendu:    { label: 'Suspendu',      Icon: AlertOctagon,  solid: '#5a6472', bg: '#eef1f5', text: '#34312a' },
  introuvable: { label: 'Indisponible',  Icon: AlertOctagon,  solid: '#8b95a3', bg: '#eef1f5', text: '#34312a' },
};

export function StatutAbonnementCard() {
  const { status, loading, statutLive } = useAbonnement();
  if (!status && !loading) return null;

  const m = STATUT_META[statutLive] ?? STATUT_META.introuvable;
  const isTrial = statutLive === 'trial';
  const isActif = statutLive === 'actif';
  const isExpire = statutLive === 'expire' || statutLive === 'suspendu';

  const days = isTrial ? status?.trial_jours_restants : status?.jours_restants_abo;
  const totalTrial = status?.trial_jours ?? 14;
  const progress =
    isTrial && days != null && totalTrial > 0
      ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, ((totalTrial - days) / totalTrial) * 100))
      : isActif && days != null
      ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, ((30 - Math.min(30, days)) / 30) * 100))
      : 100;

  const formule = status?.formule;
  const isParEmploye = formule === 'par_employe';
  const nbEmployes = status?.nb_employes_actifs ?? 0;
  const nbAutorises = status?.nb_employes_autorises;
  const prixUnitaire = status?.prix_par_employe_fcfa ?? 0;
  const prixForfait = status?.prix_forfait_fcfa ?? 0;
  const tarifMensuel = status?.prochain_montant_estime_fcfa ?? (isParEmploye ? 0 : prixForfait);
  const tarifHint = isParEmploye && nbAutorises ? `${formatFCFA(prixUnitaire)} × ${nbAutorises}` : undefined;
  const quotaLabel = nbAutorises != null ? `${nbEmployes} / ${nbAutorises}` : `${nbEmployes} / ∞`;

  return (
    <div className="ecole_m-card overflow-hidden">
      <div className="p-6 sm:p-7">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start gap-4">
          <div
            className="grid h-14 w-14 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-xl text-white shadow-[inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.2),0_6px_16px_rgba(0,0,0,0.08)]"
            style={{ background: m.solid }}
          >
            <m.Icon className="h-7 w-7" strokeWidth={2.2} />
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <div className="mb-1.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <Badge tone="neutral">Abonnement</Badge>
              <span
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[.12em]"
                style={{ background: m.bg, color: m.text }}
              >
                <StatusDot tone={isActif ? 'success' : isTrial ? 'accent' : 'danger'} pulse={isActif || isTrial} />
                {m.label}
              </span>
              {formule && (
                <Badge tone="info">
                  {isParEmploye ? `Par employé · ${quotaLabel}` : 'Forfait illimité'}
                </Badge>
              )}
            </div>

            <h1 className="ecole_m-display text-2xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.015em] text-[var(--em-text)] sm:text-3xl">
              {isTrial && <>Il vous reste <span className="tabular-nums text-[var(--em-accent)]">{days ?? '—'}</span> jour{(days ?? 0) > 1 ? 's' : ''} d'essai</>}
              {isActif && <>Abonnement actif jusqu'au <span className="text-[var(--em-accent)]">{formatDateFR(status?.date_prochaine_echeance)}</span></>}
              {isExpire && <>Votre abonnement a expiré</>}
            </h1>

            <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-1.5 max-w-xl text-[13.5px]">
              {isTrial && 'Profitez de toutes les fonctionnalités, sans engagement.'}
              {isActif && `${status?.mois_prepaye_restants ?? 0} mois prépayés à venir · Renouvellement ${status?.auto_renouvellement ? 'automatique' : 'manuel'}.`}
              {isExpire && "Pour reprendre l'accès à votre espace, réglez votre abonnement ci-dessous."}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-2 sm:w-auto sm:flex-row">
            {(isTrial || isExpire) && (
              <Link to="/abonnement/upgrade">
                <Button variant="primary" size="lg" rightIcon={<ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />}>
                  Activer maintenant
                </Button>
              </Link>
            )}
            {isActif && (
              <Link to="/abonnement/upgrade">
                <Button variant="primary" size="lg" rightIcon={<ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />}>
                  Prolonger
                </Button>
              </Link>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {(isTrial || isActif) && (
          <div className="mt-6">
            <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center justify-between text-[11.5px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text-2)]">
              <span className="uppercase tracking-[.12em]">{isTrial ? "Période d'essai" : 'Période courante'}</span>
              <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">{days ?? '—'} j restant{(days ?? 0) > 1 ? 's' : ''}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-black/[0.06]">
              <div
                className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-700 ease-out"
                style={{ width: `${progress}%`, background: m.solid }}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <KeyFact
            label={isParEmploye ? 'Estimation mensuelle' : 'Tarif mensuel'}
            value={formatFCFA(tarifMensuel)}
            Icon={Wallet}
            hint={tarifHint}
          />
          <KeyFact
            label={isTrial ? "Fin de l'essai" : 'Prochaine échéance'}
            value={formatDateFR(isTrial ? status?.trial_end : status?.date_prochaine_echeance)}
            Icon={CalendarDays}
          />
          <KeyFact
            label={isParEmploye ? 'Employés' : 'Mois prépayés'}
            value={isParEmploye ? quotaLabel : `${status?.mois_prepaye_restants ?? 0} restants`}
            Icon={isParEmploye ? Users : Clock}
            hint={
              isParEmploye && nbAutorises != null
                ? nbEmployes >= nbAutorises
                  ? 'Quota atteint'
                  : `${nbAutorises - nbEmployes} place${nbAutorises - nbEmployes > 1 ? 's' : ''} libre${nbAutorises - nbEmployes > 1 ? 's' : ''}`
                : undefined
            }
          />
          <KeyFact
            label="Renouvellement"
            value={status?.auto_renouvellement ? 'Automatique' : 'Manuel'}
            Icon={Repeat}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function KeyFact({ label, value, Icon, hint }: { label: string; value: string; Icon: any; hint?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[#F9F9F9] p-3.5">
      <div className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[var(--em-text-2)]">
        <Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={2.2} />
        <p className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[.12em]">{label}</p>
      </div>
      <p className="truncate text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">{value}</p>
      {hint && <p className="mt-0.5 truncate font-mono text-[11px] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">{hint}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: abonnement/src/components/TrialBanner.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Sparkles, Clock, ArrowRight, AlertTriangle, X, AlertOctagon } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAbonnement } from '@/lib/AbonnementContext';
import { formatDateFR } from '@/lib/utils';

const DISMISS_KEY = 'rh_trial_banner_dismissed_v1';

export function TrialBanner() {
  const { status, loading, isTrial, isActif, isBloque } = useAbonnement();
  const [dismissed, setDismissed] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setDismissed(sessionStorage.getItem(DISMISS_KEY) === '1');
  }, []);

  if (loading || !status) return null;
  const enEssai = isTrial || status.statut === 'trial';

  if (isBloque) {
    const fromTrial = !status.date_prochaine_echeance && (status.statut === 'expire' || status.statut === 'introuvable');
    return (
      <div className="border-b border-[var(--em-danger)]/30 bg-[var(--em-danger-soft)]">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-[1500px] items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 lg:px-8">
          <span className="grid h-8 w-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-[var(--em-danger)] text-white">
            <AlertOctagon className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />
          </span>
          <p className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-[13px] font-semibold leading-tight text-[var(--em-danger)]">
            {fromTrial ? 'Votre essai gratuit est terminé.' : 'Votre abonnement a expiré.'}
            <span className="ml-1 hidden font-medium sm:inline">Activez un abonnement pour continuer.</span>
          </p>
          <Link
            to="/abonnement/upgrade"
            className="inline-flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md bg-[var(--em-danger)] px-3 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:brightness-110"
          >
            Souscrire <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (enEssai) {
    const days = status.trial_jours_restants ?? 0;
    const urgent = days <= 3;
    if (dismissed && !urgent) return null;
    return (
      <div className={`border-b ${urgent ? 'border-[var(--em-danger)]/30 bg-[var(--em-danger-soft)]' : 'border-[var(--em-warning)]/30 bg-[var(--em-warning-soft)]'}`}>
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-[1500px] items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 lg:px-8">
          <span className={`grid h-8 w-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg text-white ${urgent ? 'bg-[var(--em-danger)]' : 'bg-[var(--em-warning)]'}`}>
            {urgent ? <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={2.4} /> : <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />}
          </span>
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <p className={`text-[13px] font-semibold leading-tight ${urgent ? 'text-[var(--em-danger)]' : 'text-[var(--em-warning)]'}`}>
              {urgent ? 'Votre essai gratuit se termine bientôt.' : 'Vous êtes en essai gratuit.'}
              <span className="ml-1.5 inline-flex items-center gap-1 opacity-90">
                <Clock className="h-3 w-3" />
                <span className="font-mono font-bold tabular-nums">{days} jour{days > 1 ? 's' : ''} restants</span>
                {status.trial_end && (
                  <span className="hidden sm:inline"> · expire le {formatDateFR(status.trial_end, { day: '2-digit', month: 'long' })}</span>
                )}
              </span>
            </p>
          </div>
          <Link to="/abonnement/upgrade" className="hidden h-8 shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md bg-[var(--em-text)] px-3 text-xs font-bold text-white sm:inline-flex">
            Activer <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </Link>
          {!urgent && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => { sessionStorage.setItem(DISMISS_KEY, '1'); setDismissed(true); }}
              className="grid h-7 w-7 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-md text-[var(--em-text-2)] hover:bg-black/[0.06]"
              aria-label="Masquer"
            >
              <X className="h-4 w-4" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (isActif && status.jours_restants_abo != null && status.jours_restants_abo <= 7) {
    if (dismissed) return null;
    const days = status.jours_restants_abo;
    return (
      <div className="border-b border-[var(--em-warning)]/30 bg-[var(--em-warning-soft)]">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-[1500px] items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2 lg:px-8">
          <Clock className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-[var(--em-warning)]" />
          <p className="flex-1 text-[12px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-warning)]">
            Renouvellement dans <span className="font-mono">{days} jour{days > 1 ? 's' : ''}</span>
            <span className="ml-1 hidden sm:inline"> · échéance {formatDateFR(status.date_prochaine_echeance, { day: '2-digit', month: 'long' })}</span>
          </p>
          <Link to="/abonnement/upgrade" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[12px] font-bold text-[var(--em-warning)] hover:underline">
            Prolonger <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3" />
          </Link>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => { sessionStorage.setItem(DISMISS_KEY, '1'); setDismissed(true); }}
            className="grid h-6 w-6 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-md text-[var(--em-warning)] hover:bg-black/[0.06]"
            aria-label="Masquer"
          >
            <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return null;
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: abonnement/src/components/ui/Badge.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { ReactNode } from 'react';

type Tone = 'neutral' | 'accent' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'danger' | 'info' | 'violet';

const TONES: Record<Tone, string> = {
  neutral: 'bg-[#eef1f5] text-[var(--em-text-2)]',
  accent: 'bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] text-[var(--em-accent)]',
  success: 'bg-[var(--em-success-soft)] text-[var(--em-success)]',
  warning: 'bg-[var(--em-warning-soft)] text-[var(--em-warning)]',
  danger: 'bg-[var(--em-danger-soft)] text-[var(--em-danger)]',
  info: 'bg-[var(--em-info-soft)] text-[var(--em-info)]',
  violet: 'bg-violet-100 text-violet-700',
};

export function Badge({
  tone = 'neutral',
  children,
  icon,
  className = '',
}: { tone?: Tone; children: ReactNode; icon?: ReactNode; className?: string; solid?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <span
      className={[
        'inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[.10em]',
        TONES[tone],
        className,
      ].join(' ')}
    >
      {icon}
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

export function StatusDot({ tone = 'neutral', pulse }: { tone?: Tone; pulse?: boolean }) {
  const colors: Record<Tone, string> = {
    neutral: '#8b95a3',
    accent: '#0067c0',
    success: '#0e7a0d',
    warning: '#8a5300',
    danger: '#c42b1c',
    info: '#005fb8',
    violet: '#8b5cf6',
  };
  return (
    <span className="relative inline-flex h-2 w-2">
      {pulse && (
        <span
          className="absolute inline-flex h-full w-full animate-ping rounded-full opacity-60"
          style={{ backgroundColor: colors[tone] }}
        />
      )}
      <span
        className="relative inline-flex h-2 w-2 rounded-full"
        style={{ backgroundColor: colors[tone] }}
      />
    </span>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: abonnement/src/components/ui/Button.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { forwardRef, ButtonHTMLAttributes, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { LoaderCircleIcon } from 'lucide-react';

type Variant = 'primary' | 'accent' | 'outline' | 'ghost' | 'danger' | 'dark';
type Size = 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';

interface Props extends ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: Variant;
  size?: Size;
  loading?: boolean;
  leftIcon?: ReactNode;
  rightIcon?: ReactNode;
  fullWidth?: boolean;
}

const BASE =
  'relative inline-flex select-none items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-[6px] border font-medium outline-none ' +
  'transition-[background-color,border-color,color,box-shadow,transform,filter] duration-150 ease-[cubic-bezier(0.23,1,0.32,1)] ' +
  'active:scale-[0.97] disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 ' +
  'focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--em-accent)] focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-white';

const VARIANTS: Record<Variant, string> = {
  primary:
    'border-black/10 bg-[var(--em-accent)] text-white ' +
    'shadow-[inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.14),inset_0_-1px_0_rgba(0,0,0,0.2),0_1px_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1)] ' +
    'hover:brightness-110 active:brightness-95',
  accent:
    'border-black/10 bg-[var(--em-accent)] text-white ' +
    'shadow-[inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.14),inset_0_-1px_0_rgba(0,0,0,0.2),0_1px_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1)] ' +
    'hover:brightness-110 active:brightness-95',
  dark: 'border-black/10 bg-[var(--em-text)] text-white hover:brightness-110',
  outline:
    'border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white text-[var(--em-text)] shadow-[inset_0_-1px_0_rgba(0,0,0,0.1)] hover:bg-[#F9F9F9]',
  ghost: 'border-transparent bg-transparent text-[var(--em-text)] hover:bg-black/[0.05]',
  danger:
    'border-black/10 bg-[var(--em-danger)] text-white ' +
    'shadow-[inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.12),inset_0_-1px_0_rgba(0,0,0,0.22)] hover:brightness-110',
};

const SIZES: Record<Size, string> = {
  sm: 'h-8 px-3 text-[13px] gap-1.5',
  md: 'h-9 px-4 text-[13.5px] gap-2',
  lg: 'h-11 px-5 text-[14px] gap-2',
};

export const Button = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, Props>(function Button(
  { variant = 'primary', size = 'md', loading, leftIcon, rightIcon, fullWidth, className = '', children, disabled, type = 'button', ...rest },
  ref
) {
  return (
    <button
      ref={ref}
      type={type}
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      aria-busy={loading || undefined}
      className={[BASE, VARIANTS[variant], SIZES[size], fullWidth ? 'w-full' : '', className].join(' ')}
      {...rest}
    >
      {loading ? <LoaderCircleIcon className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden /> : leftIcon}
      <span className="truncate">{children}</span>
      {!loading && rightIcon}
    </button>
  );
});


################################################################################
# FICHIER: abonnement/src/components/ui/Panel.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Inbox } from 'lucide-react';

interface PanelProps {
  title?: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  icon?: ReactNode;
  right?: ReactNode;
  children: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  bodyClassName?: string;
  noPadding?: boolean;
}

export function Panel({ title, subtitle, icon, right, children, className = '', bodyClassName = '', noPadding }: PanelProps) {
  return (
    <section className={`ecole_m-card overflow-hidden ${className}`}>
      {(title || icon || right) && (
        <header className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-[var(--em-border)] px-5 py-3.5">
          {icon && (
            <div className="grid h-9 w-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] text-[var(--em-accent)]">
              {icon}
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            {title && (
              <h3 className="ecole_m-display truncate text-[14px] font-semibold leading-tight text-[var(--em-text)]">
                {title}
              </h3>
            )}
            {subtitle && <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-0.5 truncate text-[12px]">{subtitle}</p>}
          </div>
          {right}
        </header>
      )}
      <div className={noPadding ? '' : `p-5 ${bodyClassName}`}>{children}</div>
    </section>
  );
}

export function EmptyState({
  title,
  description,
  action,
  icon,
}: { title: string; description?: string; action?: ReactNode; icon?: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-4 py-10 text-center">
      <div className="mb-4 grid h-14 w-14 place-items-center rounded-[14px] bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] text-[var(--em-accent)] ring-1 ring-[var(--em-accent)]/10">
        {icon ?? <Inbox className="h-6 w-6" strokeWidth={1.8} />}
      </div>
      <h4 className="ecole_m-display text-[15px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">{title}</h4>
      {description && <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-1.5 max-w-sm text-[13px]">{description}</p>}
      {action && <div className="mt-4">{action}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: abonnement/src/pages/AbonnementExpirePage.tsx
################################################################################

import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AlertOctagon, ArrowRight, Sparkles, ShieldOff, Database, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '../components/ui/Button';
import { Badge } from '../components/ui/Badge';
import { useAbonnement } from '@/lib/AbonnementContext';
import { formatDateFR } from '@/lib/utils';

export default function AbonnementExpirePage() {
  const { status, statutLive } = useAbonnement();
  const fromTrial = !status?.date_prochaine_echeance;
  const isSuspendu = statutLive === 'suspendu';
  const isIntrouvable = statutLive === 'introuvable';

  return (
    <div className="ecole_m ecole_m-desktop flex min-h-[70vh] items-center justify-center p-6">
      <div className="w-full max-w-2xl">
        <div className="ecole_m-card overflow-hidden">
          <div className="p-8 sm:p-10">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start gap-5">
              <div className="grid h-16 w-16 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-2xl bg-[var(--em-danger)] text-white">
                <AlertOctagon className="h-9 w-9" strokeWidth={2.2} />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <Badge tone="danger" className="mb-2">
                  {isSuspendu ? 'Abonnement suspendu' : isIntrouvable ? 'Abonnement introuvable' : fromTrial ? 'Essai terminé' : 'Abonnement expiré'}
                </Badge>
                <h1 className="ecole_m-display text-3xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] text-[var(--em-text)] sm:text-4xl">
                  {fromTrial ? <>Votre essai gratuit est <span className="text-[var(--em-danger)]">terminé</span></> : isSuspendu ? <>Votre abonnement est <span className="text-[var(--em-danger)]">suspendu</span></> : <>Votre abonnement a <span className="text-[var(--em-danger)]">expiré</span></>}
                </h1>
                <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-2 text-[14px]">
                  {fromTrial && 'Pour continuer, activez votre abonnement. Vos données sont conservées en lieu sûr.'}
                  {!fromTrial && !isSuspendu && `Votre dernier paiement couvrait la période jusqu'au ${formatDateFR(status?.date_prochaine_echeance)}. Régularisez votre situation pour reprendre l'accès.`}
                  {isSuspendu && "Contactez notre support pour rétablir votre accès."}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-7 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
              <ReassureCard Icon={Database} title="Données préservées" subtitle="Vos employés, pointages et bulletins restent intacts." />
              <ReassureCard Icon={Clock} title="Reprise immédiate" subtitle="Activation instantanée après paiement par carte." />
              <ReassureCard Icon={ShieldOff} title="Aucune perte" subtitle="Sauvegardes quotidiennes conservées 90 jours." />
            </div>

            <div className="mt-7 flex flex-col gap-3 border-t border-[var(--em-border)] pt-6 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
              <Link to="/abonnement/upgrade" className="flex-1">
                <Button variant="primary" size="lg" fullWidth rightIcon={<ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />}>
                  Activer mon abonnement
                </Button>
              </Link>
              <Link to="/abonnement">
                <Button variant="outline" size="lg">Consulter mes paiements</Button>
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ReassureCard({ Icon, title, subtitle }: { Icon: any; title: string; subtitle: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white p-4">
      <div className="mb-3 grid h-10 w-10 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] text-[var(--em-accent)]">
        <Icon className="h-5 w-5" strokeWidth={2.2} />
      </div>
      <p className="text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">{title}</p>
      <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-0.5 text-[12px]">{subtitle}</p>
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: abonnement/src/pages/AbonnementPage.tsx
################################################################################

import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowRight, RefreshCwIcon, Receipt, History, Repeat, AlertTriangleIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAbonnement } from '@/lib/AbonnementContext';
import { formatDateFR } from '@/lib/utils';
import { StatutAbonnementCard } from '../components/StatutAbonnementCard';
import { PaiementsHistorique } from '../components/PaiementsHistorique';
import { Panel, EmptyState } from '../components/ui/Panel';
import { Button } from '../components/ui/Button';
import { Badge } from '../components/ui/Badge';

export default function AbonnementPage() {
  const { status, loading, error, refresh, isActif } = useAbonnement();

  if (loading && !status) {
    return (
      <div className="ecole_m ecole_m-desktop space-y-6 p-4 md:p-6">
        <div className="h-48 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-black/[0.04]" />
        <div className="h-64 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-black/[0.04]" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error && !status) {
    return (
      <div className="ecole_m ecole_m-desktop p-4 md:p-6">
        <Panel title="Abonnement" icon={<Receipt className="h-4 w-4" />}>
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 py-8 text-center">
            <span className="grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-[var(--em-danger-soft)] text-[var(--em-danger)]">
              <AlertTriangleIcon className="h-5 w-5" />
            </span>
            <p className="text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-danger)]">Impossible de charger votre abonnement</p>
            <p className="ecole_m-muted text-[12.5px]">{error}</p>
            <Button variant="primary" size="sm" leftIcon={<RefreshCwIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />} onClick={() => refresh()}>
              Réessayer
            </Button>
          </div>
        </Panel>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const paiements = status?.paiements ?? [];
  const historiqueFormule = status?.historique_formule ?? [];

  return (
    <div className="ecole_m ecole_m-desktop p-4 md:p-6 lg:p-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl space-y-6">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h1 className="ecole_m-display text-[24px] font-semibold tracking-[-0.015em] text-[var(--em-text)]">Mon abonnement</h1>
            <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-0.5 text-[13.5px]">Statut, échéances et historique de paiement.</p>
          </div>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" leftIcon={<RefreshCwIcon className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 ${loading ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />} onClick={() => refresh()} disabled={loading}>
            Actualiser
          </Button>
        </div>

        <StatutAbonnementCard />

        <Panel title="Gérer mon abonnement" subtitle="Activer, prolonger ou changer de formule" icon={<Repeat className="h-4 w-4" />}>
          <Link to="/abonnement/upgrade" className="inline-block">
            <Button variant="primary" size="lg" rightIcon={<ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />}>
              {isActif ? 'Prolonger mon abonnement' : 'Activer mon abonnement'}
            </Button>
          </Link>
        </Panel>

        <PaiementsHistorique paiements={paiements} />

        {historiqueFormule.length > 0 && (
          <Panel title="Historique des formules" subtitle={`${historiqueFormule.length} changement${historiqueFormule.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}`} icon={<History className="h-4 w-4" />}>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {historiqueFormule.map((h, i) => (
                <div key={`${h.date}-${i}`} className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white p-3.5">
                  <div className="grid h-9 w-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-[var(--em-text)] text-white">
                    <Repeat className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      <Badge tone="neutral">{h.ancienne === 'par_employe' ? 'Par employé' : 'Forfait'}</Badge>
                      <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-[var(--em-text-2)]" />
                      <Badge tone="info">{h.nouvelle === 'par_employe' ? `Par employé${h.nb_employes ? ` (${h.nb_employes})` : ''}` : 'Forfait'}</Badge>
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-0.5 text-[11.5px] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">{formatDateFR(h.date)}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </Panel>
        )}

        {!loading && status && paiements.length === 0 && historiqueFormule.length === 0 && (
          <Panel title="Historique" icon={<Receipt className="h-4 w-4" />}>
            <EmptyState title="Aucun historique pour le moment" description="Vos paiements et changements de formule apparaîtront ici." />
          </Panel>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: abonnement/src/pages/AbonnementUpgradePage.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import '@/abonnement/src/abonnement.css';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  ArrowLeft, CreditCard, Wallet, Check, Sparkles, ShieldCheck,
  TrendingDown, ArrowRight, Globe2, Users, Layers,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  abonnementTarif,
  abonnementPaypalCreerOrdre,
  TarifResponse,
  FormuleAbo,
  PackForfait,
  PalierOption,
  DureeOption,
  PalierEmploye,
} from '@/lib/api_routes/abonnement_api';
import { Panel } from '../components/ui/Panel';
import { Button } from '../components/ui/Button';
import { Badge } from '../components/ui/Badge';
import { cn, formatEUR, formatFCFA, fcfaToEur } from '@/lib/utils';

type Method = 'paypal' | 'virement';

export default function AbonnementUpgradePage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [tarif, setTarif] = useState<TarifResponse | null>(null);
  const [formule, setFormule] = useState<FormuleAbo>('forfait');
  const [selectedMois, setSelectedMois] = useState<number>(3);
  const [selectedPalier, setSelectedPalier] = useState<PalierEmploye>(3);
  const [method, setMethod] = useState<Method>('virement');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        const t = await abonnementTarif();
        setTarif(t);
        setFormule(t.formule_active);
      } catch (e: any) {
        setError(e?.message || 'Impossible de charger les tarifs');
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    })();
  }, []);

  // ── Données dérivées selon la formule ─────────────────────────
  const forfaitPacks: PackForfait[] = useMemo(
    () => tarif?.formules.forfait.packs ?? [],
    [tarif],
  );

  const paliers: PalierOption[] = useMemo(
    () => tarif?.formules.par_employe.paliers ?? [],
    [tarif],
  );

  const selectedPalierObj: PalierOption | undefined = useMemo(
    () => paliers.find((p) => p.nb_employes === selectedPalier),
    [paliers, selectedPalier],
  );

  const dureesParEmploye: DureeOption[] = selectedPalierObj?.durees ?? [];

  // Option courante (durée sélectionnée dans la formule active)
  const currentForfait =
    formule === 'forfait'
      ? forfaitPacks.find((p) => p.mois === selectedMois) ?? null
      : null;

  const currentDuree =
    formule === 'par_employe'
      ? dureesParEmploye.find((d) => d.mois === selectedMois) ?? null
      : null;

  const totalFcfa =
    currentForfait?.total_fcfa ?? currentDuree?.total_fcfa ?? 0;

  const prixMensuelEffectif =
    currentForfait?.prix_mois_fcfa ??
    currentDuree?.prix_mois_fcfa ??
    (selectedMois > 0 ? Math.round(totalFcfa / selectedMois) : 0);

  const hasSelection =
    (formule === 'forfait' && !!currentForfait) ||
    (formule === 'par_employe' && !!currentDuree);

  // Dépassement de quota : uniquement par_employe, désormais sur le palier
  const depassement =
    formule === 'par_employe' && selectedPalierObj?.warning_depassement.actif
      ? selectedPalierObj.warning_depassement.message
      : null;

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    if (!hasSelection) return;
    setError(null);

    if (formule === 'par_employe' && depassement) {
      setError(depassement);
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      if (method === 'paypal') {
        const ordre = await abonnementPaypalCreerOrdre({
          mois: selectedMois,
          formule,
          nb_employes: formule === 'par_employe' ? selectedPalier : null,
        });
        window.location.href = ordre.approve_url;
      } else {
        const qs = new URLSearchParams({
          mois: String(selectedMois),
          formule,
        });
        if (formule === 'par_employe') {
          qs.set('nb_employes', String(selectedPalier));
        }
        navigate(`/app/abonnement/virement?${qs.toString()}`);
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      setError(e?.message || 'Une erreur est survenue. Réessayez.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="abonnement-scope space-y-6">
      {/* HERO */}
      <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-ink-200/70 bg-paper shadow-raise">
        <div
          className="pointer-events-none absolute -top-32 -right-20 w-[420px] h-[420px] rounded-full opacity-50 blur-3xl"
          style={{ background: 'radial-gradient(circle, #F5C51866 0%, transparent 60%)' }}
        />
        <div
          className="pointer-events-none absolute -bottom-24 -left-16 w-[320px] h-[320px] rounded-full opacity-30 blur-3xl"
          style={{ background: 'radial-gradient(circle, #F5C51844 0%, transparent 60%)' }}
        />
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-dotted opacity-40" />
        <div className="relative px-6 sm:px-8 py-6 sm:py-7">
          <Link
            to="/abonnement"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-ink-500 hover:text-ink-900 mb-4">
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Retour à l'abonnement
          </Link>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-ink-900 text-white text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[.16em] shadow-raise mb-3">
            <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-accent-400 animate-pulse" />
            Activation · Étape 1 sur 2
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-black text-ink-900 tracking-tight leading-tight">
            Choisissez votre{' '}
            <span className="relative inline-block">
              <span className="relative z-10">formule</span>
              <span
                className="absolute left-0 right-0 bottom-1 h-2 -z-0 rounded-sm opacity-60"
                style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, #F5C518 0%, transparent 100%)' }}
              />
            </span>
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-ink-500 max-w-2xl">
            Forfait illimité ou facturation par employé · 1, 3, 6 ou 12 mois prépayés · paiement carte ou virement.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
          {/* Sélection de la formule */}
          <Panel
            title="Type de formule"
            subtitle="Choisissez le modèle qui vous convient"
            icon={<Layers className="w-4 h-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />}>
            {loading || !tarif ? (
              <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div className="h-32 rounded-xl bg-ink-100 animate-shimmer" />
                <div className="h-32 rounded-xl bg-ink-100 animate-shimmer" />
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <FormuleCard
                  active={formule === 'forfait'}
                  onClick={() => setFormule('forfait')}
                  Icon={Sparkles}
                  title={tarif.formules.forfait.label}
                  subtitle={tarif.formules.forfait.description}
                  hint={`${formatFCFA(tarif.prix_forfait_fcfa)} / mois`}
                />
                <FormuleCard
                  active={formule === 'par_employe'}
                  onClick={() => setFormule('par_employe')}
                  Icon={Users}
                  title={tarif.formules.par_employe.label}
                  subtitle={tarif.formules.par_employe.description}
                  hint={`${formatFCFA(tarif.prix_par_employe_fcfa)} / employé / mois · ${tarif.nb_employes_actifs} actuel${
                    tarif.nb_employes_actifs > 1 ? 's' : ''
                  }`}
                />
              </div>
            )}
          </Panel>

          {/* PALIER — uniquement pour par_employe */}
          {formule === 'par_employe' && (
            <Panel
              title="Nombre d'employés"
              subtitle={tarif?.formules.par_employe.etape_1 ?? "Choisissez votre palier"}
              icon={<Users className="w-4 h-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />}>
              {loading ? (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
                  {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
                    <div key={i} className="h-24 rounded-xl bg-ink-100 animate-shimmer" />
                  ))}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
                  {paliers.map((p) => (
                    <PalierCard
                      key={p.nb_employes}
                      palier={p}
                      selected={selectedPalier === p.nb_employes}
                      onClick={() => setSelectedPalier(p.nb_employes)}
                    />
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
              {selectedPalierObj?.warning_depassement.actif && (
                <div className="mt-3 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-amber-900">
                  ⚠︎ {selectedPalierObj.warning_depassement.message}
                </div>
              )}
            </Panel>
          )}

          {/* Durée */}
          <Panel
            title="Durée d'engagement"
            subtitle={
              formule === 'par_employe'
                ? (tarif?.formules.par_employe.etape_2 ?? "Choisissez votre durée d'engagement")
                : 'Plus la durée est longue, mieux vous êtes couvert'
            }
            icon={<Sparkles className="w-4 h-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />}>
            {loading ? (
              <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="h-32 rounded-xl bg-ink-100 animate-shimmer" />
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : formule === 'forfait' ? (
              <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                {forfaitPacks.map((p) => (
                  <ForfaitPackCard
                    key={p.mois}
                    pack={p}
                    selected={selectedMois === p.mois}
                    onClick={() => setSelectedMois(p.mois)}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                {dureesParEmploye.map((d) => (
                  <DureeCard
                    key={d.mois}
                    duree={d}
                    nbEmployes={selectedPalier}
                    selected={selectedMois === d.mois}
                    onClick={() => setSelectedMois(d.mois)}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </Panel>

          {/* Méthode */}
          <Panel
            title="Mode de paiement"
            subtitle="Choisissez votre méthode préférée"
            icon={<CreditCard className="w-4 h-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />}>
            <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <MethodCard
                active={method === 'virement'}
                onClick={() => setMethod('virement')}
                Icon={Wallet}
                title="Virement bancaire"
                subtitle="Local · Devise FCFA"
                bullets={['Téléchargez le RIB', 'Joindre une preuve', 'Validation sous 24h ouvrées']}
                gradient="linear-gradient(135deg, #FFE26B 0%, #F5C518 100%)"
                solid="#F5C518"
                light
              />
            </div>
          </Panel>
        </div>

        {/* Sticky récap */}
        <div>
          <div className="lg:sticky lg:top-24 space-y-4">
            <Panel
              title="Récapitulatif"
              subtitle="Vérifiez avant de payer"
              icon={<Check className="w-4 h-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />}>
              <div className="space-y-3">
                <Row
                  label="Formule"
                  value={
                    formule === 'forfait'
                      ? 'Forfait illimité'
                      : `Par employé · ${selectedPalier} max`
                  }
                />
                <Row label="Durée" value={hasSelection ? `${selectedMois} mois` : '—'} small />
                <Row
                  label="Tarif mensuel"
                  value={hasSelection ? formatFCFA(prixMensuelEffectif) : '—'}
                  small
                />
                <Row
                  label="Méthode"
                  value={method === 'paypal' ? 'Carte bancaire' : 'Virement bancaire'}
                  small
                />
                {method === 'paypal' && hasSelection && (
                  <Row
                    label="Équivalent EUR"
                    value={formatEUR(fcfaToEur(totalFcfa))}
                    small
                    muted
                  />
                )}
                <div className="border-t border-ink-200/60 pt-3 mt-2">
                  <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
                    <span className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[.14em] font-semibold text-ink-500">
                      Total à régler
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-display text-2xl font-black text-ink-900 tabular-nums">
                      {hasSelection ? formatFCFA(totalFcfa) : '—'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {depassement && (
                <div className="mt-4 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-amber-900">
                  ⚠︎ {depassement}
                </div>
              )}

              {error && (
                <div className="mt-4 rounded-lg bg-red-50 border border-red-200 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-red-700">
                  {error}
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="mt-5 space-y-2">
                <Button
                  variant="accent"
                  fullWidth
                  size="lg"
                  loading={submitting}
                  rightIcon={!submitting ? <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" /> : undefined}
                  onClick={handleSubmit}
                  disabled={!hasSelection || loading || !!depassement}>
                  {method === 'paypal' ? 'Payer par carte' : 'Procéder au virement'}
                </Button>
                <Link to="/abonnement">
                  <Button variant="ghost" fullWidth size="sm">
                    Annuler
                  </Button>
                </Link>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-5 pt-5 border-t border-ink-200/60 space-y-2 text-[11px] text-ink-500">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" /> Paiement sécurisé
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Globe2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-600" /> Conversion EUR au taux BCEAO fixe
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-accent-600" /> Accès immédiat après confirmation
                </div>
              </div>
            </Panel>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Sous-composants
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function FormuleCard({
  active, onClick, Icon, title, subtitle, hint,
}: { active: boolean; onClick: () => void; Icon: any; title: string; subtitle: string; hint: string }) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className={cn(
        'group relative text-left rounded-2xl p-5 border-2 transition-all duration-200',
        'hover:-translate-y-0.5',
        active
          ? 'border-accent-500 bg-gradient-to-b from-accent-100/80 to-paper shadow-glow-y'
          : 'border-ink-200 bg-paper hover:border-ink-300 shadow-flat',
      )}>
      {active && (
        <span
          className="absolute top-3 right-3 w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white"
          style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #FFE26B 0%, #D9A60D 100%)' }}>
          <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" strokeWidth={3} />
        </span>
      )}
      <div
        className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-ink-900 shadow-flat mb-3"
        style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #FFE26B 0%, #F5C518 100%)' }}>
        <Icon className="w-6 h-6" strokeWidth={2.2} />
      </div>
      <p className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-ink-900">{title}</p>
      <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-500 mt-0.5">{subtitle}</p>
      <p className="text-[12px] font-mono font-semibold text-ink-700 mt-2">{hint}</p>
    </button>
  );
}

function PalierCard({
  palier, selected, onClick,
}: { palier: PalierOption; selected: boolean; onClick: () => void }) {
  const depasse = palier.warning_depassement.actif;
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className={cn(
        'group relative text-left rounded-2xl p-4 border-2 transition-all duration-200',
        'hover:-translate-y-0.5',
        selected
          ? 'border-accent-500 bg-gradient-to-b from-accent-100/80 to-paper shadow-glow-y'
          : 'border-ink-200 bg-paper hover:border-ink-300 shadow-flat',
      )}>
      {selected && (
        <span
          className="absolute top-2 right-2 w-5 h-5 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white"
          style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #FFE26B 0%, #D9A60D 100%)' }}>
          <Check className="w-3 h-3" strokeWidth={3} />
        </span>
      )}
      {palier.populaire && !selected && (
        <Badge tone="info" className="absolute -top-2 right-2">Populaire</Badge>
      )}
      <p className="font-display text-2xl font-black text-ink-900 leading-none">
        {palier.nb_employes}
      </p>
      <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[.14em] font-bold text-ink-500 mt-1">
        {palier.label}
      </p>
      {depasse && (
        <p className="mt-2 text-[10px] font-semibold text-amber-700 inline-flex items-start gap-1">
          ⚠︎ Quota dépassé
        </p>
      )}
    </button>
  );
}

function ForfaitPackCard({
  pack, selected, onClick,
}: { pack: PackForfait; selected: boolean; onClick: () => void }) {
  const prixMensuel = pack.prix_mois_fcfa ?? Math.round(pack.total_fcfa / pack.mois);
  const isBest = pack.mois === 12;
  const isPopular = pack.populaire;
  const hasOffert = pack.mois_offerts > 0;
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className={cn(
        'group relative text-left rounded-2xl p-5 border-2 transition-all duration-200',
        'hover:-translate-y-0.5',
        selected
          ? 'border-accent-500 bg-gradient-to-b from-accent-100/80 to-paper shadow-glow-y'
          : 'border-ink-200 bg-paper hover:border-ink-300 shadow-flat',
      )}>
      {selected && (
        <span
          className="absolute top-3 right-3 w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white"
          style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #FFE26B 0%, #D9A60D 100%)' }}>
          <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" strokeWidth={3} />
        </span>
      )}
      {isBest && !selected && (
        <Badge tone="accent" className="absolute -top-2 right-3">
          <TrendingDown className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> Meilleure couverture
        </Badge>
      )}
      {isPopular && !isBest && !selected && (
        <Badge tone="info" className="absolute -top-2 right-3">Populaire</Badge>
      )}
      <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[.14em] font-bold text-ink-500">
        {pack.label || 'Pack'}
      </p>
      <p className="font-display text-3xl font-black text-ink-900 mt-1 leading-none">
        {pack.mois} <span className="text-base font-bold text-ink-500">mois</span>
      </p>
      {hasOffert && (
        <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-emerald-700 mt-1.5 inline-flex items-center gap-1">
          🎁 {pack.mois_offerts} mois offert{pack.mois_offerts > 1 ? 's' : ''}
        </p>
      )}
      <p className="mt-3 text-xl font-black tabular-nums text-ink-900">
        {formatFCFA(pack.total_fcfa)}
      </p>
      <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-500 mt-0.5">
        soit{' '}
        <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-ink-700">{formatFCFA(prixMensuel)}</span> /mois
      </p>
      {pack.detail && (
        <p className="text-[10px] font-mono text-ink-500 mt-1 truncate" title={pack.detail}>
          {pack.detail}
        </p>
      )}
    </button>
  );
}

function DureeCard({
  duree, nbEmployes, selected, onClick,
}: { duree: DureeOption; nbEmployes: number; selected: boolean; onClick: () => void }) {
  const prixMensuel = duree.prix_mois_fcfa ?? Math.round(duree.total_fcfa / duree.mois);
  const isBest = duree.mois === 12;
  const isPopular = duree.mois === 3;
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className={cn(
        'group relative text-left rounded-2xl p-5 border-2 transition-all duration-200',
        'hover:-translate-y-0.5',
        selected
          ? 'border-accent-500 bg-gradient-to-b from-accent-100/80 to-paper shadow-glow-y'
          : 'border-ink-200 bg-paper hover:border-ink-300 shadow-flat',
      )}>
      {selected && (
        <span
          className="absolute top-3 right-3 w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white"
          style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #FFE26B 0%, #D9A60D 100%)' }}>
          <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" strokeWidth={3} />
        </span>
      )}
      {isBest && !selected && (
        <Badge tone="accent" className="absolute -top-2 right-3">
          <TrendingDown className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> Meilleur tarif
        </Badge>
      )}
      {isPopular && !isBest && !selected && (
        <Badge tone="info" className="absolute -top-2 right-3">Populaire</Badge>
      )}
      <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[.14em] font-bold text-ink-500">
        Engagement {duree.mois} mois
      </p>
      <p className="font-display text-3xl font-black text-ink-900 mt-1 leading-none">
        {duree.mois} <span className="text-base font-bold text-ink-500">mois</span>
      </p>
      <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-ink-700 mt-1.5 inline-flex items-center gap-1">
        <Users className="w-3 h-3" /> {nbEmployes} employé{nbEmployes > 1 ? 's' : ''} max
      </p>
      <p className="mt-3 text-xl font-black tabular-nums text-ink-900">
        {formatFCFA(duree.total_fcfa)}
      </p>
      <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-500 mt-0.5">
        soit{' '}
        <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-ink-700">{formatFCFA(prixMensuel)}</span> /mois
      </p>
      {duree.detail && (
        <p className="text-[10px] font-mono text-ink-500 mt-1 truncate" title={duree.detail}>
          {duree.detail}
        </p>
      )}
    </button>
  );
}

function MethodCard({
  active, onClick, Icon, title, subtitle, bullets, gradient, solid, tag, light,
}: { active: boolean; onClick: () => void; Icon: any; title: string; subtitle: string; bullets: string[]; gradient: string; solid: string; tag?: string; light?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className={cn(
        'group relative text-left rounded-2xl p-5 border-2 transition-all duration-200',
        'hover:-translate-y-0.5',
        active
          ? 'border-accent-500 bg-gradient-to-b from-accent-100/60 to-paper shadow-glow-y'
          : 'border-ink-200 bg-paper hover:border-ink-300 shadow-flat',
      )}>
      {tag && (
        <Badge tone="success" className="absolute -top-2 right-3">
          {tag}
        </Badge>
      )}
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <div
          className="shrink-0 w-12 h-12 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center shadow-flat"
          style={{ background: gradient, color: light ? '#0B0A07' : '#fff', boxShadow: `0 6px 14px -4px ${solid}80` }}>
          <Icon className="w-6 h-6" strokeWidth={2.2} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <p className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-ink-900">{title}</p>
          <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-500">{subtitle}</p>
        </div>
        {active && (
          <span
            className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white shrink-0"
            style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #FFE26B 0%, #D9A60D 100%)' }}>
            <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" strokeWidth={3} />
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      <ul className="mt-4 space-y-1.5">
        {bullets.map((b) => (
          <li key={b} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-[12px] text-ink-700">
            <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" strokeWidth={2.5} />
            <span>{b}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </button>
  );
}

function Row({
  label, value, small, muted,
}: { label: string; value: string; small?: boolean; muted?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
      <span
        className={cn(
          'uppercase tracking-[.14em] font-semibold',
          small ? 'text-[10px] text-ink-500' : 'text-[11px] text-ink-500',
        )}>
        {label}
      </span>
      <span
        className={cn(
          'font-semibold tabular-nums',
          small ? 'text-[12px]' : 'text-sm',
          muted ? 'text-ink-500' : 'text-ink-900',
        )}>
        {value}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}
