################################################################################
# FICHIER: abonnement/src/pages/PaypalSuccessPage.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Check, LoaderCircleIcon, AlertOctagon, ArrowRight, CreditCard, ShieldCheck, CalendarDays } from 'lucide-react';
import { abonnementPaypalCapturer, CapturerPaypalResult } from '@/lib/api_routes/abonnement_api';
import { Button } from '../components/ui/Button';
import { Badge } from '../components/ui/Badge';
import { useAbonnement } from '@/lib/AbonnementContext';
import { formatDateFR, formatFCFA } from '@/lib/utils';

type State = 'loading' | 'success' | 'error';

export default function PaypalSuccessPage() {
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const ordreId = params.get('token') || params.get('ordre_id') || '';
  const { refresh } = useAbonnement();
  const [state, setState] = useState<State>('loading');
  const [result, setResult] = useState<CapturerPaypalResult | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    (async () => {
      if (!ordreId) { setError('Référence de paiement manquante'); setState('error'); return; }
      try {
        const r = await abonnementPaypalCapturer(ordreId);
        if (cancelled) return;
        await refresh();
        setResult(r);
        setState('success');
      } catch (e: any) {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setError(e?.message || 'La capture du paiement a échoué');
        setState('error');
      }
    })();
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [ordreId, refresh]);

  const formuleLabel = result?.formule === 'par_employe' ? `par employé${result.nb_employes ? ` · ${result.nb_employes}` : ''}` : 'forfait illimité';

  return (
    <div className="ecole_m ecole_m-desktop flex min-h-[70vh] items-center justify-center p-6">
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg">
        <div className="ecole_m-card overflow-hidden p-8 text-center">
          {state === 'loading' && (
            <>
              <div className="mb-5 inline-flex h-20 w-20 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[var(--em-info-soft)]">
                <LoaderCircleIcon className="h-10 w-10 animate-spin text-[var(--em-info)]" />
              </div>
              <Badge tone="info" className="mb-3">Traitement en cours</Badge>
              <h2 className="ecole_m-display text-2xl font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">Confirmation du paiement…</h2>
              <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-2 text-[13px]">Ne fermez pas cette fenêtre.</p>
              <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-[12px] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">
                <ShieldCheck className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-[var(--em-success)]" /> Connexion sécurisée
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          {state === 'success' && result && (
            <>
              <div className="mb-5 inline-flex h-20 w-20 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[var(--em-success)]">
                <Check className="h-10 w-10 text-white" strokeWidth={3} />
              </div>
              <Badge tone="success" className="mb-3">Paiement confirmé</Badge>
              <h2 className="ecole_m-display text-2xl font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">Bienvenue parmi nous</h2>
              <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-2 text-[13px]">
                Votre abonnement <span className="font-bold text-[var(--em-text)]">{result.mois_couverts} mois</span> ({formuleLabel}) est désormais actif.
              </p>
              <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <SuccessFact label="Montant payé" value={formatFCFA(result.montant_fcfa)} Icon={CreditCard} />
                <SuccessFact label="Prochaine échéance" value={formatDateFR(result.nouvelle_echeance)} Icon={CalendarDays} />
              </div>
              <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center gap-2">
                <Link to="/"><Button variant="primary" size="lg" rightIcon={<ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />}>Accéder à mon espace</Button></Link>
                <Link to="/abonnement"><Button variant="outline" size="lg">Voir le reçu</Button></Link>
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          {state === 'error' && (
            <>
              <div className="mb-5 inline-flex h-20 w-20 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[var(--em-danger)]">
                <AlertOctagon className="h-10 w-10 text-white" strokeWidth={2.4} />
              </div>
              <Badge tone="danger" className="mb-3">Échec de la capture</Badge>
              <h2 className="ecole_m-display text-2xl font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">Paiement non finalisé</h2>
              <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-2 text-[13px]">{error || "Une erreur est survenue. Aucun montant n'a été débité."}</p>
              <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center gap-2">
                <Link to="/abonnement/upgrade"><Button variant="primary" rightIcon={<ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />}>Réessayer</Button></Link>
                <Link to="/abonnement"><Button variant="ghost">Annuler</Button></Link>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function SuccessFact({ label, value, Icon }: { label: string; value: string; Icon: any }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white p-3 text-left">
      <div className="mb-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[var(--em-text-2)]">
        <Icon className="h-3 w-3" />
        <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[.12em]">{label}</p>
      </div>
      <p className="truncate text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">{value}</p>
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: abonnement/src/pages/VirementPage.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import '@/abonnement/src/abonnement.css';
import {
  ArrowLeft, Copy, Check, Upload, FileText, Wallet, ShieldCheck,
  Sparkles, ArrowRight, X, AlertCircle, ClipboardCheck, Loader2,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Panel } from '../components/ui/Panel';
import { Button } from '../components/ui/Button';
import { Badge } from '../components/ui/Badge';
import {
  abonnementSoumettreVirement,
  abonnementGetRib,
  abonnementTarif,
  RibInfo,
  FormuleAbo,
  TarifResponse,
  PalierEmploye,
} from '@/lib/api_routes/abonnement_api';
import { useAbonnement } from '@/lib/AbonnementContext';
import { cn, formatFCFA } from '@/lib/utils';

const ALLOWED_MOIS    = [1, 3, 6, 12] as const;
const ALLOWED_PALIERS = [1, 3, 6, 12] as const;

export default function AbonnementVirementPage() {
  const [params] = useSearchParams();

  const moisParam = parseInt(params.get('mois') || '1', 10);
  const mois = (ALLOWED_MOIS as readonly number[]).includes(moisParam) ? moisParam : 1;

  const formuleParam = (params.get('formule') || 'forfait') as FormuleAbo;
  const formule: FormuleAbo = formuleParam === 'par_employe' ? 'par_employe' : 'forfait';

  // Palier d'employés — pertinent uniquement pour par_employe
  const palierParam = parseInt(params.get('nb_employes') || '0', 10);
  const nbEmployes: PalierEmploye | null =
    formule === 'par_employe' && (ALLOWED_PALIERS as readonly number[]).includes(palierParam)
      ? (palierParam as PalierEmploye)
      : null;

  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { refresh } = useAbonnement();

  const [rib, setRib] = useState<RibInfo | null>(null);
  const [tarif, setTarif] = useState<TarifResponse | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [reference, setReference] = useState('');
  const [note, setNote] = useState('');
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);

  const suggestedRef = useMemo(
    () => `RH-${mois}M-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8).toUpperCase()}`,
    [mois],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        const [r, t] = await Promise.all([abonnementGetRib(), abonnementTarif()]);
        setRib(r);
        setTarif(t);
      } catch (e: any) {
        setError(e?.message || 'Impossible de charger les informations');
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    })();
  }, []);

  // Garde-fou : si par_employe sans palier, on retourne à l'étape précédente
  useEffect(() => {
    if (formule === 'par_employe' && nbEmployes == null && !loading) {
      navigate('/abonnement/upgrade', { replace: true });
    }
  }, [formule, nbEmployes, loading, navigate]);

  // Montant calculé depuis tarif backend (forfait → packs, par_employe → palier.durees)
  const computed = useMemo(() => {
    if (!tarif) return { total: 0, prixMensuel: 0, detail: undefined as string | undefined };

    if (formule === 'forfait') {
      const pack = tarif.formules.forfait.packs.find((p) => p.mois === mois);
      if (!pack) return { total: 0, prixMensuel: 0, detail: undefined };
      return {
        total: pack.total_fcfa,
        prixMensuel: pack.prix_mois_fcfa,
        detail: pack.detail,
      };
    }

    const palier = tarif.formules.par_employe.paliers.find(
      (p) => p.nb_employes === nbEmployes,
    );
    const duree = palier?.durees.find((d) => d.mois === mois);
    if (!duree) return { total: 0, prixMensuel: 0, detail: undefined };
    return {
      total: duree.total_fcfa,
      prixMensuel: duree.prix_mois_fcfa,
      detail: duree.detail,
    };
  }, [tarif, formule, mois, nbEmployes]);

  const montant      = computed.total;
  const prixMensuel  = computed.prixMensuel ?? (montant && mois ? Math.round(montant / mois) : 0);
  const detailPack   = computed.detail;

  const copy = async (text: string, key: string) => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setCopied(key);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(null), 1800);
    } catch { /* noop */ }
  };

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const f = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!f) return;
    const maxMB = 5;
    if (f.size > maxMB * 1024 * 1024) {
      setError(`Fichier trop volumineux (max ${maxMB} Mo)`);
      return;
    }
    const allowed = ['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'application/pdf'];
    if (!allowed.includes(f.type)) {
      setError('Format accepté : JPG, PNG, PDF');
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setFile(f);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    if (reference.trim().length < 4) {
      setError('Veuillez saisir une référence de virement valide (≥ 4 caractères)');
      return;
    }

    if (formule === 'par_employe' && nbEmployes == null) {
      setError('Palier d\'employés manquant — relancez depuis l\'étape précédente');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await abonnementSoumettreVirement({
        mois,
        formule,
        nb_employes: formule === 'par_employe' ? nbEmployes : undefined,
        reference_virement: reference.trim(),
        note_entreprise: note.trim() || undefined,
        preuve: file,
      });
      await refresh();
      setSuccess(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err?.message || 'Échec de la soumission');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (success) {
    return (
      <VirementSuccess
        mois={mois}
        montant={montant}
        onClose={() => navigate('/abonnement')}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="abonnement-scope space-y-6">
      {/* HERO */}
      <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-ink-200/70 bg-paper shadow-raise">
        <div
          className="pointer-events-none absolute -top-32 -right-20 w-[420px] h-[420px] rounded-full opacity-40 blur-3xl"
          style={{ background: 'radial-gradient(circle, #F5C51866 0%, transparent 60%)' }}
        />
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-dotted opacity-30" />
        <div className="relative px-6 sm:px-8 py-6 sm:py-7">
          <Link
            to="/abonnement/upgrade"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-ink-500 hover:text-ink-900 mb-4">
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Retour aux formules
          </Link>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-ink-900 text-white text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[.16em] shadow-raise mb-3">
            <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-accent-400 animate-pulse" />
            Paiement par virement · Étape 2 sur 2
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-display text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-black text-ink-900 tracking-tight leading-tight">
            Réglez par{' '}
            <span className="relative inline-block">
              <span className="relative z-10">virement bancaire</span>
              <span
                className="absolute left-0 right-0 bottom-1 h-2 -z-0 rounded-sm opacity-60"
                style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, #F5C518 0%, transparent 100%)' }}
              />
            </span>
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-ink-500 max-w-2xl">
            Effectuez le virement sur le compte ci-dessous, puis renvoyez la
            référence et la preuve. Votre accès sera réactivé sous 24 heures
            ouvrées après validation.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
          <Panel
            title="Coordonnées bancaires"
            subtitle="Virement à effectuer sur ce compte"
            icon={<Wallet className="w-4 h-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />}
            right={<Badge tone="accent">RIB officiel</Badge>}>
            {loading || !rib ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 py-8 justify-center text-ink-500">
                <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin-slow" />
                <span className="text-sm">Chargement du RIB…</span>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-2.5">
                <RibField label="Bénéficiaire" value={rib.titulaire} keyId="t" copied={copied} onCopy={copy} />
                <RibField label="Banque"       value={rib.banque}    keyId="b" copied={copied} onCopy={copy} />
                <RibField label="Agence"       value={rib.agence}    keyId="a" copied={copied} onCopy={copy} />
                <RibField label="IBAN"         value={rib.iban}      keyId="i" copied={copied} onCopy={copy} mono />
                <RibField label="SWIFT / BIC"  value={rib.swift}     keyId="s" copied={copied} onCopy={copy} mono />

                <div className="mt-4 p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200/60">
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-2 text-amber-900">
                    <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                    <div className="text-[12px]">
                      <p className="font-bold">À indiquer en référence du virement :</p>
                      <p className="font-mono mt-0.5">{suggestedRef}</p>
                      <p className="text-amber-700 mt-1.5">
                        Vous renseignerez cette même référence dans le formulaire ci-contre.
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </Panel>

          <Panel
            title="Confirmer le virement"
            subtitle="Notifiez-nous une fois le transfert effectué"
            icon={<ClipboardCheck className="w-4 h-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />}>
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <Field label="Référence du virement" required>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={reference}
                  onChange={(e) => setReference(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder={`Ex : ${suggestedRef}`}
                  className="w-full h-11 px-3.5 rounded-lg border border-ink-200 bg-white text-sm font-mono
                             focus:border-accent-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent-200 outline-none transition"
                />
                <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-500 mt-1">
                  Telle que renseignée auprès de votre banque, ou code de transaction.
                </p>
              </Field>

              <Field label="Preuve de virement (recommandé)">
                <FileUpload
                  file={file}
                  onChange={handleFileChange}
                  onClear={() => setFile(null)}
                />
              </Field>

              <Field label="Note (facultatif)">
                <textarea
                  value={note}
                  onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}
                  rows={3}
                  placeholder="Informations complémentaires (date, banque émettrice…)"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-ink-200 bg-white text-sm
                             focus:border-accent-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent-200 outline-none transition resize-none"
                />
              </Field>

              {error && (
                <div className="rounded-lg bg-red-50 border border-red-200 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-red-700 flex items-start gap-2">
                  <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                  {error}
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <Button
                  type="submit"
                  variant="accent"
                  size="lg"
                  loading={submitting}
                  rightIcon={!submitting ? <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" /> : undefined}
                  fullWidth>
                  Soumettre la preuve
                </Button>
                <Link to="/abonnement/upgrade">
                  <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="lg">
                    Annuler
                  </Button>
                </Link>
              </div>
            </form>
          </Panel>
        </div>

        <div>
          <div className="lg:sticky lg:top-24 space-y-4">
            <Panel
              title="Récapitulatif"
              subtitle="Montant à virer"
              icon={<Sparkles className="w-4 h-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />}>
              <div className="space-y-3">
                <RecapRow
                  label="Formule"
                  value={
                    formule === 'forfait'
                      ? 'Forfait illimité'
                      : nbEmployes
                      ? `Par employé · ${nbEmployes} max`
                      : 'Par employé'
                  }
                />
                <RecapRow label="Durée" value={`${mois} mois`} small />
                {prixMensuel > 0 && (
                  <RecapRow label="Tarif mensuel" value={formatFCFA(prixMensuel)} small />
                )}
                {detailPack && (
                  <RecapRow label="Détail" value={detailPack} small muted />
                )}

                <div className="border-t border-ink-200/60 pt-3">
                  <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
                    <span className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[.14em] font-semibold text-ink-500">
                      Total à virer
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-display text-2xl font-black text-ink-900 tabular-nums">
                      {formatFCFA(montant)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-5 pt-5 border-t border-ink-200/60 space-y-2 text-[11px] text-ink-500">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" /> Compte vérifié et sécurisé
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-accent-600" /> Validation sous 24 h ouvrées
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-600" /> Reçu envoyé après confirmation
                </div>
              </div>
            </Panel>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Sous-composants (inchangés)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function RibField({
  label, value, keyId, copied, onCopy, mono,
}: { label: string; value: string; keyId: string; copied: string | null; onCopy: (v: string, k: string) => void; mono?: boolean }) {
  const isCopied = copied === keyId;
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 rounded-xl bg-paper border border-ink-200/60 hover:border-ink-300 transition group">
      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
        <p className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[.14em] font-semibold text-ink-500">{label}</p>
        <p className={cn('text-sm font-semibold text-ink-900 truncate', mono && 'font-mono')}>
          {value}
        </p>
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => onCopy(value, keyId)}
        className={cn(
          'shrink-0 h-9 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition',
          isCopied
            ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200'
            : 'bg-white border border-ink-200 text-ink-700 hover:border-ink-300 hover:bg-ink-50',
        )}>
        {isCopied ? <><Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Copié</> : <><Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Copier</>}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function Field({ label, required, children }: { label: string; required?: boolean; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label className="block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[.14em] font-bold text-ink-700 mb-1.5">
        {label}
        {required && <span className="text-red-500 ml-0.5">*</span>}
      </label>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

function FileUpload({
  file, onChange, onClear,
}: { file: File | null; onChange: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void; onClear: () => void }) {
  if (file) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg border border-ink-200 bg-white">
        <div
          className="w-9 h-9 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center text-white shrink-0"
          style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #34D399 0%, #059669 100%)' }}>
          <FileText className="w-4 h-4" />
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink-900 truncate">{file.name}</p>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-500">{(file.size / 1024).toFixed(1)} Ko</p>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClear}
          className="w-8 h-8 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center text-ink-500 hover:bg-ink-100 hover:text-ink-900 transition">
          <X className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <label className="block cursor-pointer">
      <input type="file" accept=".jpg,.webp,.png,.pdf" onChange={onChange} className="sr-only" />
      <div
        className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 py-7 px-4 rounded-xl
                   border-2 border-dashed border-ink-300 bg-paper hover:bg-ink-50
                   hover:border-accent-500 transition group">
        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center bg-ink-100 text-ink-500 group-hover:bg-accent-100 group-hover:text-accent-700 transition">
          <Upload className="w-5 h-5" />
        </div>
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink-900">Cliquez pour téléverser</p>
        <p className="text-[11px] text-ink-500">JPG, PNG ou PDF · max 5 Mo</p>
      </div>
    </label>
  );
}

function RecapRow({ label, value, small, muted }: { label: string; value: string; small?: boolean; muted?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
      <span
        className={cn(
          'uppercase tracking-[.14em] font-semibold text-ink-500',
          small ? 'text-[10px]' : 'text-[11px]',
        )}>
        {label}
      </span>
      <span
        className={cn(
          'font-semibold tabular-nums',
          small ? 'text-[12px]' : 'text-sm',
          muted ? 'text-ink-500' : 'text-ink-900',
        )}>
        {value}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

function VirementSuccess({
  mois, montant, onClose,
}: { mois: number; montant: number; onClose: () => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="min-h-[70vh] flex items-center justify-center animate-fade-in">
      <div className="max-w-lg w-full">
        <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-ink-200/70 bg-paper shadow-raise-lg">
          <div
            className="pointer-events-none absolute -top-32 -right-20 w-[420px] h-[420px] rounded-full opacity-40 blur-3xl"
            style={{ background: 'radial-gradient(circle, #34D39966 0%, transparent 60%)' }}
          />
          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-dotted opacity-30" />
          <div className="relative p-8 text-center">
            <div
              className="inline-flex w-20 h-20 rounded-full items-center justify-center mb-5 animate-scale-in"
              style={{
                background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #34D399 0%, #059669 100%)',
                boxShadow: '0 14px 30px -8px rgba(16,185,129,0.6)',
              }}>
              <Check className="w-10 h-10 text-white" strokeWidth={3} />
            </div>
            <Badge tone="success" className="mb-3">Soumission enregistrée</Badge>
            <h2 className="font-display text-3xl font-black text-ink-900">Preuve envoyée</h2>
            <p className="mt-2 text-ink-500 max-w-md mx-auto">
              Votre virement de{' '}
              <span className="font-bold text-ink-900">{formatFCFA(montant)}</span>{' '}
              pour <span className="font-bold text-ink-900">{mois} mois</span>{' '}
              est en cours de vérification. Vous recevrez un email dès la confirmation (sous 24h ouvrées).
            </p>
            <div className="mt-6 pt-6 border-t border-ink-200/60 grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 text-[11px]">
              <Step n="1" label="Soumis" done />
              <Step n="2" label="Vérification" pending />
              <Step n="3" label="Accès activé" />
            </div>
            <div className="mt-6 flex gap-2 justify-center">
              <Button variant="accent" onClick={onClose} rightIcon={<ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />}>
                Retour à l'abonnement
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Step({ n, label, done, pending }: { n: string; label: string; done?: boolean; pending?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5">
      <div
        className={cn(
          'w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold',
          done && 'bg-emerald-500 text-white',
          pending && 'border-2 border-amber-500 text-amber-700 bg-amber-50 animate-pulse',
          !done && !pending && 'border-2 border-ink-200 text-ink-400 bg-paper',
        )}>
        {done ? <Check className="w-4 h-4" strokeWidth={3} /> : n}
      </div>
      <span
        className={cn(
          'font-semibold uppercase tracking-[.14em]',
          done ? 'text-emerald-700' : pending ? 'text-amber-700' : 'text-ink-400',
        )}>
        {label}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

# FICHIER: components/MobileAppPromo.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { QRCodeSVG } from 'qrcode.react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import {
  Download, Smartphone, Apple, PlayCircle, ShieldCheck,
  Wallet, BookOpen, CalendarClock, BellRing, Play, MonitorPlay,
  LoaderCircleIcon, AlertCircleIcon, Copy, CheckIcon, PackageOpen
} from 'lucide-react';

import { getAppMobileVersions, type AppMobileVersion } from '../lib/api_ecole';

import logoApp from '../assets/app_gomor/log_app_gomor.png';
import visual1 from '../assets/app_gomor/Application mobile finances et éducation.png';
import visual2 from '../assets/app_gomor/Présentation élégante d’une application éducative mobile.png';
import visual3 from '../assets/app_gomor/screen_vide_emploi_dutemp.png';
import videoApp from '../assets/app_gomor/video_record_app.mp4';

export function MobileAppPromo() {
  const [activeMedia, setActiveMedia] = useState<'video' | 'screens'>('video');
  const [version, setVersion] = useState<AppMobileVersion | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    getAppMobileVersions()
      .then((r) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setVersion(r.latest);
      })
      .catch((e: any) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setError(e?.message || "Impossible de récupérer l'APK");
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (alive) setLoading(false);
      });
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, []);

  const downloadUrl = version?.download_url ?? "";

  const copyUrl = async () => {
    if (!downloadUrl) return;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(downloadUrl);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1800);
    } catch { /* noop */ }
  };

  return (
    <div className="ecole_m-card overflow-hidden">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="relative flex flex-col items-center overflow-hidden border-b border-[var(--em-border)] bg-gradient-to-br from-[var(--em-accent-soft)] to-white p-6 text-center sm:p-8">
        <div className="absolute -top-20 -right-20 h-64 w-64 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent)]/10 blur-3xl" />
        <div className="relative z-10">
          <motion.div
            initial={{ scale: 0.9, opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}
            className="mx-auto mb-4 grid h-24 w-24 place-items-center rounded-2xl bg-white p-3 shadow-xl ring-1 ring-black/5"
          >
            <img src={logoApp} alt="Gomor App Logo" className="h-full w-full object-contain" />
          </motion.div>
          <h2 className="ecole_m-display text-[26px] font-bold tracking-tight text-[var(--em-text)]">
            Gomor Parent
          </h2>
          <p className="ecole_m-muted mx-auto mt-2 max-w-lg text-[14px]">
            L'application officielle pour les parents. Suivez la scolarité, les finances et la vie scolaire de votre enfant en temps réel.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-0 lg:grid-cols-2">
        {/* ── Colonne gauche : Installation ────────────────────── */}
        <div className="flex flex-col border-b border-[var(--em-border)] p-6 sm:p-8 lg:border-b-0 lg:border-r">
          
          <div className="mb-8 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-success-soft)] px-3 py-1.5 text-[12px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--em-success)]">
              <ShieldCheck className="h-4 w-4" /> 100% Sécurisé
            </span>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-warning-soft)] px-3 py-1.5 text-[12px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--em-warning)]">
              <Smartphone className="h-4 w-4" /> Android (APK)
            </span>
          </div>

          {loading && (
            <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 py-10 text-[var(--em-text-2)]">
              <LoaderCircleIcon className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin text-[var(--em-accent)]" />
              <p className="text-[13.5px] font-semibold">Chargement de l'application…</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && error && (
            <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-[var(--em-danger)]/30 bg-[var(--em-danger-soft)] p-6 text-center">
              <AlertCircleIcon className="h-8 w-8 text-[var(--em-danger)]" />
              <p className="text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-danger)]">Erreur de connexion</p>
              <p className="ecole_m-muted text-[12.5px]">{error}</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && !error && !version && (
             <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[#F9F9F9] p-6 text-center">
              <PackageOpen className="h-10 w-10 text-[var(--em-text-3)]" />
              <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">APK bientôt disponible</p>
              <p className="ecole_m-muted max-w-sm text-[12.5px]">
                L'application n'est pas encore prête sur le serveur. Veuillez réessayer plus tard ou contacter l'administrateur.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading && !error && version && (
            <>
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-8 sm:flex-row sm:items-start">
                <div className="group relative shrink-0 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-[var(--em-accent)]/30 bg-white p-4 shadow-sm transition-all hover:border-[var(--em-accent)] hover:shadow-md">
                  <QRCodeSVG
                    value={downloadUrl}
                    size={150}
                    bgColor="#ffffff"
                    fgColor="#1a1d21"
                    level="H"
                    includeMargin={false}
                    imageSettings={{
                      src: logoApp,
                      x: undefined,
                      y: undefined,
                      height: 34,
                      width: 34,
                      excavate: true,
                    }}
                  />
                  <p className="mt-3 text-center text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--em-accent)]">
                    Scanner pour installer
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center gap-4 sm:items-start">
                  <h3 className="text-[18px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">Télécharger l'APK</h3>
                  <p className="text-center text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-[var(--em-text-2)] sm:text-left">
                    Scannez le QR code avec votre téléphone Android, ou téléchargez directement le fichier.
                  </p>

                  <div className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[#F9F9F9] p-3 text-[12px]">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                      <span className="font-mono text-[var(--em-text)] truncate">{version.filename}</span>
                      <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-[var(--em-accent)]">
                        {version.size_mb} Mo
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-[var(--em-text-3)]">
                      Mis à jour le {new Date(version.modified_at).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <a
                    href={downloadUrl}
                    className="ecole_m-btn ecole_m-btn--primary w-full justify-center py-2.5 shadow-lg sm:w-auto"
                  >
                    <Download className="h-4 w-4" />
                    Télécharger l'APK
                  </a>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={copyUrl}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text-2)] hover:text-[var(--em-accent)]"
                  >
                    {copied ? <CheckIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> : <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
                    {copied ? "Lien copié" : "Copier le lien direct"}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-auto pt-8">
                <p className="mb-3 text-[12px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text-2)]">Bientôt disponible sur :</p>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                  <div className="flex cursor-not-allowed items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white px-3 py-1.5 opacity-60 grayscale">
                    <PlayCircle className="h-6 w-6 text-[#5D5D5D]" />
                    <div className="text-left">
                      <p className="text-[9px] font-bold uppercase text-[var(--em-text-2)]">Bientôt</p>
                      <p className="text-[12px] font-bold text-[var(--em-text)]">Google Play</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex cursor-not-allowed items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white px-3 py-1.5 opacity-60 grayscale">
                    <Apple className="h-6 w-6 text-[#5D5D5D]" />
                    <div className="text-left">
                      <p className="text-[9px] font-bold uppercase text-[var(--em-text-2)]">Bientôt</p>
                      <p className="text-[12px] font-bold text-[var(--em-text)]">App Store</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* ── Colonne droite : Aperçu Visuel ──────── */}
        <div className="flex flex-col bg-[#F9F9F9] p-6 sm:p-8">
          <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 className="text-[16px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">Aperçu de l'application</h3>
            <div className="flex rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white p-1 shadow-sm">
              <button
                onClick={() => setActiveMedia('video')}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-[12px] font-semibold transition-colors ${activeMedia === 'video' ? 'bg-[var(--em-accent)] text-white' : 'text-[var(--em-text-2)] hover:bg-[#F0F0F0]'}`}
              >
                <Play className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Vidéo
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setActiveMedia('screens')}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-[12px] font-semibold transition-colors ${activeMedia === 'screens' ? 'bg-[var(--em-accent)] text-white' : 'text-[var(--em-text-2)] hover:bg-[#F0F0F0]'}`}
              >
                <MonitorPlay className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Captures
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="relative aspect-[9/16] w-full max-w-[280px] mx-auto overflow-hidden rounded-[2rem] border-[8px] border-[var(--em-text)] bg-black shadow-2xl">
            <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
              {activeMedia === 'video' ? (
                <motion.video
                  key="video"
                  initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                  exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
                  src={videoApp}
                  controls
                  autoPlay
                  muted
                  loop
                  className="h-full w-full object-cover"
                  poster={visual1}
                />
              ) : (
                <motion.div
                  key="screens"
                  initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                  exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
                  className="flex h-full w-full flex-col items-center justify-center bg-white p-4"
                >
                  <img src={visual3} alt="Aperçu 3" className="w-full rounded-md border border-gray-200" />
                </motion.div>
              )}
            </AnimatePresence>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-auto pt-8 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <FeatureItem icon={BookOpen} label="Notes & Bulletins" />
            <FeatureItem icon={Wallet} label="Paiements" />
            <FeatureItem icon={CalendarClock} label="Emploi du temps" />
            <FeatureItem icon={BellRing} label="Notifications" />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function FeatureItem({ icon: Icon, label }: { icon: any, label: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white p-3 shadow-sm transition-transform hover:-translate-y-0.5">
      <div className="grid h-9 w-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] text-[var(--em-accent)]">
        <Icon className="h-4.5 w-4.5" />
      </div>
      <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)] leading-tight">{label}</span>
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/classes/ClasseDetail.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowLeftRightIcon,
  BookOpenIcon,
  CalendarClockIcon,
  CalendarCogIcon,
  ImageIcon,
  ImagePlusIcon,
  LinkIcon,
  PackageIcon,
  PackagePlusIcon,
  PencilIcon,
  PrinterIcon,
  RefreshCwIcon,
  Trash2Icon,
  UnlinkIcon
} from "lucide-react";
import {
  affecterMatiereClasse,
  ajouterMaterielClasse,
  ajouterPhotoClasse,
  basculerTypeEdt,
  changerEnseignant,
  getEdtTemplateClasse,
  getEmploiDuTempsClasse,
  listerMaterielClasse,
  listerMatieresClasse,
  listerPhotosClasse,
  modifierMaterielClasse,
  plancheCartesClasseEtAttendre,
  recalculerStatsClasse,
  retirerAffectationEnseignant,
  retirerMatiereClasse,
  supprimerMaterielClasse,
  supprimerPhoto
} from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useTick } from "../../hooks/useAsync";
import { changerEnseignantFields, matiereClasseFields, retirerEnseignantFields } from "../../data/fields/pedagogie";
import { materielClasseEditFields, materielClasseFields, photoClasseFields } from "../../data/fields/services";
import { TYPE_EDT } from "../../data/enums";
import type { Row } from "../../types/ui";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../data/AsyncBlock";
import { EdtGrid } from "../data/EdtGrid";
import { MiniTable } from "../data/MiniTable";
import { ObjectView } from "../data/ObjectView";
import { EmptyState } from "../data/States";
import { AsyncButton } from "../form/AsyncButton";
import { ConfirmAction } from "../form/ConfirmAction";
import { FormAction } from "../form/FormAction";
import { Section } from "../layout/Section";
import { Tabs } from "../ui/Tabs";
import type { TabItem } from "../ui/Tabs";
import { enumField, F, req } from "../../utils/fieldBuilders";
import { fromEnum, optTemplatesCarte } from "../../utils/options";
import { obj, resolveUrl, str, valueLabel } from "../../utils/format";

const TABS: TabItem[] = [
  { id: "matieres", label: "Matières", icon: BookOpenIcon },
  { id: "edt", label: "Organisation", icon: CalendarClockIcon },
  { id: "materiel", label: "Matériel", icon: PackageIcon },
  { id: "photos", label: "Photos", icon: ImageIcon }
];

export function ClasseDetail({ classe, anneeId }: { classe: Row; anneeId: string }) {
  const id = str(classe.id);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("matieres");
  const [tick, bump] = useTick();

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 rounded-[10px] border border-black/[0.06] bg-[#F9F9F9] p-2">
        <AsyncButton
          label="Recalculer les statistiques"
          icon={RefreshCwIcon}
          size="sm"
          successMessage="Statistiques mises à jour"
          onClick={() => recalculerStatsClasse(id, anneeId)}
        />
        <FormAction
          label="Imprimer les cartes"
          icon={PrinterIcon}
          size="sm"
          fields={[F.select("template", "Modèle", { load: optTemplatesCarte })]}
          submitLabel="Générer la planche"
          successMessage="Planche prête"
          showResult
          onSubmit={(b) => plancheCartesClasseEtAttendre(id, str(b.template) || undefined)}
        />
      </div>

      <Tabs tabs={TABS} value={tab} onChange={setTab} />

      {tab === "matieres" && (
        <Section
          title="Matières enseignées"
          actions={
            <FormAction
              label="Ajouter une matière"
              icon={LinkIcon}
              size="sm"
              variant="primary"
              fields={matiereClasseFields}
              onSubmit={(b) => affecterMatiereClasse({ ...b, classe_id: id })}
              successMessage="Matière ajoutée"
              onDone={bump}
            />
          }>
          <AsyncBlock load={() => listerMatieresClasse(id)} deps={[id, tick]} empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucune matière" />}>
            {(rows) => (
              <MiniTable
                rows={rows}
                rowKey={(r, i) => str(r.matiere_classe_id) || String(i)}
                columns={[
                  { key: "matiere" },
                  { key: "coefficient" },
                  {
                    key: "enseignant_id",
                    label: "Enseignant",
                    render: (r) =>
                      r.enseignant_id ? (
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-medium text-[#0F7B0F]">
                          <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-current" aria-hidden="true" />
                          Attribué
                        </span>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-medium text-[#9D5D00]">
                          <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-current" aria-hidden="true" />
                          Non attribué
                        </span>
                      )
                  }
                ]}
                rowActions={(r) => {
                  const mc = str(r.matiere_classe_id);
                  return (
                    <>
                      <FormAction
                        label="Changer d'enseignant"
                        icon={ArrowLeftRightIcon}
                        iconOnly
                        size="sm"
                        variant="subtle"
                        fields={changerEnseignantFields()}
                        onSubmit={(b) => changerEnseignant(mc, b)}
                        successMessage="Enseignant changé"
                        onDone={bump}
                      />
                      {Boolean(r.enseignant_id) && (
                        <FormAction
                          label="Retirer l'enseignant"
                          icon={UnlinkIcon}
                          iconOnly
                          size="sm"
                          variant="subtle"
                          fields={retirerEnseignantFields()}
                          onSubmit={(b) =>
                            retirerAffectationEnseignant(mc, { date_fin: str(b.date_fin) || undefined, motif: str(b.motif) || undefined })
                          }
                          successMessage="Enseignant retiré"
                          showResult
                          onDone={bump}
                        />
                      )}
                      <ConfirmAction
                        label="Retirer la matière"
                        icon={Trash2Icon}
                        iconOnly
                        size="sm"
                        variant="subtle"
                        danger
                        message={`Retirer ${str(r.matiere)} de cette classe ?`}
                        confirmLabel="Retirer"
                        run={() => retirerMatiereClasse(mc)}
                        successMessage="Matière retirée"
                        onDone={bump}
                      />
                    </>
                  );
                }}
              />
            )}
          </AsyncBlock>
        </Section>
      )}

      {tab === "edt" && (
        <div className="space-y-5">
          <Section
            title="Emploi du temps"
            description={`Organisation actuelle : ${valueLabel(str(classe.type_organisation_edt_effectif))}`}
            actions={
              <FormAction
                label="Changer d'organisation"
                icon={CalendarCogIcon}
                size="sm"
                fields={[enumField("nouveau_type", "Nouvelle organisation", fromEnum(TYPE_EDT), req)]}
                onSubmit={(b) => basculerTypeEdt(id, b)}
                successMessage="Organisation modifiée"
                showResult
                onDone={bump}
              />
            }>
            <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-[10px] border border-black/[0.06] bg-white">
              <AsyncBlock load={() => getEmploiDuTempsClasse(id)} deps={[id, tick]}>
                {(d) => <EdtGrid grille={obj(d.grille)} />}
              </AsyncBlock>
            </div>
          </Section>
          <Section title="Modèle de la classe">
            <AsyncBlock load={() => getEdtTemplateClasse(id)} deps={[id, tick]}>
              {(d) => <ObjectView data={d} hide={["grille_vide"]} />}
            </AsyncBlock>
          </Section>
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === "materiel" && (
        <Section
          title="Matériel de la classe"
          actions={
            <FormAction
              label="Ajouter du matériel"
              icon={PackagePlusIcon}
              size="sm"
              variant="primary"
              fields={materielClasseFields}
              modalSize="lg"
              onSubmit={(b) => ajouterMaterielClasse(id, b)}
              successMessage="Matériel ajouté"
              onDone={bump}
            />
          }>
          <AsyncBlock load={() => listerMaterielClasse(id)} deps={[id, tick]} empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucun matériel" />}>
            {(rows) => (
              <MiniTable
                rows={rows}
                rowActions={(r) => (
                  <>
                    <FormAction
                      label="Modifier"
                      icon={PencilIcon}
                      iconOnly
                      size="sm"
                      variant="subtle"
                      fields={materielClasseEditFields}
                      initial={r}
                      onSubmit={(b) => modifierMaterielClasse(str(r.id), b)}
                      successMessage="Matériel modifié"
                      onDone={bump}
                    />
                    <ConfirmAction
                      label="Supprimer"
                      icon={Trash2Icon}
                      iconOnly
                      size="sm"
                      variant="subtle"
                      danger
                      message={`Supprimer « ${str(r.designation)} » ?`}
                      confirmLabel="Supprimer"
                      run={() => supprimerMaterielClasse(str(r.id))}
                      successMessage="Matériel supprimé"
                      onDone={bump}
                    />
                  </>
                )}
              />
            )}
          </AsyncBlock>
        </Section>
      )}

      {tab === "photos" && (
        <Section
          title="Photos de classe"
          actions={
            <FormAction
              label="Ajouter une photo"
              icon={ImagePlusIcon}
              size="sm"
              variant="primary"
              fields={photoClasseFields(anneeId)}
              modalSize="lg"
              onSubmit={(b) => ajouterPhotoClasse(id, { ...b, annee_scolaire_id: anneeId })}
              successMessage="Photo ajoutée"
              onDone={bump}
            />
          }>
          <AsyncBlock
            load={() => listerPhotosClasse(id, { annee_scolaire_id: anneeId })}
            deps={[id, anneeId, tick]}
            empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucune photo" />}>
            {(rows) => (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                {rows.map((p) => (
                  <figure
                    key={str(p.id)}
                    className="overflow-hidden rounded-[10px] border border-black/[0.06] bg-white shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)]">
                    <div className="bg-[#F3F3F3]">
                      <img
                        src={resolveUrl(str(p.url_photo || p.thumbnail_url))}
                        alt={str(p.titre) || "Photo de classe"}
                        loading="lazy"
                        className="aspect-[4/3] w-full object-cover"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <figcaption className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 px-2.5 py-2">
                      <span className="truncate text-[12.5px] font-medium text-[#1A1A1A]">{str(p.titre) || str(p.legende)}</span>
                      <ConfirmAction
                        label="Supprimer"
                        icon={Trash2Icon}
                        iconOnly
                        size="sm"
                        variant="subtle"
                        danger
                        message="Supprimer cette photo ?"
                        confirmLabel="Supprimer"
                        run={() => supprimerPhoto(str(p.id))}
                        successMessage="Photo supprimée"
                        onDone={bump}
                      />
                    </figcaption>
                  </figure>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </AsyncBlock>
        </Section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/dashboard/AttentionPanel.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowRightIcon, ShieldAlertIcon, WalletIcon, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";
import { getDashboardDirection, listerEleves, listerPaiementsEnAttente } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useLookup } from "../../hooks/useLookup";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../data/AsyncBlock";
import { Badge } from "../ui/Badge";
import { arr, formatDate, formatMoney, fullName, obj, str, valueLabel } from "../../utils/format";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";

const EASE = [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1] as const;

function Initial({ name, color }: { name: string; color: string }) {
  return (
    <span
      className="grid h-10 w-10 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full text-[14px] font-bold shadow-sm"
      style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${color} 15%, white)`, color }}
      aria-hidden="true">
      {(name.trim()[0] || "?").toUpperCase()}
    </span>
  );
}

export function AttentionPanel({ anneeId }: { anneeId: string }) {
  const eleves = useLookup(() => listerEleves(), fullName);

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
      {/* Carte Paiements */}
      <motion.section
        initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }} transition={{ duration: 0.3, ease: EASE }}
        className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(0,0,0,0.02)]"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[var(--em-border)] bg-gradient-to-r from-[#F9F9F9] to-white px-5 py-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <span className="grid h-9 w-9 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-[var(--em-success-soft)] text-[var(--em-success)]">
              <WalletIcon className="h-4.5 w-4.5" />
            </span>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-[14.5px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">Paiements à valider</h2>
              <p className="text-[12px] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">En attente de confirmation</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <Link to="/finance?tab=attente" className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-accent)] hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
            Traiter <ArrowRightIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </Link>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 p-3">
          <AsyncBlock
            load={() => listerPaiementsEnAttente()}
            deps={[]}
            empty={
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-8 text-center">
                <CheckCircle2 className="h-8 w-8 text-[var(--em-success)] mb-2 opacity-80" />
                <p className="text-[13.5px] font-medium text-[var(--em-text)]">Tout est à jour !</p>
                <p className="text-[12px] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">Aucun paiement en attente de validation.</p>
              </div>
            }>
            {(rows) => (
              <ul className="space-y-1">
                {rows.slice(0, 5).map((p) => (
                  <li key={str(p.id)} className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-2 py-2 transition-colors hover:bg-[#F9F9F9]">
                    <Initial name={str(p.eleve)} color="#0F7B0F" />
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <p className="truncate text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">{str(p.eleve)}</p>
                      <p className="text-[12px] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">{formatDate(p.date_paiement)}</p>
                    </div>
                    <span className="shrink-0 rounded-md bg-[var(--em-success-soft)] px-2.5 py-1 text-[13px] font-bold text-[var(--em-success)]">
                      {formatMoney(p.montant_fcfa)}
                    </span>
                  </li>
                ))}
                {rows.length > 5 && (
                  <li className="px-2 pt-2 text-center text-[12.5px] font-medium text-[var(--em-accent)]">
                    + {rows.length - 5} autres paiements
                  </li>
                )}
              </ul>
            )}
          </AsyncBlock>
        </div>
      </motion.section>

      {/* Carte Sanctions */}
      <motion.section
        initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }} transition={{ duration: 0.3, ease: EASE }}
        className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(0,0,0,0.02)]"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[var(--em-border)] bg-gradient-to-r from-[#F9F9F9] to-white px-5 py-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <span className="grid h-9 w-9 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-[var(--em-danger-soft)] text-[var(--em-danger)]">
              <ShieldAlertIcon className="h-4.5 w-4.5" />
            </span>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-[14.5px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">Discipline Récente</h2>
              <p className="text-[12px] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">Dernières sanctions</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <Link to="/discipline" className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-accent)] hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
            Détails <ArrowRightIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </Link>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 p-3">
          <AsyncBlock
            load={async () => arr(obj((await getDashboardDirection(anneeId)).discipline).sanctions_recentes)}
            deps={[anneeId]}
            empty={
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-8 text-center">
                <CheckCircle2 className="h-8 w-8 text-[var(--em-success)] mb-2 opacity-80" />
                <p className="text-[13.5px] font-medium text-[var(--em-text)]">Climat scolaire serein</p>
                <p className="text-[12px] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">Aucune sanction récente.</p>
              </div>
            }>
            {(rows) => (
              <ul className="space-y-1">
                {rows.slice(0, 5).map((s, i) => {
                  const name = eleves.get(str(s.eleve_id)) ?? "Élève";
                  return (
                    <li key={i} className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg px-2 py-2 transition-colors hover:bg-[#F9F9F9]">
                      <Initial name={name} color="#C42B1C" />
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                          <p className="truncate text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">{name}</p>
                          <Badge tone="danger">{valueLabel(str(s.type))}</Badge>
                        </div>
                        <p className="mt-0.5 truncate text-[12px] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">
                          {formatDate(s.date)} — <span className="italic">{str(s.motif)}</span>
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </li>
                  );
                })}
              </ul>
            )}
          </AsyncBlock>
        </div>
      </motion.section>
    </div>
  );
}

# FICHIER: components/dashboard/RemplissageList.tsx
