import React from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { getTauxRemplissage } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../data/AsyncBlock";
import { EmptyState } from "../data/States";
import { formatNumber, formatPct, num, str } from "../../utils/format";
import { Users, DoorOpen } from "lucide-react";

function barColor(t: number) {
  if (t >= 100) return "var(--em-danger)";
  if (t >= 85) return "var(--em-warning)";
  return "var(--em-accent)";
}

export function RemplissageList({ anneeId }: { anneeId: string }) {
  return (
    <AsyncBlock
      load={() => getTauxRemplissage(anneeId)}
      deps={[anneeId]}
      empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucune classe pour cette année" />}>
      {(rows) => {
        // Calcul global
        const totalEleves = rows.reduce((acc, r) => acc + (num(r.effectif_actuel) || 0), 0);
        const totalPlaces = rows.reduce((acc, r) => acc + (num(r.effectif_max) || 0), 0);
        const totalRestantes = rows.reduce((acc, r) => acc + (num(r.places_restantes) || 0), 0);

        return (
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(0,0,0,0.02)]">
            {/* Header Global */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-[var(--em-border)] bg-gradient-to-r from-[#F9F9F9] to-white px-6 py-4 rounded-t-xl">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <span className="grid h-10 w-10 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] text-[var(--em-accent)]">
                  <Users className="h-5 w-5" />
                </span>
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">Capacité globale de l'établissement</h2>
                  <p className="text-[12.5px] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">Répartition des effectifs sur l'ensemble des classes</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-6 text-right">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--em-text-2)]">Places libres</p>
                  <p className="text-[18px] font-bold text-[var(--em-text)]">{formatNumber(totalRestantes)}</p>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--em-text-2)]">Total Élèves</p>
                  <p className="text-[18px] font-bold text-[var(--em-text)]">{formatNumber(totalEleves)} / {formatNumber(totalPlaces)}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Grille des classes */}
            <div className="grid gap-4 p-5 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
              {rows.map((r, i) => {
                const t = num(r.taux_remplissage_pct) ?? 0;
                const color = barColor(t);
                return (
                  <motion.div
                    key={str(r.classe_id) || i}
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
                    transition={{ duration: 0.2, delay: Math.min(i, 10) * 0.03, ease: [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1] }}
                    className="group flex flex-col justify-between rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white p-4 transition-all duration-200 hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-md"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <DoorOpen className="h-4 w-4 text-[var(--em-text-2)]" />
                        <p className="truncate text-[13.5px] font-bold text-[var(--em-text)]">{str(r.classe)}</p>
                      </div>
                      <span
                        className="shrink-0 rounded-md px-2 py-0.5 text-[12px] font-bold tabular-nums"
                        style={{ backgroundColor: `color-mix(in srgb, ${color} 12%, white)`, color }}
                      >
                        {formatPct(t)}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-4">
                      <div className="h-2 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[#eef1f5]">
                        <motion.div
                          className="h-full rounded-full"
                          style={{ background: color }}
                          initial={{ width: 0 }}
                          animate={{ width: `${Math.min(100, t)}%` }}
                          transition={{ duration: 0.6, delay: Math.min(i, 10) * 0.03, ease: [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1] }}
                        />
                      </div>
                      
                      <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between text-[11.5px] font-medium">
                        <span className="text-[var(--em-text-2)]">{formatNumber(r.effectif_actuel)} / {formatNumber(r.effectif_max)} élèves</span>
                        <span className={t >= 100 ? "text-[var(--em-danger)]" : "text-[var(--em-text-3)]"}>
                          {formatNumber(r.places_restantes)} place(s)
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </motion.div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      }}
    </AsyncBlock>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/dashboard/SeedDemoButton.tsx
################################################################################

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import { Rocket, Loader2, X } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { useSchool } from "../../contexts/SchoolContext";
import {
  SEED_DEMO_AUTHORIZED_ENTREPRISE,
  lancerSeedDemo,
  getSeedDemoStatut,
} from "../../lib/api_ecole";

const POLL_MS = 3000;
const MAX_DURATION_MS = 915 * 60 * 1000; // 15 min de sécurité

export function SeedDemoButton() {
  const { entreprise } = useSchool();
  const [running, setRunning] = useState(false);
  const [taskId, setTaskId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [logTail, setLogTail] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [state, setState] = useState<string>("");
  const pollRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const startRef = useRef<number>(0);

  // ── Cleanup polling au démontage
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (pollRef.current) window.clearTimeout(pollRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  // ── N'affiche rien si l'entreprise n'est pas autorisée
  if (!entreprise || entreprise.id !== SEED_DEMO_AUTHORIZED_ENTREPRISE) {
    return null;
  }

  const stopPolling = () => {
    if (pollRef.current) {
      window.clearTimeout(pollRef.current);
      pollRef.current = null;
    }
  };

  const poll = async (tid: string) => {
    try {
      const t = await getSeedDemoStatut(tid);
      const s = String(t.state || "").toUpperCase();
      setState(s);

      // Récupérer le log depuis meta (Redis) ou result (final)
      const tail =
        t.meta?.log_tail ||
        t.result?.log_tail ||
        null;
      if (tail && Array.isArray(tail)) {
        setLogTail(tail);
      }

      if (s === "SUCCESS") {
        stopPolling();
        setRunning(false);
        toast.success("Seed démo terminé ✨");
        return;
      }
      if (s === "FAILURE" || s === "FAILED" || t.error) {
        stopPolling();
        setRunning(false);
        toast.error(t.error || "Seed échoué — voir les logs");
        return;
      }

      // Sécurité : abandonner après MAX_DURATION_MS
      if (Date.now() - startRef.current > MAX_DURATION_MS) {
        stopPolling();
        setRunning(false);
        toast.error("Seed interrompu (timeout 15 min)");
        return;
      }

      pollRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => poll(tid), POLL_MS);
    } catch (e: any) {
      // En cas d'erreur réseau ponctuelle : on retente
      pollRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => poll(tid), POLL_MS);
    }
  };

  const lancer = async () => {
    if (running) return;
    setRunning(true);
    setLogTail([]);
    setState("STARTING");
    startRef.current = Date.now();

    try {
      const res = await lancerSeedDemo();
      setTaskId(res.task_id);
      toast.success("Seed démarré — suivi en cours…");
      poll(res.task_id);
    } catch (e: any) {
      toast.error(e?.message || "Impossible de démarrer le seed");
      setRunning(false);
      setState("ERROR");
    }
  };

  const arreter = () => {
    stopPolling();
    setRunning(false);
    setState("CANCELLED");
    toast("Suivi arrêté (le seed continue côté serveur)", { icon: "ℹ️" });
  };

  return (
    <div className="ecole_m-card p-5 space-y-3">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Rocket className="h-4 w-4 text-[var(--em-accent)]" />
            <span className="text-[14px] font-semibold">Seed démo complet</span>
            {state && (
              <span className="ecole_m-muted text-[11px] font-mono uppercase">
                {state}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          <p className="ecole_m-muted text-[12px] mt-1">
            Génère 16 classes, ~600 élèves, notes, paiements, EDT, bulletins,
            absences, discipline, transport, bibliothèque — sur l'année 2025-2026.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
          {running ? (
            <>
              <Button variant="subtle" size="sm" onClick={arreter}>
                <X className="h-4 w-4" /> Arrêter le suivi
              </Button>
              <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" disabled>
                <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> En cours…
              </Button>
            </>
          ) : (
            <Button variant="primary" size="sm" onClick={lancer}>
              <Rocket className="h-4 w-4" /> Lancer le seed
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {logTail.length > 0 && (
        <pre className="text-[11px] leading-snug bg-[var(--em-surface)] border border-[var(--em-border)] p-3 rounded-md max-h-72 overflow-y-auto font-mono whitespace-pre-wrap break-all">
          {logTail.join("\n")}
        </pre>
      )}

      {taskId && !running && state === "SUCCESS" && (
        <p className="text-[12px] text-[var(--em-accent)] font-medium">
          ✓ Seed terminé. Rafraîchis la page pour voir les nouvelles données.
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/data/AsyncBlock.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { useAsync } from "../../hooks/useAsync";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState, LoadingState } from "./States";

interface AsyncBlockProps<T> {
  load: () => Promise<T>;
  deps: unknown[];
  children: (data: T, reload: () => void) => React.ReactNode;
  empty?: React.ReactNode;
  isEmpty?: (data: T) => boolean;
  enabled?: boolean;
  rows?: number;
}

export function AsyncBlock<T>({ load, deps, children, empty, isEmpty, enabled = true, rows = 3 }: AsyncBlockProps<T>) {
  const { data, loading, error, reload } = useAsync(load, deps, enabled);

  if (!enabled) return null;
  if (loading && (data === null || data === undefined)) return <LoadingState rows={rows} />;
  if (error) return <ErrorState message={error} onRetry={reload} />;

  const vide = data === null || data === undefined || (isEmpty ? isEmpty(data) : Array.isArray(data) && data.length === 0);

  if (vide) return <>{empty ?? <EmptyState compact title="Aucune information pour le moment" />}</>;
  return <>{children(data as T, reload)}</>;
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/data/CellValue.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { Badge } from "../ui/Badge";
import { formatValue, isStatusKey, statusTone, valueLabel } from "../../utils/format";

const DASH = <span className="text-[#A0A0A0]" aria-label="Non renseigné">—</span>;

export function CellValue({ k, v }: { k: string; v: unknown }) {
  if (v === null || v === undefined || v === "") return DASH;

  if (typeof v === "boolean") return <Badge tone={v ? "success" : "neutral"}>{v ? "Oui" : "Non"}</Badge>;

  if (isStatusKey(k) && typeof v === "string") return <Badge tone={statusTone(v)}>{valueLabel(v)}</Badge>;

  if (Array.isArray(v)) {
    if (v.length === 0) return DASH;
    if (v.every((x) => x === null || typeof x !== "object")) {
      return (
        <span className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
          {v.map((x, i) => (
            <span
              key={i}
              className="inline-flex h-6 items-center rounded-[5px] border border-black/[0.06] bg-[#F5F5F5] px-2 text-[12px] font-medium text-[#1A1A1A]">
              {valueLabel(String(x))}
            </span>
          ))}
        </span>
      );
    }
    return (
      <span className="inline-flex h-6 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-[5px] bg-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)]/[0.08] px-2 text-[12px] font-medium text-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)]">
        <span className="tabular-nums">{v.length}</span> élément(s)
      </span>
    );
  }

  if (typeof v === "object") return <span className="text-[12.5px] font-medium text-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)]">Voir le détail</span>;

  return <>{formatValue(k, v)}</>;
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/data/DataTable.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import type { Column, Row } from "../../types/ui";
import { humanize, isHiddenKey } from "../../utils/format";
import { CellValue } from "./CellValue";

interface DataTableProps {
  rows: Row[];
  columns?: Column[];
  onRowClick?: (row: Row) => void;
  selectedKey?: string | null;
  rowKey?: (row: Row, index: number) => string;
  rowActions?: (row: Row) => React.ReactNode;
  compact?: boolean;
}

function autoColumns(rows: Row[]): Column[] {
  const first = rows[0] ?? {};
  return Object.keys(first)
    .filter((k) => {
      if (isHiddenKey(k) || k.endsWith("_b64")) return false;
      const v = first[k];
      return !(v && typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v));
    })
    .slice(0, 7)
    .map((key) => ({ key }));
}

export function DataTable({ rows, columns, onRowClick, selectedKey, rowKey, rowActions, compact }: DataTableProps) {
  const cols = columns ?? autoColumns(rows);
  const keyOf = (r: Row, i: number) => (rowKey ? rowKey(r, i) : String(r.id ?? i));

  const cellPad = compact ? "px-3 py-2" : "px-4 py-3";
  const headPad = compact ? "px-3 py-2" : "px-4 py-2.5";

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col">
      <div className="ecole_m-scroll overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full border-separate border-spacing-0 text-left">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              {cols.map((c) => (
                <th
                  key={c.key}
                  scope="col"
                  style={{ textAlign: c.align ?? "left" }}
                  className={`sticky top-0 z-[1] whitespace-nowrap border-b border-black/[0.06] bg-[#F9F9F9]/95 text-[12px] font-semibold text-[#5D5D5D] backdrop-blur ${headPad}`}>
                  {c.label ?? humanize(c.key)}
                </th>
              ))}
              {rowActions && (
                <th scope="col" className={`sticky top-0 z-[1] w-px border-b border-black/[0.06] bg-[#F9F9F9]/95 backdrop-blur ${headPad}`}>
                  <span className="sr-only">Actions</span>
                </th>
              )}
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody>
            {rows.map((r, i) => {
              const k = keyOf(r, i);
              const selected = selectedKey === k;
              const clickable = !!onRowClick;
              return (
                <motion.tr
                  key={k}
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 4 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                  transition={{ duration: 0.2, delay: Math.min(i, 8) * 0.03, ease: [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1] }}
                  data-clickable={clickable}
                  data-selected={selected}
                  aria-selected={clickable ? selected : undefined}
                  tabIndex={clickable ? 0 : undefined}
                  onClick={clickable ? () => onRowClick!(r) : undefined}
                  onKeyDown={(e) => {
                    if (onRowClick && e.key === "Enter") onRowClick(r);
                  }}
                  className={[
                    "group outline-none transition-[background-color] duration-150",
                    "focus-visible:bg-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)]/[0.06]",
                    clickable ? "cursor-pointer" : "",
                    selected
                      ? "bg-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)]/[0.08]"
                      : clickable
                        ? "hover:bg-black/[0.03] active:bg-black/[0.05]"
                        : ""
                  ].join(" ")}>
                  {cols.map((c, ci) => (
                    <td
                      key={c.key}
                      style={{ textAlign: c.align ?? "left" }}
                      className={`relative border-b border-black/[0.045] align-middle text-[13.5px] text-[#1A1A1A] group-last:border-b-0 ${cellPad} ${
                        ci === 0 ? "font-medium" : ""
                      }`}>
                      {ci === 0 && selected && (
                        <motion.span
                          layoutId="ecole-table-selection"
                          className="absolute left-0 top-1/2 h-4 w-[3px] -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)]"
                          transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 520, damping: 40 }}
                          aria-hidden="true"
                        />
                      )}
                      {c.render ? c.render(r) : <CellValue k={c.key} v={r[c.key]} />}
                    </td>
                  ))}
                  {rowActions && (
                    <td
                      className={`border-b border-black/[0.045] group-last:border-b-0 ${compact ? "px-2 py-1.5" : "px-3 py-2"}`}
                      onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                      onKeyDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
                      <div className="flex justify-end gap-0.5">{rowActions(r)}</div>
                    </td>
                  )}
                </motion.tr>
              );
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      {!compact && rows.length > 0 && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-black/[0.06] bg-[#F9F9F9]/60 px-4 py-2 text-[12px] text-[#5D5D5D]">
          <span>
            <span className="font-semibold tabular-nums text-[#1A1A1A]">{rows.length}</span> élément(s)
          </span>
          {onRowClick && <span className="hidden sm:inline">Cliquez sur une ligne pour voir le détail</span>}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/data/EdtGrid.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { CalendarXIcon, ClockIcon, MapPinIcon, UsersIcon } from "lucide-react";
import type { Row } from "../../types/ui";
import { str, valueLabel } from "../../utils/format";

const DAYS = ["Lundi", "Mardi", "Mercredi", "Jeudi", "Vendredi", "Samedi", "Dimanche"];
const PALETTE = ["#0067C0", "#0F7B6C", "#8764B8", "#CA5010", "#C239B3", "#107C10", "#0099BC", "#E74856", "#4F6BED", "#8E562E"];

function colorFor(label: string): string {
  let h = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < label.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + label.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;
  return PALETTE[h % PALETTE.length];
}

export function EdtGrid({ grille }: { grille: Record<string, unknown> }) {
  const keys = Object.keys(grille)
    .filter((k) => /^\d+$/.test(k))
    .sort((a, b) => Number(a) - Number(b))
    .filter((k) => Number(k) < 6 || (Array.isArray(grille[k]) && (grille[k] as unknown[]).length > 0));

  if (keys.length === 0)
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 px-4 py-10 text-center">
        <span className="grid h-10 w-10 place-items-center rounded-[10px] bg-black/[0.04] text-[#8A8A8A]">
          <CalendarXIcon className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
        </span>
        <p className="text-[13.5px] text-[#5D5D5D]">Aucun créneau.</p>
      </div>
    );

  const todayIdx = (new Date().getDay() + 6) % 7;

  return (
    <div className="ecole_m-scroll overflow-x-auto p-3">
      <div className="grid min-w-[760px] gap-2.5" style={{ gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${keys.length}, minmax(0, 1fr))` }}>
        {keys.map((k) => {
          const slots = Array.isArray(grille[k]) ? (grille[k] as Row[]) : [];
          const isToday = Number(k) === todayIdx;
          return (
            <div
              key={k}
              className={`flex min-w-0 flex-col rounded-[10px] p-1.5 ${
                isToday ? "bg-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)]/[0.06] ring-1 ring-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)]/20" : "bg-[#F9F9F9]"
              }`}>
              <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 px-2 py-1.5">
                <h4 className={`text-[13px] font-semibold ${isToday ? "text-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)]" : "text-[#1A1A1A]"}`}>
                  {DAYS[Number(k)] ?? `Jour ${k}`}
                </h4>
                <span
                  className={`grid h-5 min-w-5 place-items-center rounded-full px-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold tabular-nums ${
                    isToday ? "bg-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)] text-white" : "bg-black/[0.06] text-[#5D5D5D]"
                  }`}>
                  {slots.length}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1.5">
                {slots.length === 0 && (
                  <div className="grid flex-1 place-items-center rounded-[8px] border border-dashed border-black/[0.12] px-3 py-5 text-[12.5px] text-[#8A8A8A]">
                    Libre
                  </div>
                )}
                {slots.map((c, i) => {
                  const title = str(c.matiere) || valueLabel(str(c.type)) || "Créneau";
                  const color = colorFor(title);
                  return (
                    <motion.div
                      key={i}
                      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 4 }}
                      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                      transition={{ duration: 0.2, delay: Math.min(i, 6) * 0.04, ease: [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1] }}
                      className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-[8px] border bg-white py-2.5 pl-3.5 pr-3 shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)]"
                      style={{ borderColor: `${color}2E` }}>
                      <span className="absolute inset-y-2 left-1 w-[3px] rounded-full" style={{ background: color }} aria-hidden="true" />
                      <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12px] font-semibold tabular-nums" style={{ color }}>
                        <ClockIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                        {str(c.heure_debut)} – {str(c.heure_fin)}
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-1 truncate text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A]">{title}</p>
                      {c.classe !== undefined && (
                        <p className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate text-[12.5px] text-[#5D5D5D]">
                          <UsersIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                          <span className="truncate">{str(c.classe)}</span>
                        </p>
                      )}
                      {str(c.salle) && (
                        <p className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12.5px] text-[#5D5D5D]">
                          <MapPinIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                          <span className="truncate">{str(c.salle)}</span>
                        </p>
                      )}
                    </motion.div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/data/GuidedEmpty.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { CheckIcon } from "lucide-react";
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";

interface Step {
  label: string;
  done: boolean;
}

interface GuidedEmptyProps {
  icon: LucideIcon;
  title: string;
  text?: string;
  steps?: Step[];
  color?: string;
  card?: boolean;
}

const EASE = [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1] as const;

export function GuidedEmpty({ icon: Icon, title, text, steps = [], color = "var(--em-accent)", card = false }: GuidedEmptyProps) {
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();
  const nextIndex = steps.findIndex((s) => !s.done);

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={reduce ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.24, ease: EASE }}
      className={`flex flex-col items-center px-6 py-14 text-center ${card ? "ecole_m-card" : ""}`}>
      <span
        className="mb-4 flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl"
        style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${color} 14%, transparent)`, color }}>
        <Icon className="h-7 w-7" aria-hidden="true" />
      </span>
      <h3 className="text-[16px] font-semibold">{title}</h3>
      {text ? <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-1 max-w-md text-[13px] leading-relaxed">{text}</p> : null}

      {steps.length > 0 &&
      <ol className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2" aria-label="Étapes">
          {steps.map((s, i) => {
          const isNext = i === nextIndex;
          return (
            <li key={s.label} className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span
                className={`flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] transition-colors duration-150 ease-out ${
                s.done ?
                "border-transparent bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] font-medium text-[var(--em-accent)]" :
                isNext ?
                "border-[var(--em-accent)] font-medium" :
                "ecole_m-muted border-[var(--em-border)]"}`
                }>
                  <span
                  className={`grid h-5 w-5 place-items-center rounded-full text-[11px] font-semibold ${
                  s.done ? "bg-[var(--em-accent)] text-white" : "border border-current"}`
                  }>
                    {s.done ?
                  <motion.span
                    initial={reduce ? false : { scale: 0.6, opacity: 0 }}
                    animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}
                    transition={{ duration: 0.18, ease: EASE }}>
                        <CheckIcon className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden="true" />
                      </motion.span> :
                  i + 1}
                  </span>
                  {s.label}
                  <span className="sr-only">{s.done ? " (fait)" : " (à faire)"}</span>
                </span>
                {i < steps.length - 1 ? <span className="ecole_m-muted text-[12px]" aria-hidden="true">→</span> : null}
              </li>);
        })}
        </ol>
      }
    </motion.div>);
}

################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/data/ObjectView.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { DownloadIcon, FileTextIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { downloadBase64 } from "@/lib/api";
import type { Row } from "../../types/ui";
import { humanize, isHiddenKey, resolveUrl, valueLabel } from "../../utils/format";
import { buttonClasses } from "../ui/Button";
import { CellValue } from "./CellValue";
import { DataTable } from "./DataTable";

interface ObjectViewProps {
  data: unknown;
  hide?: string[];
}

const isImageKey = (k: string) => /photo|signature|image|thumbnail|apercu|logo/.test(k) && !/nb_|_ids?$|photos_ids/.test(k);
const isB64Key = (k: string) => k.endsWith("_b64");

function isComplex(v: unknown): boolean {
  if (!v || typeof v !== "object") return false;
  if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.some((x) => x !== null && typeof x === "object");
  return true;
}

function toImgSrc(k: string, v: string): string {
  if (isB64Key(k) && !v.startsWith("data:")) return `data:image/png;base64,${v}`;
  return resolveUrl(v);
}

function Muted({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return <p className="rounded-[8px] bg-[#F9F9F9] px-3 py-2.5 text-[13px] text-[#5D5D5D]">{children}</p>;
}

export function ObjectView({ data, hide = [] }: ObjectViewProps) {
  if (data === null || data === undefined) return <Muted>Aucune information.</Muted>;
  if (typeof data !== "object") return <p className="text-[14px] text-[#1A1A1A]">{valueLabel(String(data))}</p>;

  if (Array.isArray(data)) {
    if (data.length === 0) return <Muted>Aucun élément.</Muted>;
    if (data.every((x) => x && typeof x === "object" && !Array.isArray(x))) {
      return (
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-[10px] border border-black/[0.07] bg-white shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.03)]">
          <DataTable rows={data as Row[]} compact />
        </div>
      );
    }
    return <CellValue k="liste" v={data} />;
  }

  const entries = Object.entries(data as Row).filter(([k]) => !hide.includes(k) && !isHiddenKey(k));
  const images = entries.filter(([k, v]) => isImageKey(k) && typeof v === "string" && v !== "" && !k.endsWith("pdf_b64"));
  const files = entries.filter(([k, v]) => isB64Key(k) && !isImageKey(k) && typeof v === "string" && v !== "");
  const simple = entries.filter(([k, v]) => !isComplex(v) && !isImageKey(k) && !isB64Key(k));
  const complex = entries.filter(([k, v]) => isComplex(v) && !isB64Key(k));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {images.length > 0 && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          {images.map(([k, v]) => (
            <figure key={k} className="w-28">
              <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-[10px] border border-black/[0.07] bg-[#F3F3F3] shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)]">
                <img src={toImgSrc(k, String(v))} alt={humanize(k)} className="aspect-square w-full object-cover" />
              </div>
              <figcaption className="mt-1.5 truncate text-center text-[12px] font-medium text-[#5D5D5D]">{humanize(k)}</figcaption>
            </figure>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {simple.length > 0 && (
        <dl className="grid gap-px overflow-hidden rounded-[10px] border border-black/[0.07] bg-black/[0.05] sm:grid-cols-2 sm:[&>*:last-child:nth-child(odd)]:col-span-2">
          {simple.map(([k, v]) => (
            <div key={k} className="min-w-0 bg-white px-4 py-3">
              <dt className="text-[11.5px] font-medium text-[#5D5D5D]">{humanize(k)}</dt>
              <dd className="mt-1 break-words text-[13.5px] font-medium text-[#1A1A1A]">
                <CellValue k={k} v={v} />
              </dd>
            </div>
          ))}
        </dl>
      )}

      {files.length > 0 && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {files.map(([k, v]) => (
            <button
              key={k}
              type="button"
              className={buttonClasses({ variant: "secondary", size: "sm", className: "group" })}
              onClick={() =>
                downloadBase64(
                  String(v),
                  `${humanize(k)}${k.includes("pdf") ? ".pdf" : ""}`,
                  k.includes("pdf") ? "application/pdf" : "application/octet-stream"
                )
              }>
              <FileTextIcon className="h-4 w-4 text-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)]" aria-hidden="true" />
              {humanize(k)}
              <DownloadIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-[#8A8A8A] transition-transform duration-150 ease-out group-hover:translate-y-0.5" aria-hidden="true" />
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {complex.map(([k, v]) => (
        <section key={k} className="space-y-3 border-t border-black/[0.06] pt-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="h-3.5 w-[3px] rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)]" aria-hidden="true" />
            <h4 className="text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A]">{humanize(k)}</h4>
            {Array.isArray(v) && (
              <span className="grid h-5 min-w-5 place-items-center rounded-full bg-black/[0.06] px-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold tabular-nums text-[#5D5D5D]">
                {v.length}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          <ObjectView data={v} />
        </section>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

# FICHIER: components/data/RankBadge.tsx

import React from "react";
import { num } from "../../utils/format";

export function RankBadge({ value, color }: { value: unknown; color: string }) {
  const n = num(value);
  if (n == null) return <span className="ecole_m-muted">—</span>;
  const podium = n <= 3;
  return (
    <span
      className="inline-grid h-7 min-w-[28px] place-items-center rounded-full px-2 text-[12px] font-semibold tabular-nums"
      style={podium ? { background: color, color: "#fff" } : { background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${color} 10%, transparent)`, color }}>
      {n}
    </span>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/data/StatGrid.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";

export interface StatItem {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  tone?: "default" | "success" | "danger" | "accent";
}

const TONE: Record<NonNullable<StatItem["tone"]>, string> = {
  default: "",
  success: "text-[var(--em-success)]",
  danger: "text-[var(--em-danger)]",
  accent: "text-[var(--em-accent)]"
};

export function StatGrid({ items }: {items: StatItem[];}) {
  return (
    <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-6 gap-y-3 rounded-lg bg-[var(--em-surface-2)] p-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
      {items.map((s) =>
      <div key={s.label} className="min-w-0">
          <dt className="ecole_m-muted truncate text-[12px]">{s.label}</dt>
          <dd className={`mt-0.5 truncate text-[16px] font-semibold ${TONE[s.tone ?? "default"]}`}>{s.value}</dd>
        </div>
      )}
    </dl>);

}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/data/States.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { CircleAlertIcon, InboxIcon, RefreshCwIcon, LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { buttonClasses } from "../ui/Button";

const EASE = [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1] as const;

export function LoadingState({ rows = 4 }: { rows?: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-1 p-3" aria-busy="true" aria-live="polite">
      <span className="sr-only">Chargement…</span>
      {Array.from({ length: rows }).map((_, i) => (
        <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-[8px] px-2 py-2.5" style={{ opacity: 1 - i * 0.14 }}>
          <div className="h-9 w-9 shrink-0 animate-pulse rounded-[8px] bg-black/[0.06]" />
          <div className="flex-1 space-y-2">
            <div className="h-3 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-black/[0.07]" style={{ width: `${Math.max(30, 68 - i * 8)}%` }} />
            <div className="h-2.5 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-black/[0.045]" style={{ width: `${Math.max(20, 42 - i * 5)}%` }} />
          </div>
          <div className="hidden h-6 w-16 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-black/[0.045] sm:block" />
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ErrorState({ message, onRetry }: { message: string; onRetry?: () => void }) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      role="alert"
      initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.98 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.2, ease: EASE }}
      className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3.5 px-6 py-10 text-center">
      <span className="grid h-12 w-12 place-items-center rounded-[12px] bg-[#C42B1C]/[0.08] text-[var(--em-danger,#C42B1C)] ring-1 ring-[#C42B1C]/[0.12]">
        <CircleAlertIcon className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" />
      </span>
      <div>
        <p className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A]">Impossible d'afficher ces informations</p>
        <p className="mx-auto mt-1.5 max-w-md whitespace-pre-line rounded-[8px] bg-[#C42B1C]/[0.05] px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-[#5D5D5D]">{message}</p>
      </div>
      {onRetry && (
        <button type="button" className={buttonClasses({ variant: "secondary", size: "sm", className: "group" })} onClick={onRetry}>
          <RefreshCwIcon className="h-4 w-4 transition-transform duration-300 ease-out group-hover:rotate-180" aria-hidden="true" />
          Réessayer
        </button>
      )}
    </motion.div>
  );
}

interface EmptyStateProps {
  icon?: LucideIcon;
  title: string;
  text?: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
  compact?: boolean;
}

export function EmptyState({ icon: Icon = InboxIcon, title, text, children, compact }: EmptyStateProps) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 6 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.22, ease: EASE }}
      className={`flex flex-col items-center px-6 text-center ${compact ? "gap-2.5 py-6" : "gap-4 py-14"}`}>
      <span
        className={`relative grid place-items-center rounded-[14px] bg-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)]/[0.08] text-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)] ring-1 ring-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)]/[0.12] ${
          compact ? "h-10 w-10" : "h-14 w-14"
        }`}>
        <Icon className={compact ? "h-5 w-5" : "h-7 w-7"} strokeWidth={1.8} aria-hidden="true" />
      </span>
      <div>
        <p className={`font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A] ${compact ? "text-[13.5px]" : "text-[16px]"}`}>{title}</p>
        {text && <p className="mx-auto mt-1 max-w-md text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-[#5D5D5D]">{text}</p>}
      </div>
      {children && <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">{children}</div>}
    </motion.div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/docs/DocumentationDrawer.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AnimatePresence, motion, useReducedMotion } from 'framer-motion';
import { 
  X, Search, ChevronDown, ChevronRight, Lightbulb, 
  AlertTriangle, Sparkles, BookOpen, ExternalLink
} from 'lucide-react';
import { DOCUMENTATION, DEFAULT_DOC, PageDoc } from '../../data/documentation';

interface Props {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

const EASE = [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1] as const;

export function DocumentationDrawer({ open, onClose }: Props) {
  const { pathname } = useLocation();
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  
  // Logique de routage intelligente : trouve la clé la plus longue qui correspond au pathname
  const currentDoc: PageDoc = useMemo(() => {
    const match = Object.keys(DOCUMENTATION)
      .filter(key => pathname.startsWith(key))
      .sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length)[0]; // Prend la correspondance la plus précise
    return match ? DOCUMENTATION[match] : DEFAULT_DOC;
  }, [pathname]);

  // État des accordéons (tous ouverts par défaut)
  const [openSections, setOpenSections] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  useEffect(() => {
    const defaults = currentDoc.sections.reduce((acc, sec) => ({ ...acc, [sec.title]: true }), {});
    setOpenSections(defaults);
    setSearch('');
  }, [currentDoc]);

  const toggleSection = (title: string) => {
    setOpenSections(prev => ({ ...prev, [title]: !prev[title] }));
  };

  // Filtrage par recherche
  const filteredSections = currentDoc.sections.map(sec => ({
    ...sec,
    items: sec.items.filter(item => 
      item.title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || 
      item.description.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())
    )
  })).filter(sec => sec.items.length > 0);

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      {open && (
        <>
          <motion.div 
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }} animate={{ opacity: 1 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            onClick={onClose}
            className="fixed inset-0 z-[90] bg-black/20 backdrop-blur-[2px]"
          />

          <motion.aside
            initial={{ x: '100%' }}
            animate={{ x: 0 }}
            exit={{ x: '100%' }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.3, ease: EASE }}
            className="ecole_m fixed right-0 top-0 bottom-0 z-[100] flex w-full max-w-[450px] flex-col border-l border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[#FBFBFB] shadow-[-10px_0_30px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1)]"
          >
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="flex shrink-0 items-start justify-between border-b border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white px-5 py-4">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[var(--em-accent)]">
                  <BookOpen className="h-4 w-4" />
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[.1em]">Documentation</span>
                </div>
                <h2 className="ecole_m-display mt-1 truncate text-[19px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">
                  {currentDoc.title}
                </h2>
              </div>
              <button 
                onClick={onClose}
                className="grid h-8 w-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-md text-[var(--em-text-2)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--em-border)] hover:text-[var(--em-text)]"
              >
                <X className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Barre de recherche sticky */}
            <div className="sticky top-0 z-10 shrink-0 border-b border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[#FBFBFB] px-5 py-3">
              <div className="relative">
                <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-[var(--em-text-3)]" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Rechercher un mot-clé (ex: paiement)..."
                  value={search}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                  className="ecole_m-input h-9 w-full pl-9"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Contenu défilable */}
            <div className="ecole_m-scroll flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-5 py-4">
              <p className="mb-6 text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-[var(--em-text-2)]">
                {currentDoc.description}
              </p>

              {filteredSections.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="py-10 text-center">
                  <span className="mx-auto mb-3 grid h-10 w-10 place-items-center rounded-full bg-[#eef1f5] text-[var(--em-text-3)]">
                    <Search className="h-5 w-5" />
                  </span>
                  <p className="text-[13.5px] font-medium text-[var(--em-text)]">Aucun résultat</p>
                  <p className="text-[12.5px] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">Essayez un autre mot-clé.</p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-4">
                  {filteredSections.map((sec, i) => {
                    const Icon = sec.icon;
                    const isOpen = openSections[sec.title];
                    return (
                      <motion.div 
                        key={sec.title}
                        initial={reduce ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
                        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                        transition={{ delay: i * 0.05, duration: 0.2 }}
                        className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white shadow-sm"
                      >
                        {/* Titre de section (Accordéon) */}
                        <button
                          onClick={() => toggleSection(sec.title)}
                          className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 text-left transition-colors ${
                            isOpen ? 'bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)]/30' : 'hover:bg-[#F9F9F9]'
                          }`}
                        >
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                            <span className={`grid h-7 w-7 place-items-center rounded-lg transition-colors ${
                              isOpen ? 'bg-[var(--em-accent)] text-white' : 'bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] text-[var(--em-accent)]'
                            }`}>
                              <Icon className="h-4 w-4" />
                            </span>
                            <span className={`text-[13.5px] font-semibold ${isOpen ? 'text-[var(--em-accent)]' : 'text-[var(--em-text)]'}`}>
                              {sec.title}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                          {isOpen ? <ChevronDown className="h-4 w-4 text-[var(--em-accent)]" /> : <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4 text-[var(--em-text-2)]" />}
                        </button>

                        {/* Contenu de la section */}
                        <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
                          {isOpen && (
                            <motion.div
                              initial={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
                              animate={{ height: 'auto', opacity: 1 }}
                              exit={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
                              transition={{ duration: 0.25, ease: EASE }}
                              className="border-t border-[var(--em-border)] px-4 py-1"
                            >
                              <ul className="divide-y divide-[var(--em-border)]/60">
                                {sec.items.map((item, j) => (
                                  <li key={j} className="py-3 last:pb-4">
                                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                                        <p className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">{item.title}</p>
                                        
                                        {item.badge && (
                                          <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider
                                            ${item.badge === 'Nouveau' ? 'bg-[var(--em-success-soft)] text-[var(--em-success)]' : 
                                              item.badge === 'Astuce' ? 'bg-[var(--em-warning-soft)] text-[var(--em-warning)]' : 
                                              'bg-[var(--em-danger-soft)] text-[var(--em-danger)]'}`}
                                          >
                                            {item.badge === 'Astuce' && <Lightbulb className="h-3 w-3" />}
                                            {item.badge === 'Attention' && <AlertTriangle className="h-3 w-3" />}
                                            {item.badge === 'Nouveau' && <Sparkles className="h-3 w-3" />}
                                            {item.badge}
                                          </span>
                                        )}
                                      </div>
                                      <p className="text-[12.5px] leading-relaxed text-[var(--em-text-2)]">
                                        {item.description}
                                      </p>
                                    </div>
                                  </li>
                                ))}
                              </ul>
                            </motion.div>
                          )}
                        </AnimatePresence>
                      </motion.div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Footer */}
            <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white px-5 py-3 flex items-center justify-between text-[11.5px] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">
              <span>École Manager © {new Date().getFullYear()}</span>
              <a href="#" className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[var(--em-accent)] hover:underline">
                Support <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3" />
              </a>
            </div>
          </motion.aside>
        </>
      )}
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

# FICHIER: components/docs/HelpButton.tsx
################################################################################

import React from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { HelpCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export function HelpButton({ onClick }: { onClick: () => void }) {
  return (
    <motion.button
      initial={{ scale: 0, opacity: 0 }}
      animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}
      whileHover={{ scale: 1.05 }}
      whileTap={{ scale: 0.95 }}
      transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 400, damping: 20 }}
      onClick={onClick}
      className="fixed bottom-[80px] right-6 z-[80] grid h-12 w-12 place-items-center rounded-full bg-white text-[var(--em-text-2)] shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(0,0,0,0.15)] ring-1 ring-[var(--em-border)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] hover:text-[var(--em-accent)] hover:ring-[var(--em-accent)]/30 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--em-accent)]"
      aria-label="Ouvrir la documentation"
    >
      <HelpCircle className="h-6 w-6" />
      <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 flex h-3 w-3">
        <span className="absolute inline-flex h-full w-full animate-ping rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent)] opacity-75" />
        <span className="relative inline-flex h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent)]" />
      </span>
    </motion.button>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/eleves/EleveDetail.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { AnimatePresence, motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { FolderOpenIcon, GraduationCapIcon, HeartPulseIcon, UserRoundIcon, WalletIcon } from "lucide-react";
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import type { Row } from "../../types/ui";
import { EleveProfil } from "./EleveProfil";
import { EleveScolarite } from "./EleveScolarite";
import { EleveFinance } from "./EleveFinance";
import { EleveSante } from "./EleveSante";
import { EleveDocuments } from "./EleveDocuments";
import { EASE_OUT } from "./EleveMotion";
import { fullName, str } from "../../utils/format";

const TABS: { id: string; label: string; icon: LucideIcon }[] = [
  { id: "profil", label: "Profil", icon: UserRoundIcon },
  { id: "scolarite", label: "Scolarité", icon: GraduationCapIcon },
  { id: "finance", label: "Finance", icon: WalletIcon },
  { id: "sante", label: "Suivi", icon: HeartPulseIcon },
  { id: "documents", label: "Documents", icon: FolderOpenIcon },
];

export function EleveDetail({ eleve, anneeId, onChanged }: { eleve: Row; anneeId: string; onChanged: () => void }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("profil");
  const id = str(eleve.id);
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();
  const refs = useRef<(HTMLButtonElement | null)[]>([]);

  const onKey = (e: React.KeyboardEvent, index: number) => {
    if (e.key !== "ArrowRight" && e.key !== "ArrowLeft") return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const next = (index + (e.key === "ArrowRight" ? 1 : -1) + TABS.length) % TABS.length;
    setTab(TABS[next].id);
    refs.current[next]?.focus();
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="sticky top-0 z-10 -mx-1 px-1 pb-1 pt-1 backdrop-blur-md">
        <div
          role="tablist"
          aria-label="Dossier de l'élève"
          className="flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-black/[0.03] p-1 [scrollbar-width:none]"
        >
          {TABS.map((t, i) => {
            const active = t.id === tab;
            const Icon = t.icon;
            return (
              <button
                key={t.id}
                ref={(el) => (refs.current[i] = el)}
                role="tab"
                id={`eleve-tab-${t.id}`}
                aria-selected={active}
                aria-controls={`eleve-panel-${t.id}`}
                tabIndex={active ? 0 : -1}
                onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => onKey(e, i)}
                className={`relative flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md px-3.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium outline-none transition-colors duration-150 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--em-accent)] ${
                  active ? "text-[var(--em-accent)]" : "ecole_m-muted hover:bg-black/[0.04] hover:text-inherit"
                }`}
              >
                {active && (
                  <motion.span
                    layoutId="eleve-tab-pill"
                    className="absolute inset-0 rounded-md border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[var(--em-surface,#ffffff)] shadow-[0_1px_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.08)]"
                    transition={{ duration: reduce ? 0 : 0.22, ease: EASE_OUT }}
                  />
                )}
                <Icon className="relative h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
                <span className="relative">{t.label}</span>
                {active && (
                  <motion.span
                    layoutId="eleve-tab-bar"
                    className="absolute bottom-0.5 left-1/2 h-[3px] w-4 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent)]"
                    transition={{ duration: reduce ? 0 : 0.22, ease: EASE_OUT }}
                  />
                )}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence mode="wait" initial={false}>
        <motion.div
          key={tab}
          role="tabpanel"
          id={`eleve-panel-${tab}`}
          aria-labelledby={`eleve-tab-${tab}`}
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: reduce ? 0 : 6 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          exit={{ opacity: 0, y: reduce ? 0 : -4 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.18, ease: EASE_OUT }}
        >
          {tab === "profil" && <EleveProfil eleveId={id} onChanged={onChanged} />}
          {tab === "scolarite" && <EleveScolarite eleveId={id} anneeId={anneeId} />}
          {tab === "finance" && <EleveFinance eleveId={id} />}
          {tab === "sante" && <EleveSante eleveId={id} eleveNom={fullName(eleve)} />}
          {tab === "documents" && <EleveDocuments eleveId={id} />}
        </motion.div>
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/eleves/EleveDocuments.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { DownloadIcon, FileUpIcon, Trash2Icon } from "lucide-react";
import {
  listerDocumentsEleve,
  supprimerDocumentEleve,
  telechargerDocumentEleve,
  uploaderDocumentEleve,
} from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useTick } from "../../hooks/useAsync";
import { documentEleveFields } from "../../data/fields/eleves";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../data/AsyncBlock";
import { MiniTable } from "../data/MiniTable";
import { EmptyState } from "../data/States";
import { AsyncButton } from "../form/AsyncButton";
import { ConfirmAction } from "../form/ConfirmAction";
import { FormAction } from "../form/FormAction";
import { Section } from "../layout/Section";
import { CommandBar, Stagger, StaggerItem } from "./EleveMotion";
import { saveFile } from "../../utils/files";
import { formatNumber, num, str } from "../../utils/format";

export function EleveDocuments({ eleveId }: { eleveId: string }) {
  const [tick, bump] = useTick();

  return (
    <Stagger className="space-y-5">
      <StaggerItem>
        <CommandBar hint="Acte de naissance, certificat médical, pièces des parents…">
          <FormAction
            label="Ajouter un document"
            icon={FileUpIcon}
            size="sm"
            variant="primary"
            fields={documentEleveFields}
            onSubmit={(b) => uploaderDocumentEleve(eleveId, str(b.type_document), b.fichier as File)}
            successMessage="Document ajouté"
            onDone={bump}
          />
        </CommandBar>
      </StaggerItem>

      <StaggerItem>
        <Section title="Dossier numérique">
          <AsyncBlock
            load={() => listerDocumentsEleve(eleveId)}
            deps={[eleveId, tick]}
            empty={
              <EmptyState
                compact
                title="Aucun document"
                text="Acte de naissance, certificat médical, pièces des parents…"
              />
            }
          >
            {(rows) => (
              <MiniTable
                rows={rows}
                columns={[
                  { key: "type_document", label: "Type" },
                  { key: "nom_fichier_original", label: "Fichier" },
                  {
                    key: "taille_octets",
                    label: "Taille",
                    render: (r) => `${formatNumber(Math.round((num(r.taille_octets) ?? 0) / 1024))} Ko`,
                  },
                  { key: "created_at", label: "Ajouté le" },
                ]}
                rowActions={(r) => (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5">
                    <AsyncButton
                      label="Télécharger"
                      icon={DownloadIcon}
                      iconOnly
                      size="sm"
                      variant="subtle"
                      onClick={async () => saveFile(await telechargerDocumentEleve(eleveId, str(r.id)))}
                    />
                    <ConfirmAction
                      label="Supprimer"
                      icon={Trash2Icon}
                      iconOnly
                      size="sm"
                      variant="subtle"
                      danger
                      message={`Supprimer le document « ${str(r.nom_fichier_original)} » ?`}
                      confirmLabel="Supprimer"
                      run={() => supprimerDocumentEleve(eleveId, str(r.id))}
                      successMessage="Document supprimé"
                      onDone={bump}
                    />
                  </div>
                )}
              />
            )}
          </AsyncBlock>
        </Section>
      </StaggerItem>
    </Stagger>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/eleves/EleveFinance.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { BanknoteIcon, WalletCardsIcon } from "lucide-react";
import {
  enregistrerPaiement,
  enregistrerPaiementAvecCredit,
  getSituationFinanciereComplete,
  listerMouvementsCredit,
} from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useTick } from "../../hooks/useAsync";
import { paiementFields } from "../../data/fields/eleves";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../data/AsyncBlock";
import { MiniTable } from "../data/MiniTable";
import { StatGrid } from "../data/StatGrid";
import { EmptyState } from "../data/States";
import { FormAction } from "../form/FormAction";
import { Section } from "../layout/Section";
import { CommandBar, Stagger, StaggerItem } from "./EleveMotion";
import { arr, formatMoney, num } from "../../utils/format";

export function EleveFinance({ eleveId }: { eleveId: string }) {
  const [tick, bump] = useTick();

  return (
    <Stagger className="space-y-5">
      <StaggerItem>
        <CommandBar hint="Les montants sont répartis par le serveur sur les échéances">
          <FormAction
            label="Encaisser un paiement"
            icon={BanknoteIcon}
            size="sm"
            variant="primary"
            description="Le montant est réparti automatiquement sur les échéances dues."
            fields={paiementFields()}
            onSubmit={(b) => enregistrerPaiement({ ...b, eleve_id: eleveId })}
            successMessage="Paiement enregistré"
            showResult
            onDone={bump}
          />
          <FormAction
            label="Paiement avec avoir"
            icon={WalletCardsIcon}
            size="sm"
            description="Tout excédent est conservé comme avoir au profit de l'élève."
            fields={paiementFields()}
            onSubmit={(b) => enregistrerPaiementAvecCredit({ ...b, eleve_id: eleveId })}
            successMessage="Paiement enregistré"
            showResult
            onDone={bump}
          />
        </CommandBar>
      </StaggerItem>

      <AsyncBlock load={() => getSituationFinanciereComplete(eleveId)} deps={[eleveId, tick]}>
        {(s) => (
          <div className="space-y-5">
            <StaggerItem>
              <StatGrid
                items={[
                  { label: "Total dû", value: formatMoney(s.total_du_fcfa) },
                  { label: "Total payé", value: formatMoney(s.total_paye_fcfa), tone: "success" },
                  {
                    label: "Reste à payer",
                    value: formatMoney(s.solde_restant_fcfa),
                    tone: (num(s.solde_restant_fcfa) ?? 0) > 0 ? "danger" : "default",
                  },
                  { label: "Avoir disponible", value: formatMoney(s.solde_credit_fcfa), tone: "accent" },
                ]}
              />
            </StaggerItem>

            <StaggerItem>
              <Section title="Échéancier">
                {arr(s.echeances).length ? (
                  <MiniTable rows={arr(s.echeances)} />
                ) : (
                  <EmptyState compact title="Aucune échéance" />
                )}
              </Section>
            </StaggerItem>
          </div>
        )}
      </AsyncBlock>

      <StaggerItem>
        <Section title="Mouvements d'avoir">
          <AsyncBlock
            load={() => listerMouvementsCredit(eleveId)}
            deps={[eleveId, tick]}
            empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucun mouvement" />}
          >
            {(rows) => <MiniTable rows={rows} />}
          </AsyncBlock>
        </Section>
      </StaggerItem>
    </Stagger>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/eleves/EleveMotion.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";

export const EASE_OUT = [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1] as const;

type WithChildren = { children: React.ReactNode; className?: string };

/** Apparition douce (opacité + léger glissement). */
export function Reveal({ children, className, delay = 0 }: WithChildren & { delay?: number }) {
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();
  return (
    <motion.div
      className={className}
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: reduce ? 0 : 8 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.24, ease: EASE_OUT, delay }}
    >
      {children}
    </motion.div>
  );
}

/** Conteneur qui fait apparaître ses enfants en cascade. */
export function Stagger({ children, className }: WithChildren) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      className={className}
      initial="hidden"
      animate="show"
      variants={{ hidden: {}, show: { transition: { staggerChildren: 0.05 } } }}
    >
      {children}
    </motion.div>
  );
}

export function StaggerItem({ children, className }: WithChildren) {
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();
  return (
    <motion.div
      className={className}
      variants={{
        hidden: { opacity: 0, y: reduce ? 0 : 8 },
        show: { opacity: 1, y: 0, transition: { duration: 0.24, ease: EASE_OUT } },
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </motion.div>
  );
}

/** Barre de commandes façon Windows 11 (Fluent). */
export function CommandBar({ children, className = "", hint }: WithChildren & { hint?: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[var(--em-surface,#ffffff)]/80 px-2.5 py-2 shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)] backdrop-blur-md ${className}`}
    >
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">{children}</div>
      {hint && <p className="ecole_m-muted ml-auto hidden text-[12px] md:block">{hint}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

# FICHIER: components/eleves/EleveProfil.tsx

import React from "react";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { CameraIcon, DoorOpenIcon, EyeIcon, FileDownIcon, HashIcon, KeyRoundIcon, PencilIcon } from "lucide-react";
import {
  enregistrerSortieEleve,
  genererCodeAccesEleve,
  getCartePdf,
  getCarteApercu,
  getCodeAccesEleve,
  getEleve,
  modifierEleve,
  uploaderPhotoEleve,
} from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useTick } from "../../hooks/useAsync";
import { eleveEditFields, sortieFields } from "../../data/fields/eleves";
import { FACE_CARTE } from "../../data/enums";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../data/AsyncBlock";
import { ObjectView } from "../data/ObjectView";
import { EmptyState } from "../data/States";
import { Badge } from "../ui/Badge";
import { ConfirmAction } from "../form/ConfirmAction";
import { FileAction } from "../form/FileAction";
import { FormAction } from "../form/FormAction";
import { Section } from "../layout/Section";
import { EASE_OUT, Stagger, StaggerItem } from "./EleveMotion";
import { enumField, F } from "../../utils/fieldBuilders";
import { fromEnum, optTemplatesCarte } from "../../utils/options";
import { openFile, saveFile } from "../../utils/files";
import { fullName, initials, resolveUrl, statusTone, str, valueLabel } from "../../utils/format";

const carteFields = [
  enumField("face", "Face", fromEnum(FACE_CARTE), { defaultValue: "recto" }),
  F.select("template", "Modèle", { load: optTemplatesCarte }),
];

export function EleveProfil({ eleveId, onChanged }: { eleveId: string; onChanged: () => void }) {
  const [tick, bump] = useTick();
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();
  const refresh = () => {
    bump();
    onChanged();
  };

  return (
    <AsyncBlock load={() => getEleve(eleveId)} deps={[eleveId, tick]} rows={5}>
      {(e) => {
        const name = fullName(e);
        return (
          <Stagger className="space-y-5">
            {/* ── En-tête de profil ─────────────────────────────── */}
            <StaggerItem>
              <header className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[var(--em-surface,#ffffff)] shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)]">
                <div className="h-20 bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)]" aria-hidden />
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 px-5 pb-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-end gap-4">
                    <motion.div
                      initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: reduce ? 1 : 0.96 }}
                      animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
                      transition={{ duration: 0.24, ease: EASE_OUT }}
                      className="-mt-10 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-surface,#ffffff)] p-1 shadow-[0_4px_12px_rgba(0,0,0,0.10)]"
                    >
                      {str(e.photo_url) ? (
                        <img
                          src={resolveUrl(str(e.photo_url))}
                          alt={name}
                          className="h-20 w-20 rounded-full object-cover"
                        />
                      ) : (
                        <span className="grid h-20 w-20 place-items-center rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] text-[24px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-accent)]">
                          {initials(name)}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </motion.div>
                    <div className="min-w-0 pb-0.5">
                      <h2 className="truncate text-[20px] font-semibold leading-tight">{name}</h2>
                      <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="ecole_m-muted inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-[var(--em-border)] px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[12px]">
                          <HashIcon className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden />
                          Matricule {str(e.matricule)}
                        </span>
                        <Badge tone={statusTone(str(e.statut))}>{valueLabel(str(e.statut))}</Badge>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                    <FormAction
                      label="Modifier"
                      icon={PencilIcon}
                      size="sm"
                      fields={eleveEditFields}
                      initial={e}
                      onSubmit={(b) => modifierEleve(eleveId, b)}
                      successMessage="Fiche mise à jour"
                      onDone={refresh}
                    />
                    <FileAction
                      label="Changer la photo"
                      icon={CameraIcon}
                      accept="image/*"
                      onFile={(f) => uploaderPhotoEleve(eleveId, f)}
                      successMessage="Photo mise à jour"
                      onDone={refresh}
                    />
                    {str(e.statut) === "actif" && (
                      <FormAction
                        label="Enregistrer une sortie"
                        icon={DoorOpenIcon}
                        size="sm"
                        description="Transfert, exclusion, diplôme ou abandon."
                        fields={sortieFields()}
                        onSubmit={(b) => enregistrerSortieEleve(eleveId, b)}
                        successMessage="Sortie enregistrée"
                        showResult
                        onDone={refresh}
                      />
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </header>
            </StaggerItem>

            {/* ── Corps : informations + colonne latérale ───────── */}
            <div className="grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_340px]">
              <StaggerItem>
                <Section title="Informations">
                  <ObjectView data={e} hide={["photo_url", "nom", "prenom", "matricule", "statut"]} />
                </Section>
              </StaggerItem>

              <div className="space-y-5">
                <StaggerItem>
                  <Section title="Carte scolaire">
                    <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-1">
                      <FormAction
                        label="Aperçu de la carte"
                        icon={EyeIcon}
                        size="sm"
                        fields={carteFields}
                        submitLabel="Afficher"
                        successMessage="Aperçu prêt"
                        onSubmit={async (b) => {
                          const f = await getCarteApercu(eleveId, {
                            face: str(b.face) || undefined,
                            template: str(b.template) || undefined,
                          });
                          openFile(f);
                          return f.filename;
                        }}
                      />
                      <FormAction
                        label="Télécharger la carte"
                        icon={FileDownIcon}
                        size="sm"
                        fields={[
                          F.select("template", "Modèle", { load: optTemplatesCarte }),
                          F.bool("persist", "Conserver une copie dans le dossier"),
                        ]}
                        submitLabel="Télécharger"
                        successMessage="Carte téléchargée"
                        onSubmit={async (b) => {
                          const f = await getCartePdf(eleveId, {
                            template: str(b.template) || undefined,
                            persist: b.persist === true,
                          });
                          saveFile(f);
                          return f.filename;
                        }}
                      />
                    </div>
                  </Section>
                </StaggerItem>

                <StaggerItem>
                  <Section
                    title="Code d'accès pour les parents"
                    description="Ce code permet à un parent de rattacher l'enfant à son compte."
                    actions={
                      <ConfirmAction
                        label="Générer un code"
                        icon={KeyRoundIcon}
                        size="sm"
                        message="Un nouveau code d'accès sera créé pour cet élève."
                        run={() => genererCodeAccesEleve(eleveId)}
                        successMessage="Code généré"
                        showResult
                        onDone={bump}
                      />
                    }
                  >
                    <AsyncBlock
                      load={() => getCodeAccesEleve(eleveId)}
                      deps={[eleveId, tick]}
                      isEmpty={(d) => !d}
                      empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucun code actif" />}
                    >
                      {(c) => (
                        <div className="space-y-3">
                          {c && str(c.code) && (
                            <motion.div
                              key={str(c.code)}
                              initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: reduce ? 1 : 0.96 }}
                              animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
                              transition={{ duration: 0.22, ease: EASE_OUT }}
                              className="rounded-lg border border-dashed border-[var(--em-accent)]/40 bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] px-4 py-4 text-center"
                            >
                              <p className="font-mono text-[22px] font-semibold tracking-[0.24em] text-[var(--em-accent)] select-all">
                                {str(c.code)}
                              </p>
                            </motion.div>
                          )}
                          <ObjectView data={c} hide={["code"]} />
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </AsyncBlock>
                  </Section>
                </StaggerItem>
              </div>
            </div>
          </Stagger>
        );
      }}
    </AsyncBlock>
  );
}
