import React from "react";
import { BellRingIcon, CalendarXIcon, ClipboardPlusIcon, ShieldAlertIcon, ShieldPlusIcon } from "lucide-react";
import {
  creerProtocoleUrgence,
  creerSanction,
  enregistrerVisiteInfirmerie,
  getContactUrgenceEleve,
  getJournalNotifications,
  getProtocoleUrgence,
  listerSanctionsEleve,
  listerSignatures,
  listerVisitesInfirmerie,
  notifierAbsence,
  notifierUrgenceSante,
} from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useTick } from "../../hooks/useAsync";
import { absenceFields, protocoleFields, sanctionFields, urgenceFields, visiteFields } from "../../data/fields/eleves";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../data/AsyncBlock";
import { MiniTable } from "../data/MiniTable";
import { ObjectView } from "../data/ObjectView";
import { EmptyState } from "../data/States";
import { FormAction } from "../form/FormAction";
import { Section } from "../layout/Section";
import { Stagger, StaggerItem } from "./EleveMotion";

export function EleveSante({ eleveId, eleveNom }: { eleveId: string; eleveNom: string }) {
  const [tick, bump] = useTick();
  const withE = { eleve_id: eleveId };

  return (
    <Stagger className="space-y-5">
      {/* Urgence : les deux blocs critiques côte à côte */}
      <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <StaggerItem>
          <Section title="Contact d'urgence">
            <AsyncBlock
              load={() => getContactUrgenceEleve(eleveId)}
              deps={[eleveId]}
              isEmpty={(d) => !d}
              empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucun contact renseigné" />}
            >
              {(c) => <ObjectView data={c} />}
            </AsyncBlock>
          </Section>
        </StaggerItem>

        <StaggerItem>
          <Section
            title="Protocole d'urgence"
            actions={
              <FormAction
                label="Définir un protocole"
                icon={ShieldPlusIcon}
                size="sm"
                fields={protocoleFields(false)}
                modalSize="lg"
                onSubmit={(b) => creerProtocoleUrgence({ ...b, ...withE })}
                successMessage="Protocole enregistré"
                onDone={bump}
              />
            }
          >
            <AsyncBlock
              load={() => getProtocoleUrgence(eleveId)}
              deps={[eleveId, tick]}
              isEmpty={(d) => !d}
              empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucun protocole" />}
            >
              {(p) => <ObjectView data={p} />}
            </AsyncBlock>
          </Section>
        </StaggerItem>
      </div>

      <StaggerItem>
        <Section
          title="Messages aux familles"
          actions={
            <>
              <FormAction
                label="Signaler une absence"
                icon={CalendarXIcon}
                size="sm"
                fields={absenceFields(false)}
                onSubmit={(b) => notifierAbsence({ ...b, ...withE, eleve_nom: eleveNom })}
                successMessage="Famille prévenue"
                showResult
                onDone={bump}
              />
              <FormAction
                label="Alerte santé"
                icon={BellRingIcon}
                size="sm"
                variant="danger"
                fields={urgenceFields(false)}
                onSubmit={(b) => notifierUrgenceSante({ ...b, ...withE, eleve_nom: eleveNom })}
                successMessage="Alerte envoyée"
                showResult
                onDone={bump}
              />
            </>
          }
        >
          <AsyncBlock
            load={() => getJournalNotifications(eleveId)}
            deps={[eleveId, tick]}
            empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucun message envoyé" />}
          >
            {(rows) => <MiniTable rows={rows} />}
          </AsyncBlock>
        </Section>
      </StaggerItem>

      <div className="grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-2">
        <StaggerItem>
          <Section
            title="Passages à l'infirmerie"
            actions={
              <FormAction
                label="Nouvelle visite"
                icon={ClipboardPlusIcon}
                size="sm"
                fields={visiteFields(false)}
                modalSize="lg"
                onSubmit={(b) => enregistrerVisiteInfirmerie({ ...b, ...withE })}
                successMessage="Visite enregistrée"
                onDone={bump}
              />
            }
          >
            <AsyncBlock
              load={() => listerVisitesInfirmerie(eleveId)}
              deps={[eleveId, tick]}
              empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucune visite" />}
            >
              {(rows) => <MiniTable rows={rows} />}
            </AsyncBlock>
          </Section>
        </StaggerItem>

        <StaggerItem>
          <Section
            title="Sanctions"
            actions={
              <FormAction
                label="Nouvelle sanction"
                icon={ShieldAlertIcon}
                size="sm"
                fields={sanctionFields(false)}
                onSubmit={(b) => creerSanction({ ...b, ...withE })}
                successMessage="Sanction enregistrée"
                onDone={bump}
              />
            }
          >
            <AsyncBlock
              load={() => listerSanctionsEleve(eleveId)}
              deps={[eleveId, tick]}
              empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucune sanction" />}
            >
              {(rows) => <MiniTable rows={rows} />}
            </AsyncBlock>
          </Section>
        </StaggerItem>
      </div>

      <StaggerItem>
        <Section title="Documents à signer">
          <AsyncBlock
            load={() => listerSignatures({ eleve_id: eleveId })}
            deps={[eleveId]}
            empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucun document" />}
          >
            {(rows) => <MiniTable rows={rows} />}
          </AsyncBlock>
        </Section>
      </StaggerItem>
    </Stagger>
  );
}

# FICHIER: components/eleves/EleveScolarite.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { GavelIcon, RefreshCwIcon, UserPlusIcon } from "lucide-react";
import {
  cloturerAnneeEleve,
  getLivretAnnuel,
  getLivretParcours,
  getParcoursCompletEleve,
  inscrireEleve,
  listerEmprunts,
  listerPhotosEleve,
  recalculerParcoursEleve,
} from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useTick } from "../../hooks/useAsync";
import { clotureFields, inscriptionFields } from "../../data/fields/eleves";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../data/AsyncBlock";
import { MiniTable } from "../data/MiniTable";
import { ObjectView } from "../data/ObjectView";
import { StatGrid } from "../data/StatGrid";
import { EmptyState } from "../data/States";
import { AsyncButton } from "../form/AsyncButton";
import { FormAction } from "../form/FormAction";
import { Section } from "../layout/Section";
import { InscriptionResult } from "./InscriptionResult";
import { CommandBar, Stagger, StaggerItem } from "./EleveMotion";
import { arr, formatNumber, resolveUrl, str } from "../../utils/format";

export function EleveScolarite({ eleveId, anneeId }: { eleveId: string; anneeId: string }) {
  const [tick, bump] = useTick();

  return (
    <Stagger className="space-y-5">
      <StaggerItem>
        <CommandBar hint="Inscription et décisions du conseil de classe">
          <FormAction
            label="Inscrire dans une classe"
            icon={UserPlusIcon}
            size="sm"
            variant="primary"
            fields={inscriptionFields(anneeId)}
            onSubmit={(b) => inscrireEleve({ ...b, eleve_id: eleveId })}
            successMessage="Inscription enregistrée"
            showResult
            renderResult={(r) => <InscriptionResult result={r} anneeId={anneeId} />}
            onDone={bump}
          />
          <FormAction
            label="Clôturer l'année"
            icon={GavelIcon}
            size="sm"
            description="Enregistrez la décision du conseil de classe pour l'année sélectionnée."
            fields={clotureFields(anneeId)}
            onSubmit={(b) => cloturerAnneeEleve(eleveId, anneeId, b)}
            successMessage="Année clôturée"
            onDone={bump}
          />
        </CommandBar>
      </StaggerItem>

      <StaggerItem>
        <Section title="Année en cours">
          <AsyncBlock load={() => getLivretAnnuel(eleveId, anneeId)} deps={[eleveId, anneeId, tick]}>
            {(l) => (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <StatGrid
                  items={[
                    { label: "Moyenne annuelle", value: formatNumber(l.moyenne_annuelle), tone: "accent" },
                    { label: "Périodes publiées", value: formatNumber(l.nb_periodes_publiees) },
                  ]}
                />
                {arr(l.bulletins_periode).length > 0 && <MiniTable rows={arr(l.bulletins_periode)} />}
              </div>
            )}
          </AsyncBlock>
        </Section>
      </StaggerItem>

      <div className="grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-2">
        <StaggerItem>
          <Section title="Livret scolaire">
            <AsyncBlock
              load={() => getLivretParcours(eleveId)}
              deps={[eleveId, tick]}
              empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucune année clôturée" />}
            >
              {(rows) => <MiniTable rows={rows} />}
            </AsyncBlock>
          </Section>
        </StaggerItem>

        <StaggerItem>
          <Section
            title="Parcours complet"
            actions={
              <AsyncButton
                label="Mettre à jour"
                icon={RefreshCwIcon}
                size="sm"
                successMessage="Parcours mis à jour"
                onClick={async () => {
                  await recalculerParcoursEleve(eleveId);
                  bump();
                }}
              />
            }
          >
            <AsyncBlock
              load={() => getParcoursCompletEleve(eleveId)}
              deps={[eleveId, tick]}
              isEmpty={(d) => arr(d.annees).length === 0}
              empty={
                <EmptyState
                  compact
                  title="Parcours pas encore calculé"
                  text="Cliquez sur « Mettre à jour » pour le générer."
                />
              }
            >
              {(p) => <ObjectView data={p} />}
            </AsyncBlock>
          </Section>
        </StaggerItem>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-2">
        <StaggerItem>
          <Section title="Photos de classe">
            <AsyncBlock
              load={() => listerPhotosEleve(eleveId)}
              deps={[eleveId]}
              empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucune photo" />}
            >
              {(rows) => (
                <Stagger className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
                  {rows.map((p) => (
                    <StaggerItem key={str(p.id)}>
                      <figure className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-black/[0.02] shadow-[0_1px_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.05)]">
                        <img
                          src={resolveUrl(str(p.url_photo || p.thumbnail_url))}
                          alt={str(p.titre) || "Photo de classe"}
                          loading="lazy"
                          className="aspect-[4/3] w-full object-cover"
                        />
                        {str(p.titre) && (
                          <figcaption className="truncate border-t border-[var(--em-border)] px-2.5 py-2 text-[12px] font-medium">
                            {str(p.titre)}
                          </figcaption>
                        )}
                      </figure>
                    </StaggerItem>
                  ))}
                </Stagger>
              )}
            </AsyncBlock>
          </Section>
        </StaggerItem>

        <StaggerItem>
          <Section title="Emprunts à la bibliothèque">
            <AsyncBlock
              load={() => listerEmprunts({ eleve_id: eleveId })}
              deps={[eleveId]}
              empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucun emprunt" />}
            >
              {(rows) => <MiniTable rows={rows} />}
            </AsyncBlock>
          </Section>
        </StaggerItem>
      </div>
    </Stagger>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/eleves/InscriptionResult.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { ArrowLeftRightIcon, CircleCheckIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { changerClasseInscription } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { changerClasseFields } from "../../data/fields/eleves";
import { ObjectView } from "../data/ObjectView";
import { FormAction } from "../form/FormAction";
import { EASE_OUT } from "./EleveMotion";
import { obj, str } from "../../utils/format";

/** Résultat d'une inscription : permet de corriger immédiatement la classe attribuée. */
export function InscriptionResult({ result, anneeId }: { result: unknown; anneeId: string }) {
  const inscriptionId = str(obj(obj(result).inscription).id);
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();

  return (
    <motion.div
      className="space-y-4"
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: reduce ? 0 : 6 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.22, ease: EASE_OUT }}
    >
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[var(--em-surface,#ffffff)] p-4">
        <ObjectView data={result} />
      </div>

      {inscriptionId && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] px-4 py-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px]">
            <CircleCheckIcon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-[var(--em-accent)]" aria-hidden />
            La classe attribuée peut encore être corrigée.
          </p>
          <FormAction
            label="Changer de classe"
            icon={ArrowLeftRightIcon}
            size="sm"
            fields={changerClasseFields(anneeId)}
            onSubmit={(b) => changerClasseInscription(inscriptionId, b)}
            successMessage="Classe modifiée"
            showResult
          />
        </div>
      )}
    </motion.div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/email_code_input.tsx
################################################################################

import { useRef } from 'react';

interface EmailCodeInputProps {
  value: string;
  onChange: (code: string) => void;
  onComplete?: (code: string) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
  hasError?: boolean;
  length?: number;
}

export function EmailCodeInput({
  value,
  onChange,
  onComplete,
  disabled = false,
  hasError = false,
  length = 6,
}: EmailCodeInputProps) {
  const refs = useRef<Array<HTMLInputElement | null>>([]);

  const commit = (next: string) => {
    const clean = next.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, length);
    onChange(clean);
    if (clean.length === length) onComplete?.(clean);
  };

  const focusAt = (i: number) => {
    const t = refs.current[Math.max(0, Math.min(i, length - 1))];
    t?.focus();
    t?.select();
  };

  const handleChange = (i: number, raw: string) => {
    const typed = raw.replace(/\D/g, '');
    if (!typed) return;
    const chars = value.split('');
    for (let k = 0; k < typed.length && i + k < length; k++) chars[i + k] = typed[k];
    commit(chars.join('').slice(0, length));
    focusAt(i + typed.length);
  };

  const handleKeyDown = (i: number, e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.key === 'Backspace') {
      e.preventDefault();
      const chars = value.split('');
      if (chars[i]) { chars[i] = ''; commit(chars.join('')); focusAt(i); }
      else { chars[i - 1] = ''; commit(chars.join('')); focusAt(i - 1); }
      return;
    }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') { e.preventDefault(); focusAt(i - 1); }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') { e.preventDefault(); focusAt(i + 1); }
  };

  const handlePaste = (e: React.ClipboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const pasted = e.clipboardData.getData('text').replace(/\D/g, '');
    if (!pasted) return;
    commit(pasted);
    focusAt(Math.min(pasted.length, length - 1));
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex gap-2" role="group" aria-label="Code de vérification">
      {Array.from({ length }).map((_, i) => (
        <input
          key={i}
          ref={(el) => { refs.current[i] = el; }}
          type="text"
          inputMode="numeric"
          autoComplete="one-time-code"
          maxLength={length}
          value={value[i] ?? ''}
          disabled={disabled}
          aria-label={`Chiffre ${i + 1}`}
          onChange={(e) => handleChange(i, e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => handleKeyDown(i, e)}
          onPaste={handlePaste}
          onFocus={(e) => e.currentTarget.select()}
          className="h-14 w-12 rounded-md border bg-white text-center text-xl font-mono font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A] outline-none transition-colors focus:border-[var(--em-accent)] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[var(--em-accent)]/20 disabled:bg-[#F3F3F3] disabled:text-[#8A8A8A]"
          style={{ borderColor: hasError ? 'var(--em-danger)' : 'var(--em-border)' }}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/email_verification.tsx
################################################################################

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  MailIcon, ShieldCheckIcon, LoaderCircleIcon, AlertCircleIcon,
  CheckCircle2Icon, RotateCcwIcon,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { authRequestEmailCode, authVerifyEmailCode } from '@/lib/api';
import { EmailCodeInput } from './email_code_input';

interface EmailVerificationProps {
  email: string;
  verificationToken: string | null;
  onVerified: (token: string) => void;
}

export function EmailVerification({ email, verificationToken, onVerified }: EmailVerificationProps) {
  const [codeSent, setCodeSent] = useState(false);
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [sending, setSending] = useState(false);
  const [verifying, setVerifying] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [secondsLeft, setSecondsLeft] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (secondsLeft <= 0) return;
    const t = setTimeout(() => setSecondsLeft((s) => s - 1), 1000);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [secondsLeft]);

  const requestCode = async () => {
    setError(null);
    if (!email.trim()) { setError('Email requis'); return; }
    setSending(true);
    try {
      const res = await authRequestEmailCode(email.trim());
      setCodeSent(true);
      setCode('');
      setSecondsLeft(res.expires_in_seconds ?? 600);
    } catch (e: any) {
      setError(e?.message || "Impossible d'envoyer le code");
    } finally {
      setSending(false);
    }
  };

  const verify = async (value: string) => {
    if (value.length !== 6) return;
    setError(null);
    setVerifying(true);
    try {
      const res = await authVerifyEmailCode(email.trim(), value);
      onVerified(res.verification_token);
    } catch (e: any) {
      setError(e?.message || 'Code invalide');
    } finally {
      setVerifying(false);
    }
  };

  const mmss = (s: number) =>
    `${String(Math.floor(s / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;

  /* ── État vérifié ─────────────────────────────────────── */
  if (verificationToken) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[var(--em-surface)] p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span className="grid h-10 w-10 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-[var(--em-success-soft)] text-[var(--em-success)]">
            <CheckCircle2Icon className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden />
          </span>
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <p className="text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A]">Email vérifié</p>
            <p className="text-[12.5px] text-[#5D5D5D] break-all">{email}</p>
          </div>
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-success-soft)] px-2.5 py-1 text-[11.5px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-success)]">
            <CheckCircle2Icon className="h-3 w-3" /> Valide
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  /* ── État non vérifié ─────────────────────────────────── */
  return (
    <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-accent)]/30 bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] p-4">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <ShieldCheckIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-[var(--em-accent)] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <p className="text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A]">
            Vérification de l'email
          </p>
          <p className="text-[12.5px] text-[#5D5D5D] mt-1">
            Un code à 6 chiffres sera envoyé à votre adresse. Obligatoire pour continuer.
          </p>

          {!codeSent ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={requestCode}
              disabled={sending || !email.trim()}
              className="ecole_m-btn ecole_m-btn--primary ecole_m-btn--sm mt-3 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {sending ? <LoaderCircleIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" /> : <MailIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
              Envoyer le code
            </button>
          ) : (
            <div className="mt-3 space-y-3">
              <p className="text-[12.5px] text-[#5D5D5D]">
                Code envoyé à <strong className="text-[#1A1A1A] break-all">{email}</strong>
              </p>

              <EmailCodeInput
                value={code}
                onChange={setCode}
                onComplete={verify}
                disabled={verifying}
                hasError={!!error}
              />

              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => verify(code)}
                  disabled={verifying || code.length !== 6}
                  className="ecole_m-btn ecole_m-btn--primary ecole_m-btn--sm disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {verifying
                    ? <LoaderCircleIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />
                    : <CheckCircle2Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
                  Vérifier
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={requestCode}
                  disabled={sending || secondsLeft > 0}
                  className="ecole_m-btn ecole_m-btn--secondary ecole_m-btn--sm disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  <RotateCcwIcon className="h-3 w-3" />
                  Renvoyer
                </button>
                {secondsLeft > 0 && (
                  <span className="text-[11.5px] font-mono text-[#5D5D5D]">
                    Expire dans {mmss(secondsLeft)}
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {error && (
            <p className="text-[12px] text-[var(--em-danger)] mt-2 flex items-center gap-1">
              <AlertCircleIcon className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0" /> {error}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

# FICHIER: components/enseignants/EnseignantDetail.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { AnimatePresence, motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import {
  ArrowLeftRightIcon,
  BriefcaseIcon,
  CalendarClockIcon,
  HistoryIcon,
  KeyRoundIcon,
  LineChartIcon,
  LinkIcon,
  PencilIcon,
  PowerIcon,
  RefreshCwIcon,
  TriangleAlertIcon,
  UnlinkIcon,
  UserRoundIcon,
  ShieldOffIcon,
} from "lucide-react";
import {
  affecterEnseignantMasse,
  changerEnseignant,
  genererCodeActivationEnseignant,
  getChargeHoraireEnseignant,
  getEdtEnseignant,
  getEnseignant,
  getIdentiteEnseignant,
  getParcoursEnseignant,
  getPhotoEnseignant,
  getStatsCumuleesEnseignant,
  getStatutAccesEnseignant,
  listerAffectationsEnseignant,
  listerCodesActivationEnseignant,
  modifierEnseignant,
  recalculerStatsEnseignant,
  retirerAffectationEnseignant,
  revoquerAccesEnseignant,
} from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useTick } from "../../hooks/useAsync";
import { affectationFields, enseignantEditFields, portailFields } from "../../data/fields/personnel";
import { changerEnseignantFields, retirerEnseignantFields } from "../../data/fields/pedagogie";
import type { Row } from "../../types/ui";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../data/AsyncBlock";
import { EdtGrid } from "../data/EdtGrid";
import { MiniTable } from "../data/MiniTable";
import { ObjectView } from "../data/ObjectView";
import { StatGrid } from "../data/StatGrid";
import { EmptyState } from "../data/States";
import { AsyncButton } from "../form/AsyncButton";
import { ConfirmAction } from "../form/ConfirmAction";
import { FormAction } from "../form/FormAction";
import { Section } from "../layout/Section";
import { FluentTabs } from "../ui/FluentTabs";
import type { FluentTab } from "../ui/FluentTabs";
import { CommandBar, Stagger, StaggerItem } from "../eleves/EleveMotion";
import { formatNumber, fullName, initials, obj, str } from "../../utils/format";

const MODULE_COLOR = "#8764B8";
const MODULE_SOFT = "#efe9f7";
const EASE_OUT = [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1] as const;

function Photo({ id, name }: { id: string; name: string }) {
  const [url, setUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    let u: string | null = null;
    getPhotoEnseignant(id)
      .then((f) => {
        if (!alive || !f.mimeType.startsWith("image/")) return;
        u = URL.createObjectURL(f.blob);
        setUrl(u);
      })
      .catch(() => setUrl(null));
    return () => {
      alive = false;
      if (u) URL.revokeObjectURL(u);
    };
  }, [id]);

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: reduce ? 1 : 0.96 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.24, ease: EASE_OUT }}
      className="-mt-10 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-surface,#ffffff)] p-1 shadow-[0_4px_12px_rgba(0,0,0,0.10)]"
    >
      <AnimatePresence mode="wait" initial={false}>
        {url ? (
          <motion.img
            key="photo"
            src={url}
            alt={name}
            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.2, ease: EASE_OUT }}
            className="h-20 w-20 rounded-full object-cover"
          />
        ) : (
          <span
            key="initiales"
            className="grid h-20 w-20 place-items-center rounded-full text-[24px] font-semibold"
            style={{ backgroundColor: MODULE_SOFT, color: MODULE_COLOR }}
          >
            {initials(name)}
          </span>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

const TABS: FluentTab[] = [
  { id: "profil", label: "Profil", icon: UserRoundIcon },
  { id: "affectations", label: "Affectations", icon: BriefcaseIcon },
  { id: "charge", label: "Charge & planning", icon: CalendarClockIcon },
  { id: "parcours", label: "Parcours", icon: LineChartIcon },
  { id: "portail", label: "Accès portail", icon: KeyRoundIcon },
];

export function EnseignantDetail({
  enseignant,
  anneeId,
  onChanged,
}: {
  enseignant: Row;
  anneeId: string;
  onChanged: () => void;
}) {
  const id = str(enseignant.id);
  const name = fullName(enseignant);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("profil");
  const [tick, bump] = useTick();
  const [historique, setHistorique] = useState(false);
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();

  const refresh = () => {
    bump();
    onChanged();
  };

  const subtitle = str(enseignant.specialite_principale) || str(enseignant.poste);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* ── En-tête ───────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <header className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[var(--em-surface,#ffffff)] shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)]">
        <div className="h-20" style={{ backgroundColor: MODULE_SOFT }} aria-hidden />
        <div className="flex items-end gap-4 px-5 pb-5">
          <Photo id={id} name={name} />
          <div className="min-w-0 pb-0.5">
            <h2 className="truncate text-[20px] font-semibold leading-tight">{name}</h2>
            {subtitle && (
              <p className="mt-1 truncate text-[13px] font-medium" style={{ color: MODULE_COLOR }}>
                {subtitle}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="sticky top-0 z-10 -mx-1 px-1 py-1 backdrop-blur-md">
        <FluentTabs
          tabs={TABS}
          value={tab}
          onChange={setTab}
          idPrefix="enseignant"
          label="Dossier de l'enseignant"
          accent={MODULE_COLOR}
        />
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence mode="wait" initial={false}>
        <motion.div
          key={tab}
          role="tabpanel"
          id={`enseignant-panel-${tab}`}
          aria-labelledby={`enseignant-tab-${tab}`}
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: reduce ? 0 : 6 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          exit={{ opacity: 0, y: reduce ? 0 : -4 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.18, ease: EASE_OUT }}
        >
          {/* ── Profil ──────────────────────────────────────────── */}
          {tab === "profil" && (
            <AsyncBlock load={() => getEnseignant(id)} deps={[id, tick]}>
              {(e) => (
                <Stagger className="space-y-5">
                  <StaggerItem>
                    <CommandBar>
                      <FormAction
                        label="Modifier"
                        icon={PencilIcon}
                        size="sm"
                        fields={enseignantEditFields}
                        initial={e}
                        modalSize="lg"
                        onSubmit={(b) => modifierEnseignant(id, b)}
                        successMessage="Fiche mise à jour"
                        onDone={refresh}
                      />
                      {Number(e.actif) === 1 ? (
                        <ConfirmAction
                          label="Désactiver"
                          icon={PowerIcon}
                          size="sm"
                          danger
                          message="Ses affectations en cours seront clôturées."
                          run={() => modifierEnseignant(id, { actif: 0 })}
                          successMessage="Enseignant désactivé"
                          showResult
                          onDone={refresh}
                        />
                      ) : (
                        <ConfirmAction
                          label="Réactiver"
                          icon={PowerIcon}
                          size="sm"
                          message="L'enseignant sera de nouveau actif."
                          run={() => modifierEnseignant(id, { actif: 1 })}
                          successMessage="Enseignant réactivé"
                          onDone={refresh}
                        />
                      )}
                    </CommandBar>
                  </StaggerItem>

                  <div className="grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
                    <StaggerItem>
                      <Section title="Identité pédagogique">
                        <AsyncBlock load={() => getIdentiteEnseignant(id, anneeId)} deps={[id, anneeId, tick]}>
                          {(d) => <ObjectView data={d} />}
                        </AsyncBlock>
                      </Section>
                    </StaggerItem>
                    <StaggerItem>
                      <Section title="Fiche">
                        <ObjectView data={e} hide={["nom", "prenom", "photo_b64"]} />
                      </Section>
                    </StaggerItem>
                  </div>
                </Stagger>
              )}
            </AsyncBlock>
          )}

          {/* ── Affectations ────────────────────────────────────── */}
          {tab === "affectations" && (
            <Stagger className="space-y-5">
              <StaggerItem>
                <CommandBar>
                  <FormAction
                    label="Confier des matières"
                    icon={LinkIcon}
                    size="sm"
                    variant="primary"
                    fields={affectationFields(anneeId)}
                    onSubmit={(b) => affecterEnseignantMasse(id, b)}
                    successMessage="Matières confiées"
                    showResult
                    onDone={refresh}
                  />
                  <span className="mx-1 hidden h-5 w-px bg-[var(--em-border)] sm:block" aria-hidden />
                  <label className="flex cursor-pointer select-none items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-2 py-1 text-[13px] transition-colors duration-150 hover:bg-black/[0.04]">
                    <HistoryIcon className="ecole_m-muted h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />

                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
