################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/layout/StartMenu.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { AnimatePresence, motion } from "framer-motion";
import { SearchIcon, PinIcon, PinOffIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { modules } from "../../data/modules";
import { useSchool } from "../../contexts/SchoolContext";
import { initials } from "../../utils/format";

interface StartMenuProps {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  pinned: string[];
  onTogglePin: (path: string) => void;
}

export function StartMenu({ open, onClose, pinned, onTogglePin }: StartMenuProps) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { entreprise } = useSchool();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    setQ("");
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open, onClose]);

  const list = modules.filter((m) => `${m.label} ${m.description}`.toLowerCase().includes(q.trim().toLowerCase()));
  const name = entreprise?.nom || entreprise?.email || "Mon établissement";

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      {open && (
        <>
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[45]" onClick={onClose} aria-hidden="true" />
          <div className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-x-0 bottom-[64px] z-[46] flex justify-center px-3">
            <motion.div
              role="dialog"
              aria-label="Menu Démarrer"
              className="ecole_m-mica pointer-events-auto w-full max-w-[700px] overflow-hidden rounded-xl"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 24 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, y: 16 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.24, ease: [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1] }}
            >
              <div className="px-6 pb-4 pt-6">
                <div className="relative">
                  <SearchIcon className="ecole_m-faint pointer-events-none absolute left-4 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2" />
                  <input
                    autoFocus
                    value={q}
                    onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyDown={(e) => {
                      if (e.key === "Enter" && list[0]) {
                        navigate(list[0].path);
                        onClose();
                      }
                    }}
                    placeholder="Rechercher un module"
                    aria-label="Rechercher un module"
                    className="ecole_m-input !h-10 !rounded-full pl-10"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="px-6">
                <h2 className="mb-2 text-[13.5px] font-semibold">Tous les modules</h2>
                <div className="ecole_m-scroll grid max-h-[52vh] grid-cols-3 gap-1 overflow-y-auto pb-2 sm:grid-cols-4 md:grid-cols-5">
                  {list.map((m) => {
                    const Icon = m.icon;
                    const isPinned = pinned.includes(m.path);
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={m.path}
                        className="group relative flex min-w-0 flex-col items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-2 py-3 text-center transition-colors duration-100 hover:bg-white/90 focus-within:bg-white/90"
                      >
                        {/* Bouton Épingler (apparaît au survol) */}
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            onTogglePin(m.path);
                          }}
                          className={`absolute right-1 top-1 z-10 grid h-6 w-6 place-items-center rounded-full transition-all duration-150 ${
                            isPinned
                              ? "text-[var(--em-accent)] opacity-100 hover:bg-black/5"
                              : "text-[#8A8A8A] opacity-0 hover:bg-black/5 hover:text-[var(--em-text)] group-hover:opacity-100"
                          }`}
                          aria-label={isPinned ? "Détacher de la barre" : "Épingler à la barre"}
                          title={isPinned ? "Détacher de la barre" : "Épingler à la barre"}
                        >
                          {isPinned ? <PinOffIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> : <PinIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
                        </button>

                        {/* Lien vers l'application */}
                        <Link
                          to={m.path}
                          onClick={onClose}
                          title={m.description}
                          className="flex w-full flex-col items-center gap-2 outline-none"
                        >
                          <span className="ecole_m-tile h-10 w-10 shadow-sm transition-transform duration-150 group-hover:-translate-y-0.5" style={{ background: m.color }}>
                            <Icon className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                          </span>
                          <span className="w-full truncate whitespace-nowrap text-[12.5px] font-medium">{m.label}</span>
                        </Link>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                  {list.length === 0 && <p className="ecole_m-muted col-span-full py-6 text-center text-[13.5px]">Aucun module trouvé</p>}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-black/5 bg-black/[0.03] px-6 py-3">
                <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
                  <span className="grid h-9 w-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent)] text-[13px] font-semibold text-white">
                    {initials(name)}
                  </span>
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="truncate text-[13.5px] font-semibold">{name}</p>
                    {entreprise?.email && <p className="ecole_m-muted truncate text-[12px]">{entreprise.email}</p>}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/layout/Taskbar.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from "react";
import { Link, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { Building2Icon, PinOffIcon, PinIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { modules } from "../../data/modules";
import type { ModuleDef } from "../../data/modules";
import { useSchool } from "../../contexts/SchoolContext";
import { str } from "../../utils/format";
import { Select } from "../ui/Select";
import { StartMenu } from "./StartMenu";


function isActive(m: ModuleDef, path: string) {
  if (m.path === "/") {
    return path === "/" || path === "";
  }
  return path.startsWith(m.path);
}

const STORAGE_KEY = "ecole_taskbar_pinned";

const TASK_BTN =
  "group relative grid h-11 w-11 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-[6px] outline-none " +
  "transition-[background-color,box-shadow,transform] duration-150 ease-[cubic-bezier(0.23,1,0.32,1)] " +
  "hover:bg-white/70 active:scale-[0.92] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)] " +
  "data-[active=true]:bg-white/85 data-[active=true]:shadow-[0_0_0_1px_rgba(0,0,0,0.05),0_1px_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.08)]";

const TIP =
  "pointer-events-none absolute bottom-full left-1/2 z-10 mb-2.5 -translate-x-1/2 translate-y-1 whitespace-nowrap rounded-[6px] " +
  "border border-black/[0.08] bg-[#F9F9F9]/95 px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-[#1A1A1A] opacity-0 " +
  "shadow-[0_8px_16px_rgba(0,0,0,0.12)] backdrop-blur-xl transition-[opacity,transform] duration-150 ease-out " +
  "group-hover:translate-y-0 group-hover:opacity-100 group-focus-visible:translate-y-0 group-focus-visible:opacity-100";

function Clock() {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => new Date());
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = window.setInterval(() => setNow(new Date()), 30000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(t);
  }, []);
  return (
    <div className="hidden rounded-[6px] px-2.5 py-1 text-right leading-tight text-[#1A1A1A] transition-colors duration-150 hover:bg-white/70 md:block" aria-label="Date et heure">
      <p className="text-[12px] font-semibold tabular-nums">{now.toLocaleTimeString("fr-FR", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })}</p>
      <p className="text-[11.5px] tabular-nums text-[#5D5D5D]">{now.toLocaleDateString("fr-FR")}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

function StartGlyph({ open }: { open: boolean }) {
  return (
    <motion.span
      className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-[2px]"
      aria-hidden="true"
      animate={{ rotate: open ? 90 : 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.22, ease: [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1] }}>
      {[1, 0.85, 0.85, 0.7].map((o, i) => (
        <span key={i} className="h-[9px] w-[9px] rounded-[2px] bg-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)]" style={{ opacity: o }} />
      ))}
    </motion.span>
  );
}

function ActivePill() {
  return (
    <motion.span
      layoutId="ecole-taskbar-pill"
      aria-hidden="true"
      className="absolute bottom-[3px] left-[calc(50%-8px)] h-[3px] w-4 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)]"
      transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 520, damping: 38 }}
    />
  );
}

export function Taskbar() {
  const { pathname } = useLocation();
  const [startOpen, setStartOpen] = useState(false);
  const { annees, anneeId, setAnneeId, entreprise } = useSchool();

  // Gestion des épingles
  const [pinned, setPinned] = useState<string[]>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (saved) return JSON.parse(saved);
    } catch {}
    return modules.filter((m) => m.pinned).map((m) => m.path);
  });

  const togglePin = (path: string) => {
    setPinned((prev) => {
      const next = prev.includes(path) ? prev.filter((p) => p !== path) : [...prev, path];
      try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(next)); } catch {}
      return next;
    });
  };

  // Menu contextuel (clic droit)
  const [ctxMenu, setCtxMenu] = useState<{ x: number; y: number; path: string } | null>(null);
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const closeMenu = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (menuRef.current?.contains(e.target as Node)) return;
      setCtxMenu(null);
    };
    window.addEventListener("click", closeMenu);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("click", closeMenu);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    setStartOpen(false);
    setCtxMenu(null);
  }, [pathname]);

  // Liste finale : les épinglés + l'application active si elle n'est pas épinglée
  const pinnedModules = modules.filter((m) => pinned.includes(m.path));
  const current = modules.find((m) => isActive(m, pathname));
  const items = current && !pinned.includes(current.path) ? [...pinnedModules, current] : pinnedModules;

  return (
    <>
      <StartMenu 
        open={startOpen} 
        onClose={() => setStartOpen(false)} 
        pinned={pinned} 
        onTogglePin={togglePin} 
      />
      
      {/* Menu Contextuel pour Détacher/Épingler */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {ctxMenu && (
          <motion.div
            ref={menuRef}
            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.12 }}
            className="fixed z-[100] min-w-[180px] rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white p-1 shadow-[0_8px_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.15)]"
            style={{
              left: Math.min(ctxMenu.x, window.innerWidth - 200),
              top: Math.min(ctxMenu.y, window.innerHeight - 100),
            }}
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => { togglePin(ctxMenu.path); setCtxMenu(null); }}
              className="flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-left text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] hover:text-[var(--em-accent)]"
            >
              {pinned.includes(ctxMenu.path) ? (
                <><PinOffIcon className="h-4 w-4" /> Détacher de la barre</>
              ) : (
                <><PinIcon className="h-4 w-4" /> Épingler à la barre</>
              )}
            </button>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>

      <nav
        className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-40 h-14 border-t border-black/[0.07] bg-[#F3F3F3]/80 shadow-[0_-8px_32px_rgba(0,0,0,0.06)] backdrop-blur-2xl backdrop-saturate-150"
        aria-label="Barre des tâches">
        <div className="flex h-full items-center gap-3 px-3">
          <div className="hidden min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-2.5 xl:flex">
            <span className="grid h-8 w-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-[8px] bg-[var(--em-accent,#0067C0)] text-white shadow-[inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.2),0_2px_6px_rgba(0,0,0,0.12)]">
              <Building2Icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            </span>
            <div className="min-w-0 leading-tight">
              <p className="truncate text-[13px] font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A]">{entreprise?.nom || "Mon établissement"}</p>
              {current && <p className="truncate text-[11.5px] text-[#5D5D5D]">{current.label}</p>}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="ecole_m-scroll flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-1 overflow-x-auto lg:justify-center lg:overflow-visible xl:flex-none">
            <button
              type="button"
              className={TASK_BTN}
              data-active={startOpen}
              aria-label="Démarrer"
              aria-expanded={startOpen}
              onClick={() => setStartOpen((o) => !o)}>
              <StartGlyph open={startOpen} />
              <span className={TIP}>Démarrer</span>
            </button>

            <span className="mx-1 h-6 w-px shrink-0 bg-black/[0.08]" aria-hidden="true" />

            <AnimatePresence mode="popLayout">
              {items.map((m) => {
                const active = isActive(m, pathname);
                const Icon = m.icon;
                return (
                  <motion.div
                    key={m.path}
                    layoutId={`taskbar-${m.path}`}
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.8 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
                    exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.8 }}
                    transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 500, damping: 35 }}
                    onContextMenu={(e) => {
                      e.preventDefault();
                      setCtxMenu({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY - 40, path: m.path });
                    }}
                  >
                    <Link
                      to={m.path}
                      className={TASK_BTN}
                      data-active={active}
                      aria-label={m.label}
                      aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}>
                      <span
                        className="grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-[7px] transition-transform duration-150 ease-out group-hover:-translate-y-[1px]"
                        style={{ background: active ? `${m.color}29` : `${m.color}17` }}>
                        <Icon className="h-[19px] w-[19px]" style={{ color: m.color }} strokeWidth={2.1} aria-hidden="true" />
                      </span>
                      {active && <ActivePill />}
                      <span className={TIP}>{m.label}</span>
                    </Link>
                  </motion.div>
                );
              })}
            </AnimatePresence>
          </div>

          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-end gap-2 xl:flex-1">
            {annees.length > 0 && (
              <div className="w-[150px] sm:w-[180px]">
                <Select
                  compact
                  ariaLabel="Année scolaire"
                  value={anneeId}
                  onChange={(v) => setAnneeId(String(v))}
                  options={annees.map((a) => ({ value: str(a.id), label: str(a.libelle), hint: a.est_active ? "Active" : undefined }))}
                  searchable={false}
                />
              </div>
            )}
            <Clock />
          </div>
        </div>
      </nav>
    </>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/layout/WithAnnee.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { CalendarPlusIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { useSchool } from "../../contexts/SchoolContext";
import { EmptyState, ErrorState, LoadingState } from "../data/States";

export function WithAnnee({ children }: {children: (anneeId: string) => React.ReactNode;}) {
  const { anneeId, loading, error, reload, annees } = useSchool();
  if (loading && annees.length === 0)
  return (
    <div className="ecole_m-card">
        <LoadingState />
      </div>);

  if (error)
  return (
    <div className="ecole_m-card">
        <ErrorState message={error} onRetry={reload} />
      </div>);

  if (!anneeId)
  return (
    <div className="ecole_m-card">
        <EmptyState icon={CalendarPlusIcon} title="Aucune année scolaire" text="Créez une année scolaire pour commencer à utiliser ce module.">
          <Link to="/parametres" className="ecole_m-btn ecole_m-btn--primary">
            Créer une année scolaire
          </Link>
        </EmptyState>
      </div>);

  return <>{children(anneeId)}</>;
}

# FICHIER: components/parametres/AnneeDetail.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import {
  ArrowUpRightIcon,
  CalendarDaysIcon,
  CalendarPlusIcon,
  CalendarRangeIcon,
  CheckCircle2Icon,
  InfoIcon,
  LayersIcon,
  PowerIcon } from
"lucide-react";
import { activerAnnee, creerPeriode, listerPeriodes, promouvoirAnnee } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useSchool } from "../../contexts/SchoolContext";
import type { Row } from "../../types/ui";
import { periodeFields, promotionFields } from "../../data/fields/parametres";
import { STATUT_ANNEE } from "../../data/enums";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../data/AsyncBlock";
import { MiniTable } from "../data/MiniTable";
import { ObjectView } from "../data/ObjectView";
import { EmptyState } from "../data/States";
import { ConfirmAction } from "../form/ConfirmAction";
import { FormAction } from "../form/FormAction";
import { Section } from "../layout/Section";
import { useTick } from "../../hooks/useAsync";
import { str } from "../../utils/format";

export function AnneeDetail({ annee, reload }: {annee: Row;reload: () => void;}) {
  const id = str(annee.id);
  const school = useSchool();
  const [tick, bump] = useTick();

  const refresh = () => {
    reload();
    school.reload();
  };

  const libelle = str(annee.libelle);
  const statut = str(annee.statut);
  const debut = str(annee.date_debut);
  const fin = str(annee.date_fin);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* ── En-tête de l'année ───────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <header className="ecole_m-card overflow-hidden">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start gap-4 p-5">
          <span
            className="ecole_m-tile h-12 w-12 shrink-0 rounded-lg shadow-sm"
            style={{ background: "var(--em-accent)" }}>
            <CalendarRangeIcon className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" />
          </span>

          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <h2 className="truncate text-[20px] font-semibold leading-tight tracking-tight">{libelle}</h2>

              {annee.est_active ?
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--em-accent)_12%,transparent)] px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] font-medium text-[var(--em-accent)]">
                  <CheckCircle2Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  Année active
                </span> :
              null}

              {statut ?
              <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full border border-[var(--em-border)] px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] font-medium">
                  {STATUT_ANNEE[statut] ?? statut}
                </span> :
              null}
            </div>

            {debut || fin ?
            <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[13px]">
                <CalendarDaysIcon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span>{debut || "—"}</span>
                <span aria-hidden="true">→</span>
                <span>{fin || "—"}</span>
              </p> :
            null}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Barre de commandes façon Windows 11 */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-t border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--em-accent)_4%,transparent)] px-5 py-3">
          {!annee.est_active &&
          <ConfirmAction
            label="Définir comme année active"
            icon={PowerIcon}
            variant="primary"
            message={`L'année ${str(annee.libelle)} deviendra l'année de travail par défaut.`}
            run={() => activerAnnee(id)}
            successMessage="Année activée"
            onDone={refresh} />
          }
          <FormAction
            label="Passage en année supérieure"
            icon={ArrowUpRightIcon}
            description="Inscrivez automatiquement les élèves de cette année dans les classes de l'année suivante."
            fields={promotionFields(id)}
            modalSize="xl"
            submitLabel="Lancer le passage"
            successMessage="Passage effectué"
            showResult
            onSubmit={(b) => promouvoirAnnee(id, b)}
            onDone={refresh} />
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* ── Contenu : périodes (principal) + informations ───────────── */}
      <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,22rem)]">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <Section
            title="Périodes"
            actions={
            <FormAction
              label="Ajouter une période"
              icon={CalendarPlusIcon}
              size="sm"
              fields={periodeFields}
              onSubmit={(b) => creerPeriode({ ...b, annee_scolaire_id: id })}
              successMessage="Période ajoutée"
              onDone={bump} />
            }>
            <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
              <LayersIcon className="ecole_m-muted h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
              <p className="ecole_m-muted text-[13px]">Trimestres ou semestres qui découpent l'année scolaire.</p>
            </div>
            <AsyncBlock
              load={() => listerPeriodes(id)}
              deps={[id, tick]}
              empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucune période" text="Ajoutez les trimestres ou semestres de l'année." />}>
              {(rows) =>
              <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)]">
                  <MiniTable
                  rows={rows}
                  columns={[{ key: "libelle" }, { key: "ordre" }, { key: "date_debut" }, { key: "date_fin" }, { key: "bulletins_publies" }]} />
                </div>
              }
            </AsyncBlock>
          </Section>
        </div>

        <aside className="min-w-0">
          <div className="ecole_m-card p-5">
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
              <InfoIcon className="h-4 w-4 text-[var(--em-accent)]" aria-hidden="true" />
              <h3 className="text-[14px] font-semibold">Informations</h3>
            </div>
            <ObjectView data={annee} />
          </div>
        </aside>
      </div>
    </div>);
}

import React from "react";
import { BookCopyIcon, DatabaseZapIcon, RefreshCwIcon, SparklesIcon, UsersRoundIcon, WrenchIcon, RocketIcon } from "lucide-react";
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import {
  initialisationComplete,
  initialiserEtablissement,
  recalculerParcoursTousElevesEtAttendre,
  recalculerParcoursTousEnseignantsEtAttendre,
  seedCataloguesGlobaux } from
"../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useSchool } from "../../contexts/SchoolContext";
import { ConfirmAction } from "../form/ConfirmAction";

interface Item {
  title: string;
  text: string;
  icon: LucideIcon;
  run: () => Promise<unknown>;
  label: string;
}

export function InitialisationPanel() {
  const school = useSchool();

  const principal: Item = {
    title: "Initialisation complète",
    text: "Met en place en une seule fois toute la configuration de départ : référentiel, paramètres et catalogues.",
    icon: DatabaseZapIcon,
    run: initialisationComplete,
    label: "Lancer"
  };

  const miseEnPlace: Item[] = [{
    title: "Préparer l'établissement",
    text: "Crée les éléments de base nécessaires au fonctionnement de l'établissement.",
    icon: SparklesIcon,
    run: initialiserEtablissement,
    label: "Préparer"
  }, {
    title: "Catalogues communs",
    text: "Charge les catalogues partagés (matériel scolaire, catégories de dépenses…).",
    icon: BookCopyIcon,
    run: seedCataloguesGlobaux,
    label: "Charger"
  }];

  const maintenance: Item[] = [{
    title: "Mettre à jour le parcours de tous les élèves",
    text: "Recalcule l'historique scolaire complet de chaque élève. L'opération peut prendre quelques instants.",
    icon: RefreshCwIcon,
    run: recalculerParcoursTousElevesEtAttendre,
    label: "Mettre à jour"
  }, {
    title: "Mettre à jour le parcours de tous les enseignants",
    text: "Recalcule l'historique et les statistiques de chaque enseignant.",
    icon: UsersRoundIcon,
    run: recalculerParcoursTousEnseignantsEtAttendre,
    label: "Mettre à jour"
  }];

  const renderAction = (it: Item, variant?: "primary") =>
  <ConfirmAction
    label={it.label}
    title={it.title}
    message={it.text}
    run={it.run}
    showResult
    successMessage="Opération terminée"
    onDone={school.reload}
    {...variant ? { variant } : {}} />;

  const PrincipalIcon = principal.icon;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      {/* ── Action principale ───────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <section aria-labelledby="init-principal" className="ecole_m-card overflow-hidden">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 p-6 md:flex-row md:items-center">
          <span className="ecole_m-tile h-14 w-14 shrink-0 rounded-xl shadow-sm" style={{ background: "var(--em-accent)" }}>
            <PrincipalIcon className="h-7 w-7" aria-hidden="true" />
          </span>
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <div className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-2">
              <RocketIcon className="h-4 w-4 text-[var(--em-accent)]" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span className="text-[12px] font-medium text-[var(--em-accent)]">Recommandé pour un nouvel établissement</span>
            </div>
            <h2 id="init-principal" className="text-[18px] font-semibold tracking-tight">{principal.title}</h2>
            <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-1 max-w-2xl text-[13px] leading-relaxed">{principal.text}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="shrink-0">{renderAction(principal, "primary")}</div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* ── Mise en place ───────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <section aria-labelledby="init-miseenplace">
        <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
          <SparklesIcon className="ecole_m-muted h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          <h3 id="init-miseenplace" className="text-[14px] font-semibold">Mise en place étape par étape</h3>
        </div>
        <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
          {miseEnPlace.map((it) => {
            const Icon = it.icon;
            return (
              <article key={it.title} className="ecole_m-card flex h-full flex-col p-5">
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <span
                    className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-[var(--em-accent)]"
                    style={{ background: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--em-accent) 12%, transparent)" }}>
                    <Icon className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </span>
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <h4 className="text-[14px] font-semibold">{it.title}</h4>
                    <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-1 text-[13px] leading-relaxed">{it.text}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="mt-auto flex justify-end pt-4">{renderAction(it)}</div>
              </article>);
          })}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* ── Maintenance ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <section aria-labelledby="init-maintenance">
        <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
          <WrenchIcon className="ecole_m-muted h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
          <h3 id="init-maintenance" className="text-[14px] font-semibold">Maintenance des données</h3>
        </div>
        <div className="ecole_m-card divide-y divide-[var(--em-border)] overflow-hidden">
          {maintenance.map((it) => {
            const Icon = it.icon;
            return (
              <div key={it.title} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 px-5 py-4">
                <span className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)]">
                  <Icon className="h-[18px] w-[18px]" aria-hidden="true" />
                </span>
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <p className="text-[14px] font-medium">{it.title}</p>
                  <p className="ecole_m-muted text-[13px]">{it.text}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="shrink-0">{renderAction(it)}</div>
              </div>);
          })}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>);
}

import React from "react";

                </div>
                <AsyncBlock
                load={() => listerNiveaux(str(r.id))}
                deps={[str(r.id)]}
                empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucun niveau" />}>
                  {(rows) =>
                <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)]">
                      <MiniTable rows={rows} />

              </Section>
            </div>
          } />
      </section>

      <hr className="border-[var(--em-border)]" />

      {/* ── Séries ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <section aria-labelledby="ref-series" className="space-y-3">
        <header className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-[var(--em-accent)]"
            style={{ background: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--em-accent) 12%, transparent)" }}>
            <GitBranchIcon className="h-[18px] w-[18px]" aria-hidden="true" />
          </span>
          <div>
            <h2 id="ref-series" className="text-[15px] font-semibold leading-tight">Séries</h2>
            <p className="ecole_m-muted text-[13px]">Filières proposées pour les niveaux à séries.</p>
          </div>
        </header>
        <ResourceView load={listerSeries} deps={[]} emptyTitle="Aucune série" noDetail />
      </section>
    </div>);
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/register_map.tsx
################################################################################

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import L from 'leaflet';
import 'leaflet/dist/leaflet.css';

// Fix des icônes par défaut (Leaflet ne les résout pas bien avec les bundlers)
delete (L.Icon.Default.prototype as any)._getIconUrl;
L.Icon.Default.mergeOptions({
  iconRetinaUrl: 'https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/images/marker-icon-2x.png',
  iconUrl: 'https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/images/marker-icon.png',
  shadowUrl: 'https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/images/marker-shadow.png',
});

interface RegisterMapProps {
  lat: number | null;
  lng: number | null;
  onMapClick: (lat: number, lng: number) => void;
}

// Centre par défaut : Dakar (à ajuster si tu veux un autre pays par défaut)
const DEFAULT_LAT = 14.7167;
const DEFAULT_LNG = -17.4677;

export default function RegisterMap({ lat, lng, onMapClick }: RegisterMapProps) {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mapRef = useRef<L.Map | null>(null);
  const markerRef = useRef<L.Marker | null>(null);

  // Initialisation une seule fois
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!containerRef.current || mapRef.current) return;

    const startLat = lat ?? DEFAULT_LAT;
    const startLng = lng ?? DEFAULT_LNG;

    const map = L.map(containerRef.current).setView([startLat, startLng], lat !== null ? 15 : 12);
    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {
      attribution: '&copy; OpenStreetMap contributors',
      maxZoom: 19,
    }).addTo(map);

    if (lat !== null && lng !== null) {
      markerRef.current = L.marker([lat, lng]).addTo(map);
    }

    map.on('click', (e: L.LeafletMouseEvent) => {
      const newLat = e.latlng.lat;
      const newLng = e.latlng.lng;
      if (markerRef.current) markerRef.current.setLatLng([newLat, newLng]);
      else markerRef.current = L.marker([newLat, newLng]).addTo(map);
      onMapClick(newLat, newLng);
    });

    mapRef.current = map;

    return () => {
      map.remove();
      mapRef.current = null;
      markerRef.current = null;
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  // Sync quand le parent change lat/lng (ex : bouton géolocalisation)
  useEffect(() => {
    const map = mapRef.current;
    if (!map || lat === null || lng === null) return;
    map.setView([lat, lng], Math.max(map.getZoom(), 15));
    if (markerRef.current) markerRef.current.setLatLng([lat, lng]);
    else markerRef.current = L.marker([lat, lng]).addTo(map);
  }, [lat, lng]);

  return (
    <div
      ref={containerRef}
      className="h-[320px] w-full rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-[var(--em-border)]"
      aria-label="Carte de localisation du siège"
    />
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/registres/BarreFormule.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { SigmaIcon } from "lucide-react";

interface BarreFormuleProps {
  adresse: string;
  entete: string;
  texte: string;
  actif: boolean;
  onValider: (texte: string) => void;
}

export function BarreFormule({ adresse, entete, texte, actif, onValider }: BarreFormuleProps) {
  const [valeur, setValeur] = useState(texte);

  useEffect(() => setValeur(texte), [texte, adresse]);

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-[var(--em-border)] px-3 py-1.5">
      <span
        aria-label="Cellule sélectionnée"
        className="w-[72px] shrink-0 rounded-md border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-black/[0.02] px-2 py-1 text-center font-mono text-[12px] font-medium tabular-nums">
        
        {adresse || "—"}
      </span>
      <SigmaIcon className="ecole_m-muted h-4 w-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
      <input
        value={valeur}
        disabled={!actif}
        onChange={(e) => setValeur(e.target.value)}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.key === "Enter") {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (valeur !== texte) onValider(valeur);
          } else if (e.key === "Escape") {
            e.preventDefault();
            setValeur(texte);
          }
        }}
        onBlur={() => {
          if (actif && valeur !== texte) onValider(valeur);
        }}
        placeholder={actif ? "" : "Sélectionnez une cellule"}
        aria-label={entete ? `Contenu — ${entete}` : "Contenu de la cellule"}
        className="h-8 min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-md border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-transparent px-2.5 font-mono text-[12.5px] outline-none transition-[box-shadow,border-color] duration-150 focus:border-[var(--em-accent)] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[var(--em-accent)]/20 disabled:opacity-50" />
      
      {entete &&
      <span className="ecole_m-muted hidden max-w-[240px] shrink-0 truncate text-[12px] lg:block" title={entete}>
          {entete}
        </span>
      }
    </div>);

}

import React from "react";

interface BoutonOutilProps {

  disabled?: boolean;
  primary?: boolean;
  iconOnly?: boolean;
  badge?: number;
  title?: string;
  pressed?: boolean;
}
export function BoutonOutil({
  label,
  icon: Icon,
  onClick,
  disabled,
  primary,
  iconOnly,
  badge,
  title,
  pressed
}: BoutonOutilProps) {
  return <button type="button" onClick={onClick} disabled={disabled} aria-label={iconOnly ? label : undefined} aria-pressed={pressed} title={title ?? (iconOnly ? label : undefined)} className={`inline-flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md text-[13px] font-medium outline-none transition-[background-color,color,transform,opacity] duration-150 ease-[cubic-bezier(0.23,1,0.32,1)] active:scale-[0.97] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--em-accent)] disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-40 ${iconOnly ? "w-8 justify-center" : "px-2.5"} ${primary ? "bg-[var(--em-accent)] text-white shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.12)] hover:opacity-90" : pressed ? "bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] text-[var(--em-accent)]" : "hover:bg-black/[0.05]"}`}>
      <Icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
      {!iconOnly && <span>{label}</span>}
      {badge !== undefined && badge > 0 && <span className={`grid h-[18px] min-w-[18px] place-items-center rounded-full px-1 text-[11px] font-semibold tabular-nums ${primary ? "bg-white/25 text-white" : "bg-[var(--em-accent)] text-white"}`}>
          {badge}
        </span>}
    </button>;
}

################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/registres/ContexteLigne.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { FileDownIcon, PencilIcon, RotateCcwIcon, UserRoundIcon, WalletIcon } from "lucide-react";
import type { Worksheet } from "exceljs";
import { exporterFinanceEleve, overrideEcheance, retirerOverrideEcheance } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import type { StatutOverrideEcheance, TypeRegistre } from "../../types/ui";
import { COLONNES_REGISTRE as C, FEUILLE_ECHEANCES, FEUILLE_ELEVES_DOSSIER } from "../../data/registres";
import { overrideEcheanceFields } from "../../data/fields/registres";
import type { MoteurFormules } from "../../utils/formules";
import { affichageCellule, entetesFeuille } from "../../utils/xlsx";
import { saveFile } from "../../utils/files";
import { num, str } from "../../utils/format";
import type { Tone } from "../../utils/format";
import { Badge } from "../ui/Badge";
import { AsyncButton } from "../form/AsyncButton";
import { ConfirmAction } from "../form/ConfirmAction";
import { FormAction } from "../form/FormAction";

interface ContexteLigneProps {
  type: TypeRegistre;
  ws: Worksheet;
  moteur: MoteurFormules;
  ligne: number;
  revision: number;
  onAction: (message: string) => void;
}

const MESSAGE_SYNCHRO =
"Échéance mise à jour. Le registre sera resynchronisé sous quelques secondes : rechargez-le avant d'enregistrer d'autres modifications.";

export function ContexteLigne({ type, ws, moteur, ligne, onAction }: ContexteLigneProps) {
  const h = entetesFeuille(ws);
  if (ligne < 2 || !h[C.cle]) return null;
  const cle = str(ws.getCell(ligne, h[C.cle]).value);
  if (!cle) return null;
  const v = (nom: string) => h[nom] ? affichageCellule(ws, moteur, ligne, h[nom]).texte : "";
  const brut = (nom: string) => h[nom] ? ws.getCell(ligne, h[nom]).value : null;
  const eleve = `${v(C.prenom)} ${v(C.nom)}`.trim();

  if (type === "finance" && ws.name === FEUILLE_ECHEANCES) {
    const overrideActif = v(C.overrideActif) === "Oui";
    const statut = v(C.statut);
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-2 border-b border-[var(--em-border)] bg-black/[0.02] px-4 py-2">
        <WalletIcon className="ecole_m-muted h-4 w-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-[13px]">
          <span className="font-semibold">{eleve || v(C.matricule)}</span>
          <span className="ecole_m-muted truncate">{v(C.echeance)}</span>
          {v(C.dateEcheance) && <span className="ecole_m-muted tabular-nums">{v(C.dateEcheance)}</span>}
          {statut && <Badge tone={toneStatutEcheance(statut)}>{statut}</Badge>}
          {overrideActif && <Badge tone="warning">Statut manuel : {v(C.statutManuel) || "actif"}</Badge>}
        </div>
        <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-wrap gap-2">
          <FormAction
            label="Statut manuel"
            icon={PencilIcon}
            size="sm"
            variant="primary"
            description="Le manuel prime sur l'automatique : l'échéance ne sera plus modifiée par les paiements ni par la détection d'impayés."
            fields={overrideEcheanceFields}
            initial={{
              statut: overrideActif ? str(brut(C.statutManuel)) : undefined,
              montant: overrideActif ? num(brut(C.montantManuel)) ?? undefined : undefined,
              motif: overrideActif ? str(brut(C.motifManuel)) : undefined
            }}
            submitLabel="Appliquer"
            successMessage="Statut manuel appliqué"
            showResult
            onSubmit={(b) =>
            overrideEcheance(cle, {
              statut: str(b.statut) as StatutOverrideEcheance,
              montant: str(b.statut) === "Partiel" ? num(b.montant) : undefined,
              motif: str(b.motif) || undefined
            })
            }
            onDone={() => onAction(MESSAGE_SYNCHRO)} />
          
          {overrideActif &&
          <ConfirmAction
            label="Retour en automatique"
            icon={RotateCcwIcon}
            size="sm"
            message="L'échéance reprendra son calcul automatique à partir des paiements."
            run={() => retirerOverrideEcheance(cle)}
            successMessage="Échéance repassée en automatique"
            showResult
            onDone={() => onAction(MESSAGE_SYNCHRO)} />

          }
        </div>
      </div>);

  }

  if (type === "dossier" && ws.name === FEUILLE_ELEVES_DOSSIER) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-2 border-b border-[var(--em-border)] bg-black/[0.02] px-4 py-2">
        <UserRoundIcon className="ecole_m-muted h-4 w-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-[13px]">
          <span className="font-semibold">{eleve}</span>
          <span className="ecole_m-muted font-mono text-[12px]">{v(C.matricule)}</span>
          {v(C.situation) && <span className="ecole_m-muted">{v(C.situation)}</span>}
          {v(C.statutFinancier) && <Badge tone={toneStatutEcheance(v(C.statutFinancier))}>{v(C.statutFinancier)}</Badge>}
        </div>
        <AsyncButton
          label="Exporter sa situation financière"
          icon={FileDownIcon}
          size="sm"
          successMessage="Export téléchargé"
          onClick={async () => saveFile(await exporterFinanceEleve(cle))} />
        
      </div>);

  }

  return null;
}

/** Couleurs des règles conditionnelles du registre financier (retard, avance, Partiel, Payé, Exonéré). */
function toneStatutEcheance(s: string): Tone {
  const t = s.toLowerCase();
  if (t.includes("retard")) return "danger";
  if (t.includes("avance")) return "info";
  if (t.includes("partiel")) return "warning";
  if (t.includes("payé") || t.includes("soldé") || t.includes("à jour")) return "success";
  return "neutral";
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/registres/FeuilleOnglets.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useRef } from "react";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { EASE_OUT } from "../eleves/EleveMotion";

interface FeuilleOngletsProps {
  feuilles: string[];
  valeur: string;
  onChange: (feuille: string) => void;
}

export function FeuilleOnglets({ feuilles, valeur, onChange }: FeuilleOngletsProps) {
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();
  const refs = useRef<(HTMLButtonElement | null)[]>([]);

  const onKey = (e: React.KeyboardEvent, i: number) => {
    if (e.key !== "ArrowRight" && e.key !== "ArrowLeft") return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const n = (i + (e.key === "ArrowRight" ? 1 : -1) + feuilles.length) % feuilles.length;
    onChange(feuilles[n]);
    refs.current[n]?.focus();
  };

  return (
    <div role="tablist" aria-label="Feuilles du classeur" className="ecole_m-scroll flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-end gap-0.5 overflow-x-auto px-2 [scrollbar-width:none]">
      {feuilles.map((f, i) => {
        const actif = f === valeur;
        return (
          <button
            key={f}
            ref={(el) => refs.current[i] = el}
            type="button"
            role="tab"
            aria-selected={actif}
            tabIndex={actif ? 0 : -1}
            onClick={() => onChange(f)}
            onKeyDown={(e) => onKey(e, i)}
            className={`relative shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded-b-md px-3.5 pb-2 pt-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-medium outline-none transition-colors duration-150 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--em-accent)] ${
            actif ? "text-[var(--em-accent)]" : "ecole_m-muted hover:bg-black/[0.04] hover:text-inherit"}`
            }>
            
            {actif &&
            <motion.span
              layoutId="registre-feuille-pill"
              className="absolute inset-0 rounded-b-md border border-t-0 border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[var(--em-surface,#ffffff)] shadow-[0_2px_4px_rgba(0,0,0,0.06)]"
              transition={{ duration: reduce ? 0 : 0.2, ease: EASE_OUT }} />

            }
            <span className="relative">{f}</span>
            {actif &&
            <motion.span
              layoutId="registre-feuille-bar"
              className="absolute inset-x-3 top-0 h-[2px] rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent)]"
              transition={{ duration: reduce ? 0 : 0.2, ease: EASE_OUT }} />

            }
          </button>);

      })}
    </div>);

}

################################################################################
# FICHIER: components/registres/RapportTeleversement.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { CircleCheckIcon, TriangleAlertIcon, XIcon } from "lucide-react";
import type { TeleversementRegistreResultat } from "../../types/ui";
import { LIBELLES_RAPPORT } from "../../data/registres";
import { EASE_OUT } from "../eleves/EleveMotion";
import { formatNumber, humanize } from "../../utils/format";

export function RapportTeleversement({ rapport, onFermer }: {rapport: TeleversementRegistreResultat;onFermer: () => void;}) {
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();
  const app = rapport.application as Record<string, unknown>;
  const stats = Object.entries(app).filter(([, v]) => typeof v === "number");
  const anomalies = Array.isArray(app.anomalies) ? app.anomalies as string[] : [];

  return (
    <motion.div
      role="status"
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: reduce ? 0 : -6 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      exit={{ opacity: 0, y: reduce ? 0 : -6 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.2, ease: EASE_OUT }}
      className="border-b border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] px-4 py-3">
      
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <CircleCheckIcon className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-[var(--em-accent)]" aria-hidden />
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-2">
          <p className="text-[13.5px] font-semibold">
            Registre enregistré — version {formatNumber(rapport.version)}
          </p>
          {stats.length > 0 &&
          <dl className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-1 text-[12.5px]">
              {stats.map(([k, v]) =>
            <div key={k} className="flex items-baseline gap-1.5">
                  <dt className="ecole_m-muted">{LIBELLES_RAPPORT[k] ?? humanize(k)}</dt>
                  <dd className="font-semibold tabular-nums">{formatNumber(v)}</dd>
                </div>
            )}
            </dl>
          }
          {anomalies.length > 0 &&
          <div className="rounded-md border border-[var(--em-warning)]/25 bg-[var(--em-warning-soft)] px-3 py-2 text-[12.5px] text-[var(--em-warning)]">
              <p className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 font-semibold">
                <TriangleAlertIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden />
                {anomalies.length} anomalie(s) ignorée(s)
              </p>
              <ul className="ecole_m-scroll max-h-28 list-disc space-y-0.5 overflow-y-auto pl-5">
                {anomalies.map((a, i) =>
              <li key={i}>{a}</li>
              )}
              </ul>
            </div>
          }
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onFermer}
          aria-label="Fermer le rapport"
          className="-mr-1 grid h-7 w-7 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-md outline-none transition-colors duration-150 hover:bg-black/[0.06] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--em-accent)]">
          
          <XIcon className="h-4 w-4" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </motion.div>);

}

# FICHIER: components/registres/RegistreBarreOutils.tsx

import React, { useRef } from "react";
import {
  DownloadIcon,
  EyeIcon,
  EyeOffIcon,
  LoaderCircleIcon,
  Redo2Icon,
  RefreshCwIcon,
  RotateCcwIcon,
  SaveIcon,
  SearchIcon,
  Undo2Icon,
  UploadIcon,
  XIcon } from
"lucide-react";
import { synchroRegistresEtAttendre } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import type { TypeRegistre } from "../../types/ui";

const SEP = <span className="mx-0.5 hidden h-5 w-px shrink-0 bg-[var(--em-border)] sm:block" aria-hidden />;

export function RegistreBarreOutils(p: RegistreBarreOutilsProps) {
  const input = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  return (

      <BoutonOutil
        label={p.envoi ? "Enregistrement…" : "Enregistrer"}
        icon={p.envoi ? LoaderCircleIcon : SaveIcon}
        primary
        badge={p.nbModifs}
        disabled={!p.pret || p.envoi || p.nbModifs === 0}
        title="Enregistrer et téléverser le registre (Ctrl+S)"
        onClick={p.onEnregistrer} />
