import type { FieldDef } from "../../types/ui";
import { enumField, F, req } from "../../utils/fieldBuilders";
import { fromEnum, optAnnees, optClasses, optClassesFromField } from "../../utils/options";
import { CATEGORIE_MATIERE, STATUT_ANNEE, TYPE_BAREME } from "../enums";

export const anneeFields: FieldDef[] = [
F.text("libelle", "Libellé", { ...req, full: true, placeholder: "Ex. : 2026-2027" }),
F.date("date_debut", "Rentrée", req),
F.date("date_fin", "Fin d'année", req),
enumField("statut", "Statut", fromEnum(STATUT_ANNEE), { defaultValue: "en_cours" }),
F.bool("est_active", "Définir comme année active", { defaultValue: true })];


export const periodeFields: FieldDef[] = [
F.text("libelle", "Libellé", { ...req, placeholder: "Ex. : Trimestre 1" }),
F.number("ordre", "Ordre", { min: 1 }),
F.date("date_debut", "Début", req),
F.date("date_fin", "Fin", req),
F.date("date_limite_saisie_notes", "Limite de saisie des notes"),
F.bool("bulletins_publies", "Bulletins déjà publiés")];


export const matiereFields: FieldDef[] = [
F.text("libelle", "Libellé", req),
F.text("code", "Code", { ...req, placeholder: "Ex. : MATH" }),
enumField("categorie", "Catégorie", fromEnum(CATEGORIE_MATIERE)),
F.color("couleur_affichage", "Couleur d'affichage")];


export const baremeFields: FieldDef[] = [
F.text("libelle", "Libellé", req),
enumField("type_bareme", "Type", fromEnum(TYPE_BAREME), req),
F.number("note_max", "Note maximale", { min: 1, showIf: (v) => v.type_bareme !== "competences" }),
F.number("seuil_reussite", "Seuil de réussite", { min: 0, showIf: (v) => v.type_bareme !== "competences" }),
F.list("echelle_lettres", "Échelle de lettres", {
  showIf: (v) => v.type_bareme === "lettres",
  addLabel: "Ajouter une lettre",
  itemFields: [F.text("lettre", "Lettre", req), F.number("equivalent_num", "Équivalent chiffré", req)]
}),
F.tags("echelle_competences", "Niveaux de compétence", {
  showIf: (v) => v.type_bareme === "competences",
  placeholder: "Ex. : Acquis, puis Entrée"
})];


export const typeEvaluationFields: FieldDef[] = [
F.text("libelle", "Libellé", { ...req, placeholder: "Ex. : Composition" }),
F.number("poids_dans_moyenne", "Poids dans la moyenne", { min: 0, step: 0.5 })];


export const promotionFields = (sourceAnneeId: string): FieldDef[] => [
F.select("annee_cible_id", "Année de destination", { ...req, load: optAnnees, full: true }),
F.list("regles", "Passages de classe", {
  ...req,
  addLabel: "Ajouter une classe",
  itemFields: [
  F.select("classe_source_id", "Classe actuelle", { ...req, load: optClasses(sourceAnneeId) }),
  F.select("classe_passage_id", "Classe supérieure", {
    ...req,
    load: optClassesFromField("annee_cible_id", ""),
    dependsOn: ["annee_cible_id"]
  }),
  F.select("classe_redoublement_id", "Classe de redoublement", {
    load: optClassesFromField("annee_cible_id", ""),
    dependsOn: ["annee_cible_id"]
  })]

})];

# FICHIER: data/fields/pedagogie.ts
################################################################################

import type { FieldDef, OptionItem } from "../../types/ui";
import { anneeField, eleveField, enseignantField, enumField, F, req } from "../../utils/fieldBuilders";
import {
  fromEnum,
  optClasses,
  optCycles,
  optEleves,
  optMatieres,
  optMatieresClasse,
  optMatieresClasseFromField,
  optNiveauxFromField,
  optSallesNom,
  optSeries,
  optTypesEvaluation } from
"../../utils/options";
import { todayISO } from "../../utils/format";
import { JOURS, MOTIF_CHANGEMENT, STATUT_PRESENCE } from "../enums";

const jourField = (extra: Partial<FieldDef> = {}) => enumField("jour_semaine", "Jour", fromEnum(JOURS), { numeric: true, ...extra });

export const classeCreateFields = (anneeId: string): FieldDef[] => [
F.select("_cycle", "Cycle", { ...req, load: optCycles }),
F.select("niveau_id", "Niveau", { ...req, load: optNiveauxFromField("_cycle"), dependsOn: ["_cycle"], placeholder: "Choisissez d'abord un cycle" }),
F.text("libelle", "Nom de la classe", { ...req, placeholder: "Ex. : 6ème A" }),
F.select("serie_id", "Série", { load: optSeries, help: "Uniquement pour les niveaux à séries" }),
F.number("effectif_max", "Effectif maximum", { min: 1 }),
anneeField(anneeId)];


export const matiereClasseFields: FieldDef[] = [
F.select("matiere_id", "Matière", { ...req, load: optMatieres, searchable: true }),
enseignantField("enseignant_id", "Enseignant"),
F.number("coefficient", "Coefficient", { min: 0, step: 0.5 }),
F.number("volume_horaire_hebdo", "Heures par semaine", { min: 0, step: 0.5 })];


export const changerEnseignantFields = (): FieldDef[] => [
enseignantField("nouveau_enseignant_id", "Nouvel enseignant", { ...req, full: true }),
F.date("date_effet", "À partir du", { ...req, defaultValue: todayISO() }),
enumField("motif", "Motif", fromEnum(MOTIF_CHANGEMENT), req)];


export const retirerEnseignantFields = (): FieldDef[] => [
F.date("date_fin", "Fin de l'affectation", { defaultValue: todayISO() }),
enumField("motif", "Motif", fromEnum(MOTIF_CHANGEMENT))];


export const creneauFields = (classeId: string, withForce = true): FieldDef[] => [
F.select("matiere_classe_id", "Matière", { ...req, load: optMatieresClasse(classeId), full: true }),
jourField(req),
F.select("salle", "Salle", { load: optSallesNom }),
F.time("heure_debut", "Début", req),
F.time("heure_fin", "Fin", req),
...(withForce ? [F.bool("forcer_malgre_conflits", "Enregistrer même en cas de conflit", { full: true })] : [])];


export const remplacerCreneauFields = (): FieldDef[] => [
F.date("date_effet", "À partir du", { ...req, defaultValue: todayISO() }),
F.text("motif", "Motif du changement", req),
jourField({ label: "Nouveau jour" }),
F.select("salle", "Nouvelle salle", { load: optSallesNom }),
F.time("heure_debut", "Nouvelle heure de début"),
F.time("heure_fin", "Nouvelle heure de fin")];


export const bulkFields = (matieres: OptionItem[]): FieldDef[] => [
F.list("creneaux", "Créneaux à créer", {
  ...req,
  addLabel: "Ajouter un créneau",
  itemFields: [
  F.select("matiere_classe_id", "Matière", { ...req, options: matieres }),
  jourField(req),
  F.time("heure_debut", "Début", req),
  F.time("heure_fin", "Fin", req),
  F.select("salle", "Salle", { load: optSallesNom })]

}),
F.bool("forcer_malgre_conflits", "Enregistrer même en cas de conflit")];


export const evaluationFields = (): FieldDef[] => [
F.text("libelle", "Intitulé", { ...req, full: true, placeholder: "Ex. : Devoir surveillé n°1" }),
F.select("type_evaluation_id", "Type d'évaluation", { ...req, load: optTypesEvaluation }),
F.date("date_evaluation", "Date", { ...req, defaultValue: todayISO() }),
F.number("note_max", "Note maximale", { min: 1 }),
F.number("coefficient_evaluation", "Coefficient", { min: 0.1, step: 0.5 })];


export const noteFields = (noteMax?: number): FieldDef[] => [
eleveField({ full: true }),
F.number("valeur_numerique", "Note", { min: 0, max: noteMax, showIf: (v) => v.absent !== true, help: noteMax ? `Sur ${noteMax}` : undefined }),
F.bool("absent", "Absent à l'évaluation"),
F.area("commentaire", "Commentaire")];


export const appreciationFields: FieldDef[] = [eleveField({ full: true }), F.area("texte", "Appréciation", req)];

export const devoirFields = (anneeId: string): FieldDef[] => [
F.select("_classe", "Classe", { ...req, load: optClasses(anneeId), searchable: true }),
F.select("matiere_classe_id", "Matière", {
  ...req,
  load: optMatieresClasseFromField("_classe"),
  dependsOn: ["_classe"],
  placeholder: "Choisissez d'abord une classe"
}),
F.text("titre", "Titre", { ...req, full: true }),

  path: string;

################################################################################
# FICHIER: hooks/useAsync.ts
################################################################################

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { errorMessage } from "../utils/errors";

interface AsyncState<T> {
  data: T | null;
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
}

export function useAsync<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>, deps: unknown[], enabled = true) {
  const [state, setState] = useState<AsyncState<T>>({ data: null, loading: enabled, error: null });
  const [tick, setTick] = useState(0);
  const fnRef = useRef(fn);
  fnRef.current = fn;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) {
      setState({ data: null, loading: false, error: null });
      return;
    }
    let alive = true;
    setState((s) => ({ ...s, loading: true, error: null }));
    fnRef.
    current().
    then((data) => {
      if (alive) setState({ data, loading: false, error: null });
    }).
    catch((e: unknown) => {
      if (alive) setState({ data: null, loading: false, error: errorMessage(e) });
    });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [...deps, tick, enabled]);

  const reload = useCallback(() => setTick((t) => t + 1), []);
  return { ...state, reload };
}

export function useTick(): [number, () => void] {
  const [n, setN] = useState(0);
  const bump = useCallback(() => setN((x) => x + 1), []);
  return [n, bump];
}

export function useDebounced<T>(value: T, delay = 300): T {
  const [v, setV] = useState(value);
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => setV(value), delay);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  }, [value, delay]);
  return v;
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: hooks/useLookup.ts
################################################################################

import { useMemo } from "react";
import type { Row } from "../types/ui";
import { str } from "../utils/format";
import { useAsync } from "./useAsync";

/** Charge une liste depuis le serveur et renvoie une table identifiant → libellé (affichage uniquement). */
export function useLookup(load: () => Promise<Row[]>, label: (r: Row) => string, deps: unknown[] = [], key = "id") {
  const { data } = useAsync(load, deps);
  return useMemo(() => {
    const m = new Map<string, string>();
    (data ?? []).forEach((r) => m.set(str(r[key]), label(r)));
    return m;
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [data]);
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: hooks/useRegistreClasseur.ts
################################################################################

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { CellValue, Workbook } from "exceljs";
import { telechargerRegistreClasse, televerserRegistreClasse } from "../lib/api_ecole";
import type { TeleversementRegistreResultat, TypeRegistre } from "../types/ui";
import type { ModificationCellule } from "../types/tableur";
import { MoteurFormules } from "../utils/formules";
import { chargerClasseur, exporterClasseur, feuilleActive, feuillesVisibles } from "../utils/xlsx";
import { errorMessage } from "../utils/errors";
import { saveFile } from "../utils/files";

type EtatClasseur = "chargement" | "pret" | "erreur";

const SEP = "\u0000";
const cle = (f: string, l: number, c: number) => `${f}${SEP}${l}:${c}`;
const meme = (a: CellValue | undefined, b: CellValue | undefined) => JSON.stringify(a ?? null) === JSON.stringify(b ?? null);

export function useRegistreClasseur(classeId: string, type: TypeRegistre) {
  const wbRef = useRef<Workbook | null>(null);
  const moteurRef = useRef<MoteurFormules | null>(null);
  const requete = useRef(0);
  const originaux = useRef(new Map<string, CellValue>());
  const pileAnnuler = useRef<ModificationCellule[][]>([]);
  const pileRetablir = useRef<ModificationCellule[][]>([]);

  const [etat, setEtat] = useState<EtatClasseur>("chargement");
  const [erreur, setErreur] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [nomFichier, setNomFichier] = useState("");
  const [feuilles, setFeuilles] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [feuille, setFeuille] = useState("");
  const [revision, setRevision] = useState(0);
  const [nbModifs, setNbModifs] = useState(0);
  const [peutAnnuler, setPeutAnnuler] = useState(false);
  const [peutRetablir, setPeutRetablir] = useState(false);
  const [envoi, setEnvoi] = useState(false);
  const [rapport, setRapport] = useState<TeleversementRegistreResultat | null>(null);
  const [erreurEnvoi, setErreurEnvoi] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const majPiles = () => {
    setPeutAnnuler(pileAnnuler.current.length > 0);
    setPeutRetablir(pileRetablir.current.length > 0);
  };

  const charger = useCallback(async () => {
    const id = ++requete.current;
    setEtat("chargement");
    setErreur(null);
    try {
      const f = await telechargerRegistreClasse(classeId, type);
      const wb = await chargerClasseur(f.blob);
      if (id !== requete.current) return;
      wbRef.current = wb;
      moteurRef.current = new MoteurFormules(wb);
      const noms = feuillesVisibles(wb);
      const actif = feuilleActive(wb);
      setFeuilles(noms);
      setFeuille((prev) => (noms.includes(prev) ? prev : actif && noms.includes(actif) ? actif : noms[0] ?? ""));
      setNomFichier(f.filename);
      originaux.current = new Map();
      pileAnnuler.current = [];
      pileRetablir.current = [];
      setNbModifs(0);
      majPiles();
      setRevision((r) => r + 1);
      setEtat("pret");
    } catch (e) {
      if (id !== requete.current) return;
      setErreur(errorMessage(e));
      setEtat("erreur");
    }
  }, [classeId, type]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setRapport(null);
    setErreurEnvoi(null);
    void charger();
  }, [charger]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!nbModifs) return;
    const h = (e: BeforeUnloadEvent) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.returnValue = "";
    };
    window.addEventListener("beforeunload", h);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("beforeunload", h);
  }, [nbModifs]);

  const appliquer = useCallback((mods: ModificationCellule[]): ModificationCellule[] => {
    const wb = wbRef.current;
    if (!wb) return [];
    const inverses: ModificationCellule[] = [];
    mods.forEach((m) => {
      const ws = wb.getWorksheet(m.feuille);
      if (!ws) return;
      const cell = ws.getCell(m.ligne, m.colonne);
      const avant = cell.value;
      if (meme(avant, m.valeur)) return;
      inverses.push({ ...m, valeur: avant });
      cell.value = m.valeur;
      const k = cle(m.feuille, m.ligne, m.colonne);
      if (!originaux.current.has(k)) originaux.current.set(k, avant);
      else if (meme(originaux.current.get(k), m.valeur)) originaux.current.delete(k);
    });
    if (inverses.length) {
      moteurRef.current?.invalider();
      setNbModifs(originaux.current.size);
      setRevision((r) => r + 1);
    }
    return inverses;
  }, []);

  const ecrire = useCallback(
    (mods: ModificationCellule[]) => {
      const inv = appliquer(mods);
      if (!inv.length) return;
      pileAnnuler.current.push(inv.reverse());
      pileRetablir.current = [];
      majPiles();
    },
    [appliquer]
  );

  const annuler = useCallback(() => {
    const g = pileAnnuler.current.pop();
    if (!g) return;
    pileRetablir.current.push(appliquer(g).reverse());
    majPiles();
  }, [appliquer]);

  const retablir = useCallback(() => {
    const g = pileRetablir.current.pop();
    if (!g) return;
    pileAnnuler.current.push(appliquer(g).reverse());
    majPiles();
  }, [appliquer]);

  const cellulesModifiees = useCallback((nomFeuille: string): Set<string> => {
    const out = new Set<string>();
    const prefixe = `${nomFeuille}${SEP}`;
    originaux.current.forEach((_, k) => {
      if (k.startsWith(prefixe)) out.add(k.slice(prefixe.length));
    });
    return out;
  }, []);

  const envoyer = useCallback(
    async (fichier: () => Promise<File>) => {
      setEnvoi(true);
      setErreurEnvoi(null);
      setRapport(null);
      try {
        const f = await fichier();
        const r = await televerserRegistreClasse(classeId, type, f);
        setRapport(r);
        await charger();
      } catch (e) {
        setErreurEnvoi(errorMessage(e));
      } finally {
        setEnvoi(false);
      }
    },
    [classeId, type, charger]
  );

  const enregistrer = useCallback(() => {
    const wb = wbRef.current;
    if (!wb) return Promise.resolve();
    return envoyer(() => exporterClasseur(wb, nomFichier || `registre_${type}.xlsx`));
  }, [envoyer, nomFichier, type]);

  const televerserFichier = useCallback((f: File) => envoyer(async () => f), [envoyer]);

  const telecharger = useCallback(async () => {
    saveFile(await telechargerRegistreClasse(classeId, type));
  }, [classeId, type]);

  return {
    etat,
    erreur,
    classeur: wbRef.current,
    moteur: moteurRef.current,
    nomFichier,
    feuilles,
    feuille,
    setFeuille,
    revision,
    nbModifs,
    cellulesModifiees,
    ecrire,
    annuler,
    retablir,
    peutAnnuler,
    peutRetablir,
    charger,
    enregistrer,
    televerserFichier,
    telecharger,
    envoi,
    rapport,
    fermerRapport: () => setRapport(null),
    erreurEnvoi,
    fermerErreurEnvoi: () => setErreurEnvoi(null)
  };
}



################################################################################
# FICHIER: hooks/useTableurGrille.ts
################################################################################

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { Worksheet } from "exceljs";
import type { CelluleRef, CibleCollage, TriColonne } from "../types/tableur";
import { ErreurFormule } from "../utils/formules";
import type { MoteurFormules, ValeurCalc } from "../utils/formules";
import {
  HAUTEUR_LIGNE_DEFAUT,
  affichageCellule,
  colonnesFeuille,
  hauteurLigne,
  lignesFigees,
  listeValidation,
  texteSaisie
} from "../utils/xlsx";

export const HAUTEUR_ENTETE = 26;
export const LARGEUR_NUMEROS = 52;
const SEUIL_VIRTUALISATION = 200;
const MARGE = 12;

export interface EditionCellule extends CelluleRef {
  texte: string;
  liste: string[] | null;
}

export interface OptionsGrille {
  ws: Worksheet;
  moteur: MoteurFormules;
  revision: number;
  recherche: string;
  afficherMasquees: boolean;
  selection: CelluleRef | null;
  onSelection: (s: CelluleRef) => void;
  onValider: (ligne: number, colonne: number, texte: string) => void;
  onEffacer: (cellules: CelluleRef[]) => void;
  onColler: (cibles: CibleCollage[]) => void;
  onAnnuler: () => void;
  onRetablir: () => void;
}

const borner = (v: number, min: number, max: number) => Math.max(min, Math.min(max, v));

export function useTableurGrille(o: OptionsGrille) {
  const { ws, moteur, revision, selection } = o;
  const conteneur = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const editionRef = useRef<EditionCellule | null>(null);
  const [edition, setEditionEtat] = useState<EditionCellule | null>(null);
  const [tri, setTri] = useState<TriColonne | null>(null);
  const [defil, setDefil] = useState(0);
  const [hauteur, setHauteur] = useState(600);

  const setEdition = (e: EditionCellule | null) => {
    editionRef.current = e;
    setEditionEtat(e);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    setTri(null);
    setEdition(null);
    setDefil(0);
    conteneur.current?.scrollTo({ top: 0, left: 0 });
  }, [ws]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = conteneur.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(() => setHauteur(el.clientHeight));
    ro.observe(el);
    setHauteur(el.clientHeight);
    return () => ro.disconnect();
  }, []);

  const colonnes = useMemo(
    () => colonnesFeuille(ws).filter((c) => o.afficherMasquees || !c.cachee),
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    [ws, o.afficherMasquees, revision]
  );
  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  const nbFigees = useMemo(() => Math.min(lignesFigees(ws), ws.rowCount), [ws, revision]);
  const lignesFixes = useMemo(() => Array.from({ length: nbFigees }, (_, i) => i + 1), [nbFigees]);
  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  const hauteursFixes = useMemo(() => lignesFixes.map((l) => hauteurLigne(ws, l)), [lignesFixes, ws, revision]);
  const topsFixes = useMemo(() => {
    const out: number[] = [];
    hauteursFixes.forEach((_, i) => out.push(i === 0 ? HAUTEUR_ENTETE : out[i - 1] + hauteursFixes[i - 1]));
    return out;
  }, [hauteursFixes]);

  const lignesCorps = useMemo(() => {
    let lignes: number[] = [];
    for (let l = nbFigees + 1; l <= ws.rowCount; l++) lignes.push(l);
    const q = o.recherche.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (q) {
      lignes = lignes.filter((l) => colonnes.some((c) => affichageCellule(ws, moteur, l, c.index).texte.toLowerCase().includes(q)));
    }
    if (tri) {
      const sens = tri.sens === "asc" ? 1 : -1;
      const v = new Map(lignes.map((l) => [l, moteur.valeur(ws.name, l, tri.colonne)] as const));
      lignes.sort((a, b) => comparerTri(v.get(a) ?? null, v.get(b) ?? null, sens));
    }
    return lignes;
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [ws, moteur, revision, o.recherche, colonnes, tri, nbFigees]);

  const lignes = useMemo(() => [...lignesFixes, ...lignesCorps], [lignesFixes, lignesCorps]);
  const virtualise = lignesCorps.length > SEUIL_VIRTUALISATION;
  const hauteurFixe = hauteursFixes.reduce((s, h) => s + h, 0);
  const H = HAUTEUR_LIGNE_DEFAUT;
  const debut = virtualise ? Math.max(0, Math.floor((defil - hauteurFixe) / H) - MARGE) : 0;
  const fin = virtualise ? Math.min(lignesCorps.length, Math.ceil((defil + hauteur) / H) + MARGE) : lignesCorps.length;
  const visibles = lignesCorps.slice(debut, fin);
  const espaceHaut = debut * H;
  const espaceBas = (lignesCorps.length - fin) * H;

  const deplacer = useCallback(
    (dl: number, dc: number, depuis?: CelluleRef) => {
      if (!lignes.length || !colonnes.length) return;
      const s = depuis ?? selection ?? { ligne: lignes[0], colonne: colonnes[0].index };
      const iL = Math.max(0, lignes.indexOf(s.ligne));
      const iC = Math.max(0, colonnes.findIndex((c) => c.index === s.colonne));
      o.onSelection({
        ligne: lignes[borner(iL + dl, 0, lignes.length - 1)],
        colonne: colonnes[borner(iC + dc, 0, colonnes.length - 1)].index
      });
    },
    [lignes, colonnes, selection, o]
  );

  const commencerEdition = useCallback(
    (cible?: CelluleRef, texteInitial?: string) => {
      const s = cible ?? selection;
      if (!s) return;
      const cell = ws.getCell(s.ligne, s.colonne);
      setEdition({
        ligne: s.ligne,
        colonne: s.colonne,
        texte: texteInitial ?? texteSaisie(cell.value),
        liste: listeValidation(ws, s.ligne, s.colonne)
      });
    },
    [ws, selection]
  );

  const terminerEdition = (texte: string | null, mouvement: [number, number]) => {
    const e = editionRef.current;
    if (!e) return;
    setEdition(null);
    if (texte !== null) o.onValider(e.ligne, e.colonne, texte);
    if (mouvement[0] || mouvement[1]) deplacer(mouvement[0], mouvement[1], { ligne: e.ligne, colonne: e.colonne });
    requestAnimationFrame(() => conteneur.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true }));
  };

  const setTexteEdition = (texte: string) => {
    if (editionRef.current) setEdition({ ...editionRef.current, texte });
  };

  const onToucheEdition = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    const valeur = e.currentTarget.value;
    if (e.key === "Enter") {
      e.preventDefault();
      terminerEdition(valeur, [e.shiftKey ? -1 : 1, 0]);
    } else if (e.key === "Tab") {
      e.preventDefault();
      terminerEdition(valeur, [0, e.shiftKey ? -1 : 1]);
    } else if (e.key === "Escape") {
      e.preventDefault();
      terminerEdition(null, [0, 0]);
    }
  };

  const onTouche = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (editionRef.current) return;
    const mod = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;
    const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
    if (mod && k === "z") {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (e.shiftKey) o.onRetablir();
      else o.onAnnuler();
      return;
    }
    if (mod && k === "y") {
      e.preventDefault();
      o.onRetablir();
      return;
    }
    if (mod) return;
    if (!selection) {
      if (e.key.startsWith("Arrow") || e.key === "Tab" || e.key === "Enter") {
        e.preventDefault();
        deplacer(0, 0);
      }
      return;
    }
    switch (e.key) {
      case "ArrowDown":
        e.preventDefault();
        deplacer(1, 0);
        break;
      case "ArrowUp":
        e.preventDefault();
        deplacer(-1, 0);
        break;
      case "ArrowRight":
        e.preventDefault();
        deplacer(0, 1);
        break;
      case "ArrowLeft":
        e.preventDefault();
        deplacer(0, -1);
        break;
      case "Tab":
        e.preventDefault();
        deplacer(0, e.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
        break;
      case "Enter":
        e.preventDefault();
        deplacer(e.shiftKey ? -1 : 1, 0);
        break;
      case "PageDown":
        e.preventDefault();
        deplacer(20, 0);
        break;
      case "PageUp":
        e.preventDefault();
        deplacer(-20, 0);
        break;
      case "Home":
        e.preventDefault();
        deplacer(0, -colonnes.length);
        break;
      case "End":
        e.preventDefault();
        deplacer(0, colonnes.length);
        break;
      case "F2":
        e.preventDefault();
        commencerEdition();
        break;
      case "Delete":
      case "Backspace":
        e.preventDefault();
        o.onEffacer([selection]);
        break;
      default:
        if (e.key.length === 1 && !e.altKey) {
          e.preventDefault();
          commencerEdition(undefined, e.key);
        }
    }
  };

  const onCopier = (e: React.ClipboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (editionRef.current || !selection) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    e.clipboardData.setData("text/plain", affichageCellule(ws, moteur, selection.ligne, selection.colonne).texte);
  };

  const onColler = (e: React.ClipboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (editionRef.current || !selection) return;
    const brut = e.clipboardData.getData("text/plain");
    if (!brut) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const grille = brut
      .replace(/\r\n?/g, "\n")
      .replace(/\n$/, "")
      .split("\n")
      .map((l) => l.split("\t"));
    const iL = lignes.indexOf(selection.ligne);
    const iC = colonnes.findIndex((c) => c.index === selection.colonne);
    const cibles: CibleCollage[] = [];
    grille.forEach((r, dl) =>
      r.forEach((texte, dc) => {
        const l = lignes[iL + dl];
        const c = colonnes[iC + dc];
        if (l !== undefined && c) cibles.push({ ligne: l, colonne: c.index, texte });
      })
    );
    if (cibles.length) o.onColler(cibles);
  };

  const onDefilement = (e: React.UIEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (virtualise) setDefil(e.currentTarget.scrollTop);
  };

  const basculerTri = (colonne: number) => {
    setTri((t) => (!t || t.colonne !== colonne ? { colonne, sens: "asc" } : t.sens === "asc" ? { colonne, sens: "desc" } : null));
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = conteneur.current;
    if (!el || !selection) return;
    if (virtualise) {
      const i = lignesCorps.indexOf(selection.ligne);
      if (i >= 0) {
        const haut = HAUTEUR_ENTETE + hauteurFixe + i * H;
        const visibleHaut = el.scrollTop + HAUTEUR_ENTETE + hauteurFixe;
        if (haut < visibleHaut) el.scrollTop = haut - HAUTEUR_ENTETE - hauteurFixe;
        else if (haut + H > el.scrollTop + el.clientHeight) el.scrollTop = haut + H - el.clientHeight;
      }
    }
    const id = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      el.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-cellule="${selection.ligne}:${selection.colonne}"]`)?.scrollIntoView({
        block: "nearest",
        inline: "nearest"
      });
    });
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(id);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [selection]);

  return {
    conteneur,
    colonnes,
    lignesFixes,
    hauteursFixes,
    topsFixes,
    lignesCorps,
    visibles,
    virtualise,
    espaceHaut,
    espaceBas,
    hauteurFixe,
    tri,
    basculerTri,
    edition,
    commencerEdition,
    terminerEdition,
    setTexteEdition,
    onToucheEdition,
    onTouche,
    onCopier,
    onColler,
    onDefilement
  };
}

function comparerTri(a: ValeurCalc, b: ValeurCalc, sens: number): number {
  const vide = (x: ValeurCalc) => x === null || x === "" || x instanceof ErreurFormule;
  if (vide(a) && vide(b)) return 0;
  if (vide(a)) return 1;
  if (vide(b)) return -1;
  if (typeof a === "number" && typeof b === "number") return (a - b) * sens;
  if (typeof a === "number") return -sens;
  if (typeof b === "number") return sens;
  return String(a).localeCompare(String(b), "fr", { numeric: true, sensitivity: "base" }) * sens;
}

import {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
  ReactNode,
} from 'react';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import {
  abonnementStatus,
  AbonnementStatus,
  StatutAbo,
  FormuleAbo,

} from '@/lib/api_routes/abonnement_api';
import { TOKEN_EXPIRED_EVENT } from '@/lib/api';
import { useAuth } from './auth';

// ── Types ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
interface AbonnementCtx {
  status: AbonnementStatus | null;
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  refresh: () => Promise<void>;

  // Drapeaux dérivés (pour les guards & l'UI)
  statutLive: StatutAbo | 'inconnu';
  isTrial: boolean;
  isActif: boolean;
  isExpire: boolean;
  isSuspendu: boolean;
  isIntrouvable: boolean;
  isBloque: boolean;        // expire | suspendu | introuvable → refuser l'accès produit
  hasAcces: boolean;        // trial | actif → autorise l'accès

  // Infos pratiques pour l'UI
  formule: FormuleAbo | null;
  trialJoursRestants: number | null;
  joursRestantsAbo: number | null;
  prochainMontantFcfa: number | null;

  // Quota employés (formule par_employe)
  nbEmployesActifs: number | null;
  nbEmployesAutorises: number | null;   // null = illimité (forfait)
  nbEmployesRestants: number | null;    // null = illimité
  quotaAtteint: boolean;                 // true si par_employe et restants <= 0
  quotaProche: boolean;                  // true si par_employe et restants <= 1

  // Bannière "à renouveler bientôt"
  showRenewalWarning: boolean;
}

const Ctx = createContext<AbonnementCtx | null>(null);

// ── Constantes ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS = 10 * 60_000;   // 10 min — aligné sur le cache backend (12h)
const MIN_REFRESH_GAP_MS = 2 * 100_000;     // anti-rafale sur focus répétés
const WARN_DAYS_BEFORE_EXPIRY = 5;





// ── Provider ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export function AbonnementProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { isAuthenticated } = useAuth();
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<AbonnementStatus | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const lastStatutRef = useRef<StatutAbo | 'inconnu'>('inconnu');
  const lastFetchTsRef = useRef<number>(0);

  const refresh = useCallback(async (force = false) => {
    if (!isAuthenticated) {
      setStatus(null);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    const now = Date.now();
    if (!force && now - lastFetchTsRef.current < MIN_REFRESH_GAP_MS) {
      return;
    }
    lastFetchTsRef.current = now;
    try {
      setError(null);
      const s = await abonnementStatus();
      setStatus(s);
    } catch (e: any) {
      setError(e?.message || "Impossible de charger l'abonnement");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [isAuthenticated]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isAuthenticated) {
      setStatus(null);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    refresh();
    const t = setInterval(refresh, REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [isAuthenticated, refresh]);

  // Reset à la déconnexion
  useEffect(() => {
    const onClear = () => {
      setStatus(null);
      setError(null);
      setLoading(false);
      lastStatutRef.current = 'inconnu';
    };

    window.addEventListener(TOKEN_EXPIRED_EVENT, onClear);
    return () => window.removeEventListener(TOKEN_EXPIRED_EVENT, onClear);
  }, []);

  // Resync quand l'onglet redevient visible
  useEffect(() => {
    const onVisible = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible' && isAuthenticated) {
        refresh();
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible);
  }, [isAuthenticated, refresh]);
  
  // Toasts sur transitions de statut (trial → expire, etc.)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!status) return;
    const prev = lastStatutRef.current;
    const cur = status.statut;

    if (prev !== 'inconnu' && prev !== cur) {
      if (cur === 'expire') {
        toast.error('Votre abonnement a expiré. Renouvelez pour continuer.', {
          id: 'abo-expire',
          duration: 8000,
        });
      } else if (cur === 'suspendu') {
        toast.error('Votre abonnement est suspendu. Contactez le support.', {
          id: 'abo-suspendu',
          duration: 8000,
        });
      } else if (cur === 'actif' && (prev === 'trial' || prev === 'expire')) {
        toast.success('Abonnement activé ✅', { id: 'abo-actif' });
      }
    }
    lastStatutRef.current = cur;
  }, [status]);

  // Valeur du contexte
  const value = useMemo<AbonnementCtx>(() => {
    const statutLive: StatutAbo | 'inconnu' = status?.statut ?? 'inconnu';
    const isTrial = statutLive === 'trial';
    const isActif = statutLive === 'actif';
    const isExpire = statutLive === 'expire';
    const isSuspendu = statutLive === 'suspendu';
    const isIntrouvable = statutLive === 'introuvable';
    const hasAcces = isTrial || isActif;
    const isBloque = isExpire || isSuspendu || isIntrouvable;

    const formule = (status?.formule as FormuleAbo) ?? null;
    const trialJoursRestants = status?.trial_jours_restants ?? null;
    const joursRestantsAbo = status?.jours_restants_abo ?? null;

    // Quota employés (uniquement pertinent pour la formule par_employe)
    const nbEmployesActifs = status?.nb_employes_actifs ?? null;
    const nbEmployesAutorises = status?.nb_employes_autorises ?? null;
    const nbEmployesRestants = status?.nb_employes_restants ?? null;
    const quotaAtteint =
      formule === 'par_employe' &&
      nbEmployesRestants !== null &&
      nbEmployesRestants <= 0;
    const quotaProche =
      formule === 'par_employe' &&
      nbEmployesRestants !== null &&
      nbEmployesRestants <= 1 &&
      nbEmployesRestants > 0;

    // Bannière de pré-expiration
    const showRenewalWarning =
      (isTrial && trialJoursRestants !== null && trialJoursRestants <= WARN_DAYS_BEFORE_EXPIRY) ||
      (isActif && joursRestantsAbo !== null && joursRestantsAbo <= WARN_DAYS_BEFORE_EXPIRY);

    return {
      status,
      loading,
      error,
      refresh,
      statutLive,
      isTrial,
      isActif,
      isExpire,
      isSuspendu,
      isIntrouvable,
      isBloque,
      hasAcces,
      formule,
      trialJoursRestants,
      joursRestantsAbo,
      prochainMontantFcfa: status?.prochain_montant_estime_fcfa ?? null,
      nbEmployesActifs,
      nbEmployesAutorises,
      nbEmployesRestants,
      quotaAtteint,
      quotaProche,
      showRenewalWarning,
    };
  }, [status, loading, error, refresh]);

  return <Ctx.Provider value={value}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

// ── Hook ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export function useAbonnement(): AbonnementCtx {
  const v = useContext(Ctx);
  if (!v) throw new Error('useAbonnement must be used within AbonnementProvider');
  return v;
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: lib/api.ts
################################################################################

// lib/api.ts — version corrigée (credentials include + alignement backend)
export const API_BASE = "https://rhmanager.site";

const TOKEN_KEY = "rh_token";
const ENT_KEY = "rh_entreprise";
export const SYSTEM_INBOX_ADDR = "system@internal.rh";

// ── Constantes existantes ─────────────────────────────────────
export const TOKEN_EXPIRED_EVENT = "mp-token-expired";

// Endpoints qui DOIVENT pouvoir renvoyer 401 sans déclencher de déconnexion
// (sinon boucle infinie sur la page de login)
const AUTH_WHITELIST = [
    "/auth/login",
    "/auth/register",
    "/auth/forgot-password",
    "/auth/reset-password",
    "/auth/forgot-password/via-secret-phrase",
    "/auth/forgot-password/via-recovery-key",
    "/auth/recovery-keys/regenerate",
    "/auth/email/request-code",   // ← nouveau
];

let _redirecting = false;

function isAuthWhitelisted(url: string) {
    return AUTH_WHITELIST.some((p) => url.includes(p));
}

// ✅ Vérifier qu'on n'est PAS déjà sur /login avant de rediriger
function triggerTokenExpired() {
    if (_redirecting) return;

    // Si on est déjà sur login, ne rien faire
    if (window.location.pathname.startsWith("/login")) return;

    _redirecting = true;

    try {
        logout();
    } catch {}

    window.dispatchEvent(
        new CustomEvent(TOKEN_EXPIRED_EVENT, {
            detail: { reason: "token_expired" },
        }),
    );

    const currentPath = window.location.pathname + window.location.search;
    const cleanPath = currentPath.replace(/^\/ecole/, '') || '/';
    window.location.replace(
    `/ecole/login?_redirect=${encodeURIComponent(cleanPath)}&reason=expired`,
    );

    // Reset après navigation (pour les SPAs qui ne rechargent pas)
    setTimeout(() => {
        _redirecting = false;
    }, 3000);
}

// ── GESTION DE LA PHRASE SECRÈTE (conservé pour d'autres routes si besoin) ──
let _secretPhrase: string | null = null;
let _pendingSecretRequests: Array<{
    resolve: (secret: string) => void;
    reject: (err: Error) => void;
}> = [];

export function setSecretPhrase(phrase: string | null) {
    _secretPhrase = phrase;
    const copy = [..._pendingSecretRequests];
    _pendingSecretRequests = [];
    if (phrase) {
        copy.forEach((r) => r.resolve(phrase));
    } else {
        copy.forEach((r) => r.reject(new Error("Action annulée")));
    }
}
export async function authVerifySecret(secretPhrase: string) {
    return api("/auth/verify-secret", { method: "POST", secretPhrase });
}

export function clearSecretPhrase() {
    _secretPhrase = null;
}

/**
 * Demande la phrase secrète via le SecretPhraseGuard.
 * Retourne une promesse résolue avec la phrase (ou rejetée si annulation).
 * À appeler AVANT toute opération sensible.
 */
export function requestSecretPhrase(): Promise<string> {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        _pendingSecretRequests.push({ resolve, reject });
        window.dispatchEvent(new Event("secret-phrase-required"));
    });
}

// ── helpers de stockage ─────────────────────────────────────────────
export function getToken(): string | null {
    try {
        return localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

export function setToken(t: string | null) {
    try {
        if (t) localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, t);
        else localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    } catch {}
}

export function getEntreprise(): {
    id: string;
    email: string;
    nom?: string;
} | null {
    try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem(ENT_KEY);
        return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

export function setEntreprise(
    e: { id: string; email: string; nom?: string; rhEmail?: string } | null,
) {
    try {
        if (e) localStorage.setItem(ENT_KEY, JSON.stringify(e));
        else localStorage.removeItem(ENT_KEY);
    } catch {}
}

export function logout() {
    setToken(null);
    setEntreprise(null);
}

// ── Types ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export class ApiError extends Error {
    status: number;
    data: any;
    constructor(message: string, status: number, data?: any) {
        super(message);
        this.status = status;
        this.data = data;
    }
}

interface TaskResponse {
    task_id?: string;
    message?: string;
    state?: string;
    status?: string;
    result?: any;
    error?: string;
    info?: string;
}

interface RequestOptions {
  method?: "GET" | "POST" | "PUT" | "PATCH" | "DELETE";
  body?: any;
  formData?: FormData;
  query?: Record<string, any>;
  auth?: boolean;
  rawJson?: boolean;
  rawResponse?: boolean;
  taskResultPath?: string;
  secretPhrase?: string;
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
}

// ── Construction d'URL ────────────────────────────────────────────────
export function buildUrl(path: string, query?: Record<string, any>): string {
    // Si l'API_BASE est vide, on prend l'origine de la page courante
    const base = API_BASE || window.location.origin;
    // Construction robuste : même avec des chemins relatifs, ça marche
    const url = new URL(path, base);
    if (query) {
        Object.entries(query).forEach(([k, v]) => {
            if (v === undefined || v === null || v === "") return;
            url.searchParams.set(k, String(v));
        });
    }
    return url.toString();
}

async function rawFetch(
    path: string,
    opts: RequestOptions = {},
    _isRetry = false,
): Promise<any> {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = { ...(opts.headers ?? {}) };
    const useAuth = opts.auth !== false;
    if (useAuth) {
        const t = getToken();
        if (t) headers["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${t}`;
    }

    if (opts.secretPhrase) {
        headers["X-Secret-Phrase"] = opts.secretPhrase;
    }

    let body: any = undefined;
    if (opts.formData) {
        body = opts.formData;
    } else if (opts.body !== undefined) {
        headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";
        body = JSON.stringify(opts.body);
    }

    const res = await fetch(buildUrl(path, opts.query), {
        method: opts.method ?? (body ? "POST" : "GET"),
        headers,
        body,
        credentials: "include",
    });

    // ── Abonnement expiré / requis ─────────────────────────────────
    if (
        res.status === 402 &&
        !path.includes("/abonnement") &&
        !path.includes("/auth")
    ) {
        let detail: { code?: string; statut?: string } = {};
        try {
            const data = await res.clone().json();
            detail = typeof data?.detail === "object" ? data.detail : {};
        } catch {}
        if (
            detail?.code === "abonnement_requis" &&
            !window.location.pathname.startsWith("/app/abonnement")
        ) {
            window.location.replace("/app/abonnement/expire");
        }
        throw new ApiError(
            typeof detail === "object" && "message" in detail
                ? String((detail as { message?: string }).message)
                : "Abonnement requis",
            402,
            detail,
        );
    }

    // ── Gestion intelligente du 403 ────────────────────────────────
    if (res.status === 403 && !_isRetry) {
        let errorMessage = "";
        let code = "";
        try {
            const data = await res.clone().json();
            errorMessage =
                typeof data?.detail === "string"
                    ? data.detail
                    : (data?.detail?.message ?? "");
            code = data?.detail?.code ?? "";
        } catch {}

        // a) Phrase secrète → Guard puis rejeu de la requête
        if (
            errorMessage.includes("secrète") ||
            errorMessage.includes("session expirée")
        ) {
            const secret = await requestSecretPhrase();
            await authVerifySecret(secret);
            return rawFetch(path, opts, true);
        }

        // b) Tout autre 403 → erreur remontée à l'appelant, sans déconnexion
        throw new ApiError(errorMessage || "Accès refusé", 403, null);
    }
    // ── Gestion du 401 (token expiré / invalide) ───────────────────
    if (res.status === 401 && !_isRetry && !isAuthWhitelisted(path)) {
        triggerTokenExpired();
        // On lève l'erreur quand même pour stopper les composants en attente
        throw new ApiError("Session expirée", 401, null);
    }
    if (opts.rawResponse) {
        if (!res.ok) {
            const data = await res.json().catch(() => null);

            throw new ApiError(
            data?.detail || data?.message || `Erreur ${res.status}`,
            res.status,
            data,
            );
        }

        return res;
        }

    // Suite normale (traitement JSON / erreur)
    let data: any;
    const ct = res.headers.get("content-type") || "";
    if (ct.includes("application/json")) {
        data = await res.json().catch(() => null);
    } else if (ct.includes("text/html")) {
        // Le backend a renvoyé du HTML → c'est presque sûrement un fallback SPA
        throw new ApiError(
            `Route introuvable côté serveur (HTML reçu) : ${path}`,
            res.status,
            null,
        );
    } else {
        data = await res.text().catch(() => null);
    }

    if (!res.ok) {
        const msg =
            data?.detail ||
            data?.message ||
            data?.error ||
            `Erreur ${res.status}`;
        throw new ApiError(
            typeof msg === "string" ? msg : JSON.stringify(msg),
            res.status,
            data,
        );
    }

    return data;
}

// ── Polling d'une tâche Celery ───────────────────────────────────────
async function pollTask(
    taskId: string,
    opts: {
        timeoutMs?: number;
        intervalMs?: number;
        taskResultPath?: string;
    } = {},
): Promise<any> {
    const timeout = opts.timeoutMs ?? 60000;
    const interval = opts.intervalMs ?? 800;
    const basePath = opts.taskResultPath ?? "/auth/task-result";
    const start = Date.now();

    while (Date.now() - start < timeout) {
        const data: TaskResponse = await rawFetch(`${basePath}/${taskId}`);
        const rawState = (data.status || data.state || "")
            .toString()
            .toUpperCase();
        if (rawState === "SUCCESS") return data.result;
        if (rawState === "FAILURE") {
            const errMsg =
                data.error ||
                (typeof data.result === "string" ? data.result : null) ||
                "La tâche a échoué";
            throw new ApiError(errMsg, 500, data);
        }
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, interval));
    }
    throw new ApiError("Délai dépassé pour la tâche", 504);
}

export function downloadBase64(
    b64: string,
    filename: string,
    mime = "image/png",
) {
    const clean = b64.startsWith("data:") ? b64.split(",")[1] : b64;
    const bin = atob(clean);
    const len = bin.length;
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(len);
    for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) bytes[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
    const blob = new Blob([bytes], { type: mime });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = filename;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
}

// ── Fonction api principale ──────────────────────────────────────────
export async function api(
    path: string,
    opts: RequestOptions = {},
): Promise<any> {
    const data = await rawFetch(path, opts);
    if (opts.rawJson) return data;
    if (data && typeof data === "object" && typeof data.task_id === "string") {
        return pollTask(data.task_id, { taskResultPath: opts.taskResultPath });
    }
    return data;
}

export const apiRaw = rawFetch;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// AUTH
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
export async function authRegister(payload: {
    nom: string;
    email_contact: string;
    password: string;
    secret_phrase: string;
    verification_token: string;   // ← nouveau, obligatoire
    secteur?: string;
    telephone?: string;
    adresse?: string;
    site_web?: string;
    description?: string;
    siege_lat?: number | null;
    siege_lon?: number | null;
}) {
    return api("/auth/register", {
        method: "POST",
        body: payload,
        auth: false,
    });
}
export async function authLogin(payload: { email: string; password: string }) {
    const result = await api("/auth/login", {
        method: "POST",
        body: payload,
        auth: false,
    });
    const token = result?.access_token || result?.token;
    if (token) setToken(token);
    setEntreprise({
        id: result.entreprise_id,
        email: payload.email,
        nom: result.nom,
        rhEmail: result.rh_email || undefined,
    });
    return result;
}

export async function authGetProfile(_entrepriseId?: string) {
    return api("/auth/profile/me");
}

// DOCUMENTS RH (phrase secrète retirée → plus d'argument secretPhrase)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
export interface DocumentRH {
    id: string;
    entreprise_id: string;
    name: string;
    type: "facture" | "cv" | "paye" | "document" | "formulaire" | "rapport";
    category: string;
    date_created: string;
    date_modified: string;
    size: number;
    content?: string | null;
    image_url?: string | null;
    tags: string[];
    status: "draft" | "validated" | "archived";
    employee?: string | null;
    metadata?: Record<string, any>;
}

export interface FormulaireRH {
    id: string;
    entreprise_id: string;
    name: string;
    description: string;
    form_type?: FormType;
    is_anonymous?: boolean;
    fields: Array<{
        id: string;
        type: string;
        label: string;
        required: boolean;
        placeholder?: string;
        options?: string[];
        min?: number;
        max?: number;
    }>;
    responses: Array<Record<string, any>>;
    date_created: string;
}

export interface ReportRH {
    id: string;
    entreprise_id: string;
    name: string;
    type: "monthly" | "quarterly" | "annual" | "custom";
    date_range: { start: string; end: string };
    data: Array<{
        type: string;
        label: string;
        count: number;
        validated: number;
        draft: number;
    }>;
    charts: Array<{ type: string; title: string; dataKey: string }>;
    generated_at: string;
}

}

    const fd = new FormData();

    return api("/api/documents/upload", { method: "POST", formData: fd });
}

    return api("/api/documents/forms", { method: "POST", body: data });
}

}

    return api("/api/documents/reports");
}

export async function generateReport(data: {
    name: string;
    type?: string;
    start_date: string;
    end_date: string;
}): Promise<ReportRH> {
    return api("/api/documents/reports", { method: "POST", body: data });
}

    return api(`/api/documents/reports/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// AUTH (suite) – Mot de passe oublié / clés de récupération
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export async function forgotPasswordCheck(email: string) {
    return api("/auth/forgot-password", {
        method: "POST",
        body: { email },
        auth: false,
        rawJson: true,
    });
}

export async function resetPasswordViaSecretPhrase(
    email: string,
    secretPhrase: string,
    newPassword: string,
) {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
        "X-Secret-Phrase": secretPhrase,
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
    };
    const res = await fetch(
        buildUrl("/auth/forgot-password/via-secret-phrase"),
        {
            method: "POST",
            headers,
            body: JSON.stringify({
                email,
                secret_phrase: secretPhrase,
                new_password: newPassword,
            }),
            credentials: "include",
        },
    );
    if (!res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new ApiError(
            data.detail || data.message || `Erreur ${res.status}`,
            res.status,
            data,
        );
    }
    return res.json();
}

export async function resetPasswordViaRecoveryKey(
    email: string,
    recoveryKey: string,
    newPassword: string,
) {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
        "X-Recovery-Key": recoveryKey,
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
    };
    const res = await fetch(
        buildUrl("/auth/forgot-password/via-recovery-key"),
        {
            method: "POST",
            headers,
            body: JSON.stringify({ email, new_password: newPassword }),
            credentials: "include",
        },
    );
    if (!res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new ApiError(
            data.detail || data.message || `Erreur ${res.status}`,
            res.status,
            data,
        );
    }
    return res.json();
}

export async function regenerateRecoveryKeys(
    email: string,
    secretPhrase: string,
) {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
        "X-Secret-Phrase": secretPhrase,
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
    };
    const res = await fetch(buildUrl("/auth/recovery-keys/regenerate"), {
        method: "POST",
        headers,
        body: JSON.stringify({ email }),
        credentials: "include",
    });
    if (!res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new ApiError(
            data.detail || data.message || `Erreur ${res.status}`,
            res.status,
            data,
        );
    }
    return res.json();
}

    field_id: string;
    field_type:
        | "mood"
        | "scale"
        | "choice"
        | "radio"
        | "checkbox"
        | "select"
        | "text"
        | string;
    label: string;
    total_answers: number;
    score_moyen?: number | null;
    moyenne?: number | null;
    mediane?: number | null;
    mode?: number | null;
    min?: number;
    max?: number;
    score_normalized?: number | null;
    satisfaction_rate?: number | null;
    positive_count?: number;
    negative_count?: number;
    tendance?: "positive" | "neutre" | "négative";
    filled_rate?: number;
    avg_length?: number;
    sample?: string[];
    distribution?: Array<{
        label: string;
        value: number;
        percent: number;
        score?: number;
        color?: string;
    }>;
    charts?: Array<{
        type: "bar" | "pie" | "gauge" | string;
        title: string;
        data?: any;
        value?: number;
        min?: number;
        max?: number;
        thresholds?: Array<{ at: number; color: string }>;
    }>;
}

// DOCUMENTS – Téléchargement / Aperçu / Mise à jour contenu & métadonnées
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Télécharge le fichier déchiffré d'un document (force le download navigateur).
 * Renvoie le Blob — utilisez `triggerBlobDownload` pour déclencher le téléchargement.
 */
export async function downloadDocument(
    docId: string,
): Promise<{ blob: Blob; filename: string }> {
    const t = getToken();
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (t) headers["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${t}`;

    const res = await fetch(buildUrl(`/api/documents/${docId}/download`), {
        headers,
        credentials: "include",
    });

    if (!res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new ApiError(
            data.detail || data.message || `Erreur ${res.status}`,
            res.status,
            data,
        );
    }

    // Extraire le filename depuis Content-Disposition
    const cd = res.headers.get("content-disposition") || "";
    const match = cd.match(/filename="?([^"]+)"?/i);
    const filename = match ? match[1] : `document-${docId}`;

    return { blob: await res.blob(), filename };
}

/**
 * URL d'aperçu inline (PDF, image, texte). Utilisable dans <iframe src> ou <img src>.
 * Note: le token est dans le cookie/Authorization — si vous l'utilisez dans une <iframe>,
 * préférez `fetchDocumentPreview` ci-dessous qui renvoie un objectURL authentifié.
 */
export function getDocumentPreviewUrl(docId: string): string {
    return `${API_BASE}/api/documents/${encodeURIComponent(docId)}/preview`;
}

/**
 * Récupère le fichier en preview et renvoie un objectURL utilisable dans
 * <iframe>, <img>, <embed>. Pensez à `URL.revokeObjectURL` quand vous n'en avez plus besoin.
 */
export async function fetchDocumentPreview(
    docId: string,
): Promise<{ url: string; mimeType: string; filename: string }> {
    const t = getToken();
    if (!t) throw new ApiError("Non authentifié", 401);

    const res = await fetch(buildUrl(`/api/documents/${docId}/preview`), {
        method: "GET",
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${t}` },
        credentials: "include",
    });

    if (!res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new ApiError(
            data.detail || data.message || `Erreur ${res.status}`,
            res.status,
            data,
        );
    }

    const cd = res.headers.get("content-disposition") || "";
    const match = cd.match(/filename="?([^"]+)"?/i);
    const filename = match ? match[1] : `document-${docId}`;
    const mimeType =
        res.headers.get("content-type")?.split(";")[0].trim() ||
        "application/octet-stream";

    const blob = await res.blob();
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob); // ← blob: URL, utilisable par <embed> sans auth
    return { url, mimeType, filename };
}

/**
 * Remplace le contenu binaire d'un document existant (rechiffré côté serveur).
 */
export async function updateDocumentContent(
    docId: string,
    file: File,
): Promise<{ message: string; id: string }> {
    const fd = new FormData();
    fd.append("file", file);

    const t = getToken();
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (t) headers["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${t}`;

    const res = await fetch(buildUrl(`/api/documents/${docId}/content`), {
        method: "PUT",
        headers,
        body: fd,
        credentials: "include",
    });

    if (!res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new ApiError(
            data.detail || data.message || `Erreur ${res.status}`,
            res.status,
            data,
        );
    }
    return res.json();
}

/**
 * Met à jour uniquement les métadonnées d'un document (pas le fichier).
 * Champs autorisés côté backend : category, tags, status, employee, type, meta_data, name.
 */
export async function updateDocumentMetadata(
    docId: string,
    updates: Partial<{
        name: string;
        category: string;
        tags: string[];
        status: "draft" | "validated" | "archived";
        employee: string;
        type: DocumentRH["type"];
        meta_data: Record<string, any>;
        content: string; // ← AJOUT
    }>,
): Promise<{ message: string; id: string }> {
    return api(`/api/documents/${docId}/metadata`, {
        method: "PATCH",
        body: updates,
        rawJson: true,
    });
}

/** Déclenche le téléchargement d'un Blob côté navigateur. */
export function triggerBlobDownload(blob: Blob, filename: string): void {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = filename;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
}

async function pollTaskDoc(
  taskId: string,
  opts: {
    timeoutMs?: number;
    intervalMs?: number;
    taskResultPath?: string;
  } = {},
): Promise<any> {
  const timeout = opts.timeoutMs ?? 60000;
  const interval = opts.intervalMs ?? 800;
  const basePath = opts.taskResultPath ?? "/auth/task-result";
  const start = Date.now();

  while (Date.now() - start < timeout) {
    let data: TaskResponse;
    try {
        data = await rawFetch(`${basePath}/${taskId}`);
    } catch (e) {
        if (e instanceof ApiError && (e.status === 404 || e.status === 410)) {
            throw new ApiError("Tâche introuvable ou expirée", e.status);
        }
        throw e;
    }

    // Le backend peut renvoyer `null` (ou un objet sans état) tant que la
    // tâche est en cours (état Celery "STARTED" non géré par la route, etc.).
    // Dans ce cas on considère la tâche comme toujours en attente et on
    // continue le polling — la source de vérité reste le backend.
    if (data && typeof data === "object") {
      const rawState = (data.status || data.state || "")
        .toString()
        .toUpperCase();

      if (rawState === "SUCCESS") return data.result;

      if (rawState === "FAILURE") {
        const errMsg =
          data.error ||
          (typeof data.result === "string" ? data.result : null) ||
          "La tâche a échoué";
        throw new ApiError(errMsg, 500, data);
      }
      // Tout autre état (PENDING, STARTED, etc.) → on continue d'attendre.
    }

    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, interval));
  }

  throw new ApiError("Délai dépassé pour la tâche", 504);
}

export type FormatDocumentGenere = "docx" | "xlsx";
export type DocumentEditMode = "simple" | "structure";

// Le contenu exact de `result` est produit par les tâches Celery côté backend
// (document_scan_task / document_edit_ia_task) et n'est pas contractualisé ici.
// On le laisse donc volontairement libre pour ne rien supposer.
export type ScanDocumentResult = Record<string, unknown>;
export type DocumentEditIAResult = Record<string, unknown>;

// ── Path du polling partagé (scan + edit-ia) ─────────────────────────────
const SCAN_TASK_RESULT_PATH = "/api/documents/scan/task-result";

/**
 * POST /api/documents/scan
 * Lance le scan OCR + IA d'une IMAGE (JPEG, PNG, .webp — max 10 Mo côté backend).
 * Retourne un task_id, puis on poll /scan/task-result/{task_id}.
 */
export async function documentScan(file: File): Promise<ScanDocumentResult> {
  const fd = new FormData();
  fd.append("file", file);

  // rawFetch renvoie { task_id, message } ; on poll ensuite la tâche.
  const data = await rawFetch("/api/documents/scan", {
    method: "POST",
    formData: fd,
  });

  if (data && typeof data.task_id === "string") {
    return (await pollTaskDoc(data.task_id, {
      taskResultPath: SCAN_TASK_RESULT_PATH,
    })) as ScanDocumentResult;
  }

  return data as ScanDocumentResult;
}

/**
 * GET /api/documents/scan/task-result/{task_id}
 * Lecture brute de l'état d'une tâche (sans polling).
 * Le backend renvoie { state, result?, error? }.
 */
export async function documentScanTaskResult(taskId: string): Promise<{
  state: string;
  result?: ScanDocumentResult;
  error?: string;
}> {
  return api(`/api/documents/scan/task-result/${taskId}`, { rawJson: true });
}

/**
 * POST /api/documents/scan/edit-ia
 * Applique une instruction IA sur le texte simple (mode="simple") ou la
 * structure markdown (mode="structure"). Retourne un task_id ; le polling
 * passe par le même /scan/task-result/{task_id}.
 */
export async function documentEditIA(payload: {
  mode: DocumentEditMode;
  contenu_actuel: string;
  instruction: string;
  titre?: string;
  format_recommande?: FormatDocumentGenere;
}): Promise<DocumentEditIAResult> {
  return api("/api/documents/scan/edit-ia", {
    method: "POST",
    body: {
      mode: payload.mode,
      contenu_actuel: payload.contenu_actuel,
      instruction: payload.instruction,
      titre: payload.titre ?? "Document",
      format_recommande: payload.format_recommande ?? "docx",
    },
    // api() détecte le task_id de la réponse et poll automatiquement.
    taskResultPath: SCAN_TASK_RESULT_PATH,
  });
}

// ── Récupération des fichiers générés (docx / pdf) ───────────────────────

/**
 * GET /api/documents/scan/fichier/{fichier_id}
 * Télécharge le fichier généré (docx) — le backend renvoie le binaire en
 * pièce jointe. Utilise `triggerBlobDownload` pour déclencher le download.
 */
export async function downloadScanFichier(
  fichierId: string,
): Promise<{ blob: Blob; filename: string }> {
  const t = getToken();
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (t) headers["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${t}`;

  const res = await fetch(
    buildUrl(`/api/documents/scan/fichier/${encodeURIComponent(fichierId)}`),
    { headers, credentials: "include" },
  );

  if (!res.ok) {
    const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new ApiError(
      data.detail || data.message || `Erreur ${res.status}`,
      res.status,
      data,
    );
  }

  const cd = res.headers.get("content-disposition") || "";
  const match = cd.match(/filename="?([^"]+)"?/i);
  const filename = match ? match[1] : `${fichierId}.docx`;

  return { blob: await res.blob(), filename };
}

/**
 * GET /api/documents/scan/pdf/{pdf_id}
 * Récupère l'aperçu PDF généré et renvoie un objectURL utilisable dans
 * <iframe>/<embed>/<object>. Pensez à URL.revokeObjectURL après usage.
 */
export async function fetchScanPdfPreview(
  pdfId: string,
): Promise<{ url: string; filename: string }> {
  const t = getToken();
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (t) headers["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${t}`;

  const res = await fetch(
    buildUrl(`/api/documents/scan/pdf/${encodeURIComponent(pdfId)}`),
    { headers, credentials: "include" },
  );

  if (!res.ok) {
    const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new ApiError(
      data.detail || data.message || `Erreur ${res.status}`,
      res.status,
      data,
    );
  }

  const cd = res.headers.get("content-disposition") || "";
  const match = cd.match(/filename="?([^"]+)"?/i);
  const filename = match ? match[1] : `apercu_${pdfId}.pdf`;

  const blob = await res.blob();
  return { url: URL.createObjectURL(blob), filename };
}

export function scanPdfPreviewUrl(pdfId: string): string {
  return `${API_BASE}/api/documents/scan/pdf/${encodeURIComponent(pdfId)}`;
}

/** URL directe du fichier généré (docx). */
export function scanFichierUrl(fichierId: string): string {
  return `${API_BASE}/api/documents/scan/fichier/${encodeURIComponent(fichierId)}`;
}

// AUTH — Vérification d'email par code à 6 chiffres
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export interface EmailCodeRequestResult {
    message: string;
    expires_in_seconds: number;
}

export interface EmailCodeVerifyResult {
    verified: boolean;
    verification_token: string;
    expires_in_seconds: number;
}

/** Envoie un code à 6 chiffres à l'email fourni. Rejette si provider non autorisé. */
export async function authRequestEmailCode(email: string): Promise<EmailCodeRequestResult> {
    return api("/auth/email/request-code", {
        method: "POST",
        body: { email },
        auth: false,
        rawJson: true,
    });
}

/**
 * Vérifie le code saisi. Renvoie un verification_token (valide 15 min) à
 * transmettre tel quel à authRegister — sans lui, /auth/register refuse.
 */
export async function authVerifyEmailCode(
    email: string,
    code: string,
): Promise<EmailCodeVerifyResult> {
    return api("/auth/email/verify-code", {
        method: "POST",
        body: { email, code },
        auth: false,
        rawJson: true,
    });
}

export interface WebhookEvent {
    event_type: string;
    event_source: string;
    data: Record<string, any>;
    timestamp?: string;
}

export interface WebhookResponse {
    success: boolean;
    message: string;
    event_id?: number;
}

export interface WebhookTestResponse {
    success: boolean;
    integration_id: number;
    entreprise_id: string;
    webhook_url: string;
    message: string;
}

/**
 * POST /v1/webhooks/telephony/{integration_id}
 * Envoie un événement webhook téléphonique
 */
export async function telephonyWebhookSend(
    integrationId: number,
    event: WebhookEvent,
): Promise<WebhookResponse> {
    return api(`/v1/webhooks/telephony/${integrationId}`, {
        method: "POST",
        body: event,
        rawJson: true,
    });
}

/**
 * GET /v1/webhooks/telephony/{integration_id}/test
 * Teste l'accessibilité du webhook
 */
export async function telephonyWebhookTest(
    integrationId: number,
): Promise<WebhookTestResponse> {
    return api(`/v1/webhooks/telephony/${integrationId}/test`, {
        rawJson: true,
    });
}

/**
 * POST /v1/webhooks/telephony/{integration_id}/test
 * Envoie un événement de test (sans vérification de signature)
 */
export async function telephonyWebhookTestSend(
    integrationId: number,
    event: WebhookEvent,
): Promise<WebhookResponse> {
    return api(`/v1/webhooks/telephony/${integrationId}/test`, {
        method: "POST",
        body: event,
        rawJson: true,
    });
}

}

  badges: string[];

}

export interface JobOfferDetail extends JobOfferListItem {
  entreprise_description: string | null;
  entreprise_lien_externe: string | null;
  entreprise_logo_url: string | null;
  niveau_experience: string;
  niveau_etudes: string | null;
  localisation_quartier: string | null;
  teletravail_possible: boolean;
  salaire_estime: string | null;
  mots_cles_seo: string[];
  url_source: string;
  domaine_source: string;
  date_publication_source: string | null;
  schema_jobposting: Record<string, any>;
}

export async function listJobOffers(opts: {
  categorie_metier?: string; secteur?: string; ville?: string;
  type_contrat?: string; page?: number;
} = {}): Promise<JobOfferListItem[]> {
  return api("/api/emploi/offres", { query: opts, auth: false, rawJson: true });
}

export async function getJobOffer(slug: string): Promise<JobOfferDetail> {
  return api(`/api/emploi/offres/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}`, { auth: false, rawJson: true });
}

export async function getJobOfferSuggestions(slug: string): Promise<JobOfferListItem[]> {
  return api(`/api/emploi/offres/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}/suggestions`, { auth: false, rawJson: true });
}

export interface SiteMarcheEmploi {
  site_id: string;
  domaine: string;
  nom: string;
  logo_url: string;
  nombre_offres_estime: number;
  estimation_precise: boolean;
  nouvelles_offres: number;
  echantillon: { titre: string; url: string }[];
}

export interface RapportMarche {
  poste: string;
  ville: string | null;
  total_offres_estime: number;
  estimation_precise: boolean;
  nb_sites_avec_offres: number;
  nouvelles_offres_total: number;
  sites: SiteMarcheEmploi[];
  date_generation: string;
  perime: boolean;
}

export async function getRapportMarche(
  poste: string,
  ville?: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<RapportMarche> {
  const p = poste?.trim();
  if (!p) throw new Error("poste requis");

  const query: Record<string, string> = { poste: p };
  const v = ville?.trim();
  if (v) query.ville = v;

  return api("/api/emploi/marche", { query, auth: false, rawJson: true, signal });
}

  OverrideEcheanceBody,
  OverrideEcheanceResultat,
  RegistresStatut,
  Row,
  SynchroRegistresParams,
  TacheLancee,
  TeleversementRegistreResultat,
  TypeRegistre
} from "../types/ui";
type Q = Record<string, unknown>;

const E = "/api/ecole";
const ECOLE_TASK_RESULT_PATH = `${E}/tasks`;
const s = (v: string) => encodeURIComponent(v);

export interface FichierBinaire {
  blob: Blob;
  filename: string;
  mimeType: string;
}

function extraireNomFichier(cd: string): string | null {
  const star = cd.match(/filename\*\s*=\s*(?:UTF-8|utf-8)''([^;]+)/i);
  if (star) {
    try {
      return decodeURIComponent(star[1].trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ""));
    } catch {
      return star[1].trim();
    }
  }
  const plain = cd.match(/filename\s*=\s*"?([^";]+)"?/i);
  return plain ? plain[1].trim() : null;
}

async function fetchBinaire(
path: string,
fallbackName: string,
query?: Q,
method: "GET" | "POST" = "GET")
: Promise<FichierBinaire> {
  const res: Response = await apiRaw(path, { method, query, rawResponse: true });
  const cd = res.headers.get("content-disposition") || "";
  const mimeType = res.headers.get("content-type")?.split(";")[0].trim() || "application/octet-stream";
  return { blob: await res.blob(), filename: extraireNomFichier(cd) ?? fallbackName, mimeType };
}

const get = (path: string, query?: Q) => api(path, { query, rawJson: true });
const post = (path: string, body?: unknown, query?: Q) => api(path, { method: "POST", body, query, rawJson: true });
const patch = (path: string, body: unknown) => api(path, { method: "PATCH", body, rawJson: true });
const put = (path: string, body: unknown) => api(path, { method: "PUT", body, rawJson: true });
const del = (path: string, query?: Q) => api(path, { method: "DELETE", query, rawJson: true });

  const fd = new FormData();
  fd.append("fichier", fichier);
  return api(`${E}/eleves/${s(eleveId)}/photo`, { method: "POST", formData: fd, rawJson: true });
}
export async function uploaderDocumentEleve(eleveId: string, typeDocument: string, fichier: File): Promise<Row> {
  const fd = new FormData();
  fd.append("type_document", typeDocument);
  fd.append("fichier", fichier);
  return api(`${E}/eleves/${s(eleveId)}/documents`, { method: "POST", formData: fd, rawJson: true });
}

  typeRegistre: TypeRegistre,
  fichier: File

  return api(`${E}/registres/classes/${s(classeId)}/${s(typeRegistre)}/televerser`, {
    method: "POST",

    rawJson: true
  });
}

// ── App mobile Android (APK) ─────────────────────────────────────────────
const APP_MOBILE = "/api/ecole/app-mobile";

export interface AppMobileVersion {
  filename: string;
  size_bytes: number;
  size_mb: number;
  modified_at: string;
  sha256: string | null;
  download_url: string; // URL absolue calculée côté backend
}

export interface AppMobileVersionsResponse {
  latest: AppMobileVersion | null;
  all_versions: AppMobileVersion[];
}

/** Liste les APK disponibles avec taille, date et hash SHA-256. */
export const getAppMobileVersions = (): Promise<AppMobileVersionsResponse> =>
  api(`${APP_MOBILE}/versions`, { rawJson: true, auth: false });

/** URL cible encodée dans le QR + URL publique de l'image QR. */
export const getAppMobileQrTarget = (): Promise<{ target_url: string; qr_url: string; label: string }> =>
  api(`${APP_MOBILE}/qr-url`, { rawJson: true, auth: false });












################################################################################
# FICHIER: lib/api_routes/abonnement_api.ts
################################################################################

import { api ,buildUrl,ApiError,getToken } from '@/lib/api'
import { API_BASE } from '../api';


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ABONNEMENT — Formules forfait | par_employe
// Paliers d'employés et durées sont INDÉPENDANTS :
//   - forfait     : 1 prix fixe × durée (avec mois offerts sur 6 et 12)
//   - par_employe : palier (1/3/6/12 employés) × durée (1/3/6/12 mois)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export type FormuleAbo = "forfait" | "par_employe";
export type StatutAbo =
    | "trial"
    | "actif"
    | "expire"
    | "suspendu"
    | "introuvable";
export type MoisPack = 1 | 3 | 6 | 12;
export type PalierEmploye = 1 | 3 | 6 | 12;

// ── Forfait ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export interface PackForfait {
    mois: number; // durée affichée (1/3/6/12)
    mois_factures: number; // mois réellement facturés
    mois_offerts: number; // mois_affiche - mois_factures
    nb_employes: null; // toujours null = illimité
    total_fcfa: number;
    prix_mois_fcfa: number;
    label: string;
    populaire: boolean;
    detail?: string;
    description_ligne?: string;
}

// ── Par employé ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export interface DureeOption {
    mois: number; // 1/3/6/12
    total_fcfa: number;
    prix_mois_fcfa: number;
    detail?: string;
    description_ligne?: string;
}

export interface PalierOption {
    nb_employes: PalierEmploye;
    label: string;
    populaire: boolean;
    warning_depassement: {
        actif: boolean;
        message: string | null;
    };
    durees: DureeOption[];
}
