// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// CHANGEMENT DE FORMULE
// `nb_employes` = palier (1/3/6/12) — REQUIS pour par_employe, ignoré pour forfait
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/**
 * Vérifie sans appliquer.
 * - formule = "forfait"     → nb_employes ignoré, toujours possible
 * - formule = "par_employe" → nb_employes requis (palier 1/3/6/12)
 *                             échoue si trop d'employés actifs pour ce palier
 */
export async function abonnementValiderFormule(
    formule: FormuleAbo,
    nb_employes?: PalierEmploye | number,
): Promise<ValiderFormuleResult> {
    return api("/abonnement/formule/valider", {
        method: "POST",
        body: { formule, nb_employes: nb_employes ?? null },
        rawJson: true,
    });
}

/**
 * Applique le changement (prise d'effet immédiate sur les limites d'employés).
 */
export async function abonnementChangerFormule(
    formule: FormuleAbo,
    nb_employes?: PalierEmploye | number,
): Promise<ChangerFormuleResult> {
    return api("/abonnement/formule/changer", {
        method: "POST",
        body: { formule, nb_employes: nb_employes ?? null },
        rawJson: true,
    });
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// VIREMENT MANUEL
// `mois` = durée (1/3/6/12), `nb_employes` = palier (requis si par_employe)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export async function abonnementSoumettreVirement(payload: {
    mois: MoisPack | number;
    formule: FormuleAbo;
    nb_employes?: PalierEmploye | number | null; // requis si formule = par_employe
    reference_virement: string;
    note_entreprise?: string;
    preuve?: File | null;
}): Promise<SoumettreVirementResult> {
    const fd = new FormData();
    fd.append("mois", String(payload.mois));
    fd.append("formule", payload.formule);
    if (payload.formule === "par_employe" && payload.nb_employes != null) {
        fd.append("nb_employes", String(payload.nb_employes));
    }
    fd.append("reference_virement", payload.reference_virement);
    if (payload.note_entreprise)
        fd.append("note_entreprise", payload.note_entreprise);
    if (payload.preuve) fd.append("preuve", payload.preuve);

    return api("/abonnement/virement/soumettre", {
        method: "POST",
        formData: fd,
        rawJson: true,
    });
}

/** URL absolue d'une preuve (utilisable dans <a> ou <img>). */
export function abonnementPreuveUrl(filename: string): string {
    return `${API_BASE}/abonnement/virement/preuve/${encodeURIComponent(filename)}`;
}

/** Télécharge une preuve en Blob (auth incluse). */
export async function abonnementDownloadPreuve(
    filename: string,
): Promise<Blob> {
    const t = getToken();
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (t) headers["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${t}`;
    const res = await fetch(
        buildUrl(`/abonnement/virement/preuve/${encodeURIComponent(filename)}`),
        {
            headers,
            credentials: "include",
        },
    );
    if (!res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new ApiError(
            data.detail || data.message || `Erreur ${res.status}`,
            res.status,
            data,
        );
    }
    return res.blob();
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// PAYPAL
// `mois` = durée, `nb_employes` = palier (requis si par_employe)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

export async function abonnementPaypalCreerOrdre(payload: {
    mois: MoisPack | number;
    formule: FormuleAbo;
    nb_employes?: PalierEmploye | number | null;
}): Promise<CreerOrdrePaypalResult> {
    return api("/abonnement/paypal/creer-ordre", {
        method: "POST",
        body: {
            mois: payload.mois,
            formule: payload.formule,
            nb_employes:
                payload.formule === "par_employe"
                    ? (payload.nb_employes ?? null)
                    : null,
        },
        rawJson: true,
    });
}

export async function abonnementPaypalCapturer(
    ordreId: string,
): Promise<CapturerPaypalResult> {
    return api(`/abonnement/paypal/capturer/${encodeURIComponent(ordreId)}`, {
        method: "POST",
        rawJson: true,
    });
}



################################################################################
# FICHIER: lib/auth.tsx
################################################################################

import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, ReactNode, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  getToken, getEntreprise, setToken, setEntreprise,
  logout as apiLogout,TOKEN_EXPIRED_EVENT,
} from './api';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

type Entreprise = { id: string; email: string; nom?: string; rhEmail?: string };

interface AuthCtx {
  token: string | null;
  entreprise: Entreprise | null;
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  setAuth: (token: string, entreprise: Entreprise) => void;
  logout: (reason?: string) => void;
  refresh: () => void;
}

const Ctx = createContext<AuthCtx | null>(null);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [token, setT] = useState<string | null>(() => getToken());
  const [entreprise, setE] = useState<Entreprise | null>(() => getEntreprise());

  const refresh = useCallback(() => {
    setT(getToken());
    setE(getEntreprise());
  }, []);

  const setAuth = useCallback((tok: string, e: Entreprise) => {
    setToken(tok);
    setEntreprise(e);
    setT(tok);
    setE(e);
  }, []);

  const logout = useCallback((_reason?: string) => {
    apiLogout();
    setT(null);
    setE(null);
  }, []);


  useEffect(() => {
    const onTokenExpired = () => {
      setT(null);
      setE(null);
      toast.error('Votre session a expiré. Veuillez vous reconnecter.', {
        id: 'session-expired',
        duration: 5000,
      });
    };

    window.addEventListener(TOKEN_EXPIRED_EVENT, onTokenExpired);
    return () => window.removeEventListener(TOKEN_EXPIRED_EVENT, onTokenExpired);
  }, []);

  // Écouteur pour les déconnexions forcées (IP interdite, etc.)


  // Synchronisation multi-onglets
  useEffect(() => {
    const onStorage = () => refresh();
    window.addEventListener('storage', onStorage);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('storage', onStorage);
  }, [refresh]);

  return (
    <Ctx.Provider value={{ token, entreprise, isAuthenticated: !!token, setAuth, logout, refresh }}>
      {children}
    </Ctx.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth(): AuthCtx {
  const v = useContext(Ctx);
  if (!v) throw new Error('useAuth must be used within AuthProvider');
  return v;
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: lib/utils.ts
################################################################################

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
}

// Taux BCEAO fixe : 1 EUR = 655,957 FCFA
const FCFA_PER_EUR = 655.957;

export function formatFCFA(amount: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (amount == null || Number.isNaN(amount)) return '—';
  return (
    new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', { maximumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(amount) +
    ' FCFA'
  );
}

export function fcfaToEur(fcfa: number): number {
  return fcfa / FCFA_PER_EUR;
}

export function formatEUR(amount: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (amount == null || Number.isNaN(amount)) return '—';
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', {
    style: 'currency',
    currency: 'EUR',
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(amount);
}

export function formatDateFR(
  date: string | Date | null | undefined,
  opts?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions,
): string {
  if (!date) return '—';
  const d = typeof date === 'string' ? new Date(date) : date;
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return '—';
  return d.toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
    ...opts,
  });
}

export function formatDateTimeFR(date: string | Date | null | undefined): string {
  if (!date) return '—';
  const d = typeof date === 'string' ? new Date(date) : date;
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return '—';
  return d.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
  });
}

import React, { useMemo } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { ArrowRightIcon, CalendarDaysIcon, CheckCircle2Icon, HomeIcon, SchoolIcon, LayoutGridIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { useSchool } from "../contexts/SchoolContext";
import { WithAnnee } from "../components/layout/WithAnnee";
import { DirectionOverview } from "../components/dashboard/DirectionOverview";
import { AttentionPanel } from "../components/dashboard/AttentionPanel";
import { RemplissageList } from "../components/dashboard/RemplissageList";
import { modules } from "../data/modules";
import { str } from "../utils/format";
import { SeedDemoButton } from "../components/dashboard/SeedDemoButton";

const EASE = [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1] as const;

export function Dashboard() {
  const { annee, entreprise } = useSchool();
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();

  const aujourdhui = useMemo(
    () => new Date().toLocaleDateString("fr-FR", { weekday: "long", day: "numeric", month: "long", year: "numeric" }),
    []
  );

  // Filtrer les modules pour les raccourcis (on exclut l'accueil, paramètres et abonnement)
  const shortcuts = modules.filter(m => 
    !["/", "/parametres"].includes(m.path)
  ).slice(0, 8);

  const reveal = (i: number) =>
    reduce ? {} : {
      initial: { opacity: 0, y: 15 },
      animate: { opacity: 1, y: 0 },
      transition: { duration: 0.4, ease: EASE, delay: i * 0.08 }
    };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-10">
      {/* ── En-tête (Hero) ────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <motion.header {...reveal(0)} className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-white p-6 sm:p-8">
        <div className="absolute right-0 top-0 -mr-16 -mt-16 h-64 w-64 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] opacity-50 blur-3xl" />
        <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <span className="grid h-14 w-14 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-[var(--em-accent)] text-white shadow-lg">
              <HomeIcon className="h-7 w-7" aria-hidden="true" />
            </span>
            <div>
              <p className="text-[13px] font-medium capitalize text-[var(--em-text-2)]">{aujourdhui}</p>
              <h1 className="ecole_m-display mt-0.5 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-[var(--em-text)] sm:text-3xl">
                Bonjour, {entreprise?.nom || "Direction"}
              </h1>
              <p className="mt-1 text-[13.5px] text-[var(--em-text-2)]">
                {annee ? `Année scolaire ${str(annee.libelle)} en cours` : "Bienvenue sur votre espace de gestion"}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {annee && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-start rounded-full border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[#F9F9F9] px-4 py-2 sm:self-auto">
              <CheckCircle2Icon className="h-4 w-4 text-[var(--em-accent)]" />
              <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">{str(annee.libelle)}</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </motion.header>

      {/* Seed Demo (uniquement si activé) */}
      <motion.div {...reveal(1)}>
        <SeedDemoButton />
      </motion.div>

      <WithAnnee>
        {(anneeId) => (
          <div className="space-y-6">
            {/* ── Indicateurs clés & Finance ───────────────────── */}
            <motion.div {...reveal(2)}>
              <DirectionOverview anneeId={anneeId} />
            </motion.div>

            {/* ── Points d'attention (Paiements & Discipline) ──── */}
            <motion.div {...reveal(3)}>
              <AttentionPanel anneeId={anneeId} />
            </motion.div>

            {/* ── Raccourcis Modules (Comble le vide) ──────────── */}
            <motion.section {...reveal(4)} className="ecole_m-card p-6">
              <div className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                <LayoutGridIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-[var(--em-text-2)]" />
                <h2 className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">Accès rapide aux modules</h2>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-4">
                {shortcuts.map((m) => {
                  const Icon = m.icon;
                  return (
                    <Link
                      key={m.path}
                      to={m.path}
                      className="group flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[#F9F9F9] p-4 transition-all duration-200 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-[var(--em-accent)]/30 hover:bg-white hover:shadow-md"
                    >
                      <span
                        className="mb-2 grid h-10 w-10 place-items-center rounded-lg text-white transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-110"
                        style={{ backgroundColor: m.color }}
                      >
                        <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)] text-center">{m.label}</span>
                    </Link>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </motion.section>

            {/* ── Remplissage des classes ──────────────────────── */}
            <motion.div {...reveal(5)}>
              <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 px-1">
                <SchoolIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-[var(--em-text-2)]" />
                <h2 className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-[var(--em-text)]">Répartition des classes</h2>

        )}
      </WithAnnee>

      {/* Footer */}
      <motion.div {...reveal(6)} className="flex items-center justify-center pt-4 text-[12px] text-[var(--em-text-3)]">
        <CalendarDaysIcon className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
        Données synchronisées en temps réel
      </motion.div>
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: pages/LoginPage.tsx
################################################################################

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useSearchParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import { ArrowRightIcon, Building2Icon, LoaderCircleIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import { authLogin } from '@/lib/api';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth';

export default function LoginPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { setAuth } = useAuth();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();

  useEffect(() => {
    const reason = searchParams.get('reason');
    if (reason === 'expired') {
      toast.error('Session expirée — veuillez vous reconnecter.', {
        id: 'session-expired',
        duration: 6000,
      });
      window.history.replaceState({}, '', '/login');
    }
  }, [searchParams]);

  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await authLogin({ email, password });
      const tok = res?.token || res?.access_token;
      const ent =
        res?.entreprise ??
        (res?.entreprise_id
          ? { id: res.entreprise_id, email, nom: res?.nom, rhEmail: res?.rh_email || undefined }
          : null);
      if (tok && ent) setAuth(tok, ent);
      toast.success('Connecté');
      const rawRedirect = searchParams.get('_redirect') || '/';
      const redirect = rawRedirect.replace(/^\/ecole/, '') || '/';
      navigate(redirect, { replace: true });
    } catch (err: any) {
      toast.error(err?.message || 'Échec de la connexion');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="ecole_m ecole_m-desktop min-h-screen grid lg:grid-cols-2">
      {/* Volet éditorial */}
      <aside className="hidden lg:flex flex-col justify-between p-12 bg-[var(--em-surface)] border-r border-[var(--em-border)]">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span className="grid h-9 w-9 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-[var(--em-accent)] text-white shadow-[inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.2),0_2px_6px_rgba(0,0,0,0.12)]">
            <Building2Icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
          </span>
          <div className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A]">École Manager</div>
        </div>

        <div className="max-w-md">
          <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[.16em] text-[#8A8A8A] mb-4">
            Plateforme scolaire
          </div>
          <p className="ecole_m-display text-[32px] leading-tight font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A] tracking-[-0.02em]">
            Pilotez votre <span className="text-[var(--em-accent)]">établissement</span>, les{' '}
            <span className="text-[var(--em-accent)]">élèves</span>, les{' '}
            <span className="text-[var(--em-accent)]">notes</span> et la{' '}
            <span className="text-[var(--em-accent)]">finance</span> en un seul endroit.
          </p>
          <div className="mt-6 h-[3px] w-12 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent)]" />
          <p className="mt-6 text-[14px] text-[#5D5D5D] leading-relaxed">
            Élèves, classes, emplois du temps, bulletins, vie scolaire, registres Excel.
            Une plateforme complète pour piloter votre école.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-mono tabular-nums text-[#8A8A8A]">
          <span>École Manager</span>
          <span>© {new Date().getFullYear()}</span>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* Formulaire */}
      <main className="flex items-center justify-center p-6 lg:p-12">
        <div className="w-full max-w-sm">
          <div className="lg:hidden flex items-center gap-3 mb-10">
            <span className="grid h-9 w-9 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-[var(--em-accent)] text-white shadow-[inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.2),0_2px_6px_rgba(0,0,0,0.12)]">
              <Building2Icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
            </span>
            <div className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A]">École Manager</div>
          </div>

          <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[.16em] text-[#8A8A8A] mb-3">
            Connexion
          </div>
          <h1 className="ecole_m-display text-[30px] font-semibold leading-tight tracking-[-0.015em] text-[#1A1A1A]">
            Accédez à votre <span className="text-[var(--em-accent)]">espace</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="text-[14px] text-[#5D5D5D] mt-2 mb-8">
            Saisissez vos identifiants pour continuer.
          </p>

          <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-5">
            <div>
              <label htmlFor="email" className="ecole_m-label">
                Email professionnel <span className="text-[var(--em-danger)]">*</span>
              </label>
              <input
                id="email"
                type="email"
                required
                autoComplete="email"
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="contact@etablissement.com"
                className="ecole_m-input"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label htmlFor="password" className="ecole_m-label">
                Mot de passe <span className="text-[var(--em-danger)]">*</span>
              </label>
              <input
                id="password"
                type="password"
                required
                autoComplete="current-password"
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="••••••••"
                className="ecole_m-input"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-end">
              <Link
                to="/forgot_password"
                className="text-[12px] text-[#5D5D5D] hover:text-[var(--em-accent)] border-b border-dashed border-[var(--em-border-strong)] hover:border-[var(--em-accent)] pb-px transition-colors"
              >
                Mot de passe oublié ?
              </Link>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={busy}
              className="ecole_m-btn ecole_m-btn--primary w-full justify-center h-10"
            >
              {busy ? (
                <LoaderCircleIcon className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden />
              ) : null}
              Se connecter
              {!busy && <ArrowRightIcon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />}
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="mt-10 pt-6 border-t border-[var(--em-border)] text-center text-[12px] text-[#5D5D5D]">
            Pas encore de compte ?{' '}
            <Link
              to="/signup"
              className="text-[#1A1A1A] hover:text-[var(--em-accent)] font-semibold border-b border-dashed border-[var(--em-border-strong)] hover:border-[var(--em-accent)] pb-px transition-colors"
            >
              Créer un établissement
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

import React from "react";

import {
  BookMarkedIcon, CalendarIcon, CalendarPlusIcon, ChevronRightIcon, 
  ListChecksIcon, NetworkIcon, PlusIcon, RulerIcon, SettingsIcon, 
  SparklesIcon, Trash2Icon, WalletIcon, SmartphoneIcon
} from "lucide-react";
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import {
  creerAnnee,
  creerBareme,
  creerMatiere,
  creerTypeEvaluation,
  listerAnnees,
  listerBaremes,
  listerMatieres,
  listerTypesEvaluation,
  supprimerMatiere
} from "../lib/api_ecole";
