interface TabDef {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  icon: LucideIcon;
  description: string;
}

const TABS: TabDef[] = [
  { id: "annees", label: "Années scolaires", icon: CalendarIcon, description: "Années, périodes et passage en classe supérieure" },
  { id: "matieres", label: "Matières", icon: BookMarkedIcon, description: "Disciplines enseignées dans l'établissement" },
  { id: "baremes", label: "Barèmes", icon: RulerIcon, description: "Règles de notation et seuils de réussite" },
  { id: "types", label: "Types d'évaluation", icon: ListChecksIcon, description: "Devoirs, compositions et leur poids" },
  { id: "referentiel", label: "Référentiel", icon: NetworkIcon, description: "Cycles, niveaux et séries" },
  { id: "mobile", label: "App Mobile", icon: SmartphoneIcon, description: "L'application pour les parents (téléchargement)" }, // <-- ONGLET AJOUTÉ
  { id: "abonnement", label: "Abonnement", icon: WalletIcon, description: "Statut, échéances et historique de paiement" },
  { id: "initialisation", label: "Initialisation", icon: SparklesIcon, description: "Mise en place et maintenance des données" }
];

const EASE = [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1] as const;

function AbonnementPanel() {
  const { status } = useAbonnement();
  const isActif = status?.statut === 'actif';
  const paiements = status?.paiements ?? [];

  return (
    <div className="abonnement-scope space-y-5">
      <StatutAbonnementCard />
      <Panel
        title="Gérer mon abonnement"
        subtitle="Activer, prolonger ou changer de formule"
        icon={<WalletIcon className="w-4 h-4" strokeWidth={2.4} />}
      >
        <Link to="/abonnement/upgrade" className="inline-block">
          <AboButton variant={isActif ? 'primary' : 'accent'}>
            {isActif ? 'Prolonger mon abonnement' : 'Activer mon abonnement'}
          </AboButton>
        </Link>
      </Panel>
      <PaiementsHistorique paiements={paiements} />
    </div>
  );
}

export function Parametres() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const tab = params.get("tab") ?? "annees";
  const school = useSchool();
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();

  const current = TABS.find((t) => t.id === tab) ?? TABS[0];
  const CurrentIcon = current.icon;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <header className="flex items-center gap-4">
        <span
          className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl"
          style={{ background: "color-mix(in srgb, #5A6472 14%, transparent)", color: "#5A6472" }}>
          <SettingsIcon className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" />
        </span>
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <h1 className="text-[24px] font-semibold leading-tight tracking-tight">Paramètres</h1>
          <p className="ecole_m-muted text-[14px]">Organisation de l'année, matières et règles de notation</p>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[260px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
        <nav aria-label="Rubriques des paramètres" className="lg:sticky lg:top-4 lg:self-start">
          <ul className="-mx-1 flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto px-1 pb-1 lg:mx-0 lg:flex-col lg:overflow-visible lg:px-0 lg:pb-0">
            {TABS.map((t) => {
              const Icon = t.icon;
              const active = t.id === current.id;
              return (
                <li key={t.id} className="shrink-0">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setParams({ tab: t.id })}
                    aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                    className={`relative flex w-full items-center gap-3 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-left text-[14px] outline-none transition-colors duration-150 ease-out focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--em-accent)] ${
                    active ? "font-semibold" : "ecole_m-muted hover:bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--em-accent)_6%,transparent)]"
                    }`}>
                    {active && (
                      <motion.span
                        layoutId="parametres-nav-active"
                        className="absolute inset-0 rounded-lg"
                        style={{ background: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--em-accent) 10%, transparent)" }}
                        transition={reduce ? { duration: 0 } : { type: "spring", stiffness: 500, damping: 40 }} />
                    )}
                    {active && (
                      <motion.span
                        layoutId="parametres-nav-bar"
                        className="absolute left-0 top-1/2 hidden h-4 w-[3px] -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full lg:block"
                        style={{ background: "var(--em-accent)" }}
                        transition={reduce ? { duration: 0 } : { type: "spring", stiffness: 500, damping: 40 }} />
                    )}
                    <Icon className={`relative h-[18px] w-[18px] shrink-0 ${active ? "text-[var(--em-accent)]" : ""}`} aria-hidden="true" />
                    <span className="relative flex-1">{t.label}</span>
                    {active && <ChevronRightIcon className="relative hidden h-4 w-4 text-[var(--em-accent)] lg:block" aria-hidden="true" />}
                  </button>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        </nav>

        <main className="min-w-0 space-y-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-[var(--em-border)] pb-4">
            <span
              className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-[var(--em-accent)]"
              style={{ background: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--em-accent) 12%, transparent)" }}>
              <CurrentIcon className="h-[18px] w-[18px]" aria-hidden="true" />
            </span>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <h2 className="text-[18px] font-semibold leading-tight">{current.label}</h2>
              <p className="ecole_m-muted text-[13px]">{current.description}</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <AnimatePresence mode="wait" initial={false}>
            <motion.div
              key={tab}
              initial={reduce ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              exit={reduce ? undefined : { opacity: 0, y: -4 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.2, ease: EASE }}>

              {tab === "annees" && (
                <ResourceView
                  load={listerAnnees}
                  deps={[]}
                  columns={[{ key: "libelle" }, { key: "date_debut", label: "Rentrée" }, { key: "date_fin", label: "Fin" }, { key: "statut" }, { key: "est_active", label: "Active" }]}
                  emptyTitle="Aucune année scolaire"
                  emptyText="Créez votre première année scolaire pour commencer."
                  emptyIcon={CalendarPlusIcon}
                  actions={(reload) => (
                    <FormAction
                      label="Nouvelle année"
                      icon={PlusIcon}
                      variant="primary"
                      fields={anneeFields}
                      onSubmit={creerAnnee}
                      successMessage="Année créée"
                      onDone={() => {
                        reload();
                        school.reload();
                      }} />
                  )}
                  detailTitle={(r) => str(r.libelle)}
                  detailSubtitle={(r) => r.est_active ? "Année active" : "Année scolaire"}
                  renderDetail={(r, ctx) => <AnneeDetail annee={r} reload={ctx.reload} />} />
              )}

              {tab === "matieres" && (
                <ResourceView
                  load={listerMatieres}
                  deps={[]}
                  columns={[
                    {
                      key: "libelle",
                      render: (r) => (
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                          <span className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0 rounded-full ring-2 ring-[var(--em-border)]" style={{ background: str(r.couleur_affichage) || "#c5ccd6" }} />
                          <span className="font-medium">{str(r.libelle)}</span>
                        </span>
                      )
                    },
                    { key: "code" },
                    { key: "categorie" }
                  ]}
                  emptyTitle="Aucune matière"
                  actions={(reload) => (
                    <FormAction label="Nouvelle matière" icon={PlusIcon} variant="primary" fields={matiereFields} onSubmit={creerMatiere} successMessage="Matière créée" onDone={reload} />
                  )}
                  detailTitle={(r) => str(r.libelle)}
                  rowActions={(r, reload) => (
                    <ConfirmAction
                      label="Supprimer"
                      icon={Trash2Icon}
                      iconOnly
                      size="sm"
                      variant="subtle"
                      danger
                      message={`Supprimer la matière ${str(r.libelle)} ?`}
                      confirmLabel="Supprimer"
                      run={() => supprimerMatiere(str(r.id))}
                      successMessage="Matière supprimée"
                      onDone={reload} />
                  )} />
              )}

              {tab === "baremes" && (
                <ResourceView
                  load={listerBaremes}
                  deps={[]}
                  columns={[{ key: "libelle" }, { key: "type_bareme", label: "Type" }, { key: "note_max" }, { key: "seuil_reussite" }]}
                  emptyTitle="Aucun barème"
                  actions={(reload) => (
                    <FormAction label="Nouveau barème" icon={PlusIcon} variant="primary" fields={baremeFields} modalSize="lg" onSubmit={creerBareme} successMessage="Barème créé" onDone={reload} />
                  )}
                  detailTitle={(r) => str(r.libelle)} />
              )}

              {tab === "types" && (
                <ResourceView
                  load={listerTypesEvaluation}
                  deps={[]}
                  columns={[{ key: "libelle" }, { key: "poids_dans_moyenne" }]}
                  emptyTitle="Aucun type d'évaluation"
                  actions={(reload) => (
                    <FormAction
                      label="Nouveau type"
                      icon={PlusIcon}
                      variant="primary"
                      fields={typeEvaluationFields}
                      onSubmit={creerTypeEvaluation}
                      successMessage="Type créé"
                      onDone={reload} />
                  )}
                  noDetail />
              )}

              {tab === "referentiel" && <ReferentielPanel />}
              {tab === "initialisation" && <InitialisationPanel />}
              {tab === "abonnement" && <AbonnementPanel />}
              
              {/* --- NOUVEL ONGLET APP MOBILE --- */}
              {tab === "mobile" && <MobileAppPromo />}
              
            </motion.div>
          </AnimatePresence>
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: pages/RegisterPage.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import {
  AlertCircleIcon,
  Building2Icon,
  CheckCircle2Icon,
  CheckIcon,
  ChevronLeftIcon,
  ChevronRightIcon,
  CrosshairIcon,
  EyeIcon,
  EyeOffIcon,
  GlobeIcon,
  LoaderCircleIcon,
  LockIcon,
  MailIcon,
  MapPinIcon,
  PhoneIcon,
  ShieldIcon,
  XIcon,
} from 'lucide-react';

import { authRegister } from '@/lib/api';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth';
import { EmailVerification } from '@/components/email_verification';
import RegisterMap from '@/components/register_map';

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   Types & constantes
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

interface FormState {
  nom: string;
  secteur: string;
  email_contact: string;
  telephone: string;
  adresse: string;
  site_web: string;
  description: string;
  verification_token: string | null;
  password: string;
  confirmPassword: string;
  secret_phrase: string;
  siege_lat: number | null;
  siege_lon: number | null;
}

const SECTORS = [
  'Technologies', 'Finance & Banque', 'Santé', 'Éducation',
  'Commerce & Retail', 'Industrie', 'Services',
  'Transport & Logistique', 'Immobilier', 'Autre',
];

const STEPS = [
  { id: 1, title: 'Informations', icon: Building2Icon, description: "Identité de l'établissement" },
  { id: 2, title: 'Sécurité', icon: ShieldIcon, description: 'Mot de passe et phrase secrète' },
  { id: 3, title: 'Localisation', icon: MapPinIcon, description: 'Position du siège' },
  { id: 4, title: 'Confirmation', icon: CheckIcon, description: 'Vérification finale' },
] as const;

const INITIAL: FormState = {
  nom: '',
  secteur: '',
  email_contact: '',
  telephone: '',
  adresse: '',
  site_web: '',
  description: '',
  verification_token: null,
  password: '',
  confirmPassword: '',
  secret_phrase: '',
  siege_lat: null,
  siege_lon: null,
};

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   Page
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

export default function RegisterPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { setAuth } = useAuth();

  const [step, setStep] = useState(1);
  const [form, setForm] = useState<FormState>(INITIAL);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [submitError, setSubmitError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState<any>(null);

  const [showPwd, setShowPwd] = useState(false);
  const [showConfirm, setShowConfirm] = useState(false);
  const [showSecret, setShowSecret] = useState(false);

  const [geoStatus, setGeoStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'loading' | 'granted' | 'denied'>('idle');

  /* ── Helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────── */

  const update = <K extends keyof FormState>(k: K, v: FormState[K]) => {
    setForm((p) => ({ ...p, [k]: v }));
    if (errors[k as string]) {
      setErrors((e) => {
        const n = { ...e };
        delete n[k as string];
        return n;
      });
    }
  };

  const requestGeo = useCallback(() => {
    if (!navigator.geolocation) {
      setGeoStatus('denied');
      return;
    }
    setGeoStatus('loading');
    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
      (pos) => {
        update('siege_lat', pos.coords.latitude);
        update('siege_lon', pos.coords.longitude);
        setGeoStatus('granted');
      },
      () => setGeoStatus('denied'),
      { enableHighAccuracy: true, timeout: 10000 },
    );
  }, []);

  const handleMapClick = useCallback((la: number, ln: number) => {
    setForm((p) => ({ ...p, siege_lat: la, siege_lon: ln }));
  }, []);

  /* ── Validation par étape ────────────────────────────── */

  const validate = (s: number): boolean => {
    const errs: Record<string, string> = {};

    if (s === 1) {
      if (!form.nom.trim()) errs.nom = 'Nom requis';
      if (!form.email_contact.trim()) {
        errs.email_contact = 'Email requis';
      } else if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(form.email_contact)) {
        errs.email_contact = 'Email invalide';
      }
      if (!form.verification_token) {
        errs.verification_token = 'Vérifiez votre email pour continuer';
      }
    }

    if (s === 2) {
      if (!form.password) errs.password = 'Mot de passe requis';
      else if (form.password.length < 6) errs.password = 'Minimum 6 caractères';
      if (form.password !== form.confirmPassword) {
        errs.confirmPassword = 'Les mots de passe ne correspondent pas';
      }
      if (!form.secret_phrase) errs.secret_phrase = 'Phrase secrète requise';
      else if (form.secret_phrase.length < 8) {
        errs.secret_phrase = 'Minimum 8 caractères';
      }
    }

    setErrors(errs);
    return Object.keys(errs).length === 0;
  };

  const next = () => {
    if (validate(step)) setStep((s) => Math.min(s + 1, 4));
  };

  const prev = () => setStep((s) => Math.max(s - 1, 1));

  /* ── Soumission ──────────────────────────────────────── */

  const submit = async () => {
    // Revalide les étapes critiques (l'utilisateur peut avoir navigué en arrière)
    if (!validate(1)) { setStep(1); return; }
    if (!validate(2)) { setStep(2); return; }

    setSubmitError(null);
    setSubmitting(true);

    try {
      const res = await authRegister({
        nom: form.nom.trim(),
        secteur: form.secteur,
        email_contact: form.email_contact.trim(),
        telephone: form.telephone,
        adresse: form.adresse,
        site_web: form.site_web,
        description: form.description,
        password: form.password,
        secret_phrase: form.secret_phrase,
        verification_token: form.verification_token as string,
        siege_lat: form.siege_lat,
        siege_lon: form.siege_lon,
      });

      // Si le backend renvoie un token direct, on connecte l'utilisateur
      const tok = res?.token || res?.access_token;
      const ent =
        res?.entreprise ??
        (res?.entreprise_id
          ? {
              id: res.entreprise_id,
              email: form.email_contact.trim(),
              nom: res?.nom || form.nom.trim(),
              rhEmail: res?.rh_email || undefined,
            }
          : null);
      if (tok && ent) setAuth(tok, ent);

      setSuccess(res);
      toast.success('Inscription réussie');
    } catch (e: any) {
      setSubmitError(e?.message || "Erreur lors de l'inscription");
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  /* ── Écran de succès ─────────────────────────────────── */

  if (success) {
    const keys: string[] = Array.isArray(success.recovery_keys) ? success.recovery_keys : [];
    return (
      <div className="ecole_m ecole_m-desktop min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center p-6">
        <div className="ecole_m-card max-w-lg w-full p-8">
          <div className="text-center mb-6">
            <span className="inline-grid h-12 w-12 place-items-center rounded-full bg-[var(--em-success)] text-white">
              <CheckCircle2Icon className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden />
            </span>
            <h2 className="ecole_m-display text-[22px] font-semibold mt-4 text-[#1A1A1A]">
              Inscription réussie
            </h2>
            <p className="text-[13px] text-[#5D5D5D] mt-1">
              Conservez précieusement ces clés de récupération.
            </p>
          </div>

          {keys.length > 0 && (
            <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[#F9F9F9] p-4 mb-5">
              <ul className="list-disc list-inside text-[12.5px] font-mono text-[#1A1A1A] space-y-1">
                {keys.map((k, i) => (
                  <li key={i}>{k}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="space-y-2 text-[13px] text-[#5D5D5D]">
            {success.rh_email && (
              <p>
                Email RH :{' '}
                <strong className="text-[#1A1A1A]">{success.rh_email}</strong>
              </p>
            )}
            {success.slug && (
              <p>
                Slug :{' '}
                <strong className="text-[#1A1A1A] font-mono">{success.slug}</strong>
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          <p className="text-[12px] text-[var(--em-danger)] mt-4">
            ⚠ Ces clés ne seront plus affichées.
          </p>

          <button
            onClick={() => navigate('/login', { replace: true })}
            className="ecole_m-btn ecole_m-btn--primary w-full mt-6 justify-center"
          >
            Accéder à la connexion
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  /* ── Formulaire multi-étapes ─────────────────────────── */

  return (
    <div className="ecole_m ecole_m-desktop min-h-screen flex">
      {/* ── Sidebar étapes ──────────────────────────────── */}
      <aside className="hidden lg:flex w-72 flex-col bg-[var(--em-surface)] border-r border-[var(--em-border)]">
        <div className="p-6 border-b border-[var(--em-border)]">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <span className="grid h-9 w-9 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-[var(--em-accent)] text-white shadow-[inset_0_1px_0_rgba(255,255,255,0.2),0_2px_6px_rgba(0,0,0,0.12)]">
              <Building2Icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
            </span>
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-[16px] font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A]">Inscription</h1>
              <p className="text-[12px] text-[#8A8A8A]">Créez votre espace</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <nav className="flex-1 p-4 space-y-1">
          {STEPS.map((s) => {
            const Icon = s.icon;
            const active = step === s.id;
            const done = step > s.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={s.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  if (done || active) setStep(s.id);
                }}
                disabled={!done && !active}
                className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-2.5 rounded-lg text-left transition-colors"
                style={{
                  background: active ? 'var(--em-accent-soft)' : undefined,
                  border: active
                    ? '1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--em-accent) 35%, transparent)'
                    : '1px solid transparent',
                  opacity: !done && !active ? 0.4 : 1,
                }}
              >
                <span
                  className="grid h-9 w-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg"
                  style={{
                    background: done ? 'var(--em-success)' : active ? 'var(--em-accent)' : '#F0F0F0',
                    color: done || active ? '#fff' : '#8A8A8A',
                  }}
                >
                  {done ? <CheckIcon className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Icon className="h-4 w-4" />}
                </span>
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <p className={`text-[13px] font-semibold ${active ? 'text-[#1A1A1A]' : 'text-[#5D5D5D]'}`}>
                    {s.title}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[11.5px] text-[#8A8A8A] truncate">{s.description}</p>
                </div>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        <div className="p-4 border-t border-[var(--em-border)]">
          <div className="rounded-lg bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] p-3 text-[12px] text-[#5D5D5D]">
            <ShieldIcon className="inline h-3.5 w-3.5 mr-1.5 text-[var(--em-accent)]" />
            Vos données sont chiffrées et protégées par une phrase secrète unique.
          </div>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* ── Contenu ─────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0">
        {/* Header mobile */}
        <div className="lg:hidden p-4 bg-[var(--em-surface)] border-b border-[var(--em-border)]">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <h1 className="text-[18px] font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A]">Inscription</h1>
            <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] text-[var(--em-accent)]">
              Étape {step}/4
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-1 mt-3">
            {STEPS.map((s) => (
              <div
                key={s.id}
                className="h-1 flex-1 rounded-full"
                style={{ background: s.id <= step ? 'var(--em-accent)' : 'var(--em-border)' }}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Contenu scrollable */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto p-6 lg:p-10">
          <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto space-y-5">
            {submitError && (
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg bg-[var(--em-danger-soft)] border border-[var(--em-danger)]/30 px-4 py-3 text-[13px] text-[var(--em-danger)]">
                <AlertCircleIcon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <span className="flex-1">{submitError}</span>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setSubmitError(null)}
                  aria-label="Fermer"
                  className="shrink-0"
                >
                  <XIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                </button>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* ═══ ÉTAPE 1 ═════════════════════════════ */}
            {step === 1 && (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="ecole_m-display text-[20px] font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A] tracking-[-0.01em]">
                    Informations de l'établissement
                  </h2>
                  <p className="text-[13px] text-[#5D5D5D] mt-1">
                    Renseignez les informations de base et vérifiez votre email.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <Field label="Nom de l'établissement" required error={errors.nom}>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Building2Icon className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-[#8A8A8A]" />
                    <input
                      value={form.nom}
                      onChange={(e) => update('nom', e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="École Les Palmiers"
                      className="ecole_m-input pl-10"
                    />
                  </div>
                </Field>

                <Field label="Secteur d'activité">
                  <select
                    value={form.secteur}
                    onChange={(e) => update('secteur', e.target.value)}
                    className="ecole_m-input"
                  >
                    <option value="">Sélectionnez un secteur</option>
                    {SECTORS.map((s) => (
                      <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </Field>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                  <Field label="Email de contact" required error={errors.email_contact}>
                    <div className="relative">
                      <MailIcon className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-[#8A8A8A]" />
                      <input
                        type="email"
                        value={form.email_contact}
                        onChange={(e) => {
                          update('email_contact', e.target.value);
                          // Changer l'email invalide le token → re-vérification obligatoire
                          update('verification_token', null);
                        }}
                        placeholder="contact@etablissement.com"
                        className="ecole_m-input pl-10"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </Field>

                  <Field label="Téléphone">
                    <div className="relative">
                      <PhoneIcon className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-[#8A8A8A]" />
                      <input
                        type="tel"
                        value={form.telephone}
                        onChange={(e) => update('telephone', e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="+221 77 123 45 67"
                        className="ecole_m-input pl-10"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </Field>
                </div>

                <EmailVerification
                  email={form.email_contact}
                  verificationToken={form.verification_token}
                  onVerified={(token) => update('verification_token', token)}
                />
                {errors.verification_token && (
                  <p className="text-[12px] text-[var(--em-danger)] -mt-2">
                    {errors.verification_token}
                  </p>
                )}

                <Field label="Adresse du siège">
                  <div className="relative">
                    <MapPinIcon className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-[#8A8A8A]" />
                    <input
                      value={form.adresse}
                      onChange={(e) => update('adresse', e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="123 Rue de la Paix, Dakar"
                      className="ecole_m-input pl-10"
                    />
                  </div>
                </Field>

                <Field label="Site web">
                  <div className="relative">
                    <GlobeIcon className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-[#8A8A8A]" />
                    <input
                      type="url"
                      value={form.site_web}
                      onChange={(e) => update('site_web', e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="https://www.etablissement.com"
                      className="ecole_m-input pl-10"
                    />
                  </div>
                </Field>

                <Field label="Description">
                  <textarea
                    value={form.description}
                    onChange={(e) => update('description', e.target.value)}
                    rows={3}
                    placeholder="Décrivez brièvement votre établissement…"
                    className="ecole_m-input min-h-[90px] py-2"
                  />
                </Field>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* ═══ ÉTAPE 2 ═════════════════════════════ */}
            {step === 2 && (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="ecole_m-display text-[20px] font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A] tracking-[-0.01em]">
                    Sécurité du compte
                  </h2>
                  <p className="text-[13px] text-[#5D5D5D] mt-1">
                    Définissez un mot de passe fort et une phrase secrète.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <Field label="Mot de passe" required error={errors.password}>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <LockIcon className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-[#8A8A8A]" />
                    <input
                      type={showPwd ? 'text' : 'password'}
                      value={form.password}
                      onChange={(e) => update('password', e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Minimum 6 caractères"
                      className="ecole_m-input pl-10 pr-10"
                    />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setShowPwd((v) => !v)}
                      className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#8A8A8A] hover:text-[#1A1A1A]"
                      aria-label={showPwd ? 'Masquer' : 'Afficher'}
                    >
                      {showPwd ? <EyeOffIcon className="h-4 w-4" /> : <EyeIcon className="h-4 w-4" />}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </Field>

                <Field label="Confirmer le mot de passe" required error={errors.confirmPassword}>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <LockIcon className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-[#8A8A8A]" />
                    <input
                      type={showConfirm ? 'text' : 'password'}
                      value={form.confirmPassword}
                      onChange={(e) => update('confirmPassword', e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Retapez votre mot de passe"
                      className="ecole_m-input pl-10 pr-10"
                    />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setShowConfirm((v) => !v)}
                      className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#8A8A8A] hover:text-[#1A1A1A]"
                      aria-label={showConfirm ? 'Masquer' : 'Afficher'}
                    >
                      {showConfirm ? <EyeOffIcon className="h-4 w-4" /> : <EyeIcon className="h-4 w-4" />}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </Field>

                <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[#F9F9F9] p-4 text-[12.5px] text-[#5D5D5D]">
                  <p className="font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A] flex items-center gap-2 mb-1.5">
                    <ShieldIcon className="h-4 w-4 text-[var(--em-accent)]" />
                    Phrase secrète
                  </p>
                  <p>
                    Requise pour les actions sensibles (gestion des IPs, rotation des clés,
                    réinitialisation de mot de passe).
                  </p>
                </div>

                <Field label="Phrase secrète" required error={errors.secret_phrase}>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <ShieldIcon className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-[#8A8A8A]" />
                    <input
                      type={showSecret ? 'text' : 'password'}
                      value={form.secret_phrase}
                      onChange={(e) => update('secret_phrase', e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Une phrase mémorable (min. 8 caractères)"
                      className="ecole_m-input pl-10 pr-10"
                    />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setShowSecret((v) => !v)}
                      className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#8A8A8A] hover:text-[#1A1A1A]"
                      aria-label={showSecret ? 'Masquer' : 'Afficher'}
                    >
                      {showSecret ? <EyeOffIcon className="h-4 w-4" /> : <EyeIcon className="h-4 w-4" />}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-[11.5px] text-[#8A8A8A] mt-1">
                    Exemple : « Le soleil brille sur les montagnes enneigées »
                  </p>
                </Field>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* ═══ ÉTAPE 3 ═════════════════════════════ */}
            {step === 3 && (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="ecole_m-display text-[20px] font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A] tracking-[-0.01em]">
                    Localisation du siège
                  </h2>
                  <p className="text-[13px] text-[#5D5D5D] mt-1">
                    Optionnel — sert de point de référence pour le pointage géolocalisé.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-accent)]/30 bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] p-4">
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                    <MapPinIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-[var(--em-accent)] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <div className="text-[12.5px] text-[#5D5D5D]">
                      <p className="font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A] mb-1">Point de référence</p>
                      <p>La position du siège sert de repère pour le pointage des employés.</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={requestGeo}
                    disabled={geoStatus === 'loading'}
                    className="ecole_m-btn ecole_m-btn--primary ecole_m-btn--sm"
                  >
                    {geoStatus === 'loading' ? (
                      <LoaderCircleIcon className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
                    ) : (
                      <CrosshairIcon className="h-4 w-4" />
                    )}
                    Utiliser ma position
                  </button>
                  {geoStatus === 'granted' && (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-success-soft)] px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-[var(--em-success)]">
                      <CheckCircle2Icon className="h-3 w-3" /> Géolocalisation activée
                    </span>
                  )}
                  {geoStatus === 'denied' && (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-danger-soft)] px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-[var(--em-danger)]">
                      <AlertCircleIcon className="h-3 w-3" /> Refusée
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[var(--em-surface)] p-4">
                  <p className="text-[12.5px] text-[#5D5D5D] mb-3">
                    Cliquez sur la carte pour choisir l'emplacement du siège :
                  </p>
                  <RegisterMap
                    lat={form.siege_lat}
                    lng={form.siege_lon}
                    onMapClick={handleMapClick}
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                  <Field label="Latitude">
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      step="0.000001"
                      value={form.siege_lat ?? ''}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        update('siege_lat', e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : null)
                      }
                      placeholder="14.7167"
                      className="ecole_m-input font-mono"
                    />
                  </Field>
                  <Field label="Longitude">
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      step="0.000001"
                      value={form.siege_lon ?? ''}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        update('siege_lon', e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : null)
                      }
                      placeholder="-17.4677"
                      className="ecole_m-input font-mono"
                    />
                  </Field>
                </div>

                {form.siege_lat !== null && form.siege_lon !== null && (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[#F9F9F9] p-3 text-[12.5px]">
                    <MapPinIcon className="h-4 w-4 text-[var(--em-accent)]" />
                    <span className="font-mono text-[#1A1A1A]">
                      {form.siege_lat.toFixed(6)}, {form.siege_lon.toFixed(6)}
                    </span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        update('siege_lat', null);
                        update('siege_lon', null);
                      }}
                      className="ml-auto text-[#5D5D5D] hover:text-[var(--em-danger)]"
                      aria-label="Effacer les coordonnées"
                    >
                      <XIcon className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* ═══ ÉTAPE 4 ═════════════════════════════ */}
            {step === 4 && (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="ecole_m-display text-[20px] font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A] tracking-[-0.01em]">
                    Confirmation
                  </h2>
                  <p className="text-[13px] text-[#5D5D5D] mt-1">
                    Vérifiez vos informations avant de finaliser l'inscription.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <SummarySection title="Informations établissement" icon={Building2Icon}>
                  <SummaryRow label="Nom" value={form.nom} />
                  <SummaryRow label="Secteur" value={form.secteur} />
                  <SummaryRow
                    label="Email"
                    value={form.email_contact}
                    verified={!!form.verification_token}
                  />
                  <SummaryRow label="Téléphone" value={form.telephone} />
                </SummarySection>

                <SummarySection title="Sécurité" icon={ShieldIcon}>
                  <div className="col-span-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-success-soft)] px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-[var(--em-success)]">
                      <CheckCircle2Icon className="h-3 w-3" /> Mot de passe défini
                    </span>
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-success-soft)] px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-[var(--em-success)]">
                      <CheckCircle2Icon className="h-3 w-3" /> Phrase secrète définie
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </SummarySection>

                <SummarySection title="Localisation" icon={MapPinIcon}>
                  <div className="col-span-2">
                    {form.siege_lat !== null && form.siege_lon !== null ? (
                      <span className="font-mono text-[12.5px] text-[#1A1A1A]">
                        {form.siege_lat.toFixed(4)}, {form.siege_lon.toFixed(4)}
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-[12.5px] text-[#8A8A8A]">
                        Non définie (optionnel)
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </SummarySection>

                <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-accent)]/30 bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] p-4">
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                    <ShieldIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-[var(--em-accent)] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <div className="text-[12.5px] text-[#5D5D5D]">
                      <p className="font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A] mb-1">
                        Après l'inscription
                      </p>
                      <p>
                        Vous recevrez vos clés de récupération (8 clés à usage unique) et
                        pourrez télécharger votre clé de chiffrement une seule fois.
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Footer navigation */}
        <div className="p-4 lg:p-6 bg-[var(--em-surface)] border-t border-[var(--em-border)]">
          <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto flex items-center justify-between">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={prev}
              disabled={step === 1}
              className="ecole_m-btn ecole_m-btn--subtle ecole_m-btn--sm disabled:opacity-30"
            >
              <ChevronLeftIcon className="h-4 w-4" />
              Précédent
            </button>

            {step < 4 ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={next}
                className="ecole_m-btn ecole_m-btn--primary ecole_m-btn--sm"
              >
                Suivant
                <ChevronRightIcon className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
            ) : (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={submit}
                disabled={submitting}
                className="ecole_m-btn ecole_m-btn--primary ecole_m-btn--sm"
              >
                {submitting ? (
                  <LoaderCircleIcon className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <CheckIcon className="h-4 w-4" />
                )}
                {submitting ? 'Création…' : 'Créer mon compte'}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto mt-3 text-center text-[12px] text-[#5D5D5D]">
            Déjà un compte ?{' '}
            <Link to="/login" className="text-[var(--em-accent)] font-semibold hover:underline">
              Se connecter
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   Petits composants locaux
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

function Field({
  label,
  required,
  error,
  children,
}: {
  label: string;
  required?: boolean;
  error?: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label className="ecole_m-label">
        {label}
        {required && <span className="text-[var(--em-danger)] ml-0.5">*</span>}
      </label>
      {children}
      {error && <p className="text-[12px] text-[var(--em-danger)] mt-1">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

function SummarySection({
  title,
  icon: Icon,
  children,
}: {
  title: string;
  icon: typeof Building2Icon;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[var(--em-surface)] p-4">
      <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] font-semibold text-[#1A1A1A] mb-3">
        <Icon className="h-4 w-4 text-[var(--em-accent)]" /> {title}
      </p>
      <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-[13px]">{children}</dl>
    </div>
  );
}

function SummaryRow({
  label,
  value,
  verified,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  verified?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <dt className="text-[11.5px] text-[#8A8A8A]">{label}</dt>
      <dd className="font-medium text-[#1A1A1A] flex items-center gap-1.5 break-all">
        {value || '—'}
        {verified && (
          <CheckCircle2Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-[var(--em-success)] shrink-0" />
        )}
      </dd>
    </div>
  );
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: pages/Registres.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { CalendarXIcon, FileDownIcon, FileSpreadsheetIcon, RefreshCwIcon, TriangleAlertIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { exporterFinanceEleve, getStatutRegistres, synchroRegistresEtAttendre } from "../lib/api_ecole";
import type { TypeRegistre } from "../types/ui";
import { useAsync } from "../hooks/useAsync";
import { useSchool } from "../contexts/SchoolContext";
import { ORDRE_REGISTRES } from "../data/registres";
import { exportFinanceEleveFields, synchroRegistresFields } from "../data/fields/registres";
import { PageHeader } from "../components/layout/PageHeader";
import { PageStage, Reveal, TabStage } from "../components/ui/Motion";
import { EmptyState } from "../components/data/States";
import { FormAction } from "../components/form/FormAction";
import { CommandBar } from "../components/eleves/EleveMotion";
import { RegistresMatrice } from "../components/registres/RegistresMatrice";
import { RegistreEditeur } from "../components/registres/RegistreEditeur";
import { saveFile } from "../utils/files";
import { str } from "../utils/format";

const COLOR = "#217346";

export function Registres() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const { anneeId } = useSchool();
  const { data, loading, error, reload } = useAsync(() => getStatutRegistres(), []);

  const classeId = params.get("classe") ?? "";
  const typeParam = params.get("type");
  const type = ORDRE_REGISTRES.find((t) => t === typeParam) ?? null;
  const enEdition = !!data?.annee && !!classeId && !!type && data.classes.some((c) => c.classe_id === classeId);

  const ouvrir = (c: string, t: TypeRegistre) => setParams({ classe: c, type: t });
  const fermer = () => setParams({});
  const optionsClasses = (data?.classes ?? []).map((c) => ({ value: c.classe_id, label: c.libelle }));

  return (
    <PageStage>
      <Reveal>
        <PageHeader
          icon={FileSpreadsheetIcon}
          color={COLOR}
          title="Registres"
          subtitle={data?.annee ? `Classeurs Excel des classes — ${data.annee}` : "Classeurs Excel des classes"}
        />
      </Reveal>
      <Reveal>
        {loading && !data ? (
          <div className="space-y-2" aria-busy="true" aria-label="Chargement des registres">
            {Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="h-16 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-black/[0.04]" />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : error && !data ? (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <EmptyState icon={TriangleAlertIcon} title="Impossible de charger les registres" text={error} />
            <div className="flex justify-center">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={reload}
                className="inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md bg-[var(--em-accent)] px-3 text-[13px] font-medium text-white transition-opacity duration-150 hover:opacity-90"
              >
                <RefreshCwIcon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
                Réessayer
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : data && !data.annee ? (
          <EmptyState icon={CalendarXIcon} title="Aucune année scolaire active" text="Les registres sont tenus pour l'année scolaire active." />
        ) : data ? (
          <TabStage id={enEdition ? "editeur" : "apercu"}>
            {enEdition && type ? (
              <RegistreEditeur
                classeId={classeId}
                type={type}
                classes={data.classes}
                accent={COLOR}
                onChanger={ouvrir}
                onFermer={fermer}
                onStatutChange={reload}
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <CommandBar hint="Les registres couvrent l'année scolaire active">
                  <FormAction
                    label="Synchroniser les registres"
                    icon={RefreshCwIcon}
                    size="sm"
                    variant="primary"
                    description="Recalcule les classeurs depuis la base. Une classe en échec ne bloque pas les autres."
                    fields={synchroRegistresFields(optionsClasses)}
                    submitLabel="Synchroniser"
                    successMessage="Synchronisation terminée"
                    showResult
                    onSubmit={(b) =>
                      synchroRegistresEtAttendre({
                        classe_id: str(b.classe_id) || undefined,
                        complet: b.complet === true,
                        types: Array.isArray(b.types) && b.types.length ? (b.types as TypeRegistre[]) : undefined
                      })
                    }
                    onDone={reload}
                  />
                  <FormAction
                    label="Exporter la finance d'un élève"
                    icon={FileDownIcon}
                    size="sm"
                    description="Fichier Excel calculé à la demande, jamais stocké."
                    fields={exportFinanceEleveFields(anneeId)}
                    submitLabel="Exporter"
                    successMessage="Export téléchargé"
                    onSubmit={async (b) => {
                      const f = await exporterFinanceEleve(str(b.eleve_id), str(b.annee_scolaire_id) || undefined);
                      saveFile(f);
                      return f.filename;
                    }}
                  />
                </CommandBar>
                <RegistresMatrice statut={data} onOuvrir={ouvrir} onActualiser={reload} />
              </div>
            )}
          </TabStage>
        ) : null}
      </Reveal>
    </PageStage>
  );
}



################################################################################
# FICHIER: pages/pedagogie/Analyses.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { ActivityIcon, PresentationIcon, RefreshCwIcon, SchoolIcon, TrendingUpIcon, UsersRoundIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { getClassementClasses, getClassementCombinaisonsProfs, getClassementEnseignants, recalculerCombinaisonsProfs, recalculerStatsClasse } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { PageHeader } from "../../components/layout/PageHeader";
import { ResourceView } from "../../components/layout/ResourceView";
import { WithAnnee } from "../../components/layout/WithAnnee";
import { ContextBar } from "../../components/layout/ContextBar";
import { Tabs } from "../../components/ui/Tabs";
import { LoadPicker } from "../../components/ui/LoadPicker";
import { PageStage, Reveal, TabStage } from "../../components/ui/Motion";
import { AsyncButton } from "../../components/form/AsyncButton";
import { FIABILITE, TRI_ENSEIGNANTS } from "../../data/enums";
import { fromEnum } from "../../utils/options";
import { str } from "../../utils/format";

const COLOR = "#3A5BD9";

export function Analyses() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const tab = params.get("tab") ?? "enseignants";
  const [tri, setTri] = useState("");
  const [fiab, setFiab] = useState("");

  return (
    <PageStage>
      <Reveal>
        <PageHeader icon={TrendingUpIcon} color={COLOR} title="Analyses" subtitle="Classements et indicateurs de réussite" />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <Tabs
          tabs={[
          { id: "enseignants", label: "Enseignants", icon: PresentationIcon },
          { id: "classes", label: "Classes", icon: SchoolIcon },
          { id: "equipes", label: "Équipes d'enseignants", icon: UsersRoundIcon },
          { id: "absences", label: "Absentéisme", icon: ActivityIcon }]
          }
          value={tab}
          onChange={(t) => setParams({ tab: t })} />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <WithAnnee>
          {(anneeId) =>
          <TabStage id={tab}>
              {tab === "enseignants" &&
            <>
                  <ContextBar
                icon={PresentationIcon}
                color={COLOR}
                title="Classement des enseignants"
                text="Triez selon le score de performance, la moyenne des élèves ou le taux de réussite.">
                    <LoadPicker label="Trier par" value={tri} onChange={setTri} options={fromEnum(TRI_ENSEIGNANTS)} placeholder="Par défaut" clearable />
                  </ContextBar>
                  <ResourceView
                load={() => getClassementEnseignants(anneeId, tri || undefined)}
                deps={[anneeId, tri]}
                emptyTitle="Aucune statistique"
                emptyText="Recalculez les statistiques depuis la fiche d'un enseignant."
                detailTitle={(r) => str(r.nom_complet) || "Enseignant"} />
                </>
            }

              {tab === "classes" &&
            <>
                  <ContextBar
                icon={SchoolIcon}
                color={COLOR}
                title="Classement des classes"
                text="Utilisez le bouton de recalcul d'une ligne pour actualiser les indicateurs de la classe." />
                  <ResourceView
                load={() => getClassementClasses(anneeId)}
                deps={[anneeId]}
                emptyTitle="Aucune statistique"
                detailTitle={(r) => str(r.classe_libelle || r.libelle) || "Classe"}
                rowActions={(r, reload) =>
                <AsyncButton
                  label="Recalculer"
                  icon={RefreshCwIcon}
                  iconOnly
                  size="sm"
                  variant="subtle"
                  successMessage="Statistiques mises à jour"
                  onClick={async () => {await recalculerStatsClasse(str(r.classe_id), anneeId);reload();}} />
                } />
                </>
            }

              {tab === "equipes" &&
            <>
                  <ContextBar
                icon={UsersRoundIcon}
                color={COLOR}
                title="Classement des équipes d'enseignants"
                text="Filtrez selon la fiabilité minimale de l'analyse.">
                    <LoadPicker label="Fiabilité minimale" value={fiab} onChange={setFiab} options={fromEnum(FIABILITE)} placeholder="Toutes" clearable />
                  </ContextBar>
                  <ResourceView
                load={() => getClassementCombinaisonsProfs(fiab || undefined)}
                deps={[fiab]}
                actions={(reload) =>
                <AsyncButton
                  label="Recalculer"
                  icon={RefreshCwIcon}
                  successMessage="Classement mis à jour"
                  onClick={async () => {await recalculerCombinaisonsProfs();reload();}} />
                }
                emptyTitle="Aucune équipe analysée"
                detailTitle={() => "Équipe d'enseignants"} />
                </>
            }

              {tab === "absences"}
            </TabStage>
          }
        </WithAnnee>
      </Reveal>
    </PageStage>);
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: pages/pedagogie/Bulletins.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { EyeIcon, FileTextIcon, ListOrderedIcon, ScrollTextIcon, SendIcon, SendToBackIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { getApercuBulletin, getBulletinPdf, getClassementClasse, listerBulletins, listerEleves, publierBulletin, publierBulletinsClasseEtAttendre } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { PageHeader } from "../../components/layout/PageHeader";
import { ResourceView } from "../../components/layout/ResourceView";
import { WithAnnee } from "../../components/layout/WithAnnee";
import { ContextBar } from "../../components/layout/ContextBar";
import { Tabs } from "../../components/ui/Tabs";
import { ClassePicker, PeriodePicker } from "../../components/ui/ClassePicker";
import { PageStage, Reveal, TabStage } from "../../components/ui/Motion";
import { EmptyState } from "../../components/data/States";
import { PersonCell } from "../../components/data/PersonCell";
import { RankBadge } from "../../components/data/RankBadge";
import { AsyncButton } from "../../components/form/AsyncButton";
import { FormAction } from "../../components/form/FormAction";
import { eleveOnlyField } from "../../data/fields/pedagogie";
import { useLookup } from "../../hooks/useLookup";
import { F } from "../../utils/fieldBuilders";
import { openFile } from "../../utils/files";
import { fullName, str } from "../../utils/format";

const COLOR = "#E3008C";

export function Bulletins() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const tab = params.get("tab") ?? "bulletins";
  const [classeId, setClasseId] = useState("");
  const [periodeId, setPeriodeId] = useState("");
  const ready = !!classeId && !!periodeId;
  const eleves = useLookup(() => listerEleves(), fullName);
  const ctx = { classe_id: classeId, periode_id: periodeId };
  const waiting =
  <EmptyState icon={ScrollTextIcon} title="Choisissez une classe et une période" text="Les bulletins et le classement de la classe s'afficheront ici." />;

  return (
    <PageStage>
      <Reveal>
        <PageHeader icon={ScrollTextIcon} color={COLOR} title="Bulletins" subtitle="Aperçu, publication et classement" />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <Tabs
          tabs={[
          { id: "bulletins", label: "Bulletins", icon: FileTextIcon },
          { id: "classement", label: "Classement", icon: ListOrderedIcon }]
          }
          value={tab}
          onChange={(t) => setParams({ tab: t })} />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <WithAnnee>
          {(anneeId) =>
          <div className="space-y-4">
              <ContextBar
              icon={ScrollTextIcon}
              color={COLOR}
              title="Classe et période"
              text="La sélection s'applique aux bulletins et au classement."
              steps={[
              { label: "Classe", done: !!classeId },
              { label: "Période", done: !!periodeId }]
              }>
                <ClassePicker anneeId={anneeId} value={classeId} onChange={setClasseId} />
                <PeriodePicker anneeId={anneeId} value={periodeId} onChange={setPeriodeId} />
              </ContextBar>

              <TabStage id={tab}>
                {tab === "bulletins" ?
              <ResourceView
                load={() => listerBulletins(classeId, periodeId)}
                deps={[classeId, periodeId]}
                enabled={ready}
                disabledState={waiting}
                actions={(reload) =>
                ready ?
                <>
                          <FormAction
                    label="Aperçu d'un bulletin"
                    icon={EyeIcon}
                    fields={eleveOnlyField()}
                    submitLabel="Afficher"
                    successMessage="Aperçu calculé"
                    showResult
                    onSubmit={(b) => getApercuBulletin({ eleve_id: str(b.eleve_id), ...ctx })} />
                          <FormAction
                    label="Publier un bulletin"
                    icon={SendIcon}
                    fields={eleveOnlyField()}
                    submitLabel="Publier"
                    successMessage="Bulletin publié"
                    showResult
                    onSubmit={(b) => publierBulletin({ ...b, ...ctx })}
                    onDone={reload} />
                          <FormAction
                    label="Publier toute la classe"
                    icon={SendToBackIcon}
                    variant="primary"
                    fields={[F.bool("forcer", "Republier les bulletins déjà publiés")]}
                    submitLabel="Publier"
                    successMessage="Bulletins publiés"
                    showResult
                    onSubmit={(b) => publierBulletinsClasseEtAttendre({ ...ctx, forcer: b.forcer === true })}
                    onDone={reload} />
                        </> :
                null
                }
                columns={[
                { key: "eleve_id", label: "Élève", render: (r) => <PersonCell name={eleves.get(str(r.eleve_id)) ?? "Élève"} color={COLOR} /> },
                { key: "moyenne_generale", label: "Moyenne" },
                { key: "rang", render: (r) => <RankBadge value={r.rang} color={COLOR} /> },
                { key: "mention" },
                { key: "statut" }]
                }
                emptyTitle="Aucun bulletin pour cette période"
                detailTitle={(r) => eleves.get(str(r.eleve_id)) ?? "Bulletin"}
                rowActions={(r) =>
                <AsyncButton
                  label="Ouvrir le PDF"
                  icon={FileTextIcon}
                  iconOnly
                  size="sm"
                  variant="subtle"
                  onClick={async () => openFile(await getBulletinPdf(str(r.id)))} />
                } /> :

              <ResourceView
                load={() => getClassementClasse(classeId, periodeId)}
                deps={[classeId, periodeId]}
                enabled={ready}
                disabledState={waiting}
                rowKey={(r) => str(r.eleve_id)}
                columns={[
                { key: "rang", render: (r) => <RankBadge value={r.rang} color={COLOR} /> },
                { key: "nom", label: "Élève", render: (r) => <PersonCell name={fullName(r)} color={COLOR} /> },
                { key: "moyenne_generale", label: "Moyenne" }]
                }
                emptyTitle="Aucun classement disponible"
                detailTitle={fullName} />
              }
              </TabStage>
            </div>
          }
        </WithAnnee>
      </Reveal>
    </PageStage>);
}
