# FICHIER: pages/pedagogie/Classes.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { PlusIcon, SchoolIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { creerClasseDepuisReferentiel, listerClasses } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { PageHeader } from "../../components/layout/PageHeader";
import { ResourceView } from "../../components/layout/ResourceView";
import { WithAnnee } from "../../components/layout/WithAnnee";
import { PageStage, Reveal } from "../../components/ui/Motion";
import { FormAction } from "../../components/form/FormAction";
import { ClasseDetail } from "../../components/classes/ClasseDetail";
import { classeCreateFields } from "../../data/fields/pedagogie";
import { str } from "../../utils/format";

const COLOR = "#4F6BED";

export function Classes() {
  return (
    <PageStage>
      <Reveal>
        <PageHeader icon={SchoolIcon} color={COLOR} title="Classes" subtitle="Classes, matières, organisation et matériel" />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <WithAnnee>
          {(anneeId) =>
          <ResourceView
            load={() => listerClasses(anneeId)}
            deps={[anneeId]}
            columns={[
            {
              key: "libelle",
              label: "Classe",
              render: (r) =>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <span
                  className="grid h-8 w-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg"
                  style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${COLOR} 14%, transparent)`, color: COLOR }}>
                        <SchoolIcon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                      </span>
                      <span className="truncate font-medium">{str(r.libelle)}</span>
                    </span>
            },
            { key: "effectif_max", label: "Capacité" },
            { key: "salle_habituelle", label: "Salle" },
            { key: "type_organisation_edt_effectif", label: "Organisation" },
            { key: "actif" }]
            }
            actions={(reload) =>
            <FormAction
              label="Nouvelle classe"
              icon={PlusIcon}
              variant="primary"
              fields={classeCreateFields(anneeId)}
              onSubmit={creerClasseDepuisReferentiel}
              successMessage="Classe créée"
              showResult
              onDone={reload} />
            }
            emptyTitle="Aucune classe pour cette année"
            emptyIcon={SchoolIcon}
            wide
            detailTitle={(r) => str(r.libelle)}
            renderDetail={(r) => <ClasseDetail classe={r} anneeId={anneeId} />} />
          }
        </WithAnnee>
      </Reveal>
    </PageStage>);
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: pages/pedagogie/Devoirs.tsx
################################################################################

import React from "react";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { ListChecksIcon, NotebookPenIcon, PlusIcon, SchoolIcon, SendIcon } from "lucide-react";
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { creerDevoir } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { PageHeader } from "../../components/layout/PageHeader";
import { WithAnnee } from "../../components/layout/WithAnnee";
import { EASE_OUT, PageStage, Reveal } from "../../components/ui/Motion";
import { FormAction } from "../../components/form/FormAction";
import { devoirFields } from "../../data/fields/pedagogie";

const COLOR = "#B146C2";

const ETAPES: {icon: LucideIcon;title: string;text: string;}[] = [
{ icon: SchoolIcon, title: "Classe et matière", text: "Choisissez la classe, puis la matière concernée." },
{ icon: ListChecksIcon, title: "Consignes et échéance", text: "Rédigez les consignes, la date de remise et joignez un lien si besoin." },
{ icon: SendIcon, title: "Transmission", text: "Le devoir est transmis aux élèves de la classe et à leurs familles." }];

export function Devoirs() {
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();

  return (
    <PageStage>
      <Reveal>
        <PageHeader icon={NotebookPenIcon} color={COLOR} title="Devoirs" subtitle="Travail à faire à la maison" />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <WithAnnee>
          {(anneeId) =>
          <section className="ecole_m-card overflow-hidden" aria-labelledby="devoir-title">
              <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-[1.1fr_1fr]">
                <div className="flex flex-col justify-center gap-5 p-6 md:p-8">
                  <span
                  className="grid h-14 w-14 place-items-center rounded-2xl"
                  style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${COLOR} 14%, transparent)`, color: COLOR }}>
                    <NotebookPenIcon className="h-7 w-7" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </span>
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <h2 id="devoir-title" className="text-[22px] font-semibold leading-tight tracking-tight">Donner un devoir</h2>
                    <p className="ecole_m-muted max-w-md text-[14px] leading-relaxed">
                      Le devoir est transmis aux élèves de la classe et à leurs familles.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <FormAction
                    label="Nouveau devoir"
                    icon={PlusIcon}
                    variant="primary"
                    fields={devoirFields(anneeId)}
                    modalSize="lg"
                    onSubmit={creerDevoir}
                    successMessage="Devoir publié"
                    showResult />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div
                className="border-t border-[var(--em-border)] p-6 md:p-8 lg:border-l lg:border-t-0"
                style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${COLOR} 4%, transparent)` }}>
                  <h3 className="ecole_m-muted mb-5 text-[13px] font-medium">Comment ça se passe</h3>
                  <ol className="relative space-y-6">
                    <span
                    className="absolute bottom-4 left-[17px] top-4 w-px bg-[var(--em-border)]"
                    aria-hidden="true" />
                    {ETAPES.map((e, i) =>
                  <motion.li
                    key={e.title}
                    className="relative flex gap-4"
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, x: reduce ? 0 : -8 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                    transition={{ duration: 0.24, ease: EASE_OUT, delay: reduce ? 0 : 0.12 + i * 0.06 }}>
                        <span
                      className="relative z-10 grid h-9 w-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full border bg-[var(--em-surface,white)] text-[13px] font-semibold"
                      style={{ borderColor: `color-mix(in srgb, ${COLOR} 40%, transparent)`, color: COLOR }}>
                          {i + 1}
                        </span>
                        <div className="pt-1">
                          <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[14px] font-semibold">
                            <e.icon className="h-4 w-4" style={{ color: COLOR }} aria-hidden="true" />
                            {e.title}
                          </p>
                          <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-1 text-[13px] leading-relaxed">{e.text}</p>
                        </div>
                      </motion.li>
                  )}
                  </ol>
                </div>
              </div>
            </section>
          }
        </WithAnnee>
      </Reveal>
    </PageStage>);
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: pages/pedagogie/EmploiDuTemps.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { CalendarClockIcon, CalendarRangeIcon, ClockIcon, HistoryIcon, ListPlusIcon, PlusIcon, RepeatIcon, SearchCheckIcon, UserIcon, XIcon } from "lucide-react";
import {
  creerCreneau,
  creerCreneauxBulk,
  getEdtClasse,
  getEdtClasseADate,
  getEdtEnseignant,
  getHistoriqueEdtClasse,
  listerMatieresClasse,
  remplacerCreneau,
  verifierConflitsCreneau } from
"../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useAsync, useTick } from "../../hooks/useAsync";
import { PageHeader } from "../../components/layout/PageHeader";
import { ResourceView } from "../../components/layout/ResourceView";
import { WithAnnee } from "../../components/layout/WithAnnee";
import { ContextBar } from "../../components/layout/ContextBar";
import { PanelHeader } from "../../components/layout/PanelHeader";
import { Tabs } from "../../components/ui/Tabs";
import { ClassePicker } from "../../components/ui/ClassePicker";
import { LoadPicker } from "../../components/ui/LoadPicker";
import { PageStage, Reveal, TabStage } from "../../components/ui/Motion";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../../components/data/AsyncBlock";
import { EdtGrid } from "../../components/data/EdtGrid";
import { EmptyState } from "../../components/data/States";
import { FormAction } from "../../components/form/FormAction";
import { bulkFields, creneauFields, remplacerCreneauFields } from "../../data/fields/pedagogie";
import { optEnseignants } from "../../utils/options";
import { formatDate, formatNumber, obj, str } from "../../utils/format";

const COLOR = "#0099BC";

function ClasseActions({ classeId, onDone }: {classeId: string;onDone: () => void;}) {
  const { data } = useAsync(() => listerMatieresClasse(classeId), [classeId]);
  const matieres = (data ?? []).map((m) => ({ value: str(m.matiere_classe_id), label: str(m.matiere) }));
  return (
    <>
      <FormAction label="Vérifier un horaire" icon={SearchCheckIcon} fields={creneauFields(classeId, false)} submitLabel="Vérifier" successMessage="Vérification terminée" showResult onSubmit={verifierConflitsCreneau} />
      <FormAction label="Plusieurs créneaux" icon={ListPlusIcon} fields={bulkFields(matieres)} modalSize="xl" onSubmit={(b) => creerCreneauxBulk(classeId, b)} successMessage="Créneaux créés" showResult onDone={onDone} />
      <FormAction label="Nouveau créneau" icon={PlusIcon} variant="primary" fields={creneauFields(classeId)} onSubmit={creerCreneau} successMessage="Créneau créé" showResult onDone={onDone} />
    </>);
}

function Chip({ children }: {children: React.ReactNode;}) {
  return (
    <span
      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium"
      style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${COLOR} 12%, transparent)`, color: COLOR }}>
      {children}
    </span>);
}

export function EmploiDuTemps() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const tab = params.get("tab") ?? "classe";
  const [classeId, setClasseId] = useState("");
  const [dateRef, setDateRef] = useState("");
  const [ensId, setEnsId] = useState("");
  const [tick, bump] = useTick();

  return (
    <PageStage>
      <Reveal>
        <PageHeader icon={CalendarClockIcon} color={COLOR} title="Emploi du temps" subtitle="Créneaux des classes et plannings des enseignants" />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <Tabs
          tabs={[
          { id: "classe", label: "Par classe", icon: CalendarRangeIcon },
          { id: "historique", label: "Historique des changements", icon: HistoryIcon },
          { id: "enseignant", label: "Par enseignant", icon: UserIcon }]
          }
          value={tab}
          onChange={(t) => setParams({ tab: t })} />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <WithAnnee>
          {(anneeId) =>
          <TabStage id={tab}>
              {tab === "classe" &&
            <>
                  <ContextBar
                icon={CalendarRangeIcon}
                color={COLOR}
                title="Emploi du temps de la classe"
                text="Choisissez une date pour consulter l'emploi du temps tel qu'il était à ce jour-là."
                steps={[{ label: "Classe", done: !!classeId }]}
                actions={classeId ? <ClasseActions classeId={classeId} onDone={bump} /> : null}>
                    <ClassePicker anneeId={anneeId} value={classeId} onChange={setClasseId} />
                    <label className="w-full sm:w-48">
                      <span className="ecole_m-label">À la date du</span>
                      <input type="date" className="ecole_m-input" value={dateRef} onChange={(e) => setDateRef(e.target.value)} />
                    </label>
                  </ContextBar>

                  <div className="ecole_m-card overflow-hidden">
                    {classeId ?
                <>
                        <PanelHeader
                    icon={CalendarRangeIcon}
                    color={COLOR}
                    title="Grille hebdomadaire"
                    meta={
                    dateRef ?
                    <>
                                <Chip>Au {formatDate(dateRef)}</Chip>
                                <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setDateRef("")}
                        className="ecole_m-muted grid h-7 w-7 place-items-center rounded-md transition-colors duration-150 hover:bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--em-accent)_10%,transparent)]"
                        aria-label="Retirer la date">
                                  <XIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                                </button>
                              </> :
                    null
                    } />
                        <div className="p-5">
                          <AsyncBlock
                      load={() => dateRef ? getEdtClasseADate(classeId, dateRef) : getEdtClasse(classeId)}
                      deps={[classeId, dateRef, tick]}
                      isEmpty={() => false}>
                            {(d) => <EdtGrid grille={obj(d.grille ?? d)} />}
                          </AsyncBlock>
                        </div>
                      </> :
                <EmptyState icon={CalendarRangeIcon} title="Choisissez une classe" text="L'emploi du temps de la classe s'affichera ici." />
                }
                  </div>
                </>
            }

              {tab === "historique" &&
            <>
                  <ContextBar
                icon={HistoryIcon}
                color={COLOR}
                title="Historique des créneaux"
                text="Les créneaux en vigueur peuvent être modifiés à partir d'une date donnée."
                steps={[{ label: "Classe", done: !!classeId }]}>
                    <ClassePicker anneeId={anneeId} value={classeId} onChange={setClasseId} />
                  </ContextBar>
                  <ResourceView
                load={() => getHistoriqueEdtClasse(classeId)}
                deps={[classeId, tick]}
                enabled={!!classeId}
                disabledState={<EmptyState icon={HistoryIcon} title="Choisissez une classe" />}
                rowKey={(r) => str(r.creneau_id)}
                columns={[
                { key: "matiere" },
                { key: "jour_semaine", label: "Jour" },
                { key: "heure_debut" },
                { key: "heure_fin" },
                { key: "salle" },
                { key: "date_debut_validite", label: "Depuis" },
                { key: "actif_actuellement", label: "En vigueur" }]
                }
                emptyTitle="Aucun créneau"
                noDetail
                rowActions={(r, reload) =>
                r.actif_actuellement ?
                <FormAction
                  label="Modifier ce créneau"
                  icon={RepeatIcon}
                  iconOnly
                  size="sm"
                  variant="subtle"
                  fields={remplacerCreneauFields()}
                  onSubmit={(b) => remplacerCreneau(str(r.creneau_id), b)}
                  successMessage="Créneau modifié"
                  onDone={reload} /> :
                null
                } />
                </>
            }

              {tab === "enseignant" &&
            <>
                  <ContextBar
                icon={UserIcon}
                color={COLOR}
                title="Planning d'un enseignant"
                steps={[{ label: "Enseignant", done: !!ensId }]}>
                    <LoadPicker label="Enseignant" value={ensId} onChange={setEnsId} load={optEnseignants} searchable placeholder="Rechercher un enseignant" width="sm:w-80" />
                  </ContextBar>

                  <div className="ecole_m-card overflow-hidden">
                    {ensId ?
                <AsyncBlock load={() => getEdtEnseignant(ensId)} deps={[ensId]}>
                        {(d) =>
                  <>
                            <PanelHeader
                      icon={CalendarRangeIcon}
                      color={COLOR}
                      title="Planning hebdomadaire"
                      meta={
                      <Chip>
                                  <ClockIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                                  {formatNumber(d.total_heures_semaine)} heure(s) par semaine
                                </Chip>
                      } />
                            <div className="p-5">
                              <EdtGrid grille={obj(d.grille)} />
                            </div>
                          </>
                  }
                      </AsyncBlock> :
                <EmptyState icon={UserIcon} title="Choisissez un enseignant" text="Son planning de la semaine s'affichera ici." />
                }
                  </div>
                </>
            }
            </TabStage>
          }
        </WithAnnee>
      </Reveal>
    </PageStage>);
}

# FICHIER: pages/pedagogie/Notes.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { CalendarIcon, MessageSquareTextIcon, PenLineIcon, PlusIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { creerEvaluation, listerEleves, listerEvaluations, listerNotes, saisirAppreciation, saisirNote } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useTick } from "../../hooks/useAsync";
import { useLookup } from "../../hooks/useLookup";
import { PageHeader } from "../../components/layout/PageHeader";
import { ResourceView } from "../../components/layout/ResourceView";
import { WithAnnee } from "../../components/layout/WithAnnee";
import { ContextBar } from "../../components/layout/ContextBar";
import { Section } from "../../components/layout/Section";
import { ClassePicker, MatiereClassePicker, PeriodePicker } from "../../components/ui/ClassePicker";
import { PageStage, Reveal } from "../../components/ui/Motion";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../../components/data/AsyncBlock";
import { MiniTable } from "../../components/data/MiniTable";
import { EmptyState } from "../../components/data/States";
import { PersonCell } from "../../components/data/PersonCell";
import { FormAction } from "../../components/form/FormAction";
import { appreciationFields, evaluationFields, noteFields } from "../../data/fields/pedagogie";
import type { Row } from "../../types/ui";
import { formatDate, fullName, num, str } from "../../utils/format";

const COLOR = "#CA5010";

function Chip({ children }: {children: React.ReactNode;}) {
  return (
    <span
      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium"
      style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${COLOR} 12%, transparent)`, color: COLOR }}>
      {children}
    </span>);
}

function EvaluationNotes({ evaluation }: {evaluation: Row;}) {
  const id = str(evaluation.id);
  const [tick, bump] = useTick();
  const eleves = useLookup(() => listerEleves(), fullName);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        {str(evaluation.date_evaluation) ?
        <Chip>
            <CalendarIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
            {formatDate(evaluation.date_evaluation)}
          </Chip> :
        null}
        {str(evaluation.note_max) ? <Chip>Sur {str(evaluation.note_max)}</Chip> : null}
        {str(evaluation.coefficient_evaluation) ? <Chip>Coef. {str(evaluation.coefficient_evaluation)}</Chip> : null}
      </div>

      <Section
        title="Notes"
        actions={
        <FormAction
          label="Saisir une note"
          icon={PenLineIcon}
          size="sm"
          variant="primary"
          fields={noteFields(num(evaluation.note_max) ?? undefined)}
          onSubmit={(b) => saisirNote({ ...b, evaluation_id: id })}
          successMessage="Note enregistrée"
          onDone={bump} />
        }>
        <AsyncBlock load={() => listerNotes(id)} deps={[id, tick]} empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucune note saisie" />}>
          {(rows) =>
          <MiniTable
            rows={rows}
            columns={[
            { key: "eleve_id", label: "Élève", render: (r) => <PersonCell name={eleves.get(str(r.eleve_id)) ?? "Élève"} color={COLOR} /> },
            { key: "valeur_numerique", label: "Note" },
            { key: "absent" },
            { key: "commentaire" }]
            } />
          }
        </AsyncBlock>
      </Section>
    </div>);
}

export function Notes() {
  const [classeId, setClasseId] = useState("");
  const [mcId, setMcId] = useState("");
  const [periodeId, setPeriodeId] = useState("");
  const ready = !!mcId && !!periodeId;

  return (
    <PageStage>
      <Reveal>
        <PageHeader icon={PenLineIcon} color={COLOR} title="Notes" subtitle="Évaluations, notes et appréciations" />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <WithAnnee>
          {(anneeId) =>
          <div className="space-y-4">
              <ContextBar
              icon={PenLineIcon}
              color={COLOR}
              title="Classe, matière et période"
              text="Choisissez d'abord la classe pour afficher ses matières."
              steps={[
              { label: "Classe", done: !!classeId },
              { label: "Matière", done: !!mcId },
              { label: "Période", done: !!periodeId }]
              }>
                <ClassePicker anneeId={anneeId} value={classeId} onChange={(v) => {setClasseId(v);setMcId("");}} />
                <MatiereClassePicker classeId={classeId} value={mcId} onChange={setMcId} />
                <PeriodePicker anneeId={anneeId} value={periodeId} onChange={setPeriodeId} />
              </ContextBar>

              <ResourceView
              load={() => listerEvaluations(mcId, periodeId)}
              deps={[mcId, periodeId]}
              enabled={ready}
              disabledState={<EmptyState icon={PenLineIcon} title="Choisissez une classe, une matière et une période" />}
              actions={(reload) =>
              ready ?
              <>
                      <FormAction
                  label="Appréciation"
                  icon={MessageSquareTextIcon}
                  fields={appreciationFields}
                  onSubmit={(b) => saisirAppreciation({ ...b, matiere_classe_id: mcId, periode_id: periodeId })}
                  successMessage="Appréciation enregistrée" />
                      <FormAction
                  label="Nouvelle évaluation"
                  icon={PlusIcon}
                  variant="primary"
                  fields={evaluationFields()}
                  onSubmit={(b) => creerEvaluation({ ...b, matiere_classe_id: mcId, periode_id: periodeId })}
                  successMessage="Évaluation créée"
                  onDone={reload} />
                    </> :
              null
              }
              columns={[
              { key: "libelle", render: (r) => <span className="font-medium">{str(r.libelle)}</span> },
              { key: "date_evaluation", label: "Date" },
              { key: "note_max", label: "Sur" },
              { key: "coefficient_evaluation", label: "Coef." },
              { key: "statut" }]
              }
              emptyTitle="Aucune évaluation"
              detailTitle={(r) => str(r.libelle)}
              renderDetail={(r) => <EvaluationNotes evaluation={r} />} />
            </div>
          }
        </WithAnnee>
      </Reveal>
    </PageStage>);
}

# FICHIER: pages/pedagogie/Presences.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { CalendarCheckIcon, ClipboardListIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { faireAppel, getAppel, listerAppelsClasse } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { PageHeader } from "../../components/layout/PageHeader";
import { ResourceView } from "../../components/layout/ResourceView";
import { WithAnnee } from "../../components/layout/WithAnnee";
import { ContextBar } from "../../components/layout/ContextBar";
import { ClassePicker } from "../../components/ui/ClassePicker";
import { PageStage, Reveal } from "../../components/ui/Motion";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../../components/data/AsyncBlock";
import { ObjectView } from "../../components/data/ObjectView";
import { EmptyState } from "../../components/data/States";
import { FormAction } from "../../components/form/FormAction";
import { appelFields } from "../../data/fields/pedagogie";
import { formatDate, shiftISO, str, todayISO } from "../../utils/format";

const COLOR = "#107C10";

export function Presences() {
  const [classeId, setClasseId] = useState("");
  const [debut, setDebut] = useState(shiftISO(-14));
  const [fin, setFin] = useState(todayISO());

  return (
    <PageStage>
      <Reveal>
        <PageHeader icon={CalendarCheckIcon} color={COLOR} title="Présences" subtitle="Appels en classe et suivi des absences" />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <WithAnnee>
          {(anneeId) =>
          <div className="space-y-4">
              <ContextBar
              icon={ClipboardListIcon}
              color={COLOR}
              title="Appels de la classe"
              text="Choisissez une classe et une période pour consulter les appels effectués."
              steps={[{ label: "Classe", done: !!classeId }]}>
                <ClassePicker anneeId={anneeId} value={classeId} onChange={setClasseId} />
                <label className="w-full sm:w-44">
                  <span className="ecole_m-label">Du</span>
                  <input type="date" className="ecole_m-input" value={debut} onChange={(e) => setDebut(e.target.value)} />
                </label>
                <label className="w-full sm:w-44">
                  <span className="ecole_m-label">Au</span>
                  <input type="date" className="ecole_m-input" value={fin} onChange={(e) => setFin(e.target.value)} />
                </label>
              </ContextBar>

              <ResourceView
              load={() => listerAppelsClasse(classeId, { date_debut: debut || undefined, date_fin: fin || undefined })}
              deps={[classeId, debut, fin]}
              enabled={!!classeId}
              disabledState={<EmptyState icon={ClipboardListIcon} title="Choisissez une classe" text="Les appels effectués s'afficheront ici." />}
              actions={(reload) =>
              classeId ?
              <FormAction
                label="Faire l'appel"
                icon={ClipboardListIcon}
                variant="primary"
                fields={appelFields(classeId)}
                modalSize="xl"
                onSubmit={faireAppel}
                successMessage="Appel enregistré"
                showResult
                onDone={reload} /> :
              null
              }
              emptyTitle="Aucun appel sur cette période"
              detailTitle={(r) => `Appel du ${formatDate(r.date_appel)}`}
              renderDetail={(r) =>
              <AsyncBlock load={() => getAppel(str(r.id))} deps={[str(r.id)]}>
                    {(d) => <ObjectView data={d} />}
                  </AsyncBlock>
              } />
            </div>
          }
        </WithAnnee>
      </Reveal>
    </PageStage>);
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: pages/personnes/Admissions.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { ClipboardCheckIcon, GaugeIcon, InboxIcon, UserCheckIcon, UserPlusIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { convertirCandidature, creerCandidature, getCandidature, listerCandidatures, statuerCandidature } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { ModuleHero } from "../../components/layout/ModuleHero";
import { ResourceView } from "../../components/layout/ResourceView";
import { WithAnnee } from "../../components/layout/WithAnnee";
import { PivotTabs } from "../../components/ui/PivotTabs";
import { TabTransition } from "../../components/ui/TabTransition";
import { LoadPicker } from "../../components/ui/LoadPicker";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../../components/data/AsyncBlock";
import { ObjectView } from "../../components/data/ObjectView";
import { FormAction } from "../../components/form/FormAction";
import { RemplissageList } from "../../components/dashboard/RemplissageList";
import { candidatureFields, convertirFields, statuerFields } from "../../data/fields/personnel";
import { STATUT_CANDIDATURE } from "../../data/enums";
import { fromEnum } from "../../utils/options";
import { initials, str } from "../../utils/format";

const COLOR = "#DA3B01";

const nom = (r: Record<string, unknown>) => `${str(r.prenom_candidat)} ${str(r.nom_candidat)}`.trim();

export function Admissions() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const tab = params.get("tab") ?? "candidatures";
  const [statut, setStatut] = useState("");

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <ModuleHero icon={UserPlusIcon} color={COLOR} title="Admissions" subtitle="Candidatures et places disponibles" />

      <PivotTabs
        layoutKey="admissions"
        label="Rubriques des admissions"
        tabs={[
        { id: "candidatures", label: "Candidatures", icon: InboxIcon },
        { id: "places", label: "Places disponibles", icon: GaugeIcon }]
        }
        value={tab}
        onChange={(t) => setParams({ tab: t })} />

      <WithAnnee>
        {(anneeId) =>
        <TabTransition id={tab}>
            {tab === "candidatures" ?
          <ResourceView
            load={() => listerCandidatures(statut || undefined)}
            deps={[statut]}
            columns={[
            {
              key: "nom_candidat",
              label: "Candidat",
              render: (r) =>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <span
                  className="grid h-8 w-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full text-[12px] font-semibold"
                  style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${COLOR} 14%, transparent)`, color: COLOR }}>
                        {initials(nom(r))}
                      </span>
                      <span className="font-medium">{nom(r)}</span>
                    </span>
            },
            { key: "date_naissance" },
            { key: "nom_parent", label: "Parent" },
            { key: "telephone_parent", label: "Téléphone" },
            { key: "statut" }]
            }
            toolbar={<LoadPicker label="Statut" value={statut} onChange={setStatut} options={fromEnum(STATUT_CANDIDATURE)} placeholder="Tous" clearable />}
            actions={(reload) =>
            <FormAction label="Nouvelle candidature" icon={UserPlusIcon} variant="primary" fields={candidatureFields(anneeId)} modalSize="lg" onSubmit={creerCandidature} successMessage="Candidature enregistrée" onDone={reload} />
            }
            emptyTitle="Aucune candidature"
            detailTitle={nom}
            renderDetail={(r, ctx) =>
            <div className="space-y-5">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--em-accent)_4%,transparent)] p-3">
                      <FormAction label="Changer le statut" icon={ClipboardCheckIcon} size="sm" fields={statuerFields} onSubmit={(b) => statuerCandidature(str(r.id), b)} successMessage="Statut mis à jour" onDone={ctx.reload} />
                      <FormAction
                  label="Inscrire comme élève"
                  icon={UserCheckIcon}
                  size="sm"
                  variant="primary"
                  fields={convertirFields(anneeId)}
                  onSubmit={(b) => convertirCandidature(str(r.id), b)}
                  successMessage="Élève inscrit"
                  showResult
                  onDone={ctx.reload} />
                    </div>
                    <AsyncBlock load={() => getCandidature(str(r.id))} deps={[str(r.id)]}>
                      {(d) => <ObjectView data={d} />}
                    </AsyncBlock>
                  </div>
            } /> :

          <section className="ecole_m-card p-5 md:p-6" aria-labelledby="places-title">
                <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-3">
                  <span
                className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg"
                style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${COLOR} 14%, transparent)`, color: COLOR }}>
                    <GaugeIcon className="h-[18px] w-[18px]" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </span>
                  <div>
                    <h2 id="places-title" className="text-[15px] font-semibold leading-tight">Places disponibles</h2>
                    <p className="ecole_m-muted text-[13px]">Effectifs inscrits par rapport à la capacité de chaque classe</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <RemplissageList anneeId={anneeId} />
              </section>
          }
          </TabTransition>
        }
      </WithAnnee>
    </div>);
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: pages/personnes/Eleves.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { GraduationCapIcon, UserPlusIcon, UsersIcon, UserXIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { creerEleve, listerEleves, listerElevesSansClasse } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useDebounced } from "../../hooks/useAsync";
import { ModuleHero } from "../../components/layout/ModuleHero";
import { ResourceView } from "../../components/layout/ResourceView";
import { WithAnnee } from "../../components/layout/WithAnnee";
import { PivotTabs } from "../../components/ui/PivotTabs";
import { TabTransition } from "../../components/ui/TabTransition";
import { SearchInput } from "../../components/ui/SearchInput";
import { LoadPicker } from "../../components/ui/LoadPicker";
import { FormAction } from "../../components/form/FormAction";
import { EleveDetail } from "../../components/eleves/EleveDetail";
import { InscriptionResult } from "../../components/eleves/InscriptionResult";
import { eleveCreateFields } from "../../data/fields/eleves";
import { STATUT_ELEVE } from "../../data/enums";
import type { Column } from "../../types/ui";
import { fromEnum } from "../../utils/options";
import { fullName, initials, str } from "../../utils/format";

const COLOR = "#0F7B6C";

const nameColumn: Column = {
  key: "nom",
  label: "Élève",
  render: (r) =>
  <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
      <span
      className="grid h-8 w-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full text-[12px] font-semibold"
      style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${COLOR} 14%, transparent)`, color: COLOR }}>
        {initials(fullName(r))}
      </span>
      <span className="min-w-0">
        <span className="block truncate font-medium">{fullName(r)}</span>
        {str(r.matricule) ? <span className="ecole_m-muted block truncate text-[12px]">{str(r.matricule)}</span> : null}
      </span>
    </span>
};

export function Eleves() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const tab = params.get("tab") ?? "tous";
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [statut, setStatut] = useState("");
  const dq = useDebounced(q);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <ModuleHero icon={GraduationCapIcon} color={COLOR} title="Élèves" subtitle="Dossiers, inscriptions, scolarité et suivi" />

      <PivotTabs
        layoutKey="eleves"
        label="Rubriques des élèves"
        tabs={[
        { id: "tous", label: "Tous les élèves", icon: UsersIcon },
        { id: "sans-classe", label: "Sans classe", icon: UserXIcon }]
        }
        value={tab}
        onChange={(t) => setParams({ tab: t })} />

      <WithAnnee>
        {(anneeId) =>
        <TabTransition id={tab}>
            {tab === "tous" ?
          <ResourceView
            load={() => listerEleves({ q: dq || undefined, statut: statut || undefined })}
            deps={[dq, statut]}
            columns={[{ key: "matricule" }, nameColumn, { key: "sexe" }, { key: "date_naissance" }, { key: "statut" }]}
            toolbar={
            <>
                    <SearchInput value={q} onChange={setQ} placeholder="Nom, prénom ou matricule" />
                    <LoadPicker label="Statut" value={statut} onChange={setStatut} options={fromEnum(STATUT_ELEVE)} placeholder="Tous" clearable width="sm:w-52" />
                  </>
            }
            actions={(reload) =>
            <FormAction
              label="Nouvel élève"
              icon={UserPlusIcon}
              variant="primary"
              description="L'élève est créé et inscrit directement dans sa classe."
              fields={eleveCreateFields(anneeId)}
              modalSize="lg"
              onSubmit={creerEleve}
              successMessage="Élève inscrit"
              showResult
              renderResult={(r) => <InscriptionResult result={r} anneeId={anneeId} />}
              onDone={reload} />
            }
            emptyTitle="Aucun élève trouvé"
            emptyIcon={GraduationCapIcon}
            wide
            detailTitle={fullName}
            detailSubtitle={(r) => `Matricule ${str(r.matricule)}`}
            renderDetail={(r, ctx) => <EleveDetail eleve={r} anneeId={anneeId} onChanged={ctx.reload} />} /> :

          <ResourceView
            load={listerElevesSansClasse}
            deps={[]}
            columns={[{ key: "matricule" }, nameColumn, { key: "statut" }]}
            emptyTitle="Tous les élèves ont une classe"
            wide
            detailTitle={fullName}
            detailSubtitle={(r) => `Matricule ${str(r.matricule)}`}
            renderDetail={(r, ctx) => <EleveDetail eleve={r} anneeId={anneeId} onChanged={ctx.reload} />} />
          }
          </TabTransition>
        }
      </WithAnnee>
    </div>);
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: pages/personnes/Enseignants.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { BookOpenIcon, PresentationIcon, UserPlusIcon, UsersIcon, UserCheckIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { creerEnseignant, listerClassesTitulaires, listerEnseignants, listerEnseignantsParMatiere } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useDebounced } from "../../hooks/useAsync";
import { ModuleHero } from "../../components/layout/ModuleHero";
import { ResourceView } from "../../components/layout/ResourceView";
import { WithAnnee } from "../../components/layout/WithAnnee";
import { PivotTabs } from "../../components/ui/PivotTabs";
import { TabTransition } from "../../components/ui/TabTransition";
import { SearchInput } from "../../components/ui/SearchInput";
import { LoadPicker } from "../../components/ui/LoadPicker";
import { FormAction } from "../../components/form/FormAction";
import { EnseignantDetail } from "../../components/enseignants/EnseignantDetail";
import { enseignantCreateFields } from "../../data/fields/personnel";
import { AFFICHAGE_ACTIF } from "../../data/enums";
import { fromEnum } from "../../utils/options";
import { fullName, initials, str } from "../../utils/format";

const COLOR = "#8764B8";

export function Enseignants() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const tab = params.get("tab") ?? "equipe";
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [actif, setActif] = useState("true");
  const dq = useDebounced(q);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <ModuleHero icon={PresentationIcon} color={COLOR} title="Enseignants" subtitle="Équipe pédagogique, affectations et charge horaire" />

      <PivotTabs
        layoutKey="enseignants"
        label="Rubriques des enseignants"
        tabs={[
        { id: "equipe", label: "Équipe", icon: UsersIcon },
        { id: "matieres", label: "Par matière", icon: BookOpenIcon },
        { id: "titulaires", label: "Titulaires de classe", icon: UserCheckIcon }]
        }
        value={tab}
        onChange={(t) => setParams({ tab: t })} />

      <WithAnnee>
        {(anneeId) =>
        <TabTransition id={tab}>
            {tab === "equipe" &&
          <ResourceView
            load={() => listerEnseignants({ q: dq || undefined, actif_seulement: actif === "true" })}
            deps={[dq, actif]}
            columns={[
            {
              key: "nom",
              label: "Enseignant",
              render: (r) =>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <span
                  className="grid h-8 w-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full text-[12px] font-semibold"
                  style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${COLOR} 14%, transparent)`, color: COLOR }}>
                        {initials(fullName(r))}
                      </span>
                      <span className="font-medium">{fullName(r)}</span>
                    </span>
            },
            { key: "specialite_principale", label: "Spécialité" },
            { key: "telephone" },
            { key: "nb_affectations_actives", label: "Matières confiées" },
            {
              key: "actif",
              render: (r) =>
              Number(r.actif) === 1 ?
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] font-medium text-[var(--em-accent)]">
                        <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--em-accent)]" aria-hidden="true" />
                        Actif
                      </span> :
              <span className="ecole_m-muted inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-[var(--em-border)] px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] font-medium">
                        <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-current opacity-60" aria-hidden="true" />
                        Inactif
                      </span>
            }]
            }
            toolbar={
            <>
                    <SearchInput value={q} onChange={setQ} placeholder="Nom ou spécialité" />
                    <LoadPicker label="Affichage" value={actif} onChange={setActif} options={fromEnum(AFFICHAGE_ACTIF)} width="sm:w-48" searchable={false} />
                  </>
            }
            actions={(reload) =>
            <FormAction
              label="Nouvel enseignant"
              icon={UserPlusIcon}
              variant="primary"
              fields={enseignantCreateFields(anneeId)}
              modalSize="xl"
              onSubmit={creerEnseignant}
              successMessage="Enseignant créé"
              showResult
              onDone={reload} />
            }
            emptyTitle="Aucun enseignant"
            emptyIcon={PresentationIcon}
            wide
            detailTitle={fullName}
            detailSubtitle={(r) => str(r.poste)}
            renderDetail={(r, ctx) => <EnseignantDetail enseignant={r} anneeId={anneeId} onChanged={ctx.reload} />} />
          }

            {tab === "matieres" &&
          <ResourceView load={() => listerEnseignantsParMatiere(anneeId)} deps={[anneeId]} emptyTitle="Aucune matière attribuée" detailTitle={(r) => str(r.libelle || r.matiere)} />
          }

            {tab === "titulaires" &&
          <ResourceView load={() => listerClassesTitulaires(anneeId)} deps={[anneeId]} emptyTitle="Aucune classe avec titulaire" detailTitle={(r) => str(r.libelle || r.classe_libelle)} />
          }
          </TabTransition>
        }
      </WithAnnee>
    </div>);
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: pages/personnes/Parents.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { KeyRoundIcon, LinkIcon, MailPlusIcon, TicketIcon, UserPlusIcon, UsersIcon } from "lucide-react";
import {
  creerCompteParent,
  genererCodeActivationParent,
  genererCodeEnveloppeParent,
  getCodeEnveloppeParent,
  getParent,
  lierEnfantParent,
  lierParentEleve,
  listerCodesEnveloppeParent,
  listerEnfantsParent,
  listerParents } from
"../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useDebounced, useTick } from "../../hooks/useAsync";
import { ModuleHero } from "../../components/layout/ModuleHero";
import { ResourceView } from "../../components/layout/ResourceView";
import { Section } from "../../components/layout/Section";
import { SearchInput } from "../../components/ui/SearchInput";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../../components/data/AsyncBlock";
import { MiniTable } from "../../components/data/MiniTable";
import { ObjectView } from "../../components/data/ObjectView";
import { EmptyState } from "../../components/data/States";
import { FormAction } from "../../components/form/FormAction";
import { codeActivationFields, lierEnfantFields, parentCreateFields, relationFields } from "../../data/fields/personnel";
import { F } from "../../utils/fieldBuilders";
import { fullName, initials, str } from "../../utils/format";

const COLOR = "#C239B3";

function ParentDetail({ parentId }: {parentId: string;}) {
  const [tick, bump] = useTick();

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)] p-4">
        <AsyncBlock load={() => getParent(parentId)} deps={[parentId]}>
          {(p) => <ObjectView data={p} hide={["nom", "prenom"]} />}
        </AsyncBlock>
      </div>

      <Section
        title="Enfants"
        actions={
        <>
            <FormAction label="Rattacher par code" icon={KeyRoundIcon} size="sm" fields={lierEnfantFields} onSubmit={(b) => lierEnfantParent(parentId, b)} successMessage="Enfant rattaché" showResult onDone={bump} />
            <FormAction label="Rattacher un élève" icon={LinkIcon} size="sm" fields={relationFields} onSubmit={(b) => lierParentEleve(parentId, b)} successMessage="Élève rattaché" onDone={bump} />
          </>
        }>
        <AsyncBlock load={() => listerEnfantsParent(parentId)} deps={[parentId, tick]} empty={<EmptyState compact title="Aucun enfant rattaché" />}>
          {(rows) =>
          <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)]">
              <MiniTable rows={rows} rowKey={(r, i) => str(r.eleve_id) || String(i)} />
            </div>
          }
        </AsyncBlock>
      </Section>

      <Section
        title="Code d'activation du compte"
        description="Code à remettre sous enveloppe au parent pour activer son accès."
        actions={
        <FormAction
          label="Générer un code"
          icon={TicketIcon}
          size="sm"
          fields={[F.bool("force_nouveau", "Remplacer le code existant")]}
          onSubmit={(b) => genererCodeEnveloppeParent(parentId, b.force_nouveau === true)}
          successMessage="Code généré"
          showResult
          onDone={bump} />
        }>
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <AsyncBlock load={() => getCodeEnveloppeParent(parentId)} deps={[parentId, tick]} isEmpty={(d) => !d}>
            {(c) =>
            <div className="space-y-3">
                {str(c.code) &&
              <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-[var(--em-accent)] bg-[var(--em-accent-soft)] px-4 py-5 text-center">
                    <p className="ecole_m-muted mb-1 text-[12px]">Code à remettre au parent</p>
                    <p className="select-all font-mono text-[24px] font-semibold tracking-[0.25em] text-[var(--em-accent)]">{str(c.code)}</p>
                  </div>
              }
                <ObjectView data={c} hide={["code"]} />
              </div>
            }
          </AsyncBlock>

          <AsyncBlock load={() => listerCodesEnveloppeParent(parentId)} deps={[parentId, tick]} empty={<></>}>
            {(rows) =>
            <div>
                <p className="ecole_m-muted mb-2 text-[12px] font-medium">Historique des codes</p>
                <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-[var(--em-border)]">
                  <MiniTable rows={rows} />
                </div>
              </div>
            }
          </AsyncBlock>
        </div>
      </Section>
    </div>);
}

export function Parents() {
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const dq = useDebounced(q);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <ModuleHero icon={UsersIcon} color={COLOR} title="Parents" subtitle="Comptes des familles et liens avec les élèves" />

      <ResourceView
        load={() => listerParents(dq || undefined)}
        deps={[dq]}
        columns={[
        {
          key: "nom",
          label: "Parent",
          render: (r) =>
          <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <span
              className="grid h-8 w-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full text-[12px] font-semibold"
              style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${COLOR} 14%, transparent)`, color: COLOR }}>
                  {initials(fullName(r))}
                </span>
                <span className="font-medium">{fullName(r)}</span>
              </span>
        },
        { key: "telephone" },
        { key: "email" },
        { key: "actif" }]
        }
        toolbar={<SearchInput value={q} onChange={setQ} placeholder="Nom, téléphone ou e-mail" />}
        actions={(reload) =>
        <>
            <FormAction label="Code d'activation libre" icon={MailPlusIcon} fields={codeActivationFields} onSubmit={genererCodeActivationParent} successMessage="Code généré" showResult />
            <FormAction label="Nouveau parent" icon={UserPlusIcon} variant="primary" fields={parentCreateFields} onSubmit={creerCompteParent} successMessage="Compte créé" showResult onDone={reload} />
          </>
        }
        emptyTitle="Aucun parent"
        emptyIcon={UsersIcon}
        detailTitle={fullName}
        detailSubtitle={(r) => str(r.telephone)}
        renderDetail={(r) => <ParentDetail parentId={str(r.id)} />} />
    </div>);
}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: pages/services/Bibliotheque.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowRightLeftIcon, BookOpenIcon, BookPlusIcon, BookUpIcon, LibraryIcon, RotateCcwIcon, Trash2Icon } from "lucide-react";
import { creerOuvrage, emprunterOuvrage, listerEmprunts, listerOuvrages, retournerEmprunt, supprimerOuvrage } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { useDebounced } from "../../hooks/useAsync";
import { PageHeader } from "../../components/layout/PageHeader";
import { ResourceView } from "../../components/layout/ResourceView";
import { ContextBar } from "../../components/layout/ContextBar";
import { Tabs } from "../../components/ui/Tabs";
import { SearchInput } from "../../components/ui/SearchInput";
import { LoadPicker } from "../../components/ui/LoadPicker";
import { PageStage, Reveal, TabStage } from "../../components/ui/Motion";
import { ConfirmAction } from "../../components/form/ConfirmAction";
import { FormAction } from "../../components/form/FormAction";
import { empruntFields, ouvrageFields } from "../../data/fields/services";
import { STATUT_EMPRUNT } from "../../data/enums";
import { fromEnum } from "../../utils/options";
import { str } from "../../utils/format";

const COLOR = "#8E562E";

export function Bibliotheque() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const tab = params.get("tab") ?? "ouvrages";
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [statut, setStatut] = useState("");
  const dq = useDebounced(q);

  return (
    <PageStage>
      <Reveal>
        <PageHeader icon={LibraryIcon} color={COLOR} title="Bibliothèque" subtitle="Ouvrages et emprunts" />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <Tabs
          tabs={[
          { id: "ouvrages", label: "Ouvrages", icon: BookOpenIcon },
          { id: "emprunts", label: "Emprunts", icon: ArrowRightLeftIcon }]
          }
          value={tab}
          onChange={(t) => setParams({ tab: t })} />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <TabStage id={tab}>
          {tab === "ouvrages" ?
          <>
              <ContextBar icon={BookOpenIcon} color={COLOR} title="Fonds documentaire" text="Recherchez par titre, auteur ou ISBN. Prêtez un ouvrage directement depuis sa ligne.">
                <SearchInput value={q} onChange={setQ} placeholder="Titre, auteur, ISBN" />
              </ContextBar>
              <ResourceView
              load={() => listerOuvrages(dq || undefined)}
              deps={[dq]}
              columns={[
              {
                key: "titre",
                render: (r) =>
                <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
                        <span
                    className="grid h-9 w-7 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-[4px]"
                    style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${COLOR} 16%, transparent)`, color: COLOR }}>
                          <BookOpenIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                        </span>
                        <span className="truncate font-medium">{str(r.titre)}</span>
                      </span>
              },
              { key: "auteur" },
              { key: "categorie" },
              { key: "nb_exemplaires_total", label: "Exemplaires" },
              { key: "nb_exemplaires_disponibles", label: "Disponibles" }]
              }
              actions={(reload) =>
              <FormAction label="Nouvel ouvrage" icon={BookPlusIcon} variant="primary" fields={ouvrageFields} onSubmit={creerOuvrage} successMessage="Ouvrage ajouté" onDone={reload} />
              }
              emptyTitle="Aucun ouvrage"
              detailTitle={(r) => str(r.titre)}
              renderDetail={undefined}
              rowActions={(r, reload) =>
              <>
                    <FormAction
                  label="Prêter"
                  icon={BookUpIcon}
                  iconOnly
                  size="sm"
                  variant="subtle"
                  fields={empruntFields(str(r.id))}
                  onSubmit={(b) => emprunterOuvrage({ ...b, ouvrage_id: str(r.id) })}
                  successMessage="Prêt enregistré"
                  onDone={reload} />
                    <ConfirmAction
                  label="Supprimer"
                  icon={Trash2Icon}
                  iconOnly
                  size="sm"
                  variant="subtle"
                  danger
                  message={`Supprimer « ${str(r.titre)} » ?`}
                  confirmLabel="Supprimer"
                  run={() => supprimerOuvrage(str(r.id))}
                  successMessage="Ouvrage supprimé"
                  onDone={reload} />
                  </>
              } />
            </> :

          <>
              <ContextBar icon={ArrowRightLeftIcon} color={COLOR} title="Prêts en cours et historique" text="Enregistrez le retour d'un ouvrage depuis sa ligne.">
                <LoadPicker label="Statut" value={statut} onChange={setStatut} options={fromEnum(STATUT_EMPRUNT)} placeholder="Tous" clearable />
              </ContextBar>
              <ResourceView
              load={() => listerEmprunts({ statut: statut || undefined })}
              deps={[statut]}
              actions={(reload) =>
              <FormAction label="Nouveau prêt" icon={BookUpIcon} variant="primary" fields={empruntFields()} onSubmit={emprunterOuvrage} successMessage="Prêt enregistré" onDone={reload} />
              }
              emptyTitle="Aucun emprunt"
              detailTitle={() => "Emprunt"}
              rowActions={(r, reload) =>
              str(r.statut) !== "rendu" ?
              <ConfirmAction
                label="Retour"
                icon={RotateCcwIcon}
                iconOnly
                size="sm"
                variant="subtle"
                message="Confirmer le retour de l'ouvrage ?"
                confirmLabel="Confirmer"
                run={() => retournerEmprunt(str(r.id))}
                successMessage="Retour enregistré"
                onDone={reload} /> :
              null
              } />
            </>
          }
        </TabStage>
      </Reveal>
    </PageStage>);
}

# FICHIER: pages/services/Cartes.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { DownloadIcon, IdCardIcon, LayoutTemplateIcon, PrinterIcon, SlidersHorizontalIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { getCartesTemplates, getConfigCarte, lancerPlancheCartesClasse, plancheCartesClasseEtAttendre, setConfigCarte, telechargerPlancheCartes } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { PageHeader } from "../../components/layout/PageHeader";
import { WithAnnee } from "../../components/layout/WithAnnee";
import { ContextBar } from "../../components/layout/ContextBar";
import { PanelHeader } from "../../components/layout/PanelHeader";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../../components/data/AsyncBlock";
import { ObjectView } from "../../components/data/ObjectView";
import { ClassePicker } from "../../components/ui/ClassePicker";
import { PageStage, Reveal } from "../../components/ui/Motion";
import { FormAction } from "../../components/form/FormAction";
import { SchemaForm } from "../../components/form/SchemaForm";
import { F } from "../../utils/fieldBuilders";
import { optTaillesCarte, optTemplatesCarte } from "../../utils/options";
import { saveFile } from "../../utils/files";
import { str } from "../../utils/format";

const COLOR = "#005B70";

const configFields = [
F.select("template", "Modèle par défaut", { load: optTemplatesCarte }),
F.select("size", "Format", { load: optTaillesCarte }),
F.color("bg_color", "Couleur de fond"),
F.color("accent", "Couleur d'accent"),
F.color("accent_light", "Accent clair"),
F.color("text_color", "Couleur du texte"),
F.color("sec_color", "Couleur secondaire"),
F.text("font_family", "Police"),
F.number("font_size", "Taille du texte", { min: 1 }),
F.number("corner_r", "Arrondi des coins", { min: 0 }),
F.text("qr_payload_prefix", "Préfixe du code QR"),
F.bool("show_photo", "Afficher la photo"),
F.bool("show_qr", "Afficher le code QR"),
F.bool("show_signature_line", "Afficher la ligne de signature")];

export function Cartes() {
  const [classeId, setClasseId] = useState("");

  return (
    <PageStage>
      <Reveal>
        <PageHeader icon={IdCardIcon} color={COLOR} title="Cartes scolaires" subtitle="Modèles, réglages et impression par classe" />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <WithAnnee>
          {(anneeId) =>
          <div className="grid items-start gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.15fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
              <div className="space-y-5">
                <ContextBar
                icon={PrinterIcon}
                color={COLOR}
                title="Impression par classe"
                text="Générez la planche de cartes de tous les élèves d'une classe."
                steps={[{ label: "Classe", done: !!classeId }]}
                actions={
                classeId ?
                <>
                        <FormAction
                    label="Lancer en arrière-plan"
                    icon={PrinterIcon}
                    fields={[F.select("template", "Modèle", { load: optTemplatesCarte })]}
                    submitLabel="Lancer"
                    successMessage="Génération lancée"
                    showResult
                    onSubmit={(b) => lancerPlancheCartesClasse(classeId, str(b.template) || undefined)} />
                        <FormAction
                    label="Générer et attendre"
                    icon={PrinterIcon}
                    variant="primary"
                    fields={[F.select("template", "Modèle", { load: optTemplatesCarte })]}
                    submitLabel="Générer"
                    successMessage="Planche prête"
                    showResult
                    onSubmit={(b) => plancheCartesClasseEtAttendre(classeId, str(b.template) || undefined)} />
                      </> :
                null
                }>
                  <ClassePicker anneeId={anneeId} value={classeId} onChange={setClasseId} />
                </ContextBar>

                <section className="ecole_m-card flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 px-5 py-4">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <span
                    className="grid h-9 w-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg"
                    style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${COLOR} 14%, transparent)`, color: COLOR }}>
                      <DownloadIcon className="h-[18px] w-[18px]" aria-hidden="true" />
                    </span>
                    <div>
                      <h2 className="text-[14px] font-semibold leading-tight">Planche déjà générée</h2>
                      <p className="ecole_m-muted mt-0.5 text-[12.5px]">Téléchargez une planche à partir du nom de son fichier.</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <FormAction
                  label="Télécharger une planche"
                  icon={DownloadIcon}
                  fields={[F.text("fichier", "Nom du fichier de la planche", { required: true, full: true })]}
                  submitLabel="Télécharger"
                  successMessage="Planche téléchargée"
                  onSubmit={async (b) => {
                    const f = await telechargerPlancheCartes(str(b.fichier));
                    saveFile(f);
                    return f.filename;
                  }} />
                </section>

                <section className="ecole_m-card overflow-hidden">
                  <PanelHeader icon={LayoutTemplateIcon} color={COLOR} title="Modèles disponibles" />
                  <div className="p-5">
                    <AsyncBlock load={getCartesTemplates} deps={[]}>
                      {(d) => <ObjectView data={d} />}
                    </AsyncBlock>
                  </div>
                </section>
              </div>

              <section className="ecole_m-card overflow-hidden xl:sticky xl:top-4">
                <PanelHeader icon={SlidersHorizontalIcon} color={COLOR} title="Réglages des cartes" />
                <div className="p-5">
                  <AsyncBlock load={getConfigCarte} deps={[]} isEmpty={() => false}>
                    {(c) =>
                  <SchemaForm fields={configFields} initial={c ?? undefined} submitLabel="Enregistrer" successMessage="Réglages enregistrés" onSubmit={setConfigCarte} />
                  }
                  </AsyncBlock>
                </div>
              </section>
            </div>
          }
        </WithAnnee>
      </Reveal>
    </PageStage>);
}

          load={() => listerSanctionsEleve(eleveId)}

          <FormAction label="Nouvelle sanction" icon={ShieldAlertIcon} variant="primary" fields={sanctionFields(true)} onSubmit={creerSanction} successMessage="Sanction enregistrée" onDone={reload} />
          }
          emptyTitle="Aucune sanction pour cet élève"
          detailTitle={() => "Sanction"} />
      </Reveal>
    </PageStage>);
}

# FICHIER: pages/services/Finance.tsx

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { CheckIcon, ClockIcon, FolderTreeIcon, PlusIcon, ReceiptIcon, SettingsIcon, ShoppingCartIcon, TableIcon, WalletIcon, XIcon } from "lucide-react";
import {
  approuverDemandeAchat,
  creerCategorieDepense,
  creerDemandeAchat,
  creerDepense,
  getCategoriesDepenses,
  getConfigFinance,
  listerDemandesAchat,
  listerDepenses,
  listerPaiementsEnAttente,
  rejeterPaiement,
  setConfigFinance,
  validerPaiement } from
"../../lib/api_ecole";
import { PageHeader } from "../../components/layout/PageHeader";
import { ResourceView } from "../../components/layout/ResourceView";
import { WithAnnee } from "../../components/layout/WithAnnee";
import { ContextBar } from "../../components/layout/ContextBar";
import { PanelHeader } from "../../components/layout/PanelHeader";
import { Tabs } from "../../components/ui/Tabs";
import { LoadPicker } from "../../components/ui/LoadPicker";
import { PageStage, Reveal, TabStage } from "../../components/ui/Motion";
import { AsyncBlock } from "../../components/data/AsyncBlock";
import { ObjectView } from "../../components/data/ObjectView";
import { MiniTable } from "../../components/data/MiniTable";
import { PersonCell } from "../../components/data/PersonCell";
import { ConfirmAction } from "../../components/form/ConfirmAction";
import { FormAction } from "../../components/form/FormAction";
import { SchemaForm } from "../../components/form/SchemaForm";
import { GrillesPanel, useOptionsGrille } from "../../components/finance/GrillesPanel";
import { achatFields, categorieDepenseFields, configFinanceFields, depenseFields } from "../../data/fields/services";
import { STATUT_DEMANDE_ACHAT } from "../../data/enums";
import { F, req } from "../../utils/fieldBuilders";
import { fromEnum } from "../../utils/options";
import { arr, str } from "../../utils/format";

const COLOR = "#1B7F3B";

const TABS = [
{ id: "attente", label: "Paiements à valider", icon: ClockIcon },
{ id: "grilles", label: "Frais scolaires", icon: TableIcon },
{ id: "depenses", label: "Dépenses", icon: ReceiptIcon },
{ id: "achats", label: "Demandes d'achat", icon: ShoppingCartIcon },
{ id: "categories", label: "Catégories", icon: FolderTreeIcon },
{ id: "config", label: "Réglages", icon: SettingsIcon }];

function ConfigPanel() {
  const o = useOptionsGrille();
  return (
    <section className="ecole_m-card mx-auto w-full max-w-4xl overflow-hidden">
      <PanelHeader icon={SettingsIcon} color={COLOR} title="Réglages financiers de l'établissement" />
      <div className="p-5 md:p-6">
        <AsyncBlock load={getConfigFinance} deps={[o.mois.length]} isEmpty={() => false}>
          {(c) =>
          <SchemaForm
            key={o.mois.length}
            fields={configFinanceFields(o.mois)}
            initial={c ?? undefined}
            submitLabel="Enregistrer les réglages"
            successMessage="Réglages enregistrés"
            onSubmit={setConfigFinance} />
          }
        </AsyncBlock>
      </div>
    </section>);
}

export function Finance() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const tab = params.get("tab") ?? "attente";
  const [statut, setStatut] = useState("");

  return (
    <PageStage>
      <Reveal>
        <PageHeader icon={WalletIcon} color={COLOR} title="Finance" subtitle="Encaissements, frais scolaires et dépenses" />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <Tabs tabs={TABS} value={tab} onChange={(t) => setParams({ tab: t })} />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <WithAnnee>
          {(anneeId) =>
          <TabStage id={tab}>
              {tab === "attente" &&
            <>
                  <ContextBar
                icon={ClockIcon}
                color={COLOR}
                title="Paiements déclarés en attente"
                text="Validez ou rejetez chaque paiement depuis sa ligne." />
                  <ResourceView
                load={listerPaiementsEnAttente}
                deps={[]}
                columns={[
                { key: "eleve", label: "Élève", render: (r) => <PersonCell name={str(r.eleve)} color={COLOR} /> },
                { key: "montant_fcfa", label: "Montant" },
                { key: "mode_paiement", label: "Mode" },
                { key: "reference_transaction", label: "Référence" },
                { key: "date_paiement", label: "Date" }]
                }
                emptyTitle="Aucun paiement en attente"
                detailTitle={(r) => str(r.eleve)}
                rowActions={(r, reload) =>
                <>
                        <ConfirmAction
                    label="Valider"
                    icon={CheckIcon}
                    iconOnly
                    size="sm"
                    variant="subtle"
                    message={`Valider le paiement de ${str(r.eleve)} ?`}
                    confirmLabel="Valider"
                    run={() => validerPaiement(str(r.id))}
                    successMessage="Paiement validé"
                    onDone={reload} />
                        <FormAction
                    label="Rejeter"
                    icon={XIcon}
                    iconOnly
                    size="sm"
                    variant="subtle"
                    fields={[F.area("motif", "Motif du rejet", req)]}
                    submitLabel="Rejeter"
                    onSubmit={(b) => rejeterPaiement(str(r.id), b)}
                    successMessage="Paiement rejeté"
                    onDone={reload} />
                      </>
                } />
                </>
            }

              {tab === "grilles" && <GrillesPanel anneeId={anneeId} />}

              {tab === "depenses" &&
            <ResourceView
              load={listerDepenses}
              deps={[]}
              actions={(reload) =>
              <FormAction label="Nouvelle dépense" icon={PlusIcon} variant="primary" fields={depenseFields()} onSubmit={creerDepense} successMessage="Dépense enregistrée" onDone={reload} />
              }
              emptyTitle="Aucune dépense"
              detailTitle={(r) => str(r.libelle)} />
            }

              {tab === "achats" &&
            <>
                  <ContextBar icon={ShoppingCartIcon} color={COLOR} title="Demandes d'achat" text="Les demandes en attente peuvent être approuvées depuis leur ligne.">
                    <LoadPicker label="Statut" value={statut} onChange={setStatut} options={fromEnum(STATUT_DEMANDE_ACHAT)} placeholder="Tous" clearable />
                  </ContextBar>
                  <ResourceView
                load={() => listerDemandesAchat(statut || undefined)}
                deps={[statut]}
                actions={(reload) =>
                <FormAction label="Nouvelle demande" icon={PlusIcon} variant="primary" fields={achatFields} onSubmit={creerDemandeAchat} successMessage="Demande envoyée" onDone={reload} />
                }
                emptyTitle="Aucune demande d'achat"
                detailTitle={(r) => str(r.designation)}
                rowActions={(r, reload) =>
                str(r.statut) === "en_attente" ?
                <ConfirmAction
                  label="Approuver"
                  icon={CheckIcon}
                  iconOnly
                  size="sm"
                  variant="subtle"
                  message={`Approuver « ${str(r.designation)} » ?`}
                  confirmLabel="Approuver"
                  run={() => approuverDemandeAchat(str(r.id))}
                  successMessage="Demande approuvée"
                  onDone={reload} /> :
                null
                } />
                </>
            }

              {tab === "categories" &&
            <ResourceView
              load={getCategoriesDepenses}
              deps={[]}
              columns={[
              {
                key: "code",
                render: (r) =>
                <span
                  className="inline-flex rounded-md px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[12px] font-medium"
                  style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${COLOR} 10%, transparent)`, color: COLOR }}>
                        {str(r.code)}
                      </span>
              },
              { key: "libelle", render: (r) => <span className="font-medium">{str(r.libelle)}</span> },
              { key: "compte_comptable_ohada", label: "Compte" }]
              }
              actions={(reload) =>
              <FormAction label="Nouvelle catégorie" icon={PlusIcon} variant="primary" fields={categorieDepenseFields} onSubmit={creerCategorieDepense} successMessage="Catégorie créée" onDone={reload} />
              }
              emptyTitle="Aucune catégorie"
              detailTitle={(r) => str(r.libelle)}
              renderDetail={(r) =>
              <div className="space-y-4">
                      <ObjectView data={r} hide={["enfants"]} />
                      {arr(r.enfants).length > 0 && <MiniTable rows={arr(r.enfants)} />}
                    </div>
              } />
            }

              {tab === "config" && <ConfigPanel />}
            </TabStage>
          }
        </WithAnnee>
      </Reveal>
    </PageStage>);
}

# FICHIER: pages/services/Infirmerie.tsx
################################################################################

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { BellRingIcon, ClipboardPlusIcon, HeartPulseIcon, ShieldPlusIcon, StethoscopeIcon, SyringeIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { creerProtocoleUrgence, enregistrerVisiteInfirmerie, getVaccinationsARelancer, listerVisitesInfirmerie, notifierUrgenceSante } from "../../lib/api_ecole";
import { PageHeader } from "../../components/layout/PageHeader";
import { ResourceView } from "../../components/layout/ResourceView";
import { ContextBar } from "../../components/layout/ContextBar";
import { Tabs } from "../../components/ui/Tabs";
import { LoadPicker } from "../../components/ui/LoadPicker";
import { PageStage, Reveal, TabStage } from "../../components/ui/Motion";
import { FormAction } from "../../components/form/FormAction";
import { protocoleFields, urgenceFields, visiteFields } from "../../data/fields/eleves";
import { optEleves } from "../../utils/options";

const COLOR = "#D13438";

export function Infirmerie() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const tab = params.get("tab") ?? "visites";
  const [eleveId, setEleveId] = useState("");
  const [jours, setJours] = useState("30");

  return (
    <PageStage>
      <Reveal>
        <PageHeader
          icon={HeartPulseIcon}
          color={COLOR}
          title="Infirmerie"
          subtitle="Visites, protocoles d'urgence et vaccinations"
          actions={
          <>
              <FormAction label="Protocole d'urgence" icon={ShieldPlusIcon} fields={protocoleFields(true)} modalSize="lg" onSubmit={creerProtocoleUrgence} successMessage="Protocole enregistré" />
              <FormAction label="Alerte santé" icon={BellRingIcon} variant="danger" fields={urgenceFields(true)} onSubmit={notifierUrgenceSante} successMessage="Alerte envoyée" showResult />
            </>
          } />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <Tabs
          tabs={[
          { id: "visites", label: "Visites", icon: StethoscopeIcon },
          { id: "vaccins", label: "Vaccins à relancer", icon: SyringeIcon }]
          }
          value={tab}
          onChange={(t) => setParams({ tab: t })} />
      </Reveal>

      <Reveal>
        <TabStage id={tab}>
          {tab === "visites" ?
          <>
              <ContextBar icon={StethoscopeIcon} color={COLOR} title="Registre des visites" text="Affichez toutes les visites ou celles d'un seul élève.">
                <LoadPicker label="Élève" value={eleveId} onChange={setEleveId} load={optEleves} searchable clearable placeholder="Tous les élèves" width="sm:w-80" />
              </ContextBar>
              <ResourceView
              load={() => listerVisitesInfirmerie(eleveId || undefined)}
              deps={[eleveId]}
              actions={(reload) =>
              <FormAction label="Nouvelle visite" icon={ClipboardPlusIcon} variant="primary" fields={visiteFields(true)} modalSize="lg" onSubmit={enregistrerVisiteInfirmerie} successMessage="Visite enregistrée" onDone={reload} />
              }
              emptyTitle="Aucune visite"
              detailTitle={() => "Visite à l'infirmerie"} />
            </> :

          <>
              <ContextBar icon={SyringeIcon} color={COLOR} title="Vaccinations à relancer" text="Vaccins dont l'échéance arrive dans le nombre de jours indiqué.">
                <label className="w-full sm:w-48">
                  <span className="ecole_m-label">Échéance sous (jours)</span>
                  <input type="number" min={1} className="ecole_m-input" value={jours} onChange={(e) => setJours(e.target.value)} />
                </label>
              </ContextBar>
              <ResourceView
              load={() => getVaccinationsARelancer(Number(jours) || undefined)}
              deps={[jours]}
              emptyTitle="Aucun vaccin à relancer"
              detailTitle={() => "Vaccination"} />
            </>
          }
        </TabStage>
      </Reveal>
    </PageStage>);
}

const COLOR = "#486860";

function NameCell({ icon: Icon, label }: {icon: typeof DoorOpenIcon;label: string;}) {
  return (
    <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
      <span
        className="grid h-8 w-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg"
        style={{ background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${COLOR} 14%, transparent)`, color: COLOR }}>
        <Icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden="true" />
      </span>
      <span className="truncate font-medium">{label}</span>

export function Patrimoine() {

          { id: "materiel", label: "Matériel pédagogique", icon: BoxesIcon },
          { id: "catalogue", label: "Catalogue", icon: PackageSearchIcon }]
          }
          value={tab}
          onChange={(t) => setParams({ tab: t })} />

      <Reveal>
        <TabStage id={tab}>
          {tab === "salles" &&
          <ResourceView
            load={listerSalles}
            deps={[]}
            actions={(reload) =>
            <FormAction label="Nouvelle salle" icon={PlusIcon} variant="primary" fields={salleFields} onSubmit={creerSalle} successMessage="Salle ajoutée" onDone={reload} />
            }
            emptyTitle="Aucune salle"
            emptyIcon={DoorOpenIcon}
            detailTitle={(r) => str(r.nom)} />
          }

          {tab === "materiel" &&
          <ResourceView
            load={listerMaterielPedagogique}
            deps={[]}
            actions={(reload) =>
            <FormAction label="Nouveau matériel" icon={PlusIcon} variant="primary" fields={materielPedaFields} modalSize="lg" onSubmit={creerMaterielPedagogique} successMessage="Matériel ajouté" onDone={reload} />
            }
            emptyTitle="Aucun matériel"
            emptyIcon={BoxesIcon}
            detailTitle={(r) => str(r.designation)} />
          }

          {tab === "catalogue" &&
          <ResourceView
            load={() => getCatalogueMaterielScolaire()}
            deps={[]}
            emptyTitle="Catalogue vide"
            emptyText="Chargez les catalogues communs depuis Paramètres › Initialisation."
            emptyIcon={PackageSearchIcon}
            detailTitle={(r) => str(r.libelle)} />
          }
        </TabStage>
      </Reveal>
    </PageStage>);
}
